14.25,4397.25,4528.5,4467.25,4538.5,4465.75,4331.5,4328.25,4324.0,4362.25,4510.0,4509.0,4465.75,4417.25,4262.25,4433.25,4517.25,4579.25,4657.25,4726.75,4783.0,4804.25,4807.0,4785.75,4795.25,4671.0,4817.0,4856.5,4870.25,4859.0,4804.25,4843.25,4806.0,4657.75,4743.75,4808.5,4868.5,4738.5,4893.75,4943.5,4942.25,4864.0,5004.0,5061.25,5058.25,5162.5,5155.0,5226.75,5325.75,5343.25,5372.0,5382.0,5417.25,5378.75,5438.5,5421.25,5359.0,5442.0,5580.5,5648.25,5689.0,5653.25,5792.25,5886.0,5740.5,5702.0,5812.25,5652.75,5655.75,5843.25,5918.75,5910.25,5898.0,5838.25,5801.25,5825.0,5988.25,5916.25,5974.0,5935.75,5982.5,6064.25,6099.75,6111.0,6215.25,6290.5,6309.25,6313.25,6413.5,6346.25,6340.0,6478.0,6483.75,6408.75,6667.75,6775.25,6845.75,7030.25,6755.75,6417.75,6786.75,6909.75,6804.75,7098.25,7044.0,6554.25,6594.0,6454.75,6640.75,6676.25,6669.75,6774.75,6960.0,6874.0,6960.25,7083.0,7151.5,7257.5,7222.75,7066.75,7233.5,7393.0,7360.0,7301.25,7402.0,7427.0,7387.75,7495.75,7661.25,7438.5,7544.75,7589.0,7655.25,7436.0,7172.25,7106.5,6893.25,6980.25,7031.25,6895.25,6531.0,6949.5,6623.5,6599.5,6257.25,6293.25,6434.0,6612.75,6792.75,6791.25,6877.75,6916.75,7063.75,7088.25,7156.0,7026.75,7270.35009765625,7368.75,7400.5,7604.25,7652.25,7712.5,7840.25,7865.0,7610.25,7511.5,7315.5,7133.5,7419.5,7478.25,7723.60986328125,7693.75,7851.5,7960.0,7842.75,8019.75,7702.25,7660.25,7611.75,7504.5,7690.75,7857.75,7886.0,7899.47998046875,7701.25,7767.25,7859.0,7880.75,8035.75,8160.25,8257.25,8318.75,8280.5,8417.5,8400.25,8494.0,8707.4599609375,8783.25,8810.0,8978.25,9174.5,9144.5,8997.75,9409.5,9632.25,9458.0,8454.0,8503.25,7915.75,7417.97998046875,7568.5,7522.75,8227.5,8809.0,8769.0,8718.0,9218.25,9096.5,9406.25,9560.25,9808.5,9645.0,10120.83984375,9865.5,10355.75,10837.25,10622.5,10459.0,10890.5,11122.75,11133.75,11562.0,11991.75,11548.75,11061.5,11144.8095703125,11136.5,11233.25,11724.75,11798.0,11663.5,11046.25,12075.0,11933.5,11905.75,12257.5,12526.0,12368.0,12800.0595703125,12704.5`;
  const c = closesStr.split(',').map(Number);
  const n = c.length;
  const r1 = new Array(n).fill(NaN);
  for (let i = 1; i < n; i++) r1[i] = Math.log(c[i] / c[i - 1]);

  function rollStd(win: number): number[] {
    const s = new Array(n).fill(NaN);
    for (let i = win; i < n; i++) {
      let m = 0; for (let k = i - win + 1; k <= i; k++) m += r1[k];
      m /= win;
      let v = 0; for (let k = i - win + 1; k <= i; k++) v += (r1[k] - m) ** 2;
      s[i] = Math.sqrt(v / (win - 1));
    }
    return s;
  }
  const sig8 = rollStd(8);   // valid i >= 8 (0-based; rolling 8, ddof=1)
  const sig52 = rollStd(52); // valid i >= 52
  // pooled pre-2021 exogenous constant (ddof=1 std of all weekly log returns)
  {
    const xs = r1.slice(1); const m = xs.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / xs.length;
    const v = xs.reduce((a, b) => a + (b - m) ** 2, 0) / (xs.length - 1);
    var SIGMA_POOLED = Math.sqrt(v);
  }
  const LOW = 0.01739;
  // the FROZEN 41-entry compression set (cross into compression)
  const entries: number[] = [];
  for (let e = 9; e < n; e++) {
    if (!isNaN(sig8[e]) && !isNaN(sig8[e - 1]) && sig8[e] <= LOW && sig8[e - 1] > LOW) entries.push(e);
  }
  function median(xs: number[]): number {
    const s = [...xs].sort((a, b) => a - b); const m = Math.floor(s.length / 2);
    return s.length % 2 ? s[m] : (s[m - 1] + s[m]) / 2;
  }
  // significant week i (1..n-1): |r1[i]| >= 2.5 x scale[i-1], per cell yardstick
  type Cell = { name: string; isSignif: (i: number) => boolean };
  const cells: Cell[] = [
    { name: 'C0 baseline endogenous 2.5xsig_w(8)', isSignif: i => i >= 1 && i - 1 >= 8 && Math.abs(r1[i]) >= 2.5 * sig8[i - 1] },
    { name: 'C0D baseline on 52w-valid domain', isSignif: i => i - 1 >= 52 && Math.abs(r1[i]) >= 2.5 * sig8[i - 1] },
    { name: 'C1 pooled static 2.5xsigma_pooled', isSignif: i => i >= 1 && Math.abs(r1[i]) >= 2.5 * SIGMA_POOLED },
    { name: 'C1D pooled static on 52w domain', isSignif: i => i - 1 >= 52 && Math.abs(r1[i]) >= 2.5 * SIGMA_POOLED },
    { name: 'C2 slow trailing 2.5xsig52', isSignif: i => i - 1 >= 52 && Math.abs(r1[i]) >= 2.5 * sig52[i - 1] },
  ];
  const out: any = { n_weeks: n, sigma_pooled: SIGMA_POOLED, pooled_bar: 2.5 * SIGMA_POOLED, n_entries: entries.length, cells: [] };
  for (const cell of cells) {
    const sigIdx: number[] = [];
    for (let i = 1; i < n; i++) if (cell.isSignif(i)) sigIdx.push(i);
    const gaps: number[] = [];
    for (let k = 1; k < sigIdx.length; k++) gaps.push(sigIdx[k] - sigIdx[k - 1]);
    let episodes = 0, singletons = 0, run = 0;
    for (let k = 0; k < sigIdx.length; k++) {
      if (k === 0 || sigIdx[k] - sigIdx[k - 1] > 2) { if (run === 1) singletons++; if (run > 0) episodes++; run = 1; }
      else run++;
      if (k === sigIdx.length - 1) { if (run === 1) singletons++; if (run > 0) episodes++; }
    }
    const gaps2 = gaps.filter(g => g <= 2).length;
    // the 41 frozen compression windows, per this cell's yardstick
    let windows = 0, f0 = 0, f1 = 0, f2p = 0, within4 = 0;
    for (const e of entries) {
      let h = -1;
      for (let d = 1; d <= 12 && e + d < n; d++) if (cell.isSignif(e + d)) { h = d; break; }
      if (h < 0) continue;
      windows++;
      let further = 0, w4 = 0;
      for (let d = h + 1; d <= 12 && e + d < n; d++) {
        if (cell.isSignif(e + d)) { further++; if (d - h <= 4) w4 = 1; }
      }
      if (further === 0) f0++; else if (further === 1) f1++; else f2p++;
      within4 += w4;
    }
    out.cells.push({
      cell: cell.name, n_sig_weeks: sigIdx.length, n_gaps: gaps.length, median_gap: gaps.length ? median(gaps) : null,
      gaps_le_2: gaps2, episodes, singletons,
      windows_with_event: windows, further_0: f0, further_1: f1, further_2plus: f2p, further_within_4w: within4
    });
  }
  return out;
}
main();

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""smh_robust.py - the SMH definition-elasticity audit: how much of the
machine belongs to the phenomenon, and how much to the definition?

Shipped 12 Nov 2026 (session 47, PROGRESS block 55). Tooling ONLY: no
evaluation, no forward statistics, no tests on any sealed sample, no trades.
Every number below filters date < 2021-01-01 (DESIGN_END); the holdout 2021+
is SPENT and never touched.

The theorem card's headline (compression-entry cum12 0.634 vs the matched
climatology 0.441) rests on three definitional choices: an 8-week rolling
std for sigma_w, a 2.5-sigma event bar, and the frozen cutpoint LOW = 0.01739
(labelled historically the "low-tercile cut" - in the current 8-week sig_w
distribution it sits at the 27.52nd percentile, which is why the region
occupancies tile 27.5/30.6/41.9 percent, not a third each). This audit
measures the claim's DEFINITION ELASTICITY over a small pre-committed grid,
one knob at a time, ALL cells reported, none selected:

  - the sigma window: 6/10/12 weeks around the frozen 8 (both with the
    absolute cut held at LOW, and with the cut re-matched to the frozen
    27.52nd percentile of each window's own sig_w distribution);
  - the event bar: 2.0/3.0 sigma around the frozen 2.5;
  - the compression cut: the 22.5th and 32.5nd percentile around the
    frozen 27.52nd (the cut re-quantiled, the bar and window held).

The audit's three readings (descriptive, pre-2021):
  1. THE GAP SIGN IS ROBUST. The entry-minus-climatology cum12 gap is
     POSITIVE in all 11 cells (0.106 to 0.222 around the frozen 0.193).
  2. THE FROZEN DEFINITION IS NOT THE GRID OPTIMUM. The largest gap in
     the grid belongs to the 12-week window at the absolute cut (0.222 >
     0.193); the frozen cell sits mid-grid. The headline is not a tuned
     artifact: nobody ran this grid before freezing.
  3. THE DISJOINT-BAND STRENGTH IS DEFINITION-LOCAL. The entry band sits
     fully above the climatology band in only 4 of 11 cells (including
     the frozen one); the "bands do not overlap" sentence may be quoted
     only with the frozen definition attached. The robust object is the
     positive gap, not the band separation.

Subcommands:
  status    print the definition-elasticity card (descriptive only)
  selftest  recompute every cell from the canonical pre-2021 data and
            assert equality with the embedded values, including the
            frozen-baseline anchor (41 entries, 26 events, 0.634 vs
            0.441) and the anti-tuning anchor (the frozen gap is not
            the grid maximum)

Session-60 note (12 Nov 2026): extended with the OBSERVATION-HORIZON grid (docs/smh_robustness_v0.md section 9), first executed by research/smh_horizon_ts_mirror.ts (H=12 anchor exact); the pandas horizon cells have NOT yet executed - same fix-before-freeze pre-declaration as below.

Session-61 note (12 Nov 2026): the horizon grid's first pandas run FAILED exactly as pre-declared (fix-before-freeze): every H > 12 cell returned the H=12 KM flat-extended, because horizon_cell reused smh_forecast's 12-frozen km_from_person_weeks. Fixed by authoring km_from_person_weeks_h (identical logic, range 1..H) inside this module; no constant, frozen value or TS-mirror number touched; after the fix all 5 horizon cells matched the TS mirror exactly. The session-60 readings stand as frozen.

Session-58 note (12 Nov 2026): extended with the LONE-PRINT yardstick
grid (docs/smh_robustness_v0.md section 8), first executed by the TS
mirror research/smh_loneprint_ts_mirror.ts (anchor exact); this pandas
counterpart's loneprint cells have NOT yet executed (no Python in that
sandbox). On the next Python-enabled reset, if the loneprint selftest

confirms them.

def stress_cell(win=8, rank=None):
    """One stress-grid cell -> the 8-tuple. The de-stress event is the
    crossing sig_w(t+1) <= cut; the machinery mirrors person_weeks_stress
    and the frozen stress KM (33 entries, 21 de-stresses, cum 0.636)."""
    from smh_forecast import HIGH
    df = pd.read_csv(CANONICAL, parse_dates=["date"])
    df["r1"] = np.log(df["close"] / df["close"].shift(1))
    df["sig_w"] = df["r1"].rolling(win).std(ddof=1)
    pre_all = df[df["date"] < DESIGN_END]
    cut = HIGH if rank is None else float(
        np.quantile(pre_all["sig_w"].dropna(), rank))
    df["destress_next"] = (df["sig_w"].shift(-1) <= cut).astype(float)
    df.loc[df["sig_w"].isna() | df["sig_w"].shift(-1).isna(),
           "destress_next"] = np.nan
    pre = df[df["date"] < DESIGN_END].copy().reset_index(drop=True)
    in_hi = pre["sig_w"] > cut
    prev = in_hi.shift(1).fillna(False).astype(bool)
    idx = pre.index[in_hi & (~prev) & pre["sig_w"].notna()].tolist()
    rows = []
    for i, t0 in enumerate(idx):
        for h in range(1, 13):
            if t0 + h > len(pre) - 2:
                break
            e = pre.loc[t0 + h - 1, "destress_next"]
            if np.isnan(e):
                break
            rows.append(dict(ep=i, h=h, event=int(e > 0)))
            if e > 0:
                break
    km = km_from_person_weeks(pd.DataFrame(rows))
    S, acc, cum12, se12 = 1.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0
    for h in range(1, 13):
        n, ev = km[h]
        if n > 0:
            S *= 1 - ev / n
            if ev and n - ev:
                acc += ev / (n * (n - ev))
        cum12 = 1 - S
        se12 = (S * S * acc) ** 0.5 if acc > 0 else 0.0
    n_ev = int(sum(v[1] for v in km.values()))
    lo = cum12 - Z95 * se12
    hi = cum12 + Z95 * se12
    return (len(idx), n_ev, round(cum12, 4), round(lo, 4), round(hi, 4),
            round(cum12 - 0.5, 4), lo > 0.5, round(cut, 5))


def all_stress_cells():
    cells = [("baseline (w8, HIGH)", stress_cell())]
    for w in WINDOWS_ABS:
        cells.append((f"abs-cut w{w}", stress_cell(win=w)))
    for w in WINDOWS_ABS:
        cells.append((f"rank-matched w{w}",
                      stress_cell(win=w, rank=SFROZEN_RANK)))
    for r in SCUT_RANKS:
        cells.append((f"cut-rank {r}", stress_cell(rank=r)))
    return cells


def build_series(win, bar):
    df = pd.read_csv(CANONICAL, parse_dates=["date"])
    df["r1"] = np.log(df["close"] / df["close"].shift(1))
    df["sig_w"] = df["r1"].rolling(win).std(ddof=1)
    df["ev_next"] = (df["r1"].shift(-1).abs() >= bar * df["sig_w"]).astype(float)
    df.loc[df["sig_w"].isna() | df["r1"].shift(-1).isna(), "ev_next"] = np.nan
    return df


def audit_cell(win=8, bar=2.5, rank=None):
    """One grid cell -> the 10-tuple, mirroring the frozen machinery."""
    df = build_series(win, bar)
    pre = df[df["date"] < DESIGN_END].copy().reset_index(drop=True)
    if rank is None:
        cut = LOW
    else:
        cut = float(np.quantile(pre["sig_w"].dropna(), rank))
    in_low = pre["sig_w"] <= cut
    prev = in_low.shift(1).fillna(False).astype(bool)
    idx = pre.index[in_low & (~prev) & pre["sig_w"].notna()].tolist()
    km = km_from_person_weeks(person_weeks(pre, idx))
    gw = greenwood_cum(km)
    ev = int(sum(v[1] for v in km.values()))
    ckm = km_from_person_weeks(
        person_weeks(pre, pre.index[pre["sig_w"].notna()].tolist()))
    cgw = greenwood_cum(ckm)
    lo = gw[12][0] - Z95 * gw[12][1]
    hi = gw[12][0] + Z95 * gw[12][1]
    clo = cgw[12][0] - Z95 * cgw[12][1]
    chi = cgw[12][0] + Z95 * cgw[12][1]
    return (len(idx), ev, round(gw[12][0], 4), round(lo, 4), round(hi, 4),
            round(cgw[12][0], 4), round(clo, 4), round(chi, 4),
            round(gw[12][0] - cgw[12][0], 4), lo > chi)


def all_cells():
    """The full pre-committed grid, recomputed."""
    cells = [("baseline", audit_cell())]
    for w in WINDOWS_ABS:
        cells.append((f"abs-cut w{w}", audit_cell(win=w)))
    for b in BARS:
        cells.append((f"bar {b}", audit_cell(bar=b)))
    for r in CUT_RANKS:
        cells.append((f"cut-rank {r}", audit_cell(rank=r)))
    for w in WINDOWS_ABS:
        cells.append((f"rank-matched w{w}",
                      audit_cell(win=w, rank=FROZEN_RANK)))
    return cells


def cmd_status():
    cells = all_cells()
    print("## SMH definition-elasticity audit (docs/smh_robustness_v0.md)")
    print("pre-2021 unsealed design bookkeeping only; all cells reported,")
    print("none selected; the frozen definition is unchanged by fiat.")
    print()
    print("cell                        n   ev   cum12  entry band      "
          "clim12  clim band      gap    disjoint")
    for name, c in cells:
        print(f"{name:<26} {c[0]:>3} {c[1]:>3}  {c[2]:.3f}  "
              f"[{c[3]:.3f}, {c[4]:.3f}]  {c[5]:.3f}  "
              f"[{c[6]:.3f}, {c[7]:.3f}]  {c[8]:.3f}  "
              f"{'YES' if c[9] else 'no'}")
    gaps = [c[8] for _, c in cells]
    n_disjoint = sum(1 for _, c in cells if c[9])
    base = cells[0][1]
    print()
    print("READINGS:")
    print(f"1. THE GAP SIGN IS ROBUST: positive in all {len(cells)} cells "
          f"(min {min(gaps):.3f}, max {max(gaps):.3f}, frozen "
          f"{base[8]:.3f}) - the entry-vs-climatology edge survives every")
    print("   perturbation in the pre-committed grid.")
    print("2. THE FROZEN DEFINITION IS NOT THE GRID OPTIMUM: the largest "
          "gap (0.222, the 12-week window at the absolute cut) is NOT")
    print("   the frozen cell - the headline was frozen before this grid "
          "existed, and it shows: no tuning signature.")
    print(f"3. THE DISJOINT-BAND STRENGTH IS DEFINITION-LOCAL: the entry "
          f"band clears the climatology band in only {n_disjoint} of "
          f"{len(cells)} cells (the frozen one included); the")
    print("   \"bands do not overlap\" sentence may be quoted only with "
          "the frozen definition attached.")
    print("4. BOOKKEEPING NOTE: the \"tercile cut\" label is historical - "
          "LOW = 0.01739 sits at the 27.52nd percentile of the 8-week")
    print("   pre-2021 sig_w distribution, hence the region occupancy "
          "27.5/30.6/41.9 percent; the cut is frozen as a NUMBER,")
    print("   not as a live quantile, and the forward protocol uses the "
          "number.")

    print("   compares matched rows, never bands).")
    print("CAVEATS: the frozen rival point estimate 0.4409 is unchanged")
