def cmd_status_entrydep():
    print()
    print("## THE ENTRY-SIDE DEPENDENCE AUDIT (session 67)")
    print("the CLAIM's own band construction: the entry KM band [0.4867,")
    print("0.7816] treats the 41 episodes as independent; one knob, the")
    print("entry-side dependence construction; the definition, the 41")
    print("entries and the rival frozen BY FIAT; all cells reported,")
    print("none selected; descriptive only.")
    print()
    c = entrydep_cells()
    print(f"C0 anchor: {c['C0']['entries']} entries, {c['C0']['pw']} "
          f"person-weeks, {c['C0']['events']} events, cum12 "
          f"{c['C0']['cum12']:.4f} band "
          f"[{c['C0']['band'][0]:.4f}, {c['C0']['band'][1]:.4f}]")
    for e in c["C1"]["eras"]:
        if e["entries"] == 0:
            print(f"  era {e['era']}: 0 entries")
        else:
            print(f"  era {e['era']}: {e['entries']} entries, "
                  f"{e['events']} events, cum12 {e['cum12']:.4f} "
                  f"[{e['band'][0]:.4f}, {e['band'][1]:.4f}]")
    print(f"C1 across-era: K={c['C1']['K']} mean {c['C1']['mean']:.4f} "
          f"sd {c['C1']['sd']:.4f} dependence-honest band "
          f"[{c['C1']['dep_band'][0]:.4f}, {c['C1']['dep_band'][1]:.4f}] "
          f"(vs rival era-band upper edge {RIVAL_ERA_BAND_UPPER})")
    print("C2 leave-one-era-out cum12: "
          + " ".join(f"{v:.4f}" for v in c["C2"])
          + f"  range [{c['loo_range'][0]:.4f}, {c['loo_range'][1]:.4f}]")
    print(f"C3 declustered: {c['C3']['clusters']} clusters, "
          f"{c['C3']['events']} events, cum12 {c['C3']['cum12']:.4f} "
          f"band [{c['C3']['band'][0]:.4f}, {c['C3']['band'][1]:.4f}]")
    print("READINGS: the POINT is era-stable (LOO range "
          f"{c['loo_range'][0]:.4f}-{c['loo_range'][1]:.4f}), the BAND is")
    print("not dependence-honest (the entry dep-band sits below the")
    print("rival's dependence-honest upper edge - the disjoint-band")
    print("sentence now carries BOTH attachments, definition and")
    print("dependence); the claim survives declustering; the kill lines")
    print("and the 57-of-104 paired proof are unaffected (matched rows).")
def cmd_selftest():
    problems = []
    cells = all_cells()
    frozen = dict([("baseline", BASELINE)] +
                  [(f"abs-cut w{w}", ABS_CUT[w]) for w in WINDOWS_ABS] +
                  [(f"bar {b}", BAR_CELLS[b]) for b in BARS] +
                  [(f"cut-rank {r}", RANK_CELLS[r]) for r in CUT_RANKS] +
                  [(f"rank-matched w{w}", RMATCH[w]) for w in WINDOWS_ABS])
    if len(cells) != len(frozen):
        problems.append(f"grid size {len(cells)} != {len(frozen)}")
    for name, got in cells:
        want = frozen[name]
        if got[0] != want[0] or got[1] != want[1]:
            problems.append(f"{name}: n/ev {got[0]}/{got[1]} != "
                            f"{want[0]}/{want[1]}")
        for i in range(2, 9):
            if abs(got[i] - want[i]) > 0.0015:
                problems.append(f"{name}: value[{i}] {got[i]} != {want[i]}")
        if got[9] != want[9]:
            problems.append(f"{name}: disjoint {got[9]} != {want[9]}")
        if got[8] <= 0:
            problems.append(f"{name}: gap {got[8]} not positive")
    # the frozen-baseline anchor: the audit's baseline cell IS the frozen
    # programme headline
    b = cells[0][1]
    if b[0] != 41 or b[1] != 26:
        problems.append(f"baseline {b[0]}/{b[1]} != frozen 41/26")
    if abs(b[2] - 0.634) > 0.0005 or abs(b[5] - 0.441) > 0.0005:
        problems.append(f"baseline cum12/clim12 {b[2]}/{b[5]} != 0.634/0.441")
    # the anti-tuning anchor: the frozen gap is NOT the grid maximum
    gaps = [c[8] for _, c in cells]
    if b[8] >= max(gaps):
        problems.append("frozen gap is the grid maximum: tuning signature")
    # the rank-matched cut at the frozen window reproduces LOW
    df = build_series(8, 2.5)
    pre = df[df["date"] < DESIGN_END]
    if abs(float(np.quantile(pre["sig_w"].dropna(), FROZEN_RANK)) - LOW) > 1e-4:
        problems.append("FROZEN_RANK does not reproduce the frozen LOW cut")
    # ---- the stress mirror cells ----
    from smh_forecast import HIGH
    scells = all_stress_cells()
    sfrozen = dict([("baseline (w8, HIGH)", SBASELINE)] +
                   [(f"abs-cut w{w}", SABS_CUT[w]) for w in WINDOWS_ABS] +
                   [(f"rank-matched w{w}", SRMATCH[w]) for w in WINDOWS_ABS] +
                   [(f"cut-rank {r}", SRANK_CELLS[r]) for r in SCUT_RANKS])
    if len(scells) != len(sfrozen):
        problems.append(f"stress grid size {len(scells)} != {len(sfrozen)}")
    for name, got in scells:
        want = sfrozen[name]
        if got[0] != want[0] or got[1] != want[1]:
            problems.append(f"stress {name}: n/ev {got[0]}/{got[1]} != "
                            f"{want[0]}/{want[1]}")
        for i in (2, 3, 4, 5):
            if abs(got[i] - want[i]) > 0.0015:
                problems.append(f"stress {name}: value[{i}] {got[i]} != "
                                f"{want[i]}")
        if got[6] != want[6]:
            problems.append(f"stress {name}: clears-coin {got[6]} != {want[6]}")
        if got[5] <= 0:
            problems.append(f"stress {name}: edge {got[5]} not positive")
    sb = scells[0][1]
    if sb[0] != 33 or sb[1] != 21:
        problems.append(f"stress baseline {sb[0]}/{sb[1]} != frozen 33/21")
    if abs(sb[2] - 0.636) > 0.0005:
        problems.append(f"stress baseline cum12 {sb[2]} != 0.636")
    sedges = [c[5] for _, c in scells]
    if sb[5] >= max(sedges):
        problems.append("frozen stress edge is the grid maximum: "
                        "tuning signature")
    # the stress frozen rank reproduces HIGH
    if abs(float(np.quantile(pre["sig_w"].dropna(), SFROZEN_RANK)) - HIGH) > 1e-4:
        problems.append("SFROZEN_RANK does not reproduce the frozen HIGH cut")
    # ---- the lone-print yardstick grid (session 58) ----
    lp = all_loneprint_cells()
    if len(lp) != len(LONEPRINT):
        problems.append(f"loneprint grid size {len(lp)} != {len(LONEPRINT)}")
    for name, got in lp:
        want = LONEPRINT.get(name)
        if want is None:
            problems.append(f"loneprint: unexpected cell {name}")
            continue
        for i in range(len(want)):
            if i == 2:
                if abs(got[i] - want[i]) > 0.01:
                    problems.append(f"loneprint {name}: median gap "
                                    f"{got[i]} != {want[i]}")
            elif got[i] != want[i]:
                problems.append(f"loneprint {name}: field[{i}] "
                                f"{got[i]} != {want[i]}")
        if got[4] and got[5] / got[4] < 0.77:
            problems.append(f"loneprint {name}: singleton share < 0.77")
    # the C0 anchor IS the frozen aftermath bookkeeping
    c0 = LONEPRINT["C0 baseline endogenous 2.5xsig_w(8)"]
    if c0[:6] != (47, 46, 18.5, 4, 43, 39) or c0[6:] != (26, 22, 3, 1, 1):
        problems.append("loneprint C0 anchor does not match the frozen "
                        "aftermath constants")
    # ---- the observation-horizon grid (session 60) ----
    hz = all_horizon_cells()
    if len(hz) != len(HORIZON_CELLS):
        problems.append(f"horizon grid size {len(hz)} != {len(HORIZON_CELLS)}")
    for H, got in hz:
        want = HORIZON_CELLS.get(H)
        if want is None:
            problems.append(f"horizon: unexpected cell H={H}")
            continue
        for i in range(len(want)):
            if isinstance(want[i], float):
                tol = 1.1e-3 if i in (3, 4, 7, 8) else 5e-4  # band fields
                if abs(got[i] - want[i]) > tol:
                    problems.append(f"horizon H={H}: field[{i}] "
                                    f"{got[i]} != {want[i]}")
            elif got[i] != want[i]:
                problems.append(f"horizon H={H}: field[{i}] "
                                f"{got[i]} != {want[i]}")
    # the H=12 cell IS the frozen bookkeeping (the standing anchor)
    h12 = HORIZON_CELLS[12]
    if h12[:6] != (327, 26, 0.6341, 0.487, 0.782, 5.0) or \
       (h12[6], h12[7], h12[8]) != (0.4409, 0.411, 0.471):
        problems.append("horizon H=12 anchor does not match the frozen "
                        "KM/climatology constants")
    # the gap is positive in every cell
    if any(c[9] <= 0 for c in HORIZON_CELLS.values()):
        problems.append("horizon: non-positive gap in some cell")
    # recorded structural facts: the frozen cell IS the grid gap maximum
    # (anti-tuning rests on the 12-week window predating this grid by
    # pre-registration, NOT on mid-grid placement - flagged in the doc),
    # the gap decays monotonically past 12, disjointness is definition-local
    gaps = {H: c[9] for H, c in HORIZON_CELLS.items()}
    if gaps[12] != max(gaps.values()):
        problems.append("horizon: recorded state changed - frozen gap no "
                        "longer the grid maximum (re-read the doc)")
    if not (gaps[12] > gaps[16] > gaps[20]):
        problems.append("horizon: recorded state changed - gap decay past "
                        "12 is no longer monotone")
    if {H for H, c in HORIZON_CELLS.items() if c[10]} != {12, 16}:
        problems.append("horizon: recorded state changed - disjointness "
                        "set is no longer {12, 16}")
    # ---- the climatology-counterfactual dependence grid (session 62) ----
    problems.extend(climato_problems())
    # ---- the entry-side dependence grid (session 67) ----
    problems.extend(entrydep_problems())
    if problems:
        print("SELFTEST FAIL")
        for p in problems:
            print("  -", p)
        return 1
    print("SELFTEST PASS: definition-elasticity audit reproduced from the "
          "canonical pre-2021 data: all 11 grid cells recomputed and "
          "matched (the frozen baseline 41 entries / 26 events / cum12 "
          "0.634 [0.487, 0.782] vs climatology 0.441 [0.411, 0.471], gap "
          "0.193; the window cells 6/10/12 under both cut conventions, "
          "the bar cells 2.0/3.0, the cut-percentile cells 22.5/32.5 "
          "percent); the gap positive in every cell (0.106-0.222); the "
          "frozen gap NOT the grid maximum (the anti-tuning anchor); "
          "disjointness definition-local (4 of 11 cells); the frozen rank "
          "reproducing the LOW cut exactly; AND the stress mirror's own "
          "9-cell grid (the frozen baseline 33 entries / 21 de-stresses "
          "/ cum12 0.636 [0.472, 0.801], edge over the coin 0.136; the "
          "window cells under both cut conventions, the cut-percentile "
          "cells 53/63 percent) matched with the edge positive in every "
          "cell (0.115-0.283), the frozen edge NOT the grid maximum "
          "(conservative, in the lower third), the band-clears-coin "
          "strength definition-local (5 of 9, not the frozen one), and "
          "the frozen rank reproducing the HIGH cut exactly; AND the "
          "lone-print yardstick grid reproduced (5 cells: the C0 anchor "
          "matching the frozen aftermath constants exactly 47/46/18.5/"
          "4/43/39 and windows 26 with further 22/3/1, the domain "
          "control, the pooled-static cells, the slow-trailing cell), "
          "with the singleton share >= 0.778 in every cell; AND the "
          "observation-horizon grid reproduced (5 cells: the H=12 "
          "anchor matching the frozen KM/climatology constants exactly "
          "327 person-weeks / 26 events / cum12 0.634 [0.487, 0.782] vs "
          "climatology 0.441 [0.411, 0.471], the H=8/10/16/20 cells "
          "matched), the gap positive in every cell, the recorded "
          "frozen-cell-at-grid-maximum state, the monotone gap decay "
          "past 12, and the {12, 16} disjointness set; AND the "
          "climatology-counterfactual dependence grid reproduced "
          "(the C0 anchor 0.4409 [0.411, 0.471] with independence "
          "SE 0.0154 exact, the tile cells 88/87 windows with SE "
          "0.053, the ten era blocks sd 0.094 with n_eff ~28 and "
          "30 percent of eras at or above the entry lower bound, "
          "the circular-shift seam diagnostic near-degenerate at "
          "sd 0.005 as pre-declared). All cells "
          "reported, none selected; descriptive only.")
    return 0


# ======================= THE LONE-PRINT YARDSTICK GRID =======================
# Added 12 Nov 2026 (session 58, PROGRESS block 66). Computes AFTERMATH
# v0's flagged endogeneity caveat (section 2): does "the first print is
# usually the only print" belong to the phenomenon or to the endogenous
# 2.5 x own-sig_w bar? One knob (the event-yardstick TYPE), bar 2.5
# everywhere, the 8-week sig_w / LOW cut / 41-entry set FROZEN in every
# cell; ALL 5 cells reported, none selected; descriptive only.
# Executed first by research/smh_loneprint_ts_mirror.ts (the C0 anchor
# reproduced the frozen aftermath constants exactly: 47/46/18.5/4/43/39,
# windows 26, further 22/3/1, within-4 = 1); this pandas counterpart runs
# on the next Python-enabled reset and asserts the same values.

LP_SIGMA_POOLED = 0.0325543487621479   # pre-2021 weekly log-return std, ddof=1
# cell tuple: (n_sig_weeks, n_gaps, median_gap, gaps_le_2, episodes,
#              singletons, windows_with_event, further_0, further_1,
#              further_2plus, further_within_4w)
LONEPRINT = {
    "C0 baseline endogenous 2.5xsig_w(8)": (47, 46, 18.5, 4, 43, 39, 26, 22, 3, 1, 1),
    "C0D baseline on 52w-valid domain": (45, 44, 18.0, 4, 41, 37, 26, 22, 3, 1, 1),
    "C1 pooled static 2.5xsigma_pooled": (29, 28, 3.0, 11, 18, 14, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0),
    "C1D pooled static on 52w domain": (16, 15, 6.0, 6, 10, 8, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0),
    "C2 slow trailing 2.5xsig52": (31, 30, 8.0, 6, 25, 20, 12, 7, 3, 2, 1),
}


def loneprint_frame():
    df = pd.read_csv(CANONICAL, parse_dates=["date"])
    df["r1"] = np.log(df["close"] / df["close"].shift(1))
    df["sig_w"] = df["r1"].rolling(8).std(ddof=1)    # valid i >= 8 (0-based)
    df["sig52"] = df["r1"].rolling(52).std(ddof=1)   # valid i >= 52
    pre = df[df["date"] < DESIGN_END].copy().reset_index(drop=True)
    xs = pre["r1"].dropna()
    pre["pooled"] = float(np.sqrt(
        ((xs - xs.mean()) ** 2).sum() / (len(xs) - 1)))
    return pre


def loneprint_cell(pre, yardstick):
    """One cell of the lone-print grid -> the 11-tuple.

    yardstick: 'endo8' (frozen endogenous, own domain), 'endo52d' (the
    same bar restricted to the 52-week-valid domain), 'pooled' (exogenous
    constant, own domain), 'pooled52d', 'trail52'.
    A significant week i (1..n-1) satisfies |r1[i]| >= 2.5 x scale[i-1],
    scale per the yardstick; the decision index i-1 must be in the
    yardstick's domain (52-week cells need sig52 valid at i-1).
    """
    sig = lambda i: abs(pre["r1"].iloc[i]) >= 2.5 * pre["scale"].iloc[i - 1]
    if yardstick == "endo8":
        pre["scale"] = pre["sig_w"]
        dom = lambda i: i - 1 >= 8
    elif yardstick == "endo52d":
        pre["scale"] = pre["sig_w"]
        dom = lambda i: i - 1 >= 52
    elif yardstick == "pooled":
        pre["scale"] = pre["pooled"]
        dom = lambda i: i >= 1
    elif yardstick == "pooled52d":
        pre["scale"] = pre["pooled"]
        dom = lambda i: i - 1 >= 52
    elif yardstick == "trail52":
        pre["scale"] = pre["sig52"]
        dom = lambda i: i - 1 >= 52
    else:
        raise ValueError(yardstick)
    n = len(pre)
    sig_idx = [i for i in range(1, n) if dom(i) and sig(i)]
    gaps = [b - a for a, b in zip(sig_idx, sig_idx[1:])]
    episodes = singletons = run = 0
    for k in range(len(sig_idx)):
        if k == 0 or sig_idx[k] - sig_idx[k - 1] > 2:
            if run == 1:
                singletons += 1
            if run > 0:
                episodes += 1
            run = 1
        else:
            run += 1
        if k == len(sig_idx) - 1:
            if run == 1:
                singletons += 1
            if run > 0:
                episodes += 1
    # the FROZEN 41-entry compression set, cross semantics
    in_low = pre["sig_w"] <= LOW
    prev = in_low.shift(1).fillna(False).astype(bool)
    entries = pre.index[in_low & (~prev) & pre["sig_w"].notna()].tolist()
    windows = f0 = f1 = f2p = within4 = 0
    for e in entries:
        h = -1
        for d in range(1, 13):
            if e + d >= n:
                break
            if dom(e + d) and sig(e + d):
                h = d
                break
        if h < 0:
            continue
        windows += 1
        further = 0
        w4 = 0
        for d in range(h + 1, 13):
            if e + d >= n:
                break
            if dom(e + d) and sig(e + d):
                further += 1
                if d - h <= 4:
                    w4 = 1
        if further == 0:
            f0 += 1
        elif further == 1:
            f1 += 1
        else:
            f2p += 1
        within4 += w4
    med = float(np.median(gaps)) if gaps else float("nan")
    return (len(sig_idx), len(gaps), round(med, 1),
            sum(1 for g in gaps if g <= 2), episodes, singletons,
            windows, f0, f1, f2p, within4)


def all_loneprint_cells():
    order = ["endo8", "endo52d", "pooled", "pooled52d", "trail52"]
    names = {"endo8": "C0 baseline endogenous 2.5xsig_w(8)",
             "endo52d": "C0D baseline on 52w-valid domain",
             "pooled": "C1 pooled static 2.5xsigma_pooled",
             "pooled52d": "C1D pooled static on 52w domain",
             "trail52": "C2 slow trailing 2.5xsig52"}
    return [(names[y], loneprint_cell(loneprint_frame(), y))
            for y in order]



# ======================= THE OBSERVATION-HORIZON GRID ========================
# Added 12 Nov 2026 (session 60, PROGRESS block 68). The fourth definition-
# elasticity audit: the three existing grids (compression window/bar/cut,
# stress window/cut, lone-print yardstick) all held the OBSERVATION
# HORIZON fixed at 12 weeks; this grid perturbs the horizon itself
# (H = 8/10/16/20 around the frozen 12), everything else FROZEN (8-week
# sig_w, 2.5-sigma bar, LOW cut 0.01739, cross-semantics entries).
# First executed by research/smh_horizon_ts_mirror.ts (the H=12 anchor
# reproduced the frozen constants exactly: 41 entries, 327 person-weeks,
# 26 first events, cum12 0.6341 [0.487, 0.782], climatology 0.4409
# [0.411, 0.471], median lag 5, cum4 0.2683, pool 1050); this pandas
# counterpart runs on the next Python-enabled reset inside the existing
# smh_robust verify_all check. Fix-before-freeze pre-declared: if these
# assertions fail at first run, FIX THE PANDAS SIDE, never the frozen
# TS-embedded values, unless the failure is a genuine recomputation
# mismatch - then the record, not the code, moves first.
# Descriptive only; all 5 cells reported, none selected; no cell feeds
# the forward protocol, the kill lines, the probabilities or any
# instrument; the frozen 12-week definition is unchanged BY FIAT.

HORIZON_GRID = [8, 10, 12, 16, 20]
# cell tuple: (person_weeks, events_within_H, cum_H, band_lo, band_hi,
#              median_lag, clim_cum_H, cband_lo, cband_hi, gap, disjoint)
HORIZON_CELLS = {
    8:  (250, 20, 0.4878, 0.335, 0.641, 4.0, 0.3122, 0.284, 0.340, 0.1757, False),
    10: (291, 22, 0.5366, 0.384, 0.689, 4.5, 0.3775, 0.348, 0.407, 0.1591, False),
    12: (327, 26, 0.6341, 0.487, 0.782, 5.0, 0.4409, 0.411, 0.471, 0.1933, True),
    16: (378, 30, 0.7317, 0.596, 0.867, 6.0, 0.5590, 0.529, 0.589, 0.1727, True),
    20: (420, 32, 0.7805, 0.654, 0.907, 6.0, 0.6551, 0.626, 0.684, 0.1254, False),
}


def person_weeks_h(pre, idx, H):
    """person_weeks with a horizon parameter (smh_forecast's is 12-frozen)."""
    rows = []
    for i, t0 in enumerate(idx):
        for h in range(1, H + 1):
            if t0 + h > len(pre) - 2:
                break
            e = pre.loc[t0 + h - 1, "ev_next"]
            if np.isnan(e):
                break
            rows.append(dict(ep=i, t0=t0, h=h, event=int(e > 0)))
            if e > 0:
                break
    return pd.DataFrame(rows)


def km_from_person_weeks_h(pw, H):
    """km_from_person_weeks with a horizon parameter (smh_forecast's is
    12-frozen). Session-61 fix-before-freeze: the session-60 first run
    failed because horizon_cell reused the 12-frozen smh_forecast helper,
    so every H > 12 cell returned the H=12 KM flat-extended. The frozen
    helper in smh_forecast is untouched; this is the horizon-general
    twin, identical logic with range(1, H + 1)."""
    out = {}
    for h in range(1, H + 1):
        sub = pw[pw["h"] == h]
        out[h] = (len(sub), int(sub["event"].sum()))
    return out


def greenwood_cum_h(km, H):
    """greenwood_cum with a horizon parameter (smh_theorem's is 12-frozen)."""
    S, acc, cum = 1.0, 0.0, {}
    for h in range(1, H + 1):
        n, ev = km.get(h, (0, 0))
        if n > 0:
            S *= 1 - ev / n
            if ev and n - ev:
                acc += ev / (n * (n - ev))
        cum[h] = 1 - S
    return cum[H], (S * S * acc) ** 0.5


def horizon_cell(pre, idx, H):
    """One horizon cell -> the 11-tuple, everything but H frozen."""
    pw = person_weeks_h(pre, idx, H)
    km = km_from_person_weeks_h(pw, H)
    cum, se = greenwood_cum_h(km, H)
    lo, hi = cum - Z95 * se, cum + Z95 * se
    first = pw[pw["event"] == 1]
    events = int(first["ep"].nunique())
    med = float(first["h"].median()) if len(first) else float("nan")
    cpw = person_weeks_h(pre, pre.index[pre["sig_w"].notna()].tolist(), H)
    ckm = km_from_person_weeks_h(cpw, H)
    ccum, cse = greenwood_cum_h(ckm, H)
    clo, chi = ccum - Z95 * cse, ccum + Z95 * cse
    return (len(pw), events, round(cum, 4), round(lo, 4), round(hi, 4),
            round(med, 1), round(ccum, 4), round(clo, 4), round(chi, 4),
            round(cum - ccum, 4), lo > chi)


def all_horizon_cells():
    """The full pre-committed horizon grid, recomputed."""
    df = build_series(8, 2.5)
    pre = df[df["date"] < DESIGN_END].copy().reset_index(drop=True)
    in_low = pre["sig_w"] <= LOW
    prev = in_low.shift(1).fillna(False).astype(bool)
    idx = pre.index[in_low & (~prev) & pre["sig_w"].notna()].tolist()
    return [(H, horizon_cell(pre, idx, H)) for H in HORIZON_GRID]




# ========= THE CLIMATOLOGY-COUNTERFACTUAL DEPENDENCE GRID (session 62) =========
# Added 12 Nov 2026 (session 62, PROGRESS block 70). Doc: smh_robustness_v0.md
# section 10. The five prior grids audited the CLAIM's definitions; this one
# audits the RIVAL's own band construction: the climatology counterfactual's
# Greenwood band [0.411, 0.471] treats ~1050 OVERLAPPING 12-week windows as
# independent episodes - an under-dispersed construction the session-53/59
# variable map flagged as favouring the disjoint-band headline. One knob:
# the rival's window-sampling / variance construction; the 8-week sig_w,
# the 2.5-sigma bar, the LOW cut, the 41-entry set, the 12-week horizon and
# the ENTRY side FROZEN in every cell; the frozen climatology POINT estimate
# 0.4409 unchanged BY FIAT (it is the registered rival forecast in the
# forward protocol); ALL cells reported, none selected; descriptive only.
# First executed by research/smh_climato_ts_mirror.ts (the C0 anchor
# reproduced the frozen pool constants exactly: 1050 valid weeks, cum12
# 0.4409, band [0.411, 0.471], entry 41/0.634/lb 0.487, disjoint true).
# Session-62 note: this pandas counterpart has NOT yet executed - the same
# fix-before-freeze pre-declaration as the lone-print and horizon grids; it
# runs inside the existing smh_robust verify_all check on the next
# Python-enabled reset. The C3 LCG (mulberry32) is reimplemented bit-exactly
# with 32-bit masking so the rotated draws match the TS mirror.

CLIMATO_ANCHOR = dict(pool=1050, cum12=0.4409, se=0.0154,
                      band=(0.411, 0.471))
CLIMATO_TILES = {0: (88, 0.4351, 0.0531), 6: (87, 0.4483, 0.0533)}
CLIMATO_BLOCKS = dict(weeks=105, n=10,
                      ests=[0.438, 0.286, 0.400, 0.495, 0.381,
                            0.505, 0.638, 0.390, 0.410, 0.467],
                      mean=0.441, sd=0.094, se_mean=0.0297,
                      share_ge_entry_lb=0.3, n_eff=28)
CLIMATO_ROT = dict(B=200, mean=0.4486, sd=0.00537)


def _mulberry32(seed):
    """mulberry32, bit-exact with the TS mirror (Math.imul = 32-bit mask)."""
    a = seed & 0xFFFFFFFF

    def rnd():
        nonlocal a
        a = (a + 0x6D2B79F5) & 0xFFFFFFFF
        t = ((a ^ (a >> 15)) * (a | 1)) & 0xFFFFFFFF
        t = (t + (((t ^ (t >> 7)) * (t | 61)) & 0xFFFFFFFF)) & 0xFFFFFFFF
        t = t ^ (t >> 14)
        return (t & 0xFFFFFFFF) / 4294967296.0
    return rnd


def _km12_rows(rows):
    """KM cum12 + Greenwood SE from (ep, h, event) rows - the pooled form."""
    km = {}
    for h in range(1, 13):
        sub = [e for (_, hh, e) in rows if hh == h]
        km[h] = (len(sub), int(sum(sub)))
    cum, se = greenwood_cum_h(km, 12)
    return cum, se, len({r[0] for r in rows})


def _pool_rows(idx, ev, n):
    rows = []
    for i, t0 in enumerate(idx):
        for h in range(1, 13):
            if t0 + h > n - 2:
                break
            e = ev[t0 + h - 1]
            if np.isnan(e):
                break
            rows.append((i, h, int(e > 0)))
            if e > 0:
                break
    return rows


def climato_cells():
    """The full pre-committed climato grid, recomputed from canonical data."""
    df = build_series(8, 2.5)
    pre = df[df["date"] < DESIGN_END].copy().reset_index(drop=True)
    sig = pre["sig_w"].to_numpy()
    ev = pre["ev_next"].to_numpy()
    n = len(pre)
    pool = [t for t in range(n) if not np.isnan(sig[t])]
    out = {}
    cum, se, nw = _km12_rows(_pool_rows(pool, ev, n))
    out["C0"] = dict(n_windows=nw, cum12=cum, se=se)
    for off in (0, 6):
        idx = [pool[i] for i in range(off, len(pool), 12)]
        cum, se, nw = _km12_rows(_pool_rows(idx, ev, n))
        out["C1a" if off == 0 else "C1b"] = dict(n_windows=nw,
                                                 cum12=cum, se=se)
    ests = []
    for b in range(10):
        idx = pool[b * 105:(b + 1) * 105]
        cum, se, nw = _km12_rows(_pool_rows(idx, ev, n))
        ests.append(cum)
    sd = float(np.std(ests, ddof=1))
    out["C2"] = dict(ests=[round(e, 3) for e in ests],
                     mean=float(np.mean(ests)), sd=sd,
                     se_mean=sd / np.sqrt(10))
    r = pre["r1"].to_numpy()
    rnd = _mulberry32(42)
    rots = []
    for _ in range(200):
        k = 26 + int(rnd() * (n - 52))
        rr = np.full(n, np.nan)
        for t in range(1, n):
            rr[t] = r[1 + ((t - 1 + k) % (n - 1))]
        s_rot = pd.Series(rr).rolling(8).std(ddof=1).to_numpy()
        e_rot = np.full(n, np.nan)
        for t in range(n - 1):
            if not np.isnan(s_rot[t]) and not np.isnan(rr[t + 1]):
                e_rot[t] = 1.0 if abs(rr[t + 1]) >= 2.5 * s_rot[t] else 0.0
        idx = [t for t in range(n) if not np.isnan(s_rot[t])]
        cum, se, nw = _km12_rows(_pool_rows(idx, e_rot, n))
        rots.append(cum)
    out["C3"] = dict(B=200, mean=float(np.mean(rots)),
                     sd=float(np.std(rots, ddof=1)))
    return out


def climato_problems():
    """Selftest assertions for the climato grid (fix-before-freeze)."""
    problems = []
    c = climato_cells()
    a = CLIMATO_ANCHOR
    if abs(c["C0"]["cum12"] - a["cum12"]) > 0.0005 or \
            abs(c["C0"]["se"] - a["se"]) > 0.0005:
        problems.append(f"climato C0 anchor {c['C0']['cum12']:.4f}/"
                        f"{c['C0']['se']:.4f} != {a['cum12']}/{a['se']}")
    for key, want in CLIMATO_TILES.items():
        cell = c["C1a" if key == 0 else "C1b"]
        if cell["n_windows"] != want[0] or abs(cell["cum12"] - want[1]) > 0.001 \
                or abs(cell["se"] - want[2]) > 0.001:
            problems.append(f"climato tiles offset {key}: "
                            f"{cell['n_windows']}/{cell['cum12']:.4f}/"
                            f"{cell['se']:.4f} != {want}")
        if not (0.487 < cell["cum12"] + Z95 * cell["se"]):
            problems.append(f"climato tiles offset {key}: recorded "
                            "overlap-with-entry-lb state changed")
    b = CLIMATO_BLOCKS
    if c["C2"]["ests"] != b["ests"]:
        problems.append(f"climato block estimates {c['C2']['ests']} != {b['ests']}")
    if abs(c["C2"]["sd"] - b["sd"]) > 0.001:
        problems.append(f"climato block sd {c['C2']['sd']:.4f} != {b['sd']}")
    if not (0.487 < c["C2"]["mean"] + Z95 * c["C2"]["sd"]):
        problems.append("climato: the era-level band no longer overlaps "
                        "the entry lower bound (recorded state changed)")
    if c["C3"]["B"] != CLIMATO_ROT["B"] or \
            abs(c["C3"]["sd"] - CLIMATO_ROT["sd"]) > 0.0002 or \
            abs(c["C3"]["mean"] - CLIMATO_ROT["mean"]) > 0.001:
        problems.append(f"climato C3 rotation {c['C3']['mean']:.4f}/"
                        f"{c['C3']['sd']:.5f} != "
                        f"{CLIMATO_ROT['mean']}/{CLIMATO_ROT['sd']}")
    return problems




# ========= THE ENTRY-SIDE DEPENDENCE GRID (session 67) =========
# Added 13 Nov 2026 (session 67, PROGRESS block 75). Doc:
# smh_robustness_v0.md section 11. The session-62 grid audited the RIVAL's
# band construction; this one mirrors it onto the CLAIM's own side: the
# entry KM's Greenwood band [0.4867, 0.7816] treats the 41 compression
# episodes as independent, but the episodes cluster in eras (0-8 entries
# per two-year block, 0 events in 2017-2018, 5 of 5 in 2011-2012). One
# knob, the ENTRY-side dependence/variance construction; the 8-week sig_w,
# the 2.5-sigma bar, the LOW cut, the 41-entry set, the 12-week horizon
# and the climatology side FROZEN in every cell; ALL cells reported,
# none selected; descriptive pre-2021 bookkeeping only. Cells:
#   C0 the frozen anchor (pooled KM + Greenwood, asserted identical to
#      the frozen hazard-table constants);
#   C1 era-block dispersion: per-era KM cum12 over 10 two-year blocks
#      (the 2000 tail folded into era 0), across-era mean/sd (ddof=1 over
#      the K eras with entries), dependence-honest band
#      mean +/- 1.96*sd/sqrt(K) - the entry-side mirror of climato C2;
#   C2 leave-one-era-out pooled cum12, all 10 reported;
#   C3 declustered KM: pre-committed rule - an entry joins the previous
#      entry's cluster iff its date is within 8 weeks of it; anchors are
#      the first entry of each cluster.
# First executed by research/smh_entrydep_ts_mirror.ts (13 Nov 2026; the
# C0 anchor reproduced the frozen constants exactly: 41 entries, 327
# person-weeks, 26 events, cum12 0.6341, band [0.4867, 0.7816], cum4
# 0.2683, median lag 5, KM hazards cell-for-cell). This pandas counterpart
# has NOT yet executed - the same fix-before-freeze pre-declaration as the
# horizon/lone-print/climato grids; it runs inside the existing smh_robust
# verify_all check on the next Python-enabled reset.

ENTRYDEP_ANCHOR = dict(entries=41, pw=327, events=26, cum12=0.6341,
                       band=(0.4867, 0.7816), cum4=0.2683, median=5)
ENTRYDEP_C1 = dict(K=9, mean=0.6417, sd=0.3368,
                   dep_band=(0.4216, 0.8617),
                   era_cum=[None, 1.0, 0.625, 0.5, 0.4, 1.0, 0.75,
                            0.5, 0.0, 1.0])
ENTRYDEP_C2 = dict(loo=[0.6341, 0.6053, 0.6364, 0.6410, 0.6667,
                        0.5833, 0.6061, 0.6486, 0.7027, 0.6154],
                   loo_range=(0.5833, 0.7027))
ENTRYDEP_C3 = dict(clusters=33, pw=278, events=19, cum12=0.5758,
                   band=(0.4071, 0.7444))
RIVAL_ERA_BAND_UPPER = 0.499  # session-62 climato C2, frozen


def entrydep_table():
    """The 41-entry outcome table (date, era, terminal week, outcome)."""
    df = build_series(8, 2.5)
    pre = df[df["date"] < DESIGN_END].copy().reset_index(drop=True)
    in_low = pre["sig_w"] <= LOW
    prev = in_low.shift(1).fillna(False).astype(bool)
    idx = pre.index[in_low & (~prev) & pre["sig_w"].notna()].tolist()
    pw = person_weeks(pre, idx)
    rows = []
    for i, t0 in enumerate(idx):
        sub = pw[pw["ep"] == i]
        term = int(sub["h"].max())
        outc = int(sub["event"].iloc[-1])
        yr = int(pre.loc[t0, "date"].year)
        era = 0 if yr < 2001 else (yr - 2001) // 2
        rows.append((str(pre.loc[t0, "date"].date()), era, term, outc))
    return pre, idx, pw, rows


def _entrydep_km(pre, sub_idx):
    """KM + Greenwood cum12/cum4/se for a subset of entry indices.
    Session-76 fix-before-freeze (pre-declared in session 67): the
    authored version indexed the 12-FROZEN greenwood_cum dict, which
    only holds weeks with people still at risk, so any subset whose
    episodes all terminate before week 12 raised KeyError(12) on its
    FIRST pandas run. The frozen smh_theorem/smh_forecast helpers are
    untouched; this uses the session-61 horizon-general twins at H=12,
    which hold S flat past the last observed risk week - the same KM
    extension the TS mirror executed the grid with."""
    pw = person_weeks_h(pre, sub_idx, 12)
    km = km_from_person_weeks_h(pw, 12)
    cum12, se = greenwood_cum_h(km, 12)
    cum4, _ = greenwood_cum_h(km, 4)
    return dict(pw=len(pw),
                events=int(pw["event"].sum()),
                cum12=cum12, cum4=cum4, se=se,
                band=(cum12 - Z95 * se, cum12 + Z95 * se))


def entrydep_cells():
    """The full pre-committed entry-dependence grid, recomputed."""
    pre, idx, pw, rows = entrydep_table()
    eras = [r[1] for r in rows]
    c0 = _entrydep_km(pre, idx)
    lags = sorted(r[2] for r in rows if r[3] == 1)
    med = (lags[len(lags) // 2] if len(lags) % 2
           else (lags[len(lags) // 2 - 1] + lags[len(lags) // 2]) / 2)
    c0.update(entries=len(idx), median=med)
    # C1 era blocks
    era_cells, era_cum = [], []
    for E in range(10):
        sub = [t for (t, r) in zip(idx, rows) if r[1] == E]
        if not sub:
            era_cells.append(dict(era=E, entries=0, cum12=None))
            continue
        k = _entrydep_km(pre, sub)
        era_cells.append(dict(era=E, entries=len(sub), pw=k["pw"],
                              events=k["events"], cum12=k["cum12"],
                              band=(k["cum12"] - Z95 * k["se"],
                                    k["cum12"] + Z95 * k["se"])))
        era_cum.append(k["cum12"])
    K = len(era_cum)
    mean = float(np.mean(era_cum))
    sd = float(np.std(era_cum, ddof=1))
    c1 = dict(eras=era_cells, K=K, mean=mean, sd=sd,
              dep_band=(mean - Z95 * sd / np.sqrt(K),
                        mean + Z95 * sd / np.sqrt(K)))
    # C2 leave-one-era-out
    loo = []
    for E in range(10):
        sub = [t for (t, r) in zip(idx, rows) if r[1] != E]
        loo.append(_entrydep_km(pre, sub)["cum12"])
    # C3 declustered (rule: gap <= 8 weeks joins the previous cluster)
    dates = [pd.Timestamp(r[0]) for r in rows]
    anchors, loo_range = [], (min(loo), max(loo))
    for i, t in enumerate(idx):
        if i == 0 or (dates[i] - dates[i - 1]).days > 56:
            anchors.append(t)
    k3 = _entrydep_km(pre, anchors)
    c3 = dict(clusters=len(anchors), pw=k3["pw"], events=k3["events"],
              cum12=k3["cum12"],
              band=(k3["cum12"] - Z95 * k3["se"],
                    k3["cum12"] + Z95 * k3["se"]))
    return dict(C0=c0, C1=c1, C2=loo, loo_range=loo_range, C3=c3)


def entrydep_problems():
    """Selftest assertions for the entry-dependence grid."""
    problems = []
    c = entrydep_cells()
    a = ENTRYDEP_ANCHOR
    if (c["C0"]["entries"] != a["entries"] or c["C0"]["pw"] != a["pw"]
            or c["C0"]["events"] != a["events"]):
        problems.append(f"entrydep C0 {c['C0']['entries']}/"
                        f"{c['C0']['pw']}/{c['C0']['events']} != "
                        f"{a['entries']}/{a['pw']}/{a['events']}")
    if abs(c["C0"]["cum12"] - a["cum12"]) > 0.0005 or \
            abs(c["C0"]["band"][0] - a["band"][0]) > 0.0005 or \
            abs(c["C0"]["band"][1] - a["band"][1]) > 0.0005:
        problems.append(f"entrydep C0 cum/band {c['C0']['cum12']:.4f} "
                        f"!= {a['cum12']} {a['band']}")
    if abs(c["C0"]["cum4"] - a["cum4"]) > 0.0005 or \
            c["C0"]["median"] != a["median"]:
        problems.append("entrydep C0 cum4/median drift")
    got_era_cum = [None if e["cum12"] is None else round(e["cum12"], 4)
                   for e in c["C1"]["eras"]]
    if got_era_cum != ENTRYDEP_C1["era_cum"]:
        problems.append(f"entrydep C1 era cums {got_era_cum} != "
                        f"{ENTRYDEP_C1['era_cum']}")
    if c["C1"]["K"] != ENTRYDEP_C1["K"] or \
            abs(c["C1"]["mean"] - ENTRYDEP_C1["mean"]) > 0.001 or \
            abs(c["C1"]["sd"] - ENTRYDEP_C1["sd"]) > 0.001:
        problems.append("entrydep C1 mean/sd drift")
    if not (c["C1"]["dep_band"][0] < RIVAL_ERA_BAND_UPPER):
        problems.append("entrydep: the recorded state changed - the "
                        "dependence-honest entry band now clears the "
                        "rival's era-band upper edge (it does not)")
    if [round(v, 4) for v in c["C2"]] != ENTRYDEP_C2["loo"]:
        problems.append(f"entrydep C2 loo {c['C2']} != {ENTRYDEP_C2['loo']}")
    if (round(c["loo_range"][0], 4), round(c["loo_range"][1], 4)) != \
            ENTRYDEP_C2["loo_range"]:
        problems.append("entrydep C2 range drift")
    d = ENTRYDEP_C3
    if c["C3"]["clusters"] != d["clusters"] or c["C3"]["pw"] != d["pw"] \
            or c["C3"]["events"] != d["events"] \
            or abs(c["C3"]["cum12"] - d["cum12"]) > 0.0005 \
            or abs(c["C3"]["band"][0] - d["band"][0]) > 0.0005:
        problems.append(f"entrydep C3 {c['C3']} != {d}")
    return problems
if __name__ == "__main__":
    cmd = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else "status"
    if cmd == "status":
        sys.exit(cmd_status())
    elif cmd == "selftest":
        sys.exit(cmd_selftest())
    else:
        print("usage: smh_robust.py [status|selftest]")
        sys.exit(2)

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/research/smh_robust.py

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/research/smh_score.py (10466 bytes)
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""smh_score.py - the SMH forward-log resolver and scorer.

Shipped 12 Nov 2026 (session 35, PROGRESS block 43). Tooling ONLY. This tool
completes the forward pipeline: smh_forecast.py drafts and appends rows in
advance; this tool resolves each row's outcome windows as they complete and
fills the Brier/log-loss score columns. Rows are append-only; the scorer never
adds or deletes rows and never modifies a probability column; it only fills
the empty resolution and score columns of rows whose windows have fully
completed. Nothing here is an inferential claim: descriptive forward
bookkeeping, per the protocol in docs/smh_hazard_v0.md section 5.

Subcommands:
  status    report rows by kind, windows complete/pending, rows scored
  resolve   fill resolution + score columns for completed windows (idempotent;
            re-running changes nothing)
  selftest  validate the scoring math and the resolution logic. The resolution
            replay synthesizes the 41 pre-2021 compression entries and 33
            stress entries as if they had been logged, runs the resolver, and
            asserts the resolved event counts match the frozen KM tables
            (compression: 11 events within 4w, 26 within 12w; stress: 9
            within 4w, 21 within 12w). Pre-2021 unsealed design data only.
"""
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
import smh_forecast as F  # shared loader, constants, log path, header

DESIGN_END = "2021-01-01"


def score(p, y):
    """pure scoring: p in (0,1), y in {0,1} -> (brier, logloss)"""
    brier = (p - y) ** 2
    logloss = -(y * np.log(p) + (1 - y) * np.log(1 - p))
    return float(brier), float(logloss)


def load_log():
    if not F.FORWARD_LOG.exists():
        return None
    return pd.read_csv(F.FORWARD_LOG, dtype=str)


def resolve_row(df, row):
    """Resolve one row's outcome windows from the canonical series.

    Returns (updated_fields dict, status string). status is one of:
    'pending', 'fourw_complete', 'complete', or 'unknown_entry' (entry week
    not found in the canonical series - refused).
    """
    entry = pd.Timestamp(row["entry_week"])
    dates = df["date"].reset_index(drop=True)
    pos = dates[dates == entry]
    if len(pos) == 0:
        return None, "unknown_entry"
    t0 = int(pos.index[0])
    kind = row.get("forecast_kind", "compression_first_event")
    out = {}
    if kind == "compression_first_event":
        # event flags live at decision week t (event in week t+1);
        # window h weeks = decision weeks t0..t0+h-1
        def first_event(hmax):
            for h in range(1, hmax + 1):
                if t0 + h > len(df) - 2:
                    return None
                e = df.loc[t0 + h - 1, "ev_next"]
                if np.isnan(e):
                    return None
                if e > 0:
                    return h, int(np.sign(df.loc[t0 + h, "r1"]))
            return None
        fe4, fe12 = first_event(4), first_event(12)
        if t0 + 4 <= len(df) - 1:
            out["resolution_4w_date"] = str(df.loc[t0 + 4, "date"].date())
            out["event_4w"] = "1" if fe4 is not None else "0"
            out["dir_4w"] = f"{fe4[1]:+d}" if fe4 else ""
        if t0 + 12 <= len(df) - 1:
            out["resolution_12w_date"] = str(df.loc[t0 + 12, "date"].date())
            out["event_12w"] = "1" if fe12 is not None else "0"
            out["dir_12w"] = f"{fe12[1]:+d}" if fe12 else ""
    else:  # stress_de_stress: sig_w of week t <= HIGH, t in t0+1..t0+h
        def first_destress(hmax):
            for h in range(1, hmax + 1):
                if t0 + h > len(df) - 1:
                    return None
                s = df.loc[t0 + h, "sig_w"]
                if pd.isna(s):
                    return None
                if s <= F.HIGH:
                    return h
            return None
        d4, d12 = first_destress(4), first_destress(12)
        if t0 + 4 <= len(df) - 1:
            out["resolution_4w_date"] = str(df.loc[t0 + 4, "date"].date())
            out["event_4w"] = "1" if d4 is not None else "0"
            out["dir_4w"] = ""
        if t0 + 12 <= len(df) - 1:
            out["resolution_12w_date"] = str(df.loc[t0 + 12, "date"].date())
            out["event_12w"] = "1" if d12 is not None else "0"
            out["dir_12w"] = ""
    # scores: only when both windows known and probabilities present
    have4 = ("event_4w" in out and "p_within_4w" in row
             and row["p_within_4w"] not in ("", "nan"))
    have12 = ("event_12w" in out and "p_within_12w" in row
              and row["p_within_12w"] not in ("", "nan"))
    if have4:
        b, l = score(float(row["p_within_4w"]), int(out["event_4w"]))
        out["brier_4w"], out["logloss_4w"] = f"{b:.4f}", f"{l:.4f}"
    if have12:
        b, l = score(float(row["p_within_12w"]), int(out["event_12w"]))
        out["brier_12w"], out["logloss_12w"] = f"{b:.4f}", f"{l:.4f}"
    if "event_4w" not in out:
        return out, "pending"
    return out, ("complete" if "event_12w" in out else "fourw_complete")


def cmd_status():
    log = load_log()
    if log is None or len(log) == 0:
        print("forward log: no rows yet; nothing to score (by design)")
        return 0
    df = F.load_series()
    kinds, scored, pending = {}, 0, 0
    for _, row in log.iterrows():
        kinds[row["forecast_kind"]] = kinds.get(row["forecast_kind"], 0) + 1
        fields, status = resolve_row(df, row)
        if status == "pending":
            pending += 1
        else:
            scored += 1
    print(f"forward log rows: {len(log)}  by kind: {kinds}")
    print(f"windows resolved: {scored}  pending: {pending}")
    print("run 'resolve' to fill completed windows' outcome and score columns")
    return 0


def cmd_resolve():
    log = load_log()
    if log is None or len(log) == 0:
        print("forward log: no rows yet; nothing to score (by design)")
        return 0
    df = F.load_series()
    n_filled = n_pending = 0
    for i, row in log.iterrows():
        fields, status = resolve_row(df, row)
        if status == "unknown_entry":
            print(f"REFUSED row {i}: entry week {row['entry_week']} not in canonical series")
            return 1
        if status == "pending":
            n_pending += 1
            continue
        changed = False
        for k, v in fields.items():
            cur = row.get(k, "")
            if pd.isna(cur) or cur == "" or cur != str(v):
                log.loc[i, k] = str(v)
                changed = True
        if changed:
            n_filled += 1
    log.to_csv(F.FORWARD_LOG, index=False)
    print(f"resolve: {n_filled} row(s) updated, {n_pending} window(s) still pending; "
          f"rows never added or removed; probability columns untouched")
    return 0


def cmd_selftest():
    problems = []
    # 1. pure scoring math, hand-checked
    b, l = score(0.268, 1)
    if abs(b - 0.5358) > 1e-3 or abs(l - 1.3168) > 1e-3:
        problems.append(f"score math wrong: brier {b:.4f} logloss {l:.4f}")
    b, l = score(0.636, 0)
    if abs(b - 0.4045) > 1e-3 or abs(l - 1.0106) > 1e-3:
        problems.append(f"score math wrong (y=0): brier {b:.4f} logloss {l:.4f}")
    # 2. resolution replay on the unsealed pre-2021 design episodes:
    # synthesize the 41 compression + 33 stress entries as forward rows and
    # assert the resolver reproduces the frozen KM event counts
    df = F.load_series()
    pre = df[df["date"] < DESIGN_END].reset_index(drop=True)
    cidx = F.compression_entries(pre)
    pw = F.person_weeks(pre, cidx)
    km = F.km_from_person_weeks(pw)
    km_events_4w = sum(km[h][1] for h in range(1, 5))
    km_events_12w = sum(km[h][1] for h in range(1, 13))
    ev4 = ev12 = 0
    for t0 in cidx:
        row = dict(entry_week=str(pre.loc[t0, "date"].date()),
                   forecast_kind="compression_first_event",
                   p_within_4w="0.268", p_within_12w="0.634")
        fields, status = resolve_row(df, row)
        if status != "complete":
            problems.append(f"replay row not complete: {row['entry_week']} ({status})")
            continue
        ev4 += int(fields["event_4w"])
        ev12 += int(fields["event_12w"])
    if (ev4, ev12) != (km_events_4w, km_events_12w):
        problems.append(f"compression replay {ev4}/{ev12} != KM {km_events_4w}/{km_events_12w}")
    # stress side
    in_high = pre["sig_w"] > F.HIGH
    prev_high = in_high.shift(1).fillna(False).astype(bool)
    sidx = pre.index[in_high & (~prev_high) & pre["sig_w"].notna()].tolist()
    srows = []
    for i, t0 in enumerate(sidx):
        for h in range(1, 13):
            if t0 + h > len(pre) - 2:
                break
            e = (pre.loc[t0 + h, "sig_w"] <= F.HIGH)
            if pd.isna(pre.loc[t0 + h, "sig_w"]):
                break
            srows.append((i, h, int(e)))
            if e:
                break
    skm = {}
    for h in range(1, 13):
        sub = [r for r in srows if r[1] == h]
        skm[h] = (len(sub), sum(r[2] for r in sub))
    skm_4w = sum(skm[h][1] for h in range(1, 5))
    skm_12w = sum(skm[h][1] for h in range(1, 13))
    se4 = se12 = 0
    for t0 in sidx:
        row = dict(entry_week=str(pre.loc[t0, "date"].date()),
                   forecast_kind="stress_de_stress",
                   p_within_4w="0.273", p_within_12w="0.636")
        fields, status = resolve_row(df, row)
        if status != "complete":
            problems.append(f"stress replay row not complete: {row['entry_week']}")
            continue
        se4 += int(fields["event_4w"])
        se12 += int(fields["event_12w"])
    if (se4, se12) != (skm_4w, skm_12w):
        problems.append(f"stress replay {se4}/{se12} != KM {skm_4w}/{skm_12w}")
    if problems:
        print("SELFTEST FAIL")
        for p in problems:
            print("  -", p)
        return 1
    print(f"SELFTEST PASS: score math exact; resolution replay reproduces the "
          f"frozen KM event counts (compression {ev4}/{ev12} within 4w/12w, "
          f"stress {se4}/{se12}); resolver and tables agree.")
    return 0


def main():
    cmd = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else ""
    if cmd == "selftest":
        return cmd_selftest()
    if cmd == "status":
        return cmd_status()
    if cmd == "resolve":
        return cmd_resolve()
    print(__doc__)
    return 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/research/smh_score.py

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/research/smh_sizing.py (13364 bytes)
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""smh_sizing.py - the SMH clock-keyed sizing study: does exposure sized
by the clock lower max drawdown at comparable return?

Opened 12 Nov 2026 by Andrea's explicit authorization (session 52, PROGRESS
block 60), reversing the "no further theory builds" standing decision for
exactly this one study. Tooling ONLY: no evaluation of the forward log, no
trades, nothing feeds T, V, V4h or the forward protocol. Every number below
filters date < 2021-01-01 (DESIGN_END); the holdout 2021+ is SPENT and never
touched. The design (window, cells, margins, diagnostics) was pre-registered
in docs/smh_sizing_v0.md BEFORE any cell was computed; the cells were then
computed once and frozen here; the selftest recomputes everything from the
canonical data and asserts equality with the embedded values.

The design in one paragraph: exposure decisions for week t use only data
through the close of week t-1 (no lookahead); ARMED(t) = week t falls in the
12 weeks after any compression entry t' <= t-1 (the frozen 41-entry cross
semantics, the frozen 12-week claim window, no early termination); six
pre-committed cells - C0 buyhold (m=1), C1 armed_half (0.5 when armed),
C2 voltarget (min(1, 0.15/vol8ann), the registered V convention), C3
vt_armed, C4 tmom (the T convention: long when mom12 > 0 else flat), C5
tmom_armed; weekly portfolio return = m(t)*r(t), cash earns zero, no costs;
PASS per pair requires max drawdown MAGNITUDE <= 0.75x the unscaled arm AND
total return >= 0.70x; all cells reported, none selected, no re-tuning.

The measured verdict (pre-2021, descriptive): ALL THREE PAIRS FAIL, and the
failure is diagnostic. The armed halving changed max drawdown by EXACTLY
ZERO in every pair (identical to 4+ decimals) while cutting total return
30-42 percent: the max-drawdown episodes (the dot-com collapse) contain NO
armed weeks, so the peak-to-trough ratio is mathematically untouched, and
the policy only shed exposure outside the drawdown mass. The mechanism,
measured: ZERO of the ten worst buy-hold weeks fell in armed windows
(against 41.05 percent armed occupancy); the deep-drawdown weeks (C0
drawdown <= -10 percent, 598 of them) are UNDER-represented in armed
windows (34.45 vs 41.05 percent). The clock's event is sigma-relative (a
2.5-sigma week against a quiet base is a small absolute move); equity-curve
drawdown mass is sigma-level and lives in the stress region, which the
armed policy does not touch and the vol-target arm handles mechanically
(C2: max drawdown 0.384 vs 0.695 for buy-hold, total return 4.59 vs 3.92,
mean exposure 0.79). The armed-reduction policy is DEAD per the
pre-declared margins; the vol-target observation is descriptive only, no
decision attached, no new rule.

Subcommands:
  status    print the sizing-study card (descriptive only)
  selftest  recompute every cell from the canonical pre-2021 data and
            assert equality with the embedded values, the pair verdicts
            (all FAIL), the anchors (41 entries, eval window 1045 weeks)
            and the no-lookahead structure of the armed flag
"""
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
from smh_forecast import (  # noqa: E402
    load_series, compression_entries, DESIGN_END,
)

# ---- frozen design parameters (pre-registered in docs/smh_sizing_v0.md) ----
WPY = 52.1775            # weeks per year for CAGR
VTARGET = 0.15           # the registered V convention's annualized target
ARMED_WEEKS = 12         # the frozen 12-week claim window
ARMED_CUT = 0.5          # the pre-committed half-size in armed windows
DD_MARGIN = 0.75         # PASS needs |maxDD_sized| <= 0.75 * |maxDD_unscaled|
RET_FLOOR = 0.70         # PASS needs total_sized >= 0.70 * total_unscaled
FIRST_EVAL_T = 13        # first week with sig_w(t-1), mom12(t-1), r(t) defined

# ---- the frozen results (computed once, session 52, from the committed design) ----
# cell tuple: (total_return, cagr, max_dd, calmar, mean_exposure)
CELLS_EMBEDDED = {
    "C0 buyhold":    (3.917972, 0.082783, -0.694770, 0.119151, 1.000000),
    "C1 armed_half": (2.155789, 0.059061, -0.694770, 0.085007, 0.794737),
    "C2 voltarget":  (4.591607, 0.089745, -0.384457, 0.233434, 0.793810),
    "C3 vt_armed":   (2.671520, 0.067095, -0.384457, 0.174519, 0.603041),
    "C4 tmom":       (2.991942, 0.071562, -0.249283, 0.287073, 0.666029),
    "C5 tmom_armed": (1.961332, 0.055703, -0.249283, 0.223452, 0.501914),
}
# pair verdicts per the pre-declared margins (magnitude comparison on DD)
PAIRS = (("C1 armed_half", "C0 buyhold"),
         ("C3 vt_armed", "C2 voltarget"),
         ("C5 tmom_armed", "C4 tmom"))
PAIRS_VERDICTS_EMBEDDED = {p: "FAIL" for p in PAIRS}  # all three fail both margins
# diagnostics (no decision attached)
N_ENTRIES_ANCHOR = 41                     # the frozen pre-2021 compression entries
EVAL_N = 1045                             # common evaluation weeks
EVAL_START = "2000-12-22"
EVAL_END = "2020-12-25"
ARMED_OCC = 0.4105263157894737            # armed share of the eval window
WORST10_ARMED_SHARE = 0.0                 # zero of the ten worst weeks armed
DEEPDD_WEEKS = 598                       # weeks with C0 drawdown <= -10 percent
DEEPDD_ARMED_SHARE = 0.34448160535117056  # under-represented vs occupancy
# max-drawdown episodes: (start, end, n_weeks, armed_share) - zero armed everywhere
EPISODES_EMBEDDED = {
    "C0 buyhold":    ("2001-01-19", "2002-10-04", 90, 0.0),
    "C4 tmom":       ("2000-12-22", "2002-02-08", 60, 0.0),
}


def compute_study():
    """Recompute the whole study from the canonical data. Returns a dict."""
    df = load_series()
    pre = df[df["date"] < DESIGN_END].reset_index(drop=True)
    entries = set(compression_entries(pre))
    close = pre["close"].values
    sig = pre["sig_w"].values
    mom12 = pre["mom12"].values
    dates = pre["date"]

    eval_idx = list(range(FIRST_EVAL_T, len(pre)))
    # armed(t): within ARMED_WEEKS after any entry t' <= t-1 (no lookahead:
    # the entry at t' is known only at t's close, so it acts from t'+1 on)
    armed_until = -1
    armed = np.zeros(len(eval_idx), dtype=bool)
    for j, t in enumerate(eval_idx):
        if (t - 1) in entries:
            armed_until = max(armed_until, (t - 1) + ARMED_WEEKS)
        armed[j] = t <= armed_until
    A = armed.astype(float)
    r = np.array([close[t] / close[t - 1] - 1 for t in eval_idx])
    vol8 = np.array([sig[t - 1] * np.sqrt(52) for t in eval_idx])
    m12 = np.array([mom12[t - 1] for t in eval_idx])
    vt = np.minimum(1.0, VTARGET / np.maximum(vol8, 1e-12))
    tm = (m12 > 0).astype(float)
    cells = {
        "C0 buyhold": np.ones(len(eval_idx)),
        "C1 armed_half": 1 - ARMED_CUT * A,
        "C2 voltarget": vt,
        "C3 vt_armed": vt * (1 - ARMED_CUT * A),
        "C4 tmom": tm,
        "C5 tmom_armed": tm * (1 - ARMED_CUT * A),
    }

    def metrics(m):
        eq = np.cumprod(1 + m * r)
        total = eq[-1] - 1
        yrs = len(m) / WPY
        cagr = eq[-1] ** (1 / yrs) - 1
        dd = eq / np.maximum.accumulate(eq) - 1
        maxdd = dd.min()
        tr = int(dd.argmin())
        pk = int(np.argmax(eq[:tr + 1]))
        calmar = cagr / abs(maxdd) if maxdd < 0 else float("inf")
        return total, cagr, maxdd, calmar, float(m.mean()), pk, tr

    res = {k: metrics(m) for k, m in cells.items()}
    verdicts = {}
    for sz, un in PAIRS:
        s, u = res[sz], res[un]
        dd_ok = abs(s[2]) <= DD_MARGIN * abs(u[2])
        ret_ok = s[0] >= RET_FLOOR * u[0]
        verdicts[(sz, un)] = "PASS" if (dd_ok and ret_ok) else "FAIL"

    worst10 = np.argsort(r)[:10]
    eq0 = np.cumprod(1 + cells["C0 buyhold"] * r)
    dd0 = eq0 / np.maximum.accumulate(eq0) - 1
    deep = dd0 <= -0.10
    episodes = {}
    for k in ("C0 buyhold", "C4 tmom"):
        pk, tr = res[k][5], res[k][6]
        ep = np.arange(pk, tr + 1)
        episodes[k] = (str(dates.iloc[eval_idx[pk]].date()),
                       str(dates.iloc[eval_idx[tr]].date()), len(ep),
                       float(A[ep].mean()))
    return {
        "n_entries": len(entries), "entries": entries, "eval_idx": eval_idx,
        "dates": dates,
        "cells": cells, "res": res, "verdicts": verdicts, "armed": A, "r": r,
        "worst10_armed": float(A[worst10].mean()),
        "occupancy": float(A.mean()), "deep_n": int(deep.sum()),
        "deep_armed": float(A[deep].mean()), "episodes": episodes,
        "start": str(dates.iloc[eval_idx[0]].date()),
        "end": str(dates.iloc[eval_idx[-1]].date()),
    }


def cmd_status():
    st = compute_study()
    print("== SMH clock-keyed sizing study v0 (pre-2021 design bookkeeping) ==")
    print(f"common eval window: {st['start']} -> {st['end']}  n={len(st['eval_idx'])} weeks"
          f"  (entries pre-2021: {st['n_entries']})")
    print(f"armed occupancy: {st['occupancy']:.4f}")
    print()
    print(f"{'cell':16s} {'total':>9s} {'cagr':>7s} {'maxDD':>8s} {'calmar':>7s} {'mean m':>7s}")
    for k, v in st["res"].items():
        print(f"{k:16s} {v[0]:+9.4f} {v[1]:+7.4f} {v[2]:+8.4f} {v[3]:7.4f} {v[4]:7.4f}")
    print()
    for (sz, un), v in st["verdicts"].items():
        s, u = st["res"][sz], st["res"][un]
        print(f"PAIR {sz} vs {un}: |maxDD| {abs(s[2]):.4f} vs {abs(u[2]):.4f}"
              f" (need <= {DD_MARGIN}x: {abs(s[2]) <= DD_MARGIN * abs(u[2])});"
              f" total {s[0]:+.4f} vs {u[0]:+.4f} (need >= {RET_FLOOR}x:"
              f" {s[0] >= RET_FLOOR * u[0]}) -> {v}")
    print()
    print(f"mechanism: worst-10-weeks armed share {st['worst10_armed']:.4f}"
          f" (occupancy {st['occupancy']:.4f});")
    print(f"deep-drawdown weeks (C0 dd <= -10%): {st['deep_n']},"
          f" armed share {st['deep_armed']:.4f} (UNDER-represented vs occupancy)")
    for k, e in st["episodes"].items():
        print(f"maxDD episode {k}: {e[0]} -> {e[1]}  {e[2]} weeks, armed share {e[3]:.4f}")
    print()
    print("reading: the armed-reduction policy is DEAD per the pre-declared")
    print("margins - it changed max drawdown by exactly zero (the episodes")
    print("contain no armed weeks) while cutting return 30-42 percent; the")
    print("clock's event is sigma-relative, the drawdown mass is sigma-level")
    print("and lives in stress, which only the vol-target arm addresses.")
    print("Descriptive only; nothing feeds T/V/V4h or the forward protocol.")


def cmd_selftest():
    st = compute_study()
    errs = []
    ok = lambda cond, msg: None if cond else errs.append(msg)  # noqa: E731
    tol = 0.002
    # anchors
    ok(st["n_entries"] == N_ENTRIES_ANCHOR, f"entries {st['n_entries']} != {N_ENTRIES_ANCHOR}")
    ok(len(st["eval_idx"]) == EVAL_N, f"eval n {len(st['eval_idx'])} != {EVAL_N}")
    ok(st["start"] == EVAL_START and st["end"] == EVAL_END,
       f"eval window {st['start']}..{st['end']} != {EVAL_START}..{EVAL_END}")
    # cells vs embedded
    for k, emb in CELLS_EMBEDDED.items():
        v = st["res"][k]
        for i, (got, want) in enumerate(zip(v[:5], emb)):
            ok(abs(got - want) <= tol, f"cell {k} field {i}: {got} vs {want}")
    # pair verdicts: ALL FAIL, on the magnitude comparison
    for p, v in st["verdicts"].items():
        ok(v == "FAIL", f"pair {p} verdict {v} != FAIL")
    # the mechanism anchors
    ok(abs(st["worst10_armed"] - WORST10_ARMED_SHARE) < 1e-9,
       f"worst-10 armed share {st['worst10_armed']}")
    ok(abs(st["occupancy"] - ARMED_OCC) < 1e-9, f"occupancy {st['occupancy']}")
    ok(st["deep_n"] == DEEPDD_WEEKS, f"deep weeks {st['deep_n']} != {DEEPDD_WEEKS}")
    ok(abs(st["deep_armed"] - DEEPDD_ARMED_SHARE) <= 0.001,
       f"deep armed share {st['deep_armed']}")
    for k, e in st["episodes"].items():
        emb = EPISODES_EMBEDDED[k]
        ok(e[0] == emb[0] and e[1] == emb[1] and e[2] == emb[2] and e[3] == emb[3],
           f"episode {k}: {e} vs {emb}")
    # no-lookahead structure, verified independently: armed(t) must equal
    # "some entry t' <= t-1 exists with 1 <= t - t' <= 12" (an entry week may
    # legitimately be armed by a PREVIOUS entry's still-active window; only
    # its own entry must not act on itself)
    entries = st["entries"]
    ei = st["eval_idx"]
    for j, t in enumerate(ei):
        brute = any(e <= t - 1 and 1 <= t - e <= ARMED_WEEKS for e in entries)
        ok(bool(st["armed"][j]) == brute,
           f"week {t} armed={bool(st['armed'][j])} but brute-force says {brute}")
    # the identical-maxDD phenomenon: the armed halving changes max drawdown
    # by EXACTLY zero in every pair (episode weeks are all unarmed)
    for sz, un in PAIRS:
        ok(abs(st["res"][sz][2] - st["res"][un][2]) < 1e-9,
           f"pair {sz}/{un}: maxDD changed")
    if errs:
        print("SELFTEST FAIL:", "; ".join(errs))
        return 1
    print("SELFTEST PASS: smh_sizing - all 6 cells recomputed to tolerance,"
          " 3 pair verdicts FAIL as frozen, anchors 41 entries / 1045 weeks,"
          " worst-10 armed share 0, episodes unarmed, no-lookahead verified")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    cmd = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else "status"
    if cmd == "status":
        cmd_status()
    elif cmd == "selftest":
        sys.exit(cmd_selftest())
    else:
        print(f"unknown command: {cmd}", file=sys.stderr)
        sys.exit(2)

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/research/smh_sizing.py

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/research/smh_spz.py (17386 bytes)
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""smh_spz.py - the SMH sp_z covariate study: does the AVFI sp_z state at
entry add hazard information beyond duration, and the Cox-v0.1 LOYO Brier
correction that the study's pre-registered anchor forced.

Opened 12 Nov 2026 by Andrea's explicit authorization (session 54, PROGRESS
block 62), the last untested Layer-3 cell of the architecture map. Tooling
ONLY: no evaluation of the forward log, no trades, nothing feeds T, V, V4h
or the forward protocol. Every number filters date < 2021-01-01
(DESIGN_END); the holdout 2021+ is SPENT and never touched. The design
(the 41 frozen compression entries, the person-week construction, the
NaN-exclusion rule, the three pre-committed tests T1/T2/T3, the 2.0 percent
decision bar, the honest prior of null) was pre-registered in
docs/smh_spz_covariate_v0.md BEFORE any computation.

THE ANCHOR INVESTIGATION (recorded honestly): the pre-registered pipeline
validation required reproducing the frozen session-32 Cox v0.1 LOYO Briers
(duration-only 0.1596, full 0.1574). They DO NOT reproduce under any
construction consistent with the frozen coefficients (near-constant
predictions near the pooled rate 26/327 force a per-row LOYO Brier near
0.074; an episode-level Brier at base 26/41 cannot fall below 0.232). The
session-32 Brier pair was frozen from scratch prose with no instrument and
is a defective artifact; this study corrects it on the record. The pipeline
was instead validated against the frozen session-34 TypeScript replication
(research/smh_hazard_cox_v01_report.json): intercept-only beta -2.4907,
loglik -90.7663; duration-only beta [-2.5625, 0.0129], loglik -90.7413 -
reproduced EXACTLY - and the session-32 full-model coefficients
[-3.081, +0.017, +0.225, +0.502, -1.596] to three decimals. Under the
sound construction (pooled per-row, leave-one-episode-out): intercept-only
0.07364, duration-only 0.07406, full 0.07607: the covariates make
out-of-fold prediction WORSE by 2.71 percent. The frozen v0.1 verdict
("the hazard content is DURATION, not the entry conditions") survives and
STRENGTHENS; duration-only does not beat intercept-only either, matching
the session-34 LR reading that the weekly hazard is indistinguishable
from constant at n=41.

THE COVERAGE FINDING (the study's real headline): sp_z exists in
avfi_series.csv only from 2015-04-10, so only 10 of the 41 pre-2021
compression entries carry it; 31 are NaN and excluded per the
pre-registered rule. The last untested Layer-3 cell is closed primarily
by data coverage, not by the marginal comparison.

The measured verdict (pre-2021, descriptive): sp_z ADDS NOTHING. All three
pre-committed tests reported, none selected. T1 (|sp_z| >= 1 split): 6/4
entries, 2/2 events, cum12 0.333 vs 0.500 with bands spanning most of the
unit interval - no readable difference. T2 (signed extremes): 3/3 entries,
both halves below the pre-declared 5-entry sentence threshold: counts only,
no sentence. T3 (the decision test, cloglog LOYO Brier on the 10-entry
subset, 104 person-weeks, 4 events): duration-only 0.03860 vs
duration + sp_z 0.03790, a 1.80 percent relative improvement, BELOW the
pre-declared 2.0 percent bar -> ADDS NOTHING. Divergences stayed out of
scope (no frozen definition exists; inventing one is forbidden forking).
No cell registered (register closed at three); nothing feeds the forward
protocol.

Subcommands:
  status    print the sp_z covariate study card (descriptive only)
  selftest  recompute everything from the canonical pre-2021 data and
            assert the TS-replication anchor (exact), the session-32
            coefficient anchor, the NON-reproduction of the defective
            Brier pair, the corrected LOYO values, the coverage counts,
            the T1/T2/T3 values and the ADDS NOTHING verdict
"""
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import statsmodels.api as sm
from statsmodels.genmod.families import links

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
from smh_forecast import (  # noqa: E402
    load_series, compression_entries, DESIGN_END, person_weeks,
    km_from_person_weeks, AVFI, LOW,
)
from smh_theorem import greenwood_cum  # noqa: E402

CLL = sm.families.Binomial(link=links.CLogLog())
Z95 = 1.959963985

# ---- frozen design parameters (pre-registered in docs/smh_spz_covariate_v0.md) ----
SPZ_ABS_CUT = 1.0        # |sp_z| >= 1 extreme split (threshold pre-exists, H-number era)
T2_MIN = 5               # signed halves below this get counts only, no sentence
VERDICT_BAR = 0.98       # ADDS INFORMATION needs b_spz <= 0.98 * b_dur (2.0 percent)

# ---- frozen anchors: session-34 TS replication (research/smh_hazard_cox_v01_report.json) ----
TS_INT_BETA = -2.4907
TS_INT_LLNEG = -90.7663
TS_DUR_BETA = (-2.5625, 0.0129)
TS_DUR_LLNEG = -90.7413
# ---- frozen anchors: session-32 Cox v0.1 full-model coefficients (docs/smh_cox_v01.md) ----
FULL_BETA_DOC = (-3.081, 0.017, 0.225, 0.502, -1.596)
# ---- the defective session-32 LOYO Brier pair (NOT reproduced; corrected here) ----
DEFECTIVE_DUR_BRIER = 0.1596
DEFECTIVE_FULL_BRIER = 0.1574

# ---- the frozen results (computed once, session 54, from the committed design) ----
# corrected Cox v0.1 LOYO Briers, pooled per-row, all 41 episodes
B_INT = 0.07364298785493587
B_DUR = 0.07406287296281615
B_FULL = 0.07607340263770085
FULL_VS_DUR_REL_PCT = -2.7146255532027537   # covariates WORSE out of fold
# coverage
N_ENTRIES = 41
N_SPZ = 10
N_NAN = 31
SPZ_START = "2015-04-10"
SPZ_MIN, SPZ_MED, SPZ_MAX = -1.3721408402448163, -0.0807622468284865, 2.388205892840729
# T1 extreme split (n, events, cum12, band_lo, band_hi)
T1_EXTREME = (6, 2, 0.33333333333333326, -0.04386191144724061, 0.7105285781139071)
T1_NOT = (4, 2, 0.5, 0.010009003750000023, 0.98999099625)
# T2 signed extremes (n, events)
T2_UP = (3, 0)
T2_DOWN = (3, 2)
# T3 decision test on the non-NaN subset
SUB_N, SUB_PW, SUB_EV = 10, 104, 4
B_DUR_SUB = 0.038595278366174726
B_SPZ_SUB = 0.037901635698912806
T3_REL_PCT = 1.7972215686098942
VERDICT = "ADDS NOTHING"


def _loyo(feats, pwr):
    """Pooled per-row leave-one-episode-out Brier; feats: ep -> X."""
    sq, n = 0.0, 0
    eps = sorted(feats)
    for hold in eps:
        Xtr = np.vstack([feats[e] for e in eps if e != hold])
        ytr = np.concatenate([pwr[pwr["ep"] == e]["event"].values for e in eps if e != hold])
        Xte, yte = feats[hold], pwr[pwr["ep"] == hold]["event"].values
        m = sm.GLM(ytr, Xtr, family=CLL).fit()
        p = m.predict(Xte)
        sq += float(((p - yte) ** 2).sum())
        n += len(yte)
    return sq / n


def compute_study():
    df = load_series()
    pre = df[df["date"] < DESIGN_END].reset_index(drop=True)
    av = pd.read_csv(AVFI, parse_dates=["date"])[["date", "sp_z"]]
    pre = pre.merge(av, on="date", how="left")
    entries = compression_entries(pre)
    pw = person_weeks(pre, entries)
    eps_all = sorted(pw["ep"].unique())
    spz = {i: pre.loc[entries[i], "sp_z"] for i in range(len(entries))}

    # --- pipeline anchors: pooled fits vs the frozen TS replication ---
    y = pw["event"].values.astype(float)
    m0 = sm.GLM(y, np.ones((len(pw), 1)), family=CLL).fit()
    m1 = sm.GLM(y, np.column_stack([np.ones(len(pw)), pw["h"]]), family=CLL).fit()
    mom_neg = {ep: 1.0 if pre.loc[entries[ep], "mom12"] <= 0 else 0.0 for ep in eps_all}
    rev_dn = {ep: 1.0 if pre.loc[entries[ep], "state"] == "reversal_down" else 0.0 for ep in eps_all}
    depth = {ep: float(np.log(pre.loc[entries[ep], "sig_w"] / LOW)) for ep in eps_all}

    def feats(cols, pwr=pw, eps=eps_all):
        out = {}
        for ep in eps:
            d = pwr[pwr["ep"] == ep]
            k = len(d)
            cv = {"h": d["h"].values,
                  "mom_neg": np.full(k, mom_neg[ep]),
                  "rev_dn": np.full(k, rev_dn[ep]),
                  "depth": np.full(k, depth[ep]),
                  "sp_z": np.full(k, float(spz[ep])) if ep in spz and not pd.isna(spz[ep]) else np.zeros(k)}
            out[ep] = np.column_stack([np.ones((k, 1))] + [cv[c] for c in cols])
        return out

    mF = sm.GLM(y, feats(["h", "mom_neg", "rev_dn", "depth"])[eps_all[0]]
                if False else
                np.column_stack([np.ones(len(pw)), pw["h"],
                                 [mom_neg[e] for e in pw["ep"]],
                                 [rev_dn[e] for e in pw["ep"]],
                                 [depth[e] for e in pw["ep"]]]), family=CLL).fit()

    # --- corrected LOYO Briers (all 41) ---
    b_int = _loyo(feats([]), pw)
    b_dur = _loyo(feats(["h"]), pw)
    b_full = _loyo(feats(["h", "mom_neg", "rev_dn", "depth"]), pw)

    # --- coverage ---
    eps_ok = sorted(i for i in spz if not pd.isna(spz[i]))
    pw_sub = pw[pw["ep"].isin(eps_ok)]
    vals = np.array([float(spz[i]) for i in eps_ok])

    # --- T1 ---
    t1 = {}
    for name, sel in (("extreme", True), ("not", False)):
        eps_sel = [i for i in eps_ok if (abs(spz[i]) >= SPZ_ABS_CUT) == sel]
        pws = pw[pw["ep"].isin(eps_sel)]
        g = greenwood_cum(km_from_person_weeks(pws))
        c, se = g.get(12, (float("nan"), float("nan")))
        t1[name] = (len(eps_sel), int(pws["event"].sum()), c, c - Z95 * se, c + Z95 * se)

    # --- T2 ---
    up = [i for i in eps_ok if spz[i] >= SPZ_ABS_CUT]
    dn = [i for i in eps_ok if spz[i] <= -SPZ_ABS_CUT]
    t2 = {"up": (len(up), int(pw[pw["ep"].isin(up)]["event"].sum())),
          "down": (len(dn), int(pw[pw["ep"].isin(dn)]["event"].sum()))}

    # --- T3 ---
    b_dur_sub = _loyo(feats(["h"], pwr=pw_sub, eps=eps_ok), pw_sub)
    b_spz_sub = _loyo(feats(["h", "sp_z"], pwr=pw_sub, eps=eps_ok), pw_sub)
    verdict = "ADDS INFORMATION" if b_spz_sub <= VERDICT_BAR * b_dur_sub else "ADDS NOTHING"

    return {
        "pw_n": len(pw), "episodes": len(eps_all), "events": int(pw["event"].sum()),
        "int_beta": float(m0.params[0]), "int_llneg": float(m0.llf),
        "dur_beta": [float(b) for b in m1.params], "dur_llneg": float(m1.llf),
        "full_beta": [float(b) for b in mF.params],
        "b_int": b_int, "b_dur": b_dur, "b_full": b_full,
        "full_vs_dur_rel_pct": (b_dur - b_full) / b_dur * 100.0,
        "n_spz": len(eps_ok), "n_nan": len(spz) - len(eps_ok),
        "spz_start": str(av.loc[av["sp_z"].notna(), "date"].min().date()),
        "spz_min": float(vals.min()), "spz_med": float(np.median(vals)), "spz_max": float(vals.max()),
        "t1": t1, "t2": t2,
        "sub_n": len(eps_ok), "sub_pw": len(pw_sub), "sub_ev": int(pw_sub["event"].sum()),
        "b_dur_sub": b_dur_sub, "b_spz_sub": b_spz_sub,
        "t3_rel_pct": (b_dur_sub - b_spz_sub) / b_dur_sub * 100.0,
        "verdict": verdict,
    }


def cmd_status():
    st = compute_study()
    print("== SMH sp_z covariate study v0 (pre-2021 design bookkeeping) ==")
    print(f"person-weeks {st['pw_n']}, episodes {st['episodes']}, events {st['events']}")
    print()
    print("-- anchor correction (session 54) --")
    print(f"TS replication anchor: int beta {st['int_beta']:.4f} (frozen {TS_INT_BETA}),"
          f" -loglik {st['int_llneg']:.4f} (frozen {TS_INT_LLNEG})")
    print(f"                        dur beta [{st['dur_beta'][0]:.4f}, {st['dur_beta'][1]:.4f}]"
          f" (frozen {TS_DUR_BETA}), -loglik {st['dur_llneg']:.4f} (frozen {TS_DUR_LLNEG})")
    print(f"session-32 full-model beta reproduces doc: "
          f"[{', '.join(f'{b:+.3f}' for b in st['full_beta'])}] vs {FULL_BETA_DOC}")
    print(f"DEFECTIVE session-32 LOYO Briers ({DEFECTIVE_DUR_BRIER} duration-only,"
          f" {DEFECTIVE_FULL_BRIER} full): NOT reproduced; corrected on the record")
    print(f"corrected LOYO Briers (pooled per-row, 41 folds):"
          f" intercept-only {st['b_int']:.5f}, duration-only {st['b_dur']:.5f},"
          f" full {st['b_full']:.5f}")
    print(f"covariates out of fold: {st['full_vs_dur_rel_pct']:+.2f} percent vs duration-only"
          f" (WORSE); the v0.1 verdict survives and strengthens")
    print()
    print("-- coverage --")
    print(f"sp_z defined from {st['spz_start']} only: {st['n_spz']} of {N_ENTRIES} entries carry it,"
          f" {st['n_nan']} NaN and excluded per the pre-registered rule")
    print(f"sp_z at entry: min {st['spz_min']:.3f}, median {st['spz_med']:.3f}, max {st['spz_max']:.3f}")
    print()
    print("-- T1 |sp_z| >= 1 split --")
    for k in ("extreme", "not"):
        n, ev, c, lo, hi = st["t1"][k]
        print(f"{k:8s} n={n} events={ev} cum12={c:.4f} band [{lo:.4f}, {hi:.4f}]")
    print("-- T2 signed extremes (counts only; both halves below the"
          f" {T2_MIN}-entry sentence threshold) --")
    print(f"sp_z >= +1: n={st['t2']['up'][0]} events={st['t2']['up'][1]};"
          f"  sp_z <= -1: n={st['t2']['down'][0]} events={st['t2']['down'][1]}")
    print()
    print("-- T3 decision test (cloglog LOYO Brier, non-NaN subset) --")
    print(f"subset: {st['sub_n']} episodes, {st['sub_pw']} person-weeks, {st['sub_ev']} events")
    print(f"duration-only {st['b_dur_sub']:.5f} vs duration+sp_z {st['b_spz_sub']:.5f}:"
          f" {st['t3_rel_pct']:.2f} percent relative improvement (bar: >= 2.0)")
    print(f"VERDICT: sp_z {st['verdict']}")
    print()
    print("reading: the last untested Layer-3 cell is closed, primarily by data")
    print("coverage (10 of 41 entries) and marginally by the decision test")
    print("(1.80 < 2.0 percent); Layer 3 is fully answered; divergences stay")
    print("out of scope (no frozen definition). Descriptive only; nothing")
    print("feeds T/V/V4h or the forward protocol.")


def cmd_selftest():
    st = compute_study()
    errs = []
    ok = lambda cond, msg: None if cond else errs.append(msg)  # noqa: E731
    # dataset anchors
    ok(st["pw_n"] == 327, f"person-weeks {st['pw_n']} != 327")
    ok(st["episodes"] == N_ENTRIES, f"episodes {st['episodes']} != {N_ENTRIES}")
    ok(st["events"] == 26, f"events {st['events']} != 26")
    # TS-replication pipeline anchor (exact)
    ok(abs(st["int_beta"] - TS_INT_BETA) < 1e-3, f"int beta {st['int_beta']:.4f} != {TS_INT_BETA}")
    ok(abs(st["int_llneg"] - TS_INT_LLNEG) < 1e-3, f"int -ll {st['int_llneg']:.4f} != {TS_INT_LLNEG}")
    ok(abs(st["dur_beta"][0] - TS_DUR_BETA[0]) < 1e-3 and abs(st["dur_beta"][1] - TS_DUR_BETA[1]) < 1e-3,
       f"dur beta {st['dur_beta']} != {list(TS_DUR_BETA)}")
    ok(abs(st["dur_llneg"] - TS_DUR_LLNEG) < 1e-3, f"dur -ll {st['dur_llneg']:.4f} != {TS_DUR_LLNEG}")
    for got, want in zip(st["full_beta"], FULL_BETA_DOC):
        ok(abs(got - want) < 1e-3, f"full beta {st['full_beta']} != {list(FULL_BETA_DOC)}")
    # the defective pair must NOT reproduce
    ok(abs(st["b_dur"] - DEFECTIVE_DUR_BRIER) > 0.05,
       f"duration-only LOYO {st['b_dur']:.4f} unexpectedly near defective {DEFECTIVE_DUR_BRIER}")
    ok(abs(st["b_full"] - DEFECTIVE_FULL_BRIER) > 0.05,
       f"full LOYO {st['b_full']:.4f} unexpectedly near defective {DEFECTIVE_FULL_BRIER}")
    # corrected LOYO values
    tol = 0.0005
    ok(abs(st["b_int"] - B_INT) < tol, f"b_int {st['b_int']:.6f} != {B_INT:.6f}")
    ok(abs(st["b_dur"] - B_DUR) < tol, f"b_dur {st['b_dur']:.6f} != {B_DUR:.6f}")
    ok(abs(st["b_full"] - B_FULL) < tol, f"b_full {st['b_full']:.6f} != {B_FULL:.6f}")
    ok(st["b_full"] > st["b_dur"], "corrected full LOYO should be worse than duration-only")
    # coverage
    ok(st["n_spz"] == N_SPZ, f"n_spz {st['n_spz']} != {N_SPZ}")
    ok(st["n_nan"] == N_NAN, f"n_nan {st['n_nan']} != {N_NAN}")
    ok(st["spz_start"] == SPZ_START, f"spz_start {st['spz_start']} != {SPZ_START}")
    ok(abs(st["spz_min"] - SPZ_MIN) < 1e-6 and abs(st["spz_med"] - SPZ_MED) < 1e-6
       and abs(st["spz_max"] - SPZ_MAX) < 1e-6, "sp_z entry stats drifted")
    # T1
    for key, want in (("extreme", T1_EXTREME), ("not", T1_NOT)):
        got = st["t1"][key]
        ok(got[0] == want[0] and got[1] == want[1], f"T1 {key} counts {got[:2]} != {want[:2]}")
        ok(all(abs(a - b) < 0.002 for a, b in zip(got[2:], want[2:])),
           f"T1 {key} floats {got[2:]} != {want[2:]}")
    # T2
    ok(st["t2"]["up"] == T2_UP, f"T2 up {st['t2']['up']} != {T2_UP}")
    ok(st["t2"]["down"] == T2_DOWN, f"T2 down {st['t2']['down']} != {T2_DOWN}")
    # T3
    ok(st["sub_n"] == SUB_N and st["sub_pw"] == SUB_PW and st["sub_ev"] == SUB_EV,
       f"subset anchors {st['sub_n']}/{st['sub_pw']}/{st['sub_ev']} != {SUB_N}/{SUB_PW}/{SUB_EV}")
    ok(abs(st["b_dur_sub"] - B_DUR_SUB) < tol, f"b_dur_sub {st['b_dur_sub']:.6f} != {B_DUR_SUB:.6f}")
    ok(abs(st["b_spz_sub"] - B_SPZ_SUB) < tol, f"b_spz_sub {st['b_spz_sub']:.6f} != {B_SPZ_SUB:.6f}")
    ok(abs(st["t3_rel_pct"] - T3_REL_PCT) < 0.01, f"t3 rel {st['t3_rel_pct']:.4f} != {T3_REL_PCT:.4f}")
    ok(st["verdict"] == VERDICT, f"verdict {st['verdict']} != {VERDICT}")
    if errs:
        print("SELFTEST FAIL:")
        for e in errs:
            print("  -", e)
        return 1
    print("SELFTEST PASS: smh_spz (TS anchor exact, session-32 coefficients exact,")
    print("defective Brier pair confirmed non-reproducing, corrected LOYO values,")
    print("coverage 10/41, T1/T2/T3 values, verdict ADDS NOTHING)")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    cmd = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else "status"
    if cmd == "status":
        cmd_status()
    elif cmd == "selftest":
        sys.exit(cmd_selftest())
    else:
        print("usage: smh_spz.py [status|selftest]")
        sys.exit(2)

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/research/smh_spz.py
