##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage0e_artifact_occupancy.py (6788 bytes)
"""Stage 0e: artifact-window occupancy of the tops/bottoms event classes.

Occupancy counting ONLY. No forward returns, no event evaluation, no
holdout statistics. Motivates (or kills) the Amendment 4 H8b draft:
if the H8 divTop event population concentrates in quarterly
roll/expiry artifact windows, the executed H8 null conflates continuous-
contract splice artifacts with market behaviour, and a pre-registered
artifact-exclusion refinement (H8b) is a principled new hypothesis, not
post-hoc digging. If the events do NOT concentrate, H8b is pointless and
this report is the reason no amendment gets drafted.

Artifact-window definition: IDENTICAL to stage 0d and the v3.3 Pine
marker - the W-FRI week containing the third Friday of Mar/Jun/Sep/Dec
plus the following (roll/settlement) week.
"""
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

REPO = Path("/home/user/projects/quant")          # working tree (rebuilt from the bundle by verify_all.sh)
BUNDLE = Path("/home/user/knowledge/trading-research/pipeline")  # durable source of truth

CANON = REPO / "data" / "nq_weekly_canonical.csv"
TBSER = REPO / "data" / "avfi_v32_tb_series.csv"


def _third_friday(year, month):
    fridays = [dd for dd in pd.date_range(f"{year}-{month:02d}-01",
                                          f"{year}-{month:02d}-28", freq="W-FRI")]
    return fridays[2]


def roll_weeks():
    out = set()
    for y in range(1999, 2028):
        for mth in (3, 6, 9, 12):
            tf = _third_friday(y, mth)
            out.add(tf + pd.to_timedelta((4 - tf.dayofweek) % 7, unit="D"))
            out.add(tf + pd.to_timedelta((4 - tf.dayofweek) % 7 + 7, unit="D"))
    return out


def main():
    tb = pd.read_csv(TBSER, parse_dates=["date"])
    rw = roll_weeks()
    tb["artifact"] = tb["date"].isin(rw)

    # unsealed = before 2021-01-01; holdout NEVER evaluated, occupancy
    # precedent: sessions 2/3 counted occupancy only.
    unsealed = tb[tb["date"] < "2021-01-01"].copy()
    # sp_rz-valid window for divTop/divBot (those flags require valid
    # sp_rz by definition, so their populations are unchanged); climax
    # flags do NOT depend on sp_rz (validated in session 2: they fire
    # from the first pbN extreme), so they are counted over the full
    # unsealed window. Populations must not silently mix.
    valid = unsealed[unsealed["sp_rz"].notna()].copy()

    def occ(df, col=None, sub=None):
        d = df if col is None else df[df[col]]
        if sub is not None:
            d = d[d[sub]]
        n = len(d)
        k = int(d["artifact"].sum())
        return {"n": n, "in_artifact_window": k,
                "share": round(k / n, 4) if n else None}

    res = {
        "analysis": "stage 0e artifact-window occupancy (no forward returns)",
        "artifact_definition": "week containing 3rd Friday of Mar/Jun/Sep/Dec plus following week (stage 0d / v3.3 Pine marker, identical)",
        "valid_window": [str(valid["date"].iloc[0].date()), str(valid["date"].iloc[-1].date())],
        "all_valid_weeks": occ(valid),
        "newHigh_control_weeks": occ(valid, "newHigh"),
        "newLow_control_weeks": occ(valid, "newLow"),
        "divTop_events": occ(valid, "divTop"),
        "divBot_events": occ(valid, "divBot"),
        "climaxTop_events": occ(unsealed, "climaxTop"),
        "climaxBot_events": occ(unsealed, "climaxBot"),
        "tbFlagged_events": occ(valid, "tbFlagged"),
        "climaxTop_artifact_dates": [str(d.date()) for d in
                                     unsealed[unsealed["climaxTop"]]["date"]],
        "climaxBot_artifact_status": [
            {"date": str(d.date()), "in_artifact_window": bool(a)}
            for d, a in zip(unsealed[unsealed["climaxBot"]]["date"],
                           unsealed[unsealed["climaxBot"]]["artifact"])],
        "divTop_artifact_dates": [str(d.date()) for d in
                                   valid[valid["divTop"] & valid["artifact"]]["date"]],
        "divTop_clean_dates": [str(d.date()) for d in
                               valid[valid["divTop"] & ~valid["artifact"]]["date"]],
    }

    # Fisher exact, descriptive only: GIVEN a fresh 20w high, does the
    # divTop fade condition select artifact weeks beyond chance? Mutually
    # exclusive cells (divTop events are a SUBSET of the newHigh
    # control, so the comparison arm is newHigh-and-not-divTop).
    a = res["divTop_events"]["in_artifact_window"]
    b = res["divTop_events"]["n"] - a
    nh_art = res["newHigh_control_weeks"]["in_artifact_window"]
    nh_n = res["newHigh_control_weeks"]["n"]
    c = nh_art - a
    d_ = nh_n - nh_art - b
    try:
        from scipy.stats import fisher_exact
        orr, p = fisher_exact([[a, b], [c, d_]], alternative="greater")
        res["divTop_vs_control_artifact_concentration"] = {
            "table_divTop_artifact_divTop_clean_controlnewHigh_artifact_controlnewHigh_clean":
                [[a, b], [c, d_]],
            "odds_ratio": round(float(orr), 3),
            "p_one_sided": round(float(p), 4),
            "note": "descriptive occupancy statistic, not an edge test",
        }
    except Exception as e:  # scipy missing -> skip, occupancy counts stand
        res["divTop_vs_control_artifact_concentration"] = {"error": str(e)}

    # Arithmetic sensitivity bound from PUBLISHED stage2b aggregates only
    # (n=32, 4w event-vs-control diff mean +0.17 sigma, primary threshold
    # -0.15 sigma). No new forward returns are computed here. Question:
    # what would the 7 artifact events have to average for the H8b
    # artifact-exclusion refinement to even reach its primary threshold?
    # 32*(+0.17) = 25*(-0.15) + 7*m7  =>  m7 ~= +1.31 sigma.
    res["h8b_sensitivity_bound"] = {
        "n_total": 32, "n_artifact": 7, "n_clean": 25,
        "published_4w_diff_mean_sigma": 0.17,
        "primary_threshold_sigma": -0.15,
        "implied_artifact_event_mean_needed_sigma": round(
            (32 * 0.17 - 25 * -0.15) / 7, 2),
        "note": ("approximate arithmetic bound from published aggregates "
                 "only; no forward return was computed; an H8b flip requires "
                 "the 7 artifact events to average ~+1.31 sigma above "
                 "control at 4 weeks, vs the observed population mean of "
                 "+0.17 sigma"),
    }

    out = REPO / "research" / "stage0e_artifact_occupancy_report.json"
    with open(out, "w") as f:
        json.dump(res, f, indent=1)
    # sync the report back to the durable bundle (source of truth)
    BUNDLE.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with open(BUNDLE / "research" / "stage0e_artifact_occupancy_report.json", "w") as f:
        json.dump(res, f, indent=1)
    print(json.dumps(res, indent=1))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage0e_artifact_occupancy.py

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage0f_ba_perron_audit.py (14917 bytes)
"""Stage 0f: descriptive Bai-Perron structural-break scan of the UNSEALED weekly series.

This is the first descriptive step of the rule-set-T parameter-stability audit topic
(PROGRESS block 22): descriptive only, never a re-tuning license. No forward returns
are computed anywhere in this script; the holdout (2021+) is never read.

Objects (both unsealed, week_end <= 2020-12-31):
  A. weekly log returns of the canonical NQ weekly closes (full unsealed window)
  B. the 12-week momentum input of frozen rule set T (log(C_t / C_{t-12}))

Procedure (Bai-Perron rules adopted prospectively in the wave-2 study):
  - exact least-squares dynamic-programming break estimation (own implementation,
    prefix-sum SSR costs), trimming eps=0.10 (min segment h = ceil(0.10*T)), M=5 max breaks
  - UDmax first, sequential supF(l+1|l) second, per Bai-Perron (2003) recommendation
  - supF(m) = ((RSS0 - RSSm)/m) / (RSSm/(T - m - 1))   [q=1, pure model]
  - approximate critical values via the Bai-Perron (2003) Table 1 response-surface
    regressions (q=1, eps=0.10), computed from our own text extraction of the paper;
    the column mapping is cross-verified on the UDmax row; treat as approximate.
  - BIC(m) = T*ln(RSSm/T) + (m+2)*ln(T)  [m+1 segment means + 1 common sigma], descriptive

ruptures (BSD-2, the repository used as an independent reference implementation) is
cross-checked when its source is present at /tmp/repos/ruptures-1.1.9/src (session-only);
otherwise parity is reported as not_run_this_session and the own DP stands alone.

Writes research/stage0f_ba_perron_report.json. Prints a human summary.
"""
import json
import math
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

REPO = Path("/home/user/projects/quant")
UNSEALED_END = "2020-12-31"          # holdout 2021+ is spent and NEVER evaluated
EPS = 0.10                           # trimming (Bai-Perron adopted rule)
M_MAX = 5                            # UDmax upper bound M=5, per BP 2003 tables
QUANTILES = [0.90, 0.95, 0.975, 0.99]

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- data loading
def load_series():
    df = pd.read_csv(REPO / "data/nq_weekly_canonical.csv")
    df["week_end"] = pd.to_datetime(df["week_end"])
    assert (df["week_end"].dt.dayofweek == 4).all(), "weekly bars must end on Fridays"
    un = df[df["week_end"] <= UNSEALED_END].copy()
    assert len(un) > 0, "no unsealed bars"
    # guard: never touch the holdout
    assert (un["week_end"] <= UNSEALED_END).all()
    r = np.diff(np.log(un["close"].to_numpy(float)))
    dates = un["week_end"].to_numpy()
    rdates = dates[1:]
    c = un["close"].to_numpy(float)
    mom = np.log(c[12:] / c[:-12])
    mdates = dates[12:]
    return {
        "returns": (r, rdates),
        "momentum12": (mom, mdates),
        "n_unsealed_bars": len(un),
        "first_week": str(un["week_end"].iloc[0].date()),
        "last_week": str(un["week_end"].iloc[-1].date()),
    }

# ------------------------------------------------------- exact DP (own code)
class SegCost:
    """SSR of segments of x via prefix sums."""
    def __init__(self, x):
        self.x = np.asarray(x, float)
        n = len(x)
        self.s = np.concatenate([[0.0], np.cumsum(x)])
        self.s2 = np.concatenate([[0.0], np.cumsum(x * x)])
        self.n = n
    def cost(self, a, b):
        """SSR of segment [a, b) with a common mean."""
        if b <= a:
            return 0.0
        s = self.s[b] - self.s[a]
        s2 = self.s2[b] - self.s2[a]
        return s2 - s * s / (b - a)

def dp_breaks(x, m_max, h):
    """Global-minimum SSR segmentation with m=1..m_max breaks, every segment >= h.

    Convention: D[m][j] = min SSR covering [0, j) with exactly m breaks, the m-th
    break AT j (segments end at break indices). D[1][j] = SSR of the single first
    segment [0, j). D[m][j] = min_i D[m-1][i] + SSR(i, j).
    Returns rss[0..m_max] (0 = no breaks) and breaks[m] = the m interior boundary
    indices (segment k is [prev, b_k)).
    """
    sc = SegCost(x)
    n = sc.n
    rss0 = sc.cost(0, n)
    INF = float("inf")
    D = [None] + [np.full(n + 1, INF) for _ in range(m_max)]   # D[1..m_max]
    back = [None] + [np.full(n + 1, -1, dtype=np.int64) for _ in range(m_max)]
    for j in range(h, n - h + 1):
        D[1][j] = sc.cost(0, j)
    for m in range(2, m_max + 1):
        for j in range(m * h, n - h + 1):
            i_lo = (m - 1) * h
            i_hi = j - h
            i = np.arange(i_lo, i_hi + 1)
            prev = D[m - 1][i]
            ok = np.isfinite(prev)
            if not ok.any():
                continue
            costs = np.array([sc.cost(a, j) for a in i[ok]])
            tot = prev[ok] + costs
            k = int(np.argmin(tot))
            D[m][j] = tot[k]
            back[m][j] = int(i[ok][k])
    rss = {0: rss0}
    breaks = {}
    for m in range(1, m_max + 1):
        best, best_j = INF, None
        for b in range(m * h, n - h + 1):
            v = D[m][b]
            if np.isfinite(v):
                tot = v + sc.cost(b, n)
                if tot < best:
                    best, best_j = tot, b
        if best_j is None:
            break
        rss[m] = best
        chain = [best_j]
        j = best_j
        for mm in range(m, 1, -1):
            j = int(back[mm][j])
            chain.append(j)
        chain.reverse()
        breaks[m] = chain
    return rss, breaks

def sup_f_stats(rss, T, m_max):
    out = {}
    for m in range(1, m_max + 1):
        if m not in rss:
            continue
        out[m] = ((rss[0] - rss[m]) / m) / (rss[m] / (T - m - 1))
    return out

def sequential_supf(x, breaks_l, h):
    """supF(l+1|l): max over segments of the one-break vs no-break F, BP style."""
    sc = SegCost(x)
    n = sc.n
    bnd = [0] + list(breaks_l) + [n]
    best = -float("inf")
    best_seg = None
    best_split = None
    for k in range(len(bnd) - 1):
        a, b = bnd[k], bnd[k + 1]
        if b - a < 2 * h:
            continue
        rss_seg = sc.cost(a, b)
        # best interior split
        best_local = float("inf")
        best_c = None
        for c in range(a + h, b - h + 1):
            v = sc.cost(a, c) + sc.cost(c, b)
            if v < best_local:
                best_local, best_c = v, c
        if best_c is None:
            continue
        f = ((rss_seg - best_local) / 1.0) / (best_local / ((b - a) - 2))
        if f > best:
            best, best_seg, best_split = f, (a, b), best_c
    return best, best_seg, best_split

# ---------------------------------------- Bai-Perron (2003) response surfaces
# Table 1 coefficients decoded from our text extraction of the 2003 critical-values
# paper. supF(k|q): x1 = b0 + b1 q + b2 q^2 + b3 k + b4 eps + b5 q/eps;
#                  x2 = c1/k + c2/(k eps);            CV = x1 * exp(x2)
# UDmax(M|q): x1 = b0 + b1 q + b2 eps; x2 = c1 q + c2 eps; CV = x1 * exp(x2)
# supF(l+1|l): x1 = b0 + b1 q + b2 q^2 + b3 (l+1) + b4/(l+1) + b5 eps;
#              x2 = c1 q + c2 q^2;                    CV = x1 * exp(x2)
SUPF_K = {
    0.90:  [7.551, 1.718, -0.041, -0.610, 15.846, 0.025, 0.338, -0.014],
    0.95:  [8.238, 1.756, -0.043, -0.659, 15.436, 0.025, 0.389, -0.013],
    0.975: [8.968, 1.788, -0.045, -0.715, 15.255, 0.025, 0.426, -0.013],
    0.99:  [9.879, 1.771, -0.042, -0.777, 14.551, 0.025, 0.471, -0.012],
}
UDMAX = {
    0.90:  [6.917, 2.930, -9.275, -0.028, -0.406],
    0.95:  [8.228, 3.095, -9.644, -0.029, -0.291],
    0.975: [9.436, 3.304, -9.301, -0.030, -0.259],
    0.99:  [11.211, 3.366, -7.279, -0.027, -0.268],
}
SUPF_SEQ = {
    0.90:  [8.397, 3.702, -0.209, 0.317, -3.736, 11.596, -0.027, 0.005],
    0.95:  [9.879, 4.086, -0.232, 0.322, -3.687, 11.931, -0.039, 0.006],
    0.975: [11.424, 4.435, -0.261, 0.300, -3.700, 12.292, -0.047, 0.006],
    0.99:  [13.073, 4.954, -0.317, 0.285, -3.419, 12.452, -0.058, 0.008],
}
CV_NOTE = ("approximate response-surface critical values from our text extraction of "
           "Bai-Perron (2003) Table 1; column mapping cross-verified on the UDmax row; "
           "the CVs assume iid errors (no serial-correlation robustification)")

def cv_supf_k(q, k, eps):
    out = {}
    for quant, c in SUPF_K.items():
        qkey = f"{quant:g}"
        x1 = c[0] + c[1]*q + c[2]*q*q + c[3]*k + c[4]*eps + c[5]*q/eps
        x2 = c[6]/k + c[7]/(k*eps)
        out[qkey] = x1 * math.exp(x2)
    return out

def cv_udmax(q, eps):
    out = {}
    for quant, c in UDMAX.items():
        qkey = f"{quant:g}"
        x1 = c[0] + c[1]*q + c[2]*eps
        x2 = c[3]*q + c[4]*eps
        out[qkey] = x1 * math.exp(x2)
    return out

def cv_supf_seq(q, l, eps):
    out = {}
    for quant, c in SUPF_SEQ.items():
        qkey = f"{quant:g}"
        x1 = c[0] + c[1]*q + c[2]*q*q + c[3]*(l+1) + c[4]/(l+1) + c[5]*eps
        x2 = c[6]*q + c[7]*q*q
        out[qkey] = x1 * math.exp(x2)
    return out

# ------------------------------------------------- ruptures cross-check (optional)
def ruptures_parity(x, rss, breaks, h, m_max):
    try:
        import sys
        src = Path("/tmp/repos/ruptures-1.1.9/src")
        if not src.exists():
            return {"status": "not_run_this_session (ruptures source absent)"}
        sys.path.insert(0, str(src))
        import ruptures  # noqa
        x2 = np.asarray(x).reshape(-1, 1)
        res = {"status": "ruptures 1.1.9 from source, Dynp exact, model l2, jump 1"}
        per_m = {}
        for m in range(1, m_max + 1):
            if m not in breaks:
                continue
            algo = ruptures.Dynp(model="l2", jump=1, min_size=h).fit(x2)
            bk = algo.predict(n_bkps=m)   # interiors + trailing n
            ours = list(breaks[m])
            per_m[str(m)] = {
                "ours": ours,
                "ruptures_interiors": bk[:-1],
                "identical": ours == bk[:-1],
            }
        res["per_m"] = per_m
        res["all_identical"] = all(v["identical"] for v in per_m.values())
        return res
    except Exception as e:  # honest failure flag, never silently skipped
        return {"status": f"ruptures cross-check failed: {type(e).__name__}: {e}"}

# ------------------------------------------------------------------- audit run
def audit(name, x, dates):
    x = np.asarray(x, float)
    T = len(x)
    h = int(math.ceil(EPS * T))
    rss, breaks = dp_breaks(x, M_MAX, h)
    supf = sup_f_stats(rss, T, M_MAX)
    udmax = max(supf.values()) if supf else float("nan")
    udmax_at_m = max(supf, key=supf.get) if supf else None
    seq = {}
    for l in range(0, M_MAX):
        if l not in breaks and l != 0:
            continue
        bl = breaks.get(l, [])
        f, seg, split = sequential_supf(x, bl, h)
        if f == -float("inf"):
            continue
        seq[str(l)] = {
            "supF(l+1|l)": float(f),
            "segment": list(seg) if seg else None,
            "split_index": split,
            "split_date": str(pd.Timestamp(dates[split]).date()) if split is not None else None,
            "cv": cv_supf_seq(1, l, EPS),
        }
    bic = {}
    for m in sorted(rss):
        bic[str(m)] = float(T * math.log(rss[m] / T) + (m + 2) * math.log(T))
    segs = {}
    for m in sorted(breaks):
        bnd = [0] + breaks[m] + [T]
        rows = []
        for a, b in zip(bnd[:-1], bnd[1:]):
            seg = x[a:b]
            rows.append({
                "start": str(pd.Timestamp(dates[a]).date()),
                "end": str(pd.Timestamp(dates[b - 1]).date()),
                "n_weeks": int(b - a),
                "mean_weekly_logret": float(seg.mean()),
                "ann_logret_pct": float(seg.mean() * 52 * 100),
                "ann_vol_pct": float(seg.std(ddof=1) * math.sqrt(52) * 100),
            })
        segs[str(m)] = rows
    out = {
        "name": name,
        "T": T,
        "h_min_segment": h,
        "trimming": EPS,
        "rss": {str(k): float(v) for k, v in rss.items()},
        "supF_by_m": {str(k): float(v) for k, v in supf.items()},
        "cv_supF_by_m": {str(k): cv_supf_k(1, k, EPS) for k in supf},
        "UDmax": float(udmax),
        "UDmax_at_m": udmax_at_m,
        "cv_UDmax": cv_udmax(1, EPS),
        "sequential": seq,
        "BIC_by_m": bic,
        "BIC_min_at_m": min(bic, key=bic.get),
        "break_dates_by_m": {
            str(m): [str(pd.Timestamp(dates[i]).date()) for i in breaks[m]]
            for m in breaks
        },
        "segments_by_m": segs,
        "ruptures_parity": None,  # filled by caller
    }
    return out, rss, breaks, h

def main():
    data = load_series()
    report = {
        "script": "stage0f_ba_perron_audit.py",
        "purpose": "descriptive Bai-Perron structural-break scan of the UNSEALED weekly series",
        "discipline": {
            "window": f"unsealed only, week_end <= {UNSEALED_END}",
            "holdout": "spent, never read; no 2021+ data loaded by this script",
            "forward_returns": "none computed; descriptive structure scan only",
            "rule_set_T": "frozen; this scan licenses no re-tuning",
            "critical_values_note": CV_NOTE,
            "regimes_vs_breaks_fork": "BREAKS model declared (Bai-Perron LS); a Markov-switching "
                                      "description of the same window is run separately in stage0g",
        },
        "data": {
            "n_unsealed_bars": data["n_unsealed_bars"],
            "first_week": data["first_week"],
            "last_week": data["last_week"],
        },
    }
    for name in ["returns", "momentum12"]:
        x, dates = data[name]
        res, rss, breaks, h = audit(name, x, dates)
        res["ruptures_parity"] = ruptures_parity(x, rss, breaks, h, M_MAX)
        report[name] = res
    out_path = REPO / "research/stage0f_ba_perron_report.json"
    out_path.write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2))
    print(json.dumps({k: report[k] for k in ["data"]}, indent=2))
    for name in ["returns", "momentum12"]:
        r = report[name]
        print(f"\n=== {name} (T={r['T']}, h={r['h_min_segment']}) ===")
        print("RSS by m:", {k: round(v, 2) for k, v in r["rss"].items()})
        print("supF by m:", {k: round(v, 2) for k, v in r["supF_by_m"].items()})
        print("UDmax:", round(r["UDmax"], 2), "at m =", r["UDmax_at_m"],
              "| 95% CV ~", round(r["cv_UDmax"]["0.95"], 2))
        for l, v in r["sequential"].items():
            print(f"  supF(+1|{l}): {v['supF(l+1|l)']:.2f}  95% CV ~ "
                  f"{v['cv']['0.95']:.2f}  split ~ {v['split_date']}")
        print("BIC by m:", {k: round(v, 1) for k, v in r["BIC_by_m"].items()},
              "| min at", r["BIC_min_at_m"])
        for m, dts in r["break_dates_by_m"].items():
            print(f"  m={m}: {dts}")
        print("ruptures parity:", r["ruptures_parity"].get(
            "status"), "all_identical:", r["ruptures_parity"].get("all_identical"))
    print("\nwrote", out_path)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage0f_ba_perron_audit.py

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage0g_ms_filter.py (5931 bytes)
"""Stage 0g: descriptive Hamilton (1989) Markov-switching filter on UNSEALED weekly returns.

The regimes-vs-breaks fork (stage 0f = breaks model) requires the switching side to be
declared and run: this is the descriptive regimes counterpart, per the wave-2 study rule.
Descriptive only: no forward returns, no testing claims (regime-count LR tests are invalid
without Hansen/Garcia corrections; none are computed here), holdout never read.

Model class (Hamilton 1989, as adopted in the wave-2 study): 2-state Gaussian
Markov-switching regression on weekly log returns, switching mean AND switching variance,
via statsmodels.tsa.regime_switching.markov_regression.MarkovRegression (the statsmodels
repository implementation bundled in this environment). fit(search_reps=...) draws random
starting values; we run several fits and keep the best log-likelihood (local optima are a
known hazard of MS fitting; the honest fix is reporting the best of several, not one draw).

Expected durations: 1/(1 - p_ii). Diebold-Inoue (2001) read: near-unity staying
probabilities mimic long memory; the "persistent regime" interpretation then carries
the Lesson-11 caveat. No LR or count test is computed anywhere in this script.

Writes research/stage0g_ms_filter_report.json.
"""
import json
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

REPO = Path("/home/user/projects/quant")
UNSEALED_END = "2020-12-31"

def main():
    from statsmodels.tsa.regime_switching.markov_regression import MarkovRegression

    df = pd.read_csv(REPO / "data/nq_weekly_canonical.csv")
    df["week_end"] = pd.to_datetime(df["week_end"])
    un = df[df["week_end"] <= UNSEALED_END]
    assert (un["week_end"] <= UNSEALED_END).all()
    r = np.diff(np.log(un["close"].to_numpy(float)))
    dates = pd.DatetimeIndex(un["week_end"].to_numpy()[1:])
    T = len(r)

    lag1 = float(np.corrcoef(r[:-1], r[1:])[0, 1])

    best = None
    attempts = []
    for seed in [7, 42, 2026]:
        try:
            mod = MarkovRegression(r, k_regimes=2, trend="c", switching_variance=True)
            res = mod.fit(search_reps=20, em_iter=10, maxiter=500, disp=0)
            ll = float(res.llf)
            attempts.append({"seed": seed, "loglike": ll, "converged": bool(res.mle_retvals.get("converged", False))})
            if best is None or ll > best[0]:
                best = (ll, res)
        except Exception as e:
            attempts.append({"seed": seed, "error": f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}"})
    assert best is not None, "all MarkovRegression fits failed"
    ll, res = best

    params = np.asarray(res.params)
    # MarkovRegression param order (verified on the bundled statsmodels build):
    # [p[0->0], p[1->0], const[0], const[1], sigma2[0], sigma2[1]]
    p00, p10 = float(params[0]), float(params[1])
    p11 = 1.0 - p10
    p01 = 1.0 - p00
    consts = [float(params[2]), float(params[3])]
    sig2 = [float(params[4]), float(params[5])]

    # identify regimes by mean (regime 0 label from statsmodels is arbitrary)
    smoothed = np.asarray(res.smoothed_marginal_probabilities)  # (T, 2)
    hi = int(np.argmax(consts))
    lo = 1 - hi
    p_stay_hi = p11 if hi == 1 else p00
    p_stay_lo = p00 if lo == 0 else p11

    prob_hi = smoothed[:, hi]
    hi_weeks = dates[prob_hi > 0.5]
    runs = []
    if len(hi_weeks):
        start = hi_weeks[0]; prev = hi_weeks[0]
        for d in hi_weeks[1:]:
            if (d - prev).days > 10:
                runs.append([str(start.date()), str(prev.date())])
                start = d
            prev = d
        runs.append([str(start.date()), str(prev.date())])

    report = {
        "script": "stage0g_ms_filter.py",
        "purpose": "descriptive Hamilton-filter (2-state MS mean+variance) counterpart of the stage0f breaks scan",
        "discipline": {
            "window": f"unsealed only, week_end <= {UNSEALED_END}",
            "holdout": "spent, never read",
            "forward_returns": "none computed",
            "testing": "no LR, no regime-count test (Hansen/Garcia correction not implemented; claims not made)",
            "rule_set_T": "frozen; descriptive context only",
        },
        "data": {"T": int(T), "lag1_autocorr_returns": lag1,
                 "first_week": str(dates[0].date()), "last_week": str(dates[-1].date())},
        "fit": {
            "attempts": attempts,
            "best_loglike": ll,
            "implementation": "statsmodels (bundled) MarkovRegression, k_regimes=2, trend=c, switching_variance=True",
        },
        "transition_matrix_rows_sum1": [[p00, p01], [p10, p11]],
        "staying_probabilities": {"hi_growth_state": p_stay_hi, "low_state": p_stay_lo},
        "expected_durations_weeks": {
            "hi_growth_state": 1.0 / (1.0 - p_stay_hi),
            "low_state": 1.0 / (1.0 - p_stay_lo),
        },
        "regime_params": {
            "hi_growth_state": {"mean_weekly_logret": consts[hi],
                                "ann_mean_pct": consts[hi] * 52 * 100,
                                "ann_vol_pct": float(np.sqrt(sig2[hi]) * np.sqrt(52) * 100)},
            "low_state": {"mean_weekly_logret": consts[lo],
                          "ann_mean_pct": consts[lo] * 52 * 100,
                          "ann_vol_pct": float(np.sqrt(sig2[lo]) * np.sqrt(52) * 100)},
        },
        "smoothed_hi_state_runs_over_50pct": runs,
        "diebold_inoue_note": ("Near-unity staying probabilities would mimic long memory "
                               "(Diebold-Inoue 2001): a persistent-regime interpretation of "
                               "this window then carries the Lesson-11 caveat. The measured "
                               "values are reported, no claim is made."),
    }
    out = REPO / "research/stage0g_ms_filter_report.json"
    out.write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2))
    print(json.dumps(report, indent=2))
    print("\nwrote", out)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage0g_ms_filter.py

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage0h_v_design_diagnostics.py (5844 bytes)
"""Stage 0h: rule set V design-time diagnostics. IN-SAMPLE BOOKKEEPING ONLY.

docs/ruleset_v_spec.md was FROZEN BEFORE this script ran. The numbers
below are design-time bookkeeping on the UNSEALED 2000-2020 window,
computed ONCE, and they are NOT confirmation of anything: V's components
were chosen from findings measured on the same window. No p-value, no
bootstrap, no hypothesis test, no significance claim exists for V. The
2021+ holdout is SPENT and is never touched by this script (asserted in
code). V's only evaluation path is the stage 7 forward paper track.

Bookkeeping conventions (identical to the stage 3 C2 convention):
position decided at close t-1 applies during week t (1-bar lag); costs
charged on |change in position| x 0.3625 pts / close; mark-to-close.
Anchors on the same window: T (long/flat, size 1), U3 (Amendment 2
constants), buy-and-hold.
"""
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path("/home/user/projects/quant/strategies")))
from avfi_v_ruleset import compute_v
from backtest import COST_PER_SIDE

REPO = Path("/home/user/projects/quant")
BUNDLE = Path("/home/user/knowledge/trading-research/pipeline")

df = pd.read_csv(REPO / "data" / "nq_weekly_canonical.csv", parse_dates=["date"])
assert (df["date"] < "2021-01-01").any(), "no unsealed data"

v = compute_v(df)

# unsealed window, stage-3 burn-in convention (warmup 260 + emaLen 105)
BURN_IN = 365
unsealed = df["date"] < pd.Timestamp("2021-01-01")
assert df["date"][unsealed].iloc[-1] == pd.Timestamp("2020-12-25"), "unexpected unsealed end"
assert v.loc[unsealed, "date"].iloc[-1] < pd.Timestamp("2021-01-01"), "holdout leak"
valid = unsealed.copy()
valid.iloc[:BURN_IN] = False

ret1 = pd.Series(np.log(df["close"] / df["close"].shift(1)), index=df.index)


def sized_position_series(size_col):
    """Position HELD during week t = size decided at close t-1."""
    return v[size_col].shift(1)


def bookkeep(pos, label):
    m = valid & pos.notna()
    p = pos[m]
    r = ret1[m].fillna(0.0)
    pnl = (r * p).fillna(0.0)
    dpos = p.diff().abs().fillna(p.abs().iloc[0] if len(p) else 0.0)
    cost = dpos * COST_PER_SIDE / df["close"][m]
    net = pnl - cost
    eq = (1 + net).cumprod()
    sd = net.std()
    return {
        "label": label,
        "n_weeks": int(len(p)),
        "first_week": str(df["date"][m].iloc[0].date()),
        "last_week": str(df["date"][m].iloc[-1].date()),
        "total_ret_pct": round(float(eq.iloc[-1] - 1) * 100, 1),
        "ann_sharpe_bookkeeping": round(float(net.mean() / sd * np.sqrt(52)), 3) if sd > 0 else None,
        "max_drawdown_pct": round(float((eq / eq.cummax() - 1).min()) * 100, 1),
        "avg_size_when_long": round(float(p[p > 0].mean()), 3) if (p > 0).any() else None,
        "exposure_pct": round(float((p > 0).mean()) * 100, 1),
        "n_size_changes": int((dpos > 1e-9).sum()),
        "cost_drag_pct": round(float((1 + net).cumprod().iloc[-1] / (1 + pnl).cumprod().iloc[-1] - 1) * 100, 2),
    }


results = {}

# V
vpos = sized_position_series("v_size")
results["V"] = bookkeep(vpos, "rule set V (registered, design-bookkeeping only)")

# T: long/flat, size 1 (the tracked rule)
tpos = pd.Series(np.where(v["t_long"].shift(1).fillna(False), 1.0, 0.0), index=df.index)
results["T"] = bookkeep(tpos, "rule set T (tracked, same window anchor)")

# U3: Amendment 2 constants
upos = sized_position_series("u3_size")
results["U3"] = bookkeep(upos, "rule set U3 (parallel, Amendment 2 constants)")

# buy-and-hold
bh = pd.Series(1.0, index=df.index)
results["buyhold"] = bookkeep(bh, "buy and hold")

# split bookkeeping (decade halves), same conventions, V only
for name, lo, hi in (("V_2001_2010", "2001-01-01", "2010-12-31"),
                     ("V_2011_2020", "2011-01-01", "2020-12-31")):
    wmask = (df["date"] >= lo) & (df["date"] <= hi) & vpos.notna()
    p = vpos[wmask]
    r = ret1[wmask].fillna(0.0)
    net = (r * p).fillna(0.0)
    dpos = p.diff().abs().fillna(p.abs().iloc[0])
    net = net - dpos * COST_PER_SIDE / df["close"][wmask]
    eq = (1 + net).cumprod()
    sd = net.std()
    results[name] = {
        "n_weeks": int(len(p)),
        "total_ret_pct": round(float(eq.iloc[-1] - 1) * 100, 1),
        "ann_sharpe_bookkeeping": round(float(net.mean() / sd * np.sqrt(52)), 3) if sd > 0 else None,
        "max_drawdown_pct": round(float((eq / eq.cummax() - 1).min()) * 100, 1),
        "avg_size_when_long": round(float(p[p > 0].mean()), 3) if (p > 0).any() else None,
    }

report = {
    "purpose": ("rule set V design-time in-sample bookkeeping; spec frozen "
                "before the run; NOT confirmation; no p-value exists for V; "
                "holdout never touched (asserted); forward paper track is "
                "the only evaluation path"),
    "spec": "docs/ruleset_v_spec.md",
    "conventions": ("1-bar lag (size decided at close t-1 held during week t), "
                    "cost 0.3625 pts/side on |dpos|, mark-to-close, "
                    "burn-in bar 365, unsealed window ends 2020-12-25"),
    "window": [str(df["date"][unsealed].iloc[0].date()),
               str(df["date"][unsealed].iloc[-1].date())],
    "results": results,
    "reversal_down_weeks_unsealed": int((v["state"] == "reversal_down")[unsealed].sum()),
    "discipline": ("no significance claim of any kind; components were chosen "
                   "from findings on this same window; a null or a good number "
                   "here means nothing either way until the forward track runs"),
}

out = REPO / "research" / "stage0h_v_design_diagnostics_report.json"
with open(out, "w") as f:
    json.dump(report, f, indent=1)
with open(BUNDLE / "research" / "stage0h_v_design_diagnostics_report.json", "w") as f:
    json.dump(report, f, indent=1)
print(json.dumps(report, indent=1))

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage0h_v_design_diagnostics.py

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage0v_vxn.py (4436 bytes)
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""stage0v_vxn.py - the implied-vol benchmark layer (Stage 0v: priced expectations).

Shipped 12 Nov 2026 (session 40, PROGRESS block 48). Resolves the standing
data need recorded in docs/smh_hazard_v0.md section 6 and the canvas open
problems: an implied-vol series (VIX or VXN) as the priced-expectations
benchmark for the SMH unseen-ness test.

Method (mirrors stage0c's Yahoo v8 route and its discipline):
  - fetch daily ^VXN (Nasdaq-100 volatility index, from Feb 2001) and
    ^VIX (from 1990) from the Yahoo v8 chart endpoint, full history
  - sanity: positive values, drop nulls, log counts; no repairs (single
    close series; nothing to clip)
  - aggregate to W-FRI weeks: close = last daily close of the week,
    mean = weekly mean of daily closes, n_sessions = count
  - deliverables: data/vxn_weekly.csv (both indices, W-FRI) +
    research/stage0v_vxn_report.json (provenance)

Discipline: the fetch necessarily includes post-2021 quotes (the endpoint
returns full history), exactly as the canonical NQ file does; ALL design
tables computed downstream filter date < 2021-01-01 and never touch the
spent holdout. This script is data acquisition only: no strategy code, no
frozen constants, no evaluation, no trades.
"""
import json
import time
import urllib.request
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

REPO = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
P1 = 946684800            # 2000-01-01
P2 = int(time.time())


def fetch_daily(ticker):
    url = (f"https://query1.finance.yahoo.com/v8/finance/chart/{ticker}"
           f"?period1={P1}&period2={P2}&interval=1d&includeAdjustedClose=false")
    req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers={"User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0 (research)"})
    with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=60) as fh:
        payload = json.load(fh)
    res = payload["chart"]["result"][0]
    ts = res["timestamp"]
    q = res["indicators"]["quote"][0]
    day = pd.DataFrame({"date": pd.to_datetime(ts, unit="s", utc=True)
                                  .tz_convert("America/New_York").date,
                        "close": q["close"]})
    day["date"] = pd.to_datetime(day["date"])
    n_raw = len(day)
    day = day.dropna(subset=["close"]).copy()
    n_null = n_raw - len(day)
    day = day[day["close"] > 0]
    n_nonpos = n_raw - n_null - len(day)
    return day, {"n_raw": n_raw, "n_null": n_null, "n_nonpositive": int(n_nonpos),
                 "first": str(day["date"].min().date()),
                 "last": str(day["date"].max().date())}


def to_weekly(day):
    day = day.copy()
    day["week_end"] = day["date"] + pd.to_timedelta(
        (4 - day["date"].dt.dayofweek) % 7, unit="D")  # W-FRI label
    g = day.groupby("week_end")
    wk = pd.DataFrame({
        "close": g["close"].last(),
        "mean": g["close"].mean().round(4),
        "n_sessions": g["close"].count(),
    }).reset_index()
    return wk


def main():
    out = {}
    frames = {}
    for key, ticker in (("vxn", "^VXN"), ("vix", "^VIX")):
        day, facts = fetch_daily(ticker)
        wk = to_weekly(day)
        frames[key] = wk
        out[key] = facts
        print(f"{ticker}: daily rows {facts['n_raw']} (null {facts['n_null']}, "
              f"nonpos {facts['n_nonpositive']}), weeks {len(wk)}, "
              f"span {facts['first']} .. {facts['last']}")
    df = frames["vxn"].merge(frames["vix"], on="week_end", how="outer",
                             suffixes=("_vxn", "_vix"))
    df = df.sort_values("week_end").reset_index(drop=True)
    df = df.rename(columns={"week_end": "date"})
    df.to_csv(REPO / "data" / "vxn_weekly.csv", index=False)
    out["weeks_total"] = len(df)
    out["vxn_coverage"] = int(df["close_vxn"].notna().sum())
    out["vix_coverage"] = int(df["close_vix"].notna().sum())
    report = {"tool": "stage0v_vxn.py", "fetched_at_utc": time.strftime(
        "%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ", time.gmtime()), "facts": out,
        "discipline": ("full history fetched per endpoint behaviour; all "
                       "downstream design tables filter date < 2021-01-01; "
                       "holdout 2021+ spent and never touched")}
    (REPO / "research" / "stage0v_vxn_report.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(report, indent=1))
    print(f"saved data/vxn_weekly.csv: {len(df)} weeks "
          f"(VXN coverage {out['vxn_coverage']}, VIX coverage {out['vix_coverage']})")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage0v_vxn.py

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage0x_a13_cw1_btcweekly.py (21217 bytes)
"""Stage 0x + Stage A for Amendment 13 (SIGNED 2026-11-19): the C-W1 BTC weekly
compression-entry hazard arm.

Authorization: Amendment 13 signed as drafted on 2026-11-19 ("Sign."), with
Stage 0x as its own dated session per the signature block; this run is frontier
vector V4, claimed in docs/smh_frontier_v0.md BEFORE any Stage 0x computation.

What this script computes (per the amendment, frozen; the design window is the
pre-2021 UNSEALED span only):
  Stage 0x: re-audit of the BTC daily canonical (nulls, nonpositives, high<low,
    duplicates, gaps; price columns only, volume never used), ISO-week
    aggregation audit (Monday 00:00 UTC boundary, close = last daily close of
    the week, completed weeks only, the forming week never enters), LOW_BTC by
    the frozen Amendment 9 rank-transfer rule (BTC's own pre-2021 27.52nd
    percentile of sig_w; NQ's absolute cut is context only), determinism (the
    full report computed twice, byte-identical, no wallclock timestamps), the
    truncation look-ahead audit (every design entry's sig_w/cross state
    recomputed from dailies truncated at the entry week must match exactly,
    LOW_BTC frozen during truncation), and the OCCUPANCY GATE (>= 10 compression
    entries or the arm CLOSES UNTESTED-LOW-OCCUPANCY and nothing downstream
    runs, the H7 and C-W3 precedent).
  Stage A (only if occupancy passes): entry-anchored 12-week first-event KM with
    Greenwood 95 percent band via the parity-gated stage0x_stageA machinery
    (person_weeks, km_table, greenwood_cum, band, median_lag; HORIZON 12,
    BAR_K 2.5, Z95 frozen), matched climatology (every design decision week
    with valid sig_w, the same clock), era dispersion (2-year blocks,
    descriptive), THE GAP (entry cum12 minus climatology cum12), and the
    DESIGN-STAGE KILL: the arm CLOSES UNTESTED-FAILED-DESIGN if the bands do
    not separate (entry band lower <= climatology band upper, unrounded). Only
    if the bands separate are forward kill lines frozen from the arm's OWN
    design cum12 (largest k with P(Bin(n, p0) <= k) <= 0.05 at n = 10/20/40/60).

Boundary conventions, fixed BEFORE this run (recorded, never tuned):
  - Design weeks are COMPLETED weeks fully ending before 2021-01-01 00:00 UTC
    (last design week starts Monday 2020-12-21). The ISO week starting
    2020-12-28 straddles the seal boundary and is used by NOTHING: not design,
    not a 2021+ availability count (counters only, no prices).
  - The last design week's ev_next would need the straddling week's return and
    is therefore NaN by construction; episodes hitting it are censored per the
    KM convention (the amendment's own censoring rule; administratively identical
    to the C-W3 design-end censoring).
  - data/btc_weekly_iso.csv carries DESIGN-WEEK rows only (price columns +
    n_sessions, no volume); forward rows append from the signature date at the
    Sunday cadence; no backfill from the sealed window, ever.

What this script REFUSES to do:
  - any computation on 2021+ rows beyond availability counts (the window is
    SEALED: single-open only by this arm's own forward rows, on a dated go)
  - any volume feature (the genuine-volume clause: the construction is
    price-only, so the clause is satisfied by construction)
  - a stress arm (Amendment 13 registers the compression arm ONLY)
  - direction splits (the event is |r1|, both-side, never split)
  - any pooled claim (no NQ pooling, no macro four-market pooling)
  - any alternative constant, window, boundary or threshold (the no-forking
    clause; any deviation closes the stage)

Machinery validation BEFORE the market: the 18-check NQ parity gate
(stage0x_stageA.nq_parity) must PASS or nothing is computed; plus a synthetic
selftest of the ISO-week aggregation and the design-window boundary rules.

Usage: python research/stage0x_a13_cw1_btcweekly.py
"""
import json
import math
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
import stage0x_stageA as sa  # the parity-gated frozen machinery, reused verbatim

REPO = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
DESIGN_END = pd.Timestamp("2021-01-01", tz="UTC")   # seal boundary
SIGMA_WIN = 8          # frozen: 8-week trailing stdev of weekly log returns
LOW_RANK = sa.LOW_RANK  # 0.2752, the frozen rank-transfer percentile
OCCUPANCY_MIN = 10


# ------------------------------------------------------- daily load/audit ----
def load_daily():
    df = pd.read_csv(REPO / "data" / "btc_daily_canonical.csv")
    df["date"] = pd.to_datetime(df["date"], utc=True)
    df = df.sort_values("date").reset_index(drop=True)
    price = ["open", "high", "low", "close"]
    n_days = len(df)
    n_null = int(df[price].isna().any(axis=1).sum())
    n_nonpos = int((df[price] <= 0).any(axis=1).sum())
    bad_hl = int((df["high"] < df["low"]).sum())
    dup = int(df["date"].duplicated().sum())
    gaps = []
    d = df["date"].diff().dt.days
    for i, g in d.items():
        if pd.notna(g) and g > 1:
            gaps.append({"after": str(df.at[i - 1, "date"].date()), "days": int(g)})
    audit = {"n_days": n_days, "n_null_core": n_null, "n_nonpositive": n_nonpos,
             "bad_high_low": bad_hl, "duplicates": dup,
             "calendar_gaps_gt_1d": gaps,
             "first_date": str(df["date"].iloc[0].date()),
             "last_date": str(df["date"].iloc[-1].date())}
    return df, audit


# ------------------------------------------------- weekly aggregation core ----
def weekly_frame(day, design_end=DESIGN_END):
    """ISO-week aggregation. Returns (weeks_all, audit, design).
    weeks_all: one row per ISO week present in the daily data.
    design: completed weeks fully ending before design_end, index 0..n-1,
    with r1/sig_w/ev_next computed INSIDE the design frame only."""
    d = day.copy()
    d["week_start"] = d["date"] - pd.to_timedelta(d["date"].dt.weekday, unit="D")
    d["week_end"] = d["week_start"] + pd.Timedelta(days=7)
    g = d.groupby("week_start")
    wk = pd.DataFrame({
        "date": list(g.groups.keys()),
        "open": g["open"].first().values,
        "high": g["high"].max().values,
        "low": g["low"].min().values,
        "close": g["close"].last().values,
        "n_sessions": g.size().values,
    })
    wk["week_end"] = wk["date"] + pd.Timedelta(days=7)
    wk = wk.sort_values("date").reset_index(drop=True)
    last_day = d["date"].max()
    wk["completed"] = wk["week_end"] <= last_day + pd.Timedelta(days=1)

    n_forming = int((~wk["completed"]).sum())
    wks_d = d["week_start"].nunique()
    missing = []
    ws = wk["date"]
    for i in range(1, len(ws)):
        if (ws.iloc[i] - ws.iloc[i - 1]).days > 7:
            missing.append({"after": str(ws.iloc[i - 1].date()), "weeks_gap": int((ws.iloc[i] - ws.iloc[i - 1]).days // 7)})
    partial = [{"week_start": str(r.date.date()), "n_sessions": int(r.n_sessions)}
               for r in wk.itertuples() if r.completed and r.n_sessions < 7]
    audit = {
        "week_boundary": "UTC ISO weeks, Monday 00:00 boundary",
        "all_mondays": bool((wk["date"].dt.weekday == 0).all()),
        "n_week_groups": int(len(wk)),
        "n_sessions_sum_equals_days": bool(int(wk["n_sessions"].sum()) == len(d)),
        "n_sessions_sum": int(wk["n_sessions"].sum()),
        "n_completed": int(wk["completed"].sum()),
        "n_forming": n_forming,
        "partial_completed_weeks": partial,
        "missing_iso_weeks": missing,
        "first_week_start": str(ws.iloc[0].date()),
    }
    design = wk[wk["completed"] & (wk["week_end"] <= design_end)].reset_index(drop=True)
    design["r1"] = np.log(design["close"] / design["close"].shift(1))
    design["sig_w"] = design["r1"].rolling(SIGMA_WIN).std(ddof=1)
    design["ev_next"] = (design["r1"].shift(-1).abs() >= sa.BAR_K * design["sig_w"]).astype(float)
    design.loc[design["sig_w"].isna() | design["r1"].shift(-1).isna(), "ev_next"] = np.nan
    return wk, audit, design


def entry_row(design, i):
    return {
        "week_start": str(design.at[i, "date"].date()),
        "week_end_exclusive": str(design.at[i, "week_end"].date()),
        "close": float(design.at[i, "close"]),
        "sig_w": float(design.at[i, "sig_w"]),
    }


def look_ahead_audit(day, design, cidx, low_btc):
    """Every design entry's sig_w/cross state recomputed from dailies
    truncated at the entry week must match exactly (C-W2 semantics).
    LOW_BTC stays FROZEN during truncation."""
    n_checked = 0
    for i in cidx:
        cut = day[day["date"] < design.at[i, "week_end"]].reset_index(drop=True)
        wk, _a, dtr = weekly_frame(cut, design_end=pd.Timestamp("2100-01-01", tz="UTC"))
        # the truncated frame's completed weeks are exactly design weeks 0..i
        assert len(dtr) == i + 1, ("truncation length", i, len(dtr))
        a, b = design.at[i, "sig_w"], dtr.at[i, "sig_w"]
        if isinstance(a, float) and math.isnan(a):
            assert math.isnan(b), ("sig_w nan mismatch", i)
        else:
            assert a == b, ("sig_w not bit-identical", i, a, b)
        cidx_tr = sa.cross_entries(dtr, "sig_w", low_btc)
        assert i in cidx_tr, ("entry not reproduced under truncation", i)
        n_checked += 1
    return n_checked


# --------------------------------------------------------------- selftest ----
def selftest():
    idx = pd.date_range("2020-01-01", periods=60, freq="D", tz="UTC")
    # 2020-01-01 is a Wednesday: the first ISO week is partial (start Mon 2019-12-30)
    day = pd.DataFrame({
        "date": idx, "open": np.linspace(100, 130, 60),
        "high": np.linspace(101, 131, 60), "low": np.linspace(99, 129, 60),
        "close": np.linspace(100.5, 130.5, 60)})
    wk, audit, design = weekly_frame(day, design_end=pd.Timestamp("2020-02-15", tz="UTC"))
    assert audit["all_mondays"]
    assert str(wk["date"].iloc[0].date()) == "2019-12-30"
    assert wk["n_sessions"].iloc[0] == 5          # Wed-Sun
    assert audit["n_sessions_sum_equals_days"]
    assert audit["n_forming"] == 1                # the last week is mid-week
    # completed weeks fully ending before 2020-02-15: last one starts 2020-02-03
    assert str(design["date"].iloc[-1].date()) == "2020-02-03"
    assert str(design["week_end"].iloc[-1].date()) == "2020-02-10"
    # weekly close = last daily close of the week
    last_week = day[day["date"] < design["week_end"].iloc[-1]]
    assert design["close"].iloc[-1] == last_week["close"].iloc[-1]
    # sig_w: rolling 8 std ddof=1 of weekly log returns, hand-checked
    closes = pd.Series([100, 101, 99, 100, 102, 101, 100, 103, 102.0])
    r1 = np.log(closes / closes.shift(1))
    assert math.isnan(r1.rolling(8).std(ddof=1).iloc[7])   # only 7 valid returns
    assert abs(r1.rolling(8).std(ddof=1).iloc[8]
               - np.std(r1.iloc[1:9], ddof=1)) < 1e-12
    # cross semantics frozen: first crossing at or below cut, not dwell
    df = pd.DataFrame({"sig_w": [0.05, 0.04, 0.03, 0.03, 0.05]})
    idx_c = sa.cross_entries(df, "sig_w", 0.03)
    assert idx_c == [2]                            # in at 2, not 3 (dwell refused)
    return True


# ------------------------------------------------------------ the pipeline ----
def compute_report():
    day, daily_audit = load_daily()
    wk, weekly_audit, design = weekly_frame(day)

    # design-window rank transfer (LOW_BTC frozen once, here)
    valid = design.dropna(subset=["sig_w"])
    low_btc = float(valid["sig_w"].quantile(LOW_RANK))

    cidx = sa.cross_entries(design, "sig_w", low_btc)
    occupancy = len(cidx)

    n_look = 0
    look_pass = True
    if occupancy > 0:
        try:
            n_look = look_ahead_audit(day, design, cidx, low_btc)
        except AssertionError as e:
            look_pass = False
            lookahead_error = str(e)
    if not look_pass:
        raise SystemExit(f"LOOK-AHEAD AUDIT FAILED: {lookahead_error}")

    holdout_days = int((day["date"] >= DESIGN_END).sum())
    completed = wk[wk["completed"]]
    weeks_2021 = completed[completed["date"] >= DESIGN_END]
    straddle = completed[(completed["week_end"] > DESIGN_END)
                         & (completed["date"] < DESIGN_END)]

    report = {
        "claim": "C-W1 (Amendment 13, SIGNED 2026-11-19): after a compression-"
                 "range entry on BTC WEEKLY bars, the hazard of a "
                 "volatility-scaled significant move within 12 weeks exceeds "
                 "matched climatology",
        "frontier": "V4, claimed in docs/smh_frontier_v0.md, session 130, "
                    "before any Stage 0x computation",
        "data_vintage": daily_audit["last_date"],
        "design_window": {
            "first_completed_week": str(design["date"].iloc[0].date()) if len(design) else None,
            "last_design_week_start": str(design["date"].iloc[-1].date()) if len(design) else None,
            "weeks_fully_ending_before_2021": int(len(design)),
            "weeks_valid_sig_w": int(len(valid)),
            "sig_w_window": SIGMA_WIN,
            "2021plus_availability": {
                "daily_rows": holdout_days,
                "completed_weeks_starting_2021plus": int(len(weeks_2021)),
                "boundary_week_note": (f"{len(straddle)} completed ISO week(s) "
                                        "straddling 2021-01-01 (starts 2020-12-28): "
                                        "used by nothing, not design, not 2021+ "
                                        "counts, prices never read"),
                "sealed": "single-open only by this arm's own forward rows, on a dated go"},
        },
        "stage0x_audit": {"daily": daily_audit, "weekly": weekly_audit},
        "low_btc": {
            "rule": "frozen rank transfer: BTC's own design-window sig_w quantile at "
                    "the 27.52nd percentile (Amendment 9 convention)",
            "low_btc": low_btc,
            "nq_absolute_cut_context_only": sa.NQ_LOW,
        },
        "nq_parity_gate": None,   # filled by caller
        "stage0x_gates": {
            "occupancy": occupancy,
            "occupancy_min": OCCUPANCY_MIN,
            "determinism_pass": None,
            "lookahead_entries_checked": n_look,
            "lookahead_pass": look_pass,
        },
        "stageA": None,
        "kill_lines": None,
        "verdict": None,
        "refusals": [
            "no 2021+ computation beyond availability counts (sealed)",
            "no volume feature (price-only by construction)",
            "no stress arm (Amendment 13 registers the compression arm only)",
            "direction refused (the event is |r1|, both-side)",
            "no pooling with NQ or the macro four-market family",
            "no alternative constant/window/boundary (the no-forking clause)",
        ],
    }

    if occupancy < OCCUPANCY_MIN:
        report["stageA"] = {"verdict": "REFUSED: occupancy gate failed"}
        report["verdict"] = ("UNTESTED-LOW-OCCUPANCY: %d compression entries < %d; "
                             "the arm CLOSES, nothing downstream runs "
                             "(H7 and C-W3 precedent)" % (occupancy, OCCUPANCY_MIN))
        report["entries"] = [entry_row(design, i) for i in cidx]
        return report

    # ---------------- Stage A (parity-gated machinery, reused verbatim) ----
    pw = sa.person_weeks(design, cidx, "ev_next")
    km = sa.km_table(pw)
    gw = sa.greenwood_cum(km)
    clim_idx = design.index[design["sig_w"].notna()].tolist()
    cpw = sa.person_weeks(design, clim_idx, "ev_next")
    ckm = sa.km_table(cpw)
    cgw = sa.greenwood_cum(ckm)

    cum4, cum12 = gw[4][0], gw[12][0]
    clim4, clim12 = cgw[4][0], cgw[12][0]
    entry_lo12 = cum12 - sa.Z95 * gw[12][1]
    entry_hi12 = cum12 + sa.Z95 * gw[12][1]
    clim_lo12 = clim12 - sa.Z95 * cgw[12][1]
    clim_hi12 = clim12 + sa.Z95 * cgw[12][1]
    separate = entry_lo12 > clim_hi12          # unrounded comparison

    era_disp = []
    eras = {}
    for i in cidx:
        yr = design.at[i, "date"].year
        eras.setdefault(yr - yr % 2, []).append(i)
    for era in sorted(eras):
        epw = sa.person_weeks(design, eras[era], "ev_next")
        ekm = sa.km_table(epw)
        egw = sa.greenwood_cum(ekm)
        era_disp.append({
            "era": f"{era}-{era + 1}",
            "entries": len(eras[era]),
            "person_weeks": len(epw),
            "first_events": len({r["ep"] for r in epw if r["event"] == 1}),
            "cum12": round(egw[12][0], 4) if 12 in egw else None,
        })

    stage_a = {
        "entries": [entry_row(design, i) for i in cidx],
        "person_weeks": len(pw),
        "person_week_consistency": len(pw) == sum(km[h][0] for h in km),
        "first_events": len({r["ep"] for r in pw if r["event"] == 1}),
        "median_lag": sa.median_lag(pw),
        "cum4": round(cum4, 4), "cum12": round(cum12, 4),
        "band4": sa.band(gw, 4), "band12": sa.band(gw, 12),
        "climatology": {
            "decision_weeks": len(clim_idx),
            "person_weeks": len(cpw),
            "cum4": round(clim4, 4), "cum12": round(clim12, 4),
            "band4": sa.band(cgw, 4), "band12": sa.band(cgw, 12),
        },
        "salience_gap12": round(cum12 - clim12, 4),
        "salience_gap4": round(cum4 - clim4, 4),
        "gap_sign": "positive" if cum12 > clim12 else
                    ("negative" if cum12 < clim12 else "zero"),
        "band_separation": {
            "entry_band12_lower": entry_lo12, "entry_band12_upper": entry_hi12,
            "clim_band12_lower": clim_lo12, "clim_band12_upper": clim_hi12,
            "separate": bool(separate),
            "comparison": "unrounded, as frozen in the amendment",
        },
        "era_dispersion": era_disp,
        "overlap_caveat": "12-week windows overlap heavily; bands are "
                           "independence-style; the era dispersion is the honest "
                           "context (design bookkeeping, never proof)",
    }
    report["stageA"] = stage_a

    if not separate:
        report["verdict"] = ("UNTESTED-FAILED-DESIGN: the entry cum12 band does "
                             "not clear the matched climatology band; the arm "
                             "CLOSES before any forward row "
                             "(entry band lower %.4f <= clim band upper %.4f)"
                             % (entry_lo12, clim_hi12))
    else:
        kill = {n: sa.kill_line(n, cum12) for n in (10, 20, 40, 60)}
        report["kill_lines"] = {
            "p0_design_cum12": round(cum12, 4),
            "rule": "largest k with P(Bin(n, p0) <= k) <= 0.05",
            "n10": kill[10], "n20": kill[20], "n40": kill[40], "n60": kill[60],
        }
        report["verdict"] = ("DESIGN SURVIVES STAGE A (bands separate); kill "
                             "lines frozen above from the arm's own design cum12 "
                             "BEFORE any forward row; forward rows accrue from "
                             "the 2026-11-19 signature at the Sunday cadence; "
                             "design statistics are bookkeeping, never proof")
    return report


def main():
    selftest()
    ok, checks, summary = sa.nq_parity()
    print("NQ PARITY GATE:", "PASS" if ok else "FAIL")
    if not ok:
        for k, v in checks.items():
            if not v:
                print("  FAILED CHECK:", k)
        print("REFUSED: machinery failed NQ parity; no Stage 0x/A computed.")
        sys.exit(1)

    r1 = compute_report()
    r2 = compute_report()
    s1 = json.dumps(r1, indent=1)
    s2 = json.dumps(r2, indent=1)
    determinism = s1 == s2
    r1["nq_parity_gate"] = {"pass": True, "recomputed": summary}
    r1["stage0x_gates"]["determinism_pass"] = determinism

    out = REPO / "research" / "stage0x_a13_cw1_btcweekly_report.json"
    out.write_text(json.dumps(r1, indent=1))

    # design-week weekly CSV (price columns + n_sessions, no volume; design
    # weeks only, forward rows append from the signature date, never backfill)
    day, _ = load_daily()
    _wk, _audit, design = weekly_frame(day)
    design[["date", "open", "high", "low", "close", "n_sessions"]].to_csv(
        REPO / "data" / "btc_weekly_iso.csv", index=False)

    print("VERDICT:", r1["verdict"])
    print("occupancy:", r1["stage0x_gates"]["occupancy"],
          "| LOW_BTC:", round(r1["low_btc"]["low_btc"], 6))
    print("design weeks:", r1["design_window"]["weeks_fully_ending_before_2021"],
          "| valid sig_w weeks:", r1["design_window"]["weeks_valid_sig_w"])
    if r1["stageA"] and r1["stageA"].get("climatology"):
        sa_out = r1["stageA"]
        print("entry cum12", sa_out["cum12"], sa_out["band12"])
        print("clim  cum12", sa_out["climatology"]["cum12"], sa_out["climatology"]["band12"])
        print("gap12", sa_out["salience_gap12"], "| separate:",
              sa_out["band_separation"]["separate"])
        print("era_disp", sa_out["era_dispersion"])
    if r1["kill_lines"]:
        print("kill_lines", {k: v for k, v in r1["kill_lines"].items() if k.startswith("n")})
    print("determinism_pass:", determinism,
          "| lookahead checked:", r1["stage0x_gates"]["lookahead_entries_checked"])


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage0x_a13_cw1_btcweekly.py

  "direction refused (the event is |r1|, both-side)",
  "no pooling with NQ or the macro four-market family",
  "no alternative constant/window/boundary (the no-forking clause)"
 ]
}
##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage0x_a13_cw1_btcweekly_report.json

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage0x_btc.py (16925 bytes)
"""Stage 0x + Stage A for C-W3 (Amendment 12, SIGNED 2026-11-14; BTC arm).

Authorization: Andrea's dated directive of 2026-11-16 in the main conversation
("find what to do whilst I set up l2 environment ... actions of valuable
outcomes till or not needing l2 set up"), read as the go for the pre-registered,
already-signed Stage 0x/A design bookkeeping (the amendment's Section 7 note:
runs only on his dated go or his next directive). Nothing else is unblocked by
this reading; the L2 layer stays blocked on data; the 2021+ window stays sealed.

What this script computes (PRE-2021 UNSEALED design window only, per Section 2):
  Stage 0x: BTC-USD Yahoo daily fetch with the house sanity/repair rules
    (stage0c conventions, UTC dates for crypto); primed-state determinism and
    look-ahead audit (the arm's admission test, C-W2 semantics); occupancy
    count with the >=10-entry gate (H7 precedent: fewer closes the arm
    UNTESTED-LOW-OCCUPANCY); 2021+ availability counts only.
  Stage A: entry-anchored first-4-sigma-touch KM over the 60-calendar-day
    horizon with Greenwood band; matched climatology (clock started from every
    design-window day with valid sigma_d and range_pos, same barrier rule,
    overlap caveat inherited); era-block dispersion (2-year blocks) as the
    dependence context per the session-62/67 doctrine; kill lines by the
    frozen rule: largest k with P(Bin(n, p0) <= k) <= 0.05, p0 = the arm's own
    design cum60, at n = 10/20/40/60, frozen BEFORE any forward row.

What this script REFUSES to do:
  - any computation on 2021+ rows beyond availability counts (sealed fresh
    holdout: single-open only by this arm's own forward rows)
  - direction splits (refused everywhere on this branch)
  - any pooled claim with the macro/NQ programme
  - any volume feature (the primed state is price-only; the Yahoo volume
    caveat is reported, never used)

Machinery selftest BEFORE the market: synthetic fixtures for the Wilder ATR,
the prior-bar percentile rank, the comp_days reset, range_pos, the barrier
touch, administrative censoring and the KM risk set; determinism (double run
equality) and the truncation look-ahead audit (every entry's state recomputed
from data cut at the entry day must match exactly).

Usage: python research/stage0x_btc.py
"""
import json
import math
import time
import urllib.request
from datetime import timedelta
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

REPO = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
DESIGN_END = pd.Timestamp("2021-01-01", tz="UTC")   # exclusive; design is pre-2021
HORIZON_DAYS = 60
ATR_N = 30
RANK_WIN = 52
RVOL_CUT = 33.0
COMP_MIN = 20
RANGE_WIN = 365
RANGE_CUT = 0.35
SIGMA_N = 30
BARRIER_K = 4.0
OCCUPANCY_MIN = 10
Z95 = 1.959963985

URL = ("https://query1.finance.yahoo.com/v8/finance/chart/BTC-USD"
       f"?period1=1388534400&period2={int(time.time())}"
       "&interval=1d&includeAdjustedClose=false")


# ----------------------------------------------------------------- fetch ----
def fetch_btc():
    req = urllib.request.Request(URL, headers={"User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0 (research)"})
    with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=60) as fh:
        payload = json.load(fh)
    res = payload["chart"]["result"][0]
    ts = res["timestamp"]
    q = res["indicators"]["quote"][0]
    day = pd.DataFrame({
        "date": pd.to_datetime(ts, unit="s", utc=True).tz_convert("UTC").date,
        "open": q["open"], "high": q["high"], "low": q["low"], "close": q["close"],
        "volume": q["volume"],
    })
    day["date"] = pd.to_datetime(day["date"], utc=True)
    n_raw = len(day)
    repairs = []
    day = day.dropna(subset=["open", "high", "low", "close"]).copy()
    n_null = n_raw - len(day)
    day = day[day[["open", "high", "low", "close"]].gt(0).all(axis=1)]
    n_nonpos = n_raw - n_null - len(day)
    bad_hl = int((day["high"] < day["low"]).sum())
    day.loc[day["high"] < day["low"], ["high", "low"]] = np.nan
    day = day.dropna(subset=["high", "low"])
    for i, row in day.iterrows():
        rep = None
        if row["close"] > row["high"]:
            day.at[i, "close"] = row["high"]; rep = "close>high clipped to high"
        elif row["close"] < row["low"]:
            day.at[i, "close"] = row["low"]; rep = "close<low clipped to low"
        if rep:
            repairs.append({"date": str(row["date"].date()), "issue": rep})
    day = day.sort_values("date").reset_index(drop=True)
    dup = int(day["date"].duplicated().sum())
    if dup:
        day = day.drop_duplicates(subset="date", keep="last").reset_index(drop=True)
    gaps = []
    d = day["date"].diff().dt.days
    for i, g in d.items():
        if pd.notna(g) and g > 1:
            gaps.append({"after": str(day.at[i - 1, "date"].date()), "days": int(g)})
    audit = {"n_raw": n_raw, "n_null_core": int(n_null), "n_nonpositive": int(n_nonpos),
             "bad_high_low": bad_hl, "repairs": repairs, "duplicates": dup,
             "calendar_gaps_gt_1d": gaps, "zero_volume_days": int((day["volume"] == 0).sum()),
             "first_date": str(day["date"].iloc[0].date()),
             "last_date": str(day["date"].iloc[-1].date())}
    return day, audit


# ------------------------------------------------------ primed-state core ----
def wilder_atr(high, low, close, n=ATR_N):
    prev_close = close.shift(1)
    tr = pd.concat([high - low, (high - prev_close).abs(),
                    (low - prev_close).abs()], axis=1).max(axis=1)
    tr.iloc[0] = high.iloc[0] - low.iloc[0]
    atr = pd.Series(np.nan, index=high.index)
    atr.iloc[n - 1] = tr.iloc[:n].mean()
    for i in range(n, len(tr)):
        atr.iloc[i] = (atr.iloc[i - 1] * (n - 1) + tr.iloc[i]) / n
    return atr


def state_frame(day):
    """Decision-time state: every value at row t is a function of rows <= t
    only (rvol_pct via the PRIOR bar's ATR rank; range_pos via row t close;
    sigma_d via trailing returns through row t)."""
    df = day.copy()
    df["atr_d"] = wilder_atr(df["high"], df["low"], df["close"])
    prior = df["atr_d"].shift(1)
    df["rvol_pct"] = prior.rolling(RANK_WIN).apply(
        lambda w: 100.0 * np.sum(w <= w.iloc[-1]) / len(w), raw=False)
    comp = []
    run = 0
    for v in df["rvol_pct"]:
        run = run + 1 if pd.notna(v) and v <= RVOL_CUT else 0
        comp.append(run)
    df["comp_days"] = comp
    lo = df["low"].rolling(RANGE_WIN, min_periods=RANGE_WIN).min()
    hi = df["high"].rolling(RANGE_WIN, min_periods=RANGE_WIN).max()
    df["range_pos"] = (df["close"] - lo) / (hi - lo)
    df["r1"] = np.log(df["close"] / df["close"].shift(1))
    df["sigma_d"] = df["r1"].rolling(SIGMA_N).std(ddof=1)
    df["primed"] = ((df["comp_days"] >= COMP_MIN) & (df["rvol_pct"] <= RVOL_CUT)
                    & (df["range_pos"] <= RANGE_CUT))
    df["primed_prev"] = df["primed"].shift(1, fill_value=False)
    df["entry"] = df["primed"] & ~df["primed_prev"]
    return df


def clock_of(df, entry_i):
    """(event_day, censor_day) for the 60-day window after entry_i.
    event_day: first calendar day (1..60) whose bar touches either barrier.
    censor_day: last observable day (60, or the design end if earlier).
    Administrative censoring at the design end; the 2021+ window is sealed."""
    e_date = df.at[entry_i, "date"]
    e_close = df.at[entry_i, "close"]
    e_sigma = df.at[entry_i, "sigma_d"]
    # SPEC REPAIR (recorded 2026-11-16, Stage 0x, before any forward row, for
    # Andrea's ratification): the amendment's literal "entry_close + 4*sigma_d"
    # is dimensionally incoherent (sigma_d is a dimensionless log-return stdev,
    # measured 0.0115-0.0454 here); the literal form places the barriers within
    # ~0.02-0.2% of the close and 1933/1934 climatology clocks touch on day 1.
    # Repaired to the unique log-return-consistent scaling
    # entry_close * exp(+-4*sigma_d). No alternative scaling was computed or
    # compared (no tuning); the frozen K=4 and horizon=60 are unchanged.
    up = e_close * math.exp(BARRIER_K * e_sigma)
    dn = e_close * math.exp(-BARRIER_K * e_sigma)
    last = min(e_date + timedelta(days=HORIZON_DAYS), DESIGN_END - timedelta(days=1))
    win = df[(df["date"] > e_date) & (df["date"] <= last)]
    for _, row in win.iterrows():
        if row["high"] >= up or row["low"] <= dn:
            return (row["date"] - e_date).days, None
    return None, (last - e_date).days


def km_curves(clocks, horizon=HORIZON_DAYS):
    """clocks: list of (event_day or None, censor_day or None).
    Risk set at day d: clocks with (event_day is not None and event_day >= d)
    or (event_day is None and censor_day >= d)."""
    cum_surv = 1.0
    var = 0.0
    curve = []
    for d in range(1, horizon + 1):
        n = 0
        e = 0
        for ev, cens in clocks:
            if ev is not None and ev >= d:
                n += 1
                if ev == d:
                    e += 1
            elif ev is None and cens is not None and cens >= d:
                n += 1
        if n > 0 and e > 0:
            h = e / n
            cum_surv *= (1 - h)
            if 1 - h > 0:
                var += h / (1 - h) / n   # Greenwood; h=1 collapses the band
        se = math.sqrt(max(var, 0.0))
        curve.append({"day": d, "n_risk": n, "events": e,
                      "cum": 1.0 - cum_surv,
                      "lo": max(0.0, 1.0 - cum_surv - Z95 * se),
                      "hi": min(1.0, 1.0 - cum_surv + Z95 * se)})
    return curve


def kmax(n, p0):
    """Largest k with P(Bin(n, p0) <= k) <= 0.05 (the frozen kill-line rule)."""
    best = None
    cdf = 0.0
    for j in range(n + 1):
        cdf += math.comb(n, j) * p0 ** j * (1 - p0) ** (n - j)
        if cdf <= 0.05:
            best = j
    return best


# --------------------------------------------------------------- selftest ----
def selftest():
    idx = pd.date_range("2020-01-01", periods=40, freq="D", tz="UTC")
    h = pd.Series(np.linspace(100, 110, 40), index=idx)
    l = pd.Series(np.linspace(99, 109, 40), index=idx)
    c = pd.Series(np.linspace(99.5, 109.5, 40), index=idx)
    atr = wilder_atr(h, l, c, n=5)
    assert abs(atr.iloc[4] - (h - l).iloc[:5].mean()) < 1e-12
    assert abs(atr.iloc[5] - (atr.iloc[4] * 4 + (h.iloc[5] - l.iloc[5])) / 5) < 1e-12
    # comp reset
    runs = []
    run = 0
    for v in [1.0, 2.0, 40.0, 1.0, 1.0]:
        run = run + 1 if v <= 33 else 0
        runs.append(run)
    assert runs == [1, 2, 0, 1, 2]
    # touch + administrative censor: barrier touch on day 5
    df = pd.DataFrame({
        "date": pd.date_range("2020-01-01", periods=90, freq="D", tz="UTC"),
        "high": [110.0] * 90, "low": [90.0] * 90, "close": [100.0] * 90,
        "sigma_d": [0.01] * 90})
    # window opens the day AFTER entry: first candidate is index 6 (day 1),
    # high 110 >= 100*exp(4*0.01)=104.08 -> event on window day 1
    ev, cens = clock_of(df, 5)
    assert ev == 1 and cens is None
    # wider barriers -> no touch, full-window censor at day 60
    df3 = df.copy()
    df3["sigma_d"] = 0.1
    ev2, cens2 = clock_of(df3, 6)
    assert ev2 is None and cens2 == 60
    # KM risk set: one clock failing on day 1 leaves the risk set after day 1
    cur = km_curves([(1, None)])
    assert cur[0]["n_risk"] == 1 and cur[0]["events"] == 1
    assert cur[1]["n_risk"] == 0 and abs(cur[1]["cum"] - 1.0) < 1e-12
    # censoring: a clock censored on day 10 leaves the risk set after day 10
    cur2 = km_curves([(None, 10)])
    assert cur2[9]["n_risk"] == 1 and cur2[10]["n_risk"] == 0
    # kill-line rule: P(Bin(10,0.5)<=1)=0.0107 <= 0.05, P(<=2)=0.0547 > 0.05
    assert kmax(10, 0.5) == 1
    assert kmax(10, 0.0) is None   # degenerate p0: empty lower tail, nothing passes at 5%
    assert kmax(10, 1.0) == 9   # P(<=9)=0 -> 9; k=10 gives 1.0 > 0.05
    return True


def main():
    selftest()
    day, audit = fetch_btc()
    day.to_csv(REPO / "data" / "btc_daily_canonical.csv", index=False)

    design = day[day["date"] < DESIGN_END].reset_index(drop=True)
    holdout_avail = int((day["date"] >= DESIGN_END).sum())

    s1 = state_frame(design)
    s2 = state_frame(design)
    pd.testing.assert_frame_equal(s1, s2)

    # look-ahead audit: every entry's state recomputed from data cut at the
    # entry day must match exactly
    n_checked = 0
    for i in np.where(s1["entry"])[0]:
        cut = design.iloc[: i + 1].reset_index(drop=True)
        sc = state_frame(cut)
        for col in ["atr_d", "rvol_pct", "comp_days", "range_pos", "sigma_d",
                    "close", "entry"]:
            a, b = s1.at[i, col], sc.at[i, col]
            if isinstance(a, float) and math.isnan(a):
                assert math.isnan(b)
            else:
                assert a == b, (col, i)
        n_checked += 1

    entries = np.where(s1["entry"])[0]
    occupancy = len(entries)

    entry_clocks, entry_rows = [], []
    for i in entries:
        ev, cens = clock_of(s1, i)
        entry_clocks.append((ev, cens))
        entry_rows.append({
            "entry_date": str(s1.at[i, "date"].date()),
            "entry_close": float(s1.at[i, "close"]),
            "sigma_d": float(s1.at[i, "sigma_d"]),
            "barrier_up": float(s1.at[i, "close"] * math.exp(BARRIER_K * s1.at[i, "sigma_d"])),
            "barrier_dn": float(s1.at[i, "close"] * math.exp(-BARRIER_K * s1.at[i, "sigma_d"])),
            "event_day": ev, "censor_day": cens})

    clim_pool = s1[(s1["sigma_d"].notna()) & (s1["range_pos"].notna())].index
    clim_clocks = [clock_of(s1, i) for i in clim_pool]

    km_entry = km_curves(entry_clocks)
    km_clim = km_curves(clim_clocks)
    cum60_e = km_entry[-1]["cum"]
    cum60_c = km_clim[-1]["cum"]

    # era dispersion (2-year calendar blocks), descriptive
    eras = {}
    for i, clock in zip(entries, entry_clocks):
        yr = s1.at[i, "date"].year
        era = f"{yr - yr % 2}-{yr - yr % 2 + 1}"
        eras.setdefault(era, []).append(clock)
    era_disp = []
    for era in sorted(eras):
        cur = km_curves(eras[era])
        n_ev = sum(1 for ev, c in eras[era] if ev is not None)
        era_disp.append({"era": era, "n_entries": len(eras[era]),
                         "events": n_ev, "cum60": round(cur[-1]["cum"], 4)})

    kill = {n: kmax(n, cum60_e) for n in (10, 20, 40, 60)}

    report = {
        "claim": "C-W3 primed-state entry on BTC daily; first 4-sigma barrier "
                 "touch within 60 calendar days vs matched climatology",
        "amendment": "Amendment 12 (SIGNED 2026-11-14); go directive 2026-11-16",
        "design_window": {"start": audit["first_date"], "end_exclusive": "2021-01-01",
                          "n_days": int(len(design)),
                          "holdout_2021plus_availability_days": holdout_avail},
        "stage0x_audit": audit,
        "stage0x_gates": {
            "occupancy": occupancy,
            "occupancy_min": OCCUPANCY_MIN,
            "occupancy_pass": occupancy >= OCCUPANCY_MIN,
            "determinism_pass": True,
            "lookahead_entries_checked": n_checked,
            "lookahead_pass": True,
            "volume_caveat": "Yahoo BTC-USD volume is consolidated-feed; the "
                             "primed state is price-only; no volume feature used"},
        "stageA": {
            "entries": entry_rows,
            "entry_cum60": round(cum60_e, 4),
            "entry_band60": [round(km_entry[-1]["lo"], 4), round(km_entry[-1]["hi"], 4)],
            "climatology_pool_days": int(len(clim_pool)),
            "climatology_cum60": round(cum60_c, 4),
            "climatology_band60": [round(km_clim[-1]["lo"], 4), round(km_clim[-1]["hi"], 4)],
            "gap": round(cum60_e - cum60_c, 4),
            "overlap_caveat": "heavy 60-day overlap inherited; bands are "
                              "independence-style, the session-62/67 dependence "
                              "doctrine applies; the era dispersion is the "
                              "honest context",
            "era_dispersion": era_disp},
        "kill_lines": {"p0_design_cum60": round(cum60_e, 4),
                       "rule": "largest k with P(Bin(n, p0) <= k) <= 0.05",
                       "n10": kill[10], "n20": kill[20], "n40": kill[40], "n60": kill[60]},
        "refusals": ["no 2021+ computation beyond availability counts",
                     "direction refused", "no pooling with macro/NQ",
                     "no volume features"],
    }
    (REPO / "research" / "stage0x_btc_report.json").write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=1))
    print(json.dumps({k: report[k] for k in
                      ["design_window", "stage0x_gates", "kill_lines"]}, indent=1))
    print("entry_cum60", report["stageA"]["entry_cum60"], report["stageA"]["entry_band60"])
    print("clim_cum60", report["stageA"]["climatology_cum60"], report["stageA"]["climatology_band60"])
    print("gap", report["stageA"]["gap"])
    print("era_disp", era_disp)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage0x_btc.py
