##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage0x_crossmarket.py (8284 bytes)
"""Stage 0x (cross-market SMH bookkeeping): Gold GC=F / Nikkei ^N225 / DAX ^GDAXI.

Amendment 9 (DRAFT, UNSIGNED) tooling. Authored 2026-11-12, NEVER RUN.
Runs only on a Python-enabled reset (verify_all.sh rebuilds /home/user/projects/quant
from the bundle first; run this script from the repo root of that tree).

What this script does (pre-signature, bookkeeping only):
  - fetch daily bars per symbol from the Yahoo v8 chart endpoint (stage0c
    convention: sanity, repair, thin-volume flag), W-FRI aggregation
  - sig_w = 8-week rolling std (ddof=1) of weekly close-to-close log returns
  - PRE-2021 (< 2021-01-01) occupancy bookkeeping ONLY:
      weeks, first valid sig_w, region tiling under the RANK-TRANSFER cuts
      (27.52nd / 58.10th percentiles of that market's own pre-2021 sig_w),
      compression/stress entry counts by cross semantics, and the NQ
      absolute cuts (LOW 0.01739 / HIGH 0.02697) as reported context only.
  - 2021+ rows are stored but NEVER computed on (availability counts only,
    the stage0v precedent; each market's 2021+ window is a fresh sealed
    holdout per the amendment).

What this script REFUSES without --amendment9-signed (hard gate):
  - event flags (2.5-sigma bar), hazards, KM curves, climatology, forward
    returns, any 2021+ computation beyond availability counts.
  Those are Stage A evaluation per Amendment 9 and run only after Andrea's
  signature is recorded in docs/amendment9_crossmarket_smh.md.

Usage:
  python research/stage0x_crossmarket.py                 # bookkeeping + report
  python research/stage0x_crossmarket.py --amendment9-signed   # (refused until the doc carries a signature line; Stage A machinery to be authored only after signature)
"""
import json
import sys
import time
import urllib.request
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

REPO = Path("/home/user/projects/quant")
(REPO / "data").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

SYMBOLS = {"GOLD": "GC=F", "NIKKEI": "^N225", "DAX": "^GDAXI"}
LOW_RANK = 0.2752      # measured percentile rank of the frozen NQ LOW cut (session 47)
HIGH_RANK = 0.5810     # measured percentile rank of the frozen NQ HIGH cut (session 48)
NQ_LOW, NQ_HIGH = 0.01739, 0.02697   # NQ absolute cuts, context only, never selected
HOLDOUT_START = pd.Timestamp("2021-01-01")
SIGNED = "--amendment9-signed" in sys.argv

if SIGNED:
    # Refuse until the amendment doc actually carries a recorded signature.
    amendment = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "docs" / "amendment9_crossmarket_smh.md"
    text = amendment.read_text() if amendment.exists() else ""
    sig_section = text.split("## 8. Sign-off")[-1] if "## 8. Sign-off" in text else ""
    if "PENDING" in sig_section or "____" in sig_section:
        print("REFUSED: Amendment 9 carries no recorded signature. Stage A does not run.")
        sys.exit(1)
    print("Amendment 9 signature detected: Stage A evaluation machinery is NOT yet "
          "authored in this script (per amendment Section 7 it is authored only after "
          "sign-off). Bookkeeping continues; no evaluation performed this run.")


def fetch_weekly(symbol: str) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """stage0c convention fetch + W-FRI aggregation."""
    p1, p2 = 946684800, int(time.time())
    url = (f"https://query1.finance.yahoo.com/v8/finance/chart/{symbol}"
           f"?period1={p1}&period2={p2}&interval=1d&includeAdjustedClose=false")
    req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers={"User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0 (research)"})
    with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=60) as fh:
        payload = json.load(fh)
    res = payload["chart"]["result"][0]
    ts = res["timestamp"]
    q = res["indicators"]["quote"][0]
    day = pd.DataFrame({
        "date": pd.to_datetime(ts, unit="s", utc=True).tz_convert("America/New_York").date,
        "open": q["open"], "high": q["high"], "low": q["low"],
        "close": q["close"], "volume": q["volume"],
    })
    day["date"] = pd.to_datetime(day["date"])
    n_raw = len(day)
    repairs = []
    day = day.dropna(subset=["open", "high", "low", "close"]).copy()
    n_null = n_raw - len(day)
    day = day[day[["open", "high", "low", "close"]].gt(0).all(axis=1)]
    day.loc[day["high"] < day["low"], ["high", "low"]] = np.nan
    day = day.dropna(subset=["high", "low"])
    for i, row in day.iterrows():
        rep = None
        if row["close"] > row["high"]:
            day.at[i, "close"] = row["high"]; rep = "close>high clipped"
        elif row["close"] < row["low"]:
            day.at[i, "close"] = row["low"]; rep = "close<low clipped"
        if rep:
            repairs.append({"date": str(row["date"].date()), "issue": rep})
    day = day.sort_values("date").reset_index(drop=True)
    day["week_end"] = day["date"] + pd.to_timedelta((4 - day["date"].dt.dayofweek) % 7, "D")
    wk = day.groupby("week_end").agg(
        open=("open", "first"), high=("high", "max"), low=("low", "min"),
        close=("close", "last"), volume=("volume", "sum"), n_sessions=("date", "count"),
    ).reset_index()
    wk["date"] = wk["week_end"]
    return wk, {"n_raw": n_raw, "n_null_dropped": n_null, "n_repairs": len(repairs), "repairs": repairs}


def bookkeeping(label: str, wk: pd.DataFrame) -> dict:
    """PRE-2021 occupancy bookkeeping only. No events, no forward returns, no 2021+ stats."""
    wk = wk.sort_values("date").reset_index(drop=True)
    wk["r1"] = np.log(wk["close"] / wk["close"].shift(1))
    wk["sig_w"] = wk["r1"].rolling(8).std(ddof=1)
    pre = wk[wk["date"] < HOLDOUT_START].copy()
    post = wk[wk["date"] >= HOLDOUT_START]
    valid = pre.dropna(subset=["sig_w"])
    if valid.empty:
        return {"label": label, "error": "no pre-2021 valid sig_w weeks; arm closes without evaluation"}
    low_cut = float(valid["sig_w"].quantile(LOW_RANK))
    high_cut = float(valid["sig_w"].quantile(HIGH_RANK))
    in_low = valid["sig_w"] <= low_cut
    in_high = valid["sig_w"] > high_cut
    c_entries = int((in_low & ~in_low.shift(1, fill_value=False)).sum())
    s_entries = int((in_high & ~in_high.shift(1, fill_value=False)).sum())
    occ = {
        "compression": float(in_low.mean()), "mid": float((~in_low & ~in_high).mean()),
        "stress": float(in_high.mean()),
    }
    # absolute-cut context (reported, never selected)
    a_low = (valid["sig_w"] <= NQ_LOW).mean()
    a_high = (valid["sig_w"] > NQ_HIGH).mean()
    return {
        "label": label,
        "weeks_pre2021": int(len(pre)),
        "weeks_valid_sig": int(len(valid)),
        "first_valid_sig_week": str(valid["date"].iloc[0].date()),
        "last_valid_sig_week": str(valid["date"].iloc[-1].date()),
        "rank_transfer_cuts": {"low": low_cut, "high": high_cut,
                                "low_rank": LOW_RANK, "high_rank": HIGH_RANK},
        "occupancy_rank_cuts": occ,
        "compression_entries_rank_cuts": c_entries,
        "stress_entries_rank_cuts": s_entries,
        "compression_occupancy_gate": "UNTESTABLE-arm-closes" if c_entries < 10 else "passable",
        "abs_cut_context_only": {"low_occupancy": float(a_low), "high_occupancy": float(a_high)},
        "holdout_weeks_available_only": int(len(post)),
        "note": ("availability counts only on 2021+; no event, hazard, KM, forward "
                 "return, or any 2021+ computation without --amendment9-signed and "
                 "authored Stage A machinery"),
    }


def main() -> None:
    report = {"fetched_at_utc": time.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ", time.gmtime()),
              "amendment9_signed": bool(SIGNED), "markets": {}}
    for label, sym in SYMBOLS.items():
        try:
            wk, fetch_info = fetch_weekly(sym)
            wk.to_csv(REPO / "data" / f"crossmarket_{label.lower()}_weekly.csv", index=False)
            report["markets"][label] = {"symbol": sym, "fetch": fetch_info,
                                        **bookkeeping(label, wk)}
        except Exception as exc:  # symbol unavailable etc.: record, no substitution
            report["markets"][label] = {"symbol": sym, "error": f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"}
    out = REPO / "research" / "stage0x_crossmarket_report.json"
    out.write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2, default=str))
    print(json.dumps(report, indent=2, default=str))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage0x_crossmarket.py

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage0x_stageA.py (13504 bytes)
"""Stage 0x Stage A (cross-market SMH design bookkeeping, Amendment 9 SIGNED branch).

Authored 2026-11-14 AFTER Andrea's recorded signature in
docs/amendment9_crossmarket_smh.md (per amendment Section 7: Stage A machinery is
authored only after sign-off). Pre-registration: amendment Section 5 Stage A.

What this script computes (per market, PRE-2021 UNSEALED design window only):
  - compression entries by frozen cross semantics (rank-transfer cuts, each
    market's own 27.52nd / 58.10th percentile of its pre-2021 sig_w)
  - entry-anchored 12-week first-significant-week KM with Greenwood 95% band
  - matched climatology KM (same clock started from every pre-2021 decision
    week with valid sig_w; the NQ construction, overlap caveat inherited)
  - the salience gap (entry cum12 minus climatology cum12) and its sign
  - the stress/de-stress arm mirrored from the frozen NQ stress layer
  - per-market forward kill lines at n = 10/20/40/60 scored rows from that
    market's OWN Stage-A design cum12 (5% one-sided binomial quantile rule,
    the frozen NQ convention), to be frozen in docs/amendment9_market_addendum.md
    BEFORE that market's first forward row.

What this script REFUSES to do:
  - any computation on 2021+ rows beyond availability counts already reported
    by stage0x_crossmarket.py bookkeeping (each market's 2021+ window is a
    FRESH SEALED HOLDOUT: single-open per market, only on a dated go)
  - direction splits (direction is forward-only everywhere; no seductive cell
    is created cross-market)
  - any pooled cross-market claim (family max-t across the three markets is
    required for any pooled statement; per-market numbers only are reported)

Machinery validation BEFORE the markets: the same code path recomputes the
frozen NQ constants from the canonical NQ weekly file with the frozen NQ
cuts (independent reimplementation parity, the lesson-14 convention):
41 entries, 327 person-weeks, 26 first events, cum4 0.268 / cum12 0.634,
Greenwood h12 (0.487, 0.634, 0.782), climatology cum12 0.441 band
(0.411, 0.441, 0.471), stress 33 entries / cum12 0.636, kill lines 3/8/19/31.
If any parity assertion fails, nothing is computed on the new markets.

Usage:
  python research/stage0x_stageA.py            # NQ parity gate, then the 3 markets
"""
import json
import math
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

REPO = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
DESIGN_END = pd.Timestamp("2021-01-01")
HORIZON = 12
BAR_K = 2.5
LOW_RANK, HIGH_RANK = 0.2752, 0.5810
Z95 = 1.959963985

# Frozen NQ constants (docs/smh_entry_theorem_v0.md, smh_theorem.py) for parity.
NQ_LOW, NQ_HIGH = 0.01739, 0.02697
NQ_FROZEN = {
    "entries": 41, "person_weeks": 327, "first_events": 26, "median_lag": 5,
    "cum4": 0.268, "cum12": 0.634,
    "band4": (0.133, 0.268, 0.404), "band12": (0.487, 0.634, 0.782),
    "clim_cum4": 0.168, "clim_cum12": 0.4409, "clim_band12": (0.411, 0.441, 0.471),
    "stress_entries": 33, "stress_person_weeks": 258, "stress_events": 21,
    "stress_cum4": 0.273, "stress_cum12": 0.636,
    "stress_band12": (0.472, 0.636, 0.801), "stress_median_lag": 6,
    "kill_lines": {10: 3, 20: 8, 40: 19, 60: 31},
}
SYMBOLS = {"GOLD": "GC=F", "NIKKEI": "^N225", "DAX": "^GDAXI"}


def load_weekly(path: Path, low: float, high: float):
    """Independent loader mirroring the frozen NQ conventions."""
    df = pd.read_csv(path, parse_dates=["date"])
    df = df.sort_values("date").reset_index(drop=True)
    df["r1"] = np.log(df["close"] / df["close"].shift(1))
    df["sig_w"] = df["r1"].rolling(8).std(ddof=1)
    df["ev_next"] = (df["r1"].shift(-1).abs() >= BAR_K * df["sig_w"]).astype(float)
    df.loc[df["sig_w"].isna() | df["r1"].shift(-1).isna(), "ev_next"] = np.nan
    df["destress_next"] = (df["sig_w"].shift(-1) <= high).astype(float)
    df.loc[df["sig_w"].isna() | df["sig_w"].shift(-1).isna(), "destress_next"] = np.nan
    return df


def cross_entries(df, region_col, cut, negate=False):
    """Frozen cross semantics: in_region & ~prev_in_region, valid sig_w only."""
    if negate:
        inside = df[region_col] > cut
    else:
        inside = df[region_col] <= cut
    prev = inside.shift(1).fillna(False).astype(bool)  # session-31 dtype lesson
    return df.index[inside & (~prev) & df[region_col].notna()].tolist()


def person_weeks(df, idx, event_col):
    """Verbatim mirror of the frozen person-week construction (smh_forecast)."""
    rows = []
    for i, t0 in enumerate(idx):
        for h in range(1, HORIZON + 1):
            if t0 + h > len(df) - 2:
                break
            e = df.loc[t0 + h - 1, event_col]
            if np.isnan(e):
                break
            rows.append(dict(ep=i, t0=int(t0), h=h, event=int(e > 0)))
            if e > 0:
                break
    return rows


def km_table(pw):
    km = {}
    for h in range(1, HORIZON + 1):
        rows = [r for r in pw if r["h"] == h]
        km[h] = (len(rows), sum(r["event"] for r in rows))
    return km


def greenwood_cum(km):
    """h -> (cum, greenwood se). Verbatim mirror of smh_theorem.greenwood_cum."""
    S, acc, out = 1.0, 0.0, {}
    for h in range(1, HORIZON + 1):
        n, ev = km[h]
        if n > 0:
            S *= 1 - ev / n
            if ev and n - ev:
                acc += ev / (n * (n - ev))
            out[h] = (1 - S, (S * S * acc) ** 0.5)
    return out


def band(gw, h):
    cum, se = gw[h]
    return (round(max(0.0, cum - Z95 * se), 3), round(cum, 3),
            round(min(1.0, cum + Z95 * se), 3))


def median_lag(pw):
    lags = []
    seen = set()
    for r in pw:
        if r["event"] == 1 and r["ep"] not in seen:
            seen.add(r["ep"])
            lags.append(r["h"])
    if not lags:
        return None
    s = sorted(lags)
    m = len(s) // 2
    return (s[m] + s[-m - 1]) / 2 if len(s) % 2 == 0 else s[m]


def binom_cdf(k, n, p):
    return sum(math.comb(n, i) * p ** i * (1 - p) ** (n - i) for i in range(0, k + 1))


def kill_line(n, p0):
    """Largest k with P(Bin(n, p0) <= k) <= 0.05 (the frozen NQ convention)."""
    k = -1
    while binom_cdf(k + 1, n, p0) <= 0.05:
        k += 1
    return k


def stage_a(df, low, high, gate_min=10):
    """Stage A design bookkeeping for one market with given cuts."""
    pre = df[df["date"] < DESIGN_END].copy().reset_index(drop=True)
    valid = pre.dropna(subset=["sig_w"])
    cidx = cross_entries(pre, "sig_w", low)
    sidx = cross_entries(pre, "sig_w", high, negate=True)
    res = {
        "weeks_pre2021": int(len(pre)),
        "weeks_valid_sig": int(len(valid)),
        "rank_cuts": {"low": round(low, 6), "high": round(high, 6)},
        "compression_entries": len(cidx),
        "stress_entries": len(sidx),
    }
    # occupancy gate (pre-declared, the H7 precedent)
    if len(cidx) < gate_min:
        res["compression_arm"] = {"verdict": "UNTESTABLE: <10 entries, arm closes without evaluation"}
        res["compression_kill_lines"] = None
    else:
        pw = person_weeks(pre, cidx, "ev_next")
        km = km_table(pw)
        gw = greenwood_cum(km)
        clim_idx = pre.index[pre["sig_w"].notna()].tolist()
        cpw = person_weeks(pre, clim_idx, "ev_next")
        ckm = km_table(cpw)
        cgw = greenwood_cum(ckm)
        cum12, cum4 = gw[12][0], gw[4][0]
        clim12, clim4 = cgw[12][0], cgw[4][0]
        res["compression_arm"] = {
            "person_weeks": len(pw),
            "person_week_consistency": len(pw) == sum(km[h][0] for h in km),
            "km_hazards": {str(h): [km[h][0], km[h][1]] for h in km},
            "first_events": sum(1 for r in pw if r["event"] == 1) and len(
                {r["ep"] for r in pw if r["event"] == 1}),
            "median_lag": median_lag(pw),
            "cum4": round(cum4, 4), "cum12": round(cum12, 4),
            "band4": band(gw, 4), "band12": band(gw, 12),
            "climatology": {"person_weeks": len(cpw),
                            "cum4": round(clim4, 4), "cum12": round(clim12, 4),
                            "band12": band(cgw, 12)},
            "salience_gap12": round(cum12 - clim12, 4),
            "salience_gap4": round(cum4 - clim4, 4),
            "gap_sign": "positive" if cum12 > clim12 else ("negative" if cum12 < clim12 else "zero"),
        }
        res["compression_kill_lines"] = {str(n): kill_line(n, cum12) for n in (10, 20, 40, 60)}
    # stress arm (de-stress event, no bar knob; may survive where compression cannot)
    if len(sidx) < gate_min:
        res["stress_arm"] = {"verdict": "UNTESTABLE: <10 entries, arm closes without evaluation"}
        res["stress_kill_lines"] = None
    else:
        spw = person_weeks(pre, sidx, "destress_next")
        skm = km_table(spw)
        sgw = greenwood_cum(skm)
        res["stress_arm"] = {
            "person_weeks": len(spw),
            "person_week_consistency": len(spw) == sum(skm[h][0] for h in skm),
            "first_events": len({r["ep"] for r in spw if r["event"] == 1}),
            "median_lag": median_lag(spw),
            "cum4": round(sgw[4][0], 4), "cum12": round(sgw[12][0], 4),
            "band4": band(sgw, 4), "band12": band(sgw, 12),
            "comparator": "coin 0.5 (the frozen NQ stress convention)",
        }
        res["stress_kill_lines"] = {str(n): kill_line(n, sgw[12][0]) for n in (10, 20, 40, 60)}
    res["multiplicity_note"] = ("per-market design bookkeeping only; any pooled "
                               "cross-market claim carries a family max-t across "
                               "the three markets; design statistics are "
                               "bookkeeping, never proof; no 2021+ computation "
                               "beyond availability counts")
    return res


def nq_parity():
    """Independent recomputation of the frozen NQ constants; hard gate."""
    df = load_weekly(REPO / "data" / "nq_weekly_canonical.csv", NQ_LOW, NQ_HIGH)
    pre = df[df["date"] < DESIGN_END].copy().reset_index(drop=True)
    cidx = cross_entries(pre, "sig_w", NQ_LOW)
    pw = person_weeks(pre, cidx, "ev_next")
    km = km_table(pw)
    gw = greenwood_cum(km)
    clim_idx = pre.index[pre["sig_w"].notna()].tolist()
    cpw = person_weeks(pre, clim_idx, "ev_next")
    cgw = greenwood_cum(km_table(cpw))
    sidx = cross_entries(pre, "sig_w", NQ_HIGH, negate=True)
    spw = person_weeks(pre, sidx, "destress_next")
    sgw = greenwood_cum(km_table(spw))
    f = NQ_FROZEN
    checks = {
        "entries": len(cidx) == f["entries"],
        "person_weeks": len(pw) == f["person_weeks"],
        "consistency": len(pw) == sum(km[h][0] for h in km),
        "first_events": len({r["ep"] for r in pw if r["event"] == 1}) == f["first_events"],
        "median_lag": median_lag(pw) == f["median_lag"],
        "cum4": abs(gw[4][0] - f["cum4"]) < 0.002,
        "cum12": abs(gw[12][0] - f["cum12"]) < 0.002,
        "band4": all(abs(a - b) < 0.003 for a, b in zip(band(gw, 4), f["band4"])),
        "band12": all(abs(a - b) < 0.003 for a, b in zip(band(gw, 12), f["band12"])),
        "clim_cum4": abs(cgw[4][0] - f["clim_cum4"]) < 0.002,
        "clim_cum12": abs(cgw[12][0] - f["clim_cum12"]) < 0.002,
        "clim_band12": all(abs(a - b) < 0.003 for a, b in zip(band(cgw, 12), f["clim_band12"])),
        "stress_entries": len(sidx) == f["stress_entries"],
        "stress_person_weeks": len(spw) == f["stress_person_weeks"],
        "stress_events": len({r["ep"] for r in spw if r["event"] == 1}) == f["stress_events"],
        "stress_cum12": abs(sgw[12][0] - f["stress_cum12"]) < 0.002,
        "stress_band12": all(abs(a - b) < 0.003 for a, b in zip(band(sgw, 12), f["stress_band12"])),
        "kill_lines": all(kill_line(n, gw[12][0]) == f["kill_lines"][n] for n in (10, 20, 40, 60)),
    }
    ok = all(checks.values())
    return ok, checks, {
        "entries": len(cidx), "person_weeks": len(pw), "cum12": round(gw[12][0], 4),
        "clim_cum12": round(cgw[12][0], 4), "stress_cum12": round(sgw[12][0], 4),
        "kill_lines": {str(n): kill_line(n, gw[12][0]) for n in (10, 20, 40, 60)},
    }


def main():
    ok, checks, summary = nq_parity()
    print("NQ PARITY GATE:", "PASS" if ok else "FAIL")
    for k, v in checks.items():
        if not v:
            print("  FAILED CHECK:", k)
    print("  recomputed:", json.dumps(summary))
    if not ok:
        print("REFUSED: Stage A machinery failed NQ parity; no market computed.")
        sys.exit(1)
    report = {"fetched_by": "stage0x_crossmarket.py", "stage": "A (design bookkeeping, pre-2021 only)",
              "nq_parity_gate": {"pass": ok, "recomputed": summary}, "markets": {}}
    for label in SYMBOLS:
        path = REPO / "data" / f"crossmarket_{label.lower()}_weekly.csv"
        df = pd.read_csv(path, parse_dates=["date"])
        # rank-transfer cuts: frozen convention, each market's own pre-2021 quantiles
        full = df.sort_values("date").reset_index(drop=True)
        full["r1"] = np.log(full["close"] / full["close"].shift(1))
        full["sig_w"] = full["r1"].rolling(8).std(ddof=1)
        valid = full[full["date"] < DESIGN_END].dropna(subset=["sig_w"])
        low = float(valid["sig_w"].quantile(LOW_RANK))
        high = float(valid["sig_w"].quantile(HIGH_RANK))
        mdf = load_weekly(path, low, high)
        report["markets"][label] = stage_a(mdf, low, high)
    out = REPO / "research" / "stage0x_stageA_report.json"
    out.write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2))
    print(json.dumps(report["markets"], indent=2))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage0x_stageA.py

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage1_replication.py (4975 bytes)
"""Stage 1: replication unit tests + AVFI series export.

T1 EWM matches pandas ewm(halflife, adjust=False) (1e-12)
T2 pine_ema matches pandas alpha=2/(len+1) (1e-12)
T3 multiplicative scale invariance: price x1000, volume x7 leaves every
   indicator series unchanged (1e-12); flow3 scales exactly by 7
T4 additive price shift CHANGES outputs (correct behavior)
T5 warmup gating: avfi3/avfi2/emaV2/states first valid at index == warmup;
   research burn-in downstream = warmup + emaLen = 365
T6 gate: dead zone and saturation exact
T7 Parkinson sigmaPk within 10% of true sigma on fine-grid simulated GBM

Writes data/avfi_series.csv + research/stage1_replication_report.json.
"""
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path("/home/user/projects/quant/strategies")))
from avfi_replication import (compute_avfi, DEFAULT_PARAMS, ewm_halflife,
                              pine_ema, _w_of)

REPO = Path("/home/user/projects/quant")
results = {}

rng = np.random.default_rng(7)
x = pd.Series(rng.normal(size=600))
ours = ewm_halflife(x, 65.0)
results["T1_ewm_vs_pandas_maxdiff"] = float((ours - x.ewm(halflife=65.0, adjust=False).mean()).abs().max())

ours2 = pine_ema(x, 105)
results["T2_pineema_vs_pandas_maxdiff"] = float((ours2 - x.ewm(alpha=2.0 / 106.0, adjust=False).mean()).abs().max())

df = pd.read_csv(REPO / "data" / "nq_weekly_canonical.csv", parse_dates=["date"])
n = len(df)
out = compute_avfi(df)

df_scaled = df.copy()
for c in ("open", "high", "low", "close"):
    df_scaled[c] = df_scaled[c] * 1000.0
df_scaled["volume"] = df_scaled["volume"] * 7.0
out_s = compute_avfi(df_scaled)
diffs = {}
for c in ("sigma3", "w3", "rho3", "avfi3", "avfi2", "emaV2", "spread", "sp_z"):
    a_, b_ = out[c].to_numpy(float), out_s[c].to_numpy(float)
    m = ~np.isnan(a_)
    diffs[c] = float(np.nanmax(np.abs(a_[m] - b_[m]))) if m.any() else None
fa, fb = out["flow3"].to_numpy(float), out_s["flow3"].to_numpy(float)
m = ~np.isnan(fa)
diffs["flow3_scales_exactly_x7"] = float(np.nanmax(np.abs(fb[m] / 7.0 - fa[m])))
results["T3_scale_invariance_maxdiff"] = max(v for kk, v in diffs.items()
                                             if kk != "flow3_scales_exactly_x7" and v is not None)
results["T3_detail"] = diffs

df_shift = df.copy()
for c in ("open", "high", "low", "close"):
    df_shift[c] = df_shift[c] + 5000.0
out_sh = compute_avfi(df_shift)
results["T4_additive_shift_changes_spread"] = bool(
    np.nanmax(np.abs(out["spread"].to_numpy(float) -
                     out_sh["spread"].to_numpy(float))) > 1e-9)

w, e = DEFAULT_PARAMS["warmup"], DEFAULT_PARAMS["emaLen"]
first_avfi3 = int(out["avfi3"].first_valid_index()) if out["avfi3"].notna().any() else None
first_emaV2 = int(out["emaV2"].first_valid_index()) if out["emaV2"].notna().any() else None
first_state = int(out.index[out["state"] != ""][0]) if (out["state"] != "").any() else None
results["T5_first_avfi3_index"] = first_avfi3
results["T5_first_emaV2_index"] = first_emaV2
results["T5_first_state_index"] = first_state
results["T5_research_burn_in"] = w + e

k_, a_ = DEFAULT_PARAMS["k"], DEFAULT_PARAMS["deadZone"]
z = pd.Series([0.0, 0.1, 0.2, 0.3, 1.0, -0.1, -0.2, -0.5, -5.0, 5.0])
xx = k_ * np.maximum(0.0, z.abs() - a_)
exp_w = np.tanh(xx) * np.sign(z)
got_w = _w_of(z, k_, a_).to_numpy()
results["T6_gate_maxdiff"] = float(np.abs(got_w - exp_w).max())
results["T6_deadzone_zero_inside"] = bool(got_w[0] == 0.0 and got_w[1] == 0.0 and got_w[2] == 0.0)
results["T6_saturation_bounded"] = bool(np.abs(got_w).max() <= 1.0)

rng2 = np.random.default_rng(123)
n_weeks, steps_per_week = 5000, 200
z_path = rng2.normal(0, 0.0319, size=(n_weeks, steps_per_week))
logp = np.hstack([np.zeros((n_weeks, 1)), np.cumsum(z_path, axis=1)])
hl = logp.max(axis=1) - logp.min(axis=1)  # log(H/L) = range of log-price path
sigma_pk_sim = np.sqrt((hl ** 2).mean() / (4 * np.log(2)))
true_sigma_week = 0.0319 * np.sqrt(steps_per_week)
results["T7_parkinson_vs_true_pct_bias"] = float(sigma_pk_sim / true_sigma_week - 1.0)

checks = {
    "T1": results["T1_ewm_vs_pandas_maxdiff"] < 1e-12,
    "T2": results["T2_pineema_vs_pandas_maxdiff"] < 1e-12,
    "T3": results["T3_scale_invariance_maxdiff"] < 1e-12,
    "T4": results["T4_additive_shift_changes_spread"],
    "T5": (first_avfi3 == w) and (first_emaV2 == w) and (first_state == w),
    "T6": results["T6_gate_maxdiff"] < 1e-14 and results["T6_deadzone_zero_inside"],
    "T7": abs(results["T7_parkinson_vs_true_pct_bias"]) < 0.10,
}
results["all_passed"] = bool(all(checks.values()))
results["checks"] = {kk: bool(vv) for kk, vv in checks.items()}
results["note_tradingview_crosscheck"] = "pending: needs manual chart comparison by Andrea"
results["n_bars"] = int(n)

out.to_csv(REPO / "data" / "avfi_series.csv", index=False)
(REPO / "research" / "stage1_replication_report.json").write_text(json.dumps(results, indent=2))
print("all_passed:", results["all_passed"], "| n_bars:", n)

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage1_replication.py

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage1b_overlay_parity.py (5041 bytes)
"""Stage 1b: numeric parity test of the AVFI v3.1 Pine research overlay.

The v3.1 Pine script (strategies/avfi_v31_research.pine) cannot be compiled
locally; its core AVFI math is identical to the validated reference, but the
NEW overlay formulas were hand-written for this edition. This test
re-implements the overlay formulas exactly as written in Pine, in pandas on
the current canonical weekly bars, and checks they reproduce the stage 7
snapshot card. Pine-syntax validation still requires the TradingView paste;
this closes the arithmetic half of the gap.

Pine formulas under test (verbatim from v3.1):
  mom12     = log(close / close[momLen])                  momLen = 12
  mom12Last = log(close[1] / close[momLen+1])
  tLong     = mom12 > 0            tLongLast = mom12Last > 0
  r_w       = log(close / close[1])
  vol8      = sqrt( sum((r_w - sma(r_w, 8))^2, 8) / 7 ) * sqrt(52)
  volFactor = min(1, 0.20 / (vol8 + EPS))
  throttle  = 0.5 if state == "Reversal down" else 1.0
  u3Size    = tLong ? volFactor * throttle : 0

The stage 7 snapshot computes the same quantities with the completed-bar
convention (last completed close = the reference bar). The Pine panel shows
both "last closed week" (matches the snapshot exactly) and "if this week
closes now" (matches the snapshot of the forming bar). This test checks the
completed-bar pair, which is what the paper log records.
"""
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path("/home/user/projects/quant/strategies")))
from avfi_replication import compute_avfi

REPO = Path("/home/user/projects/quant")
EPS, MOM_LEN, VOL_WIN, VOL_TGT, THROTTLE = 1e-12, 12, 8, 0.20, 0.5

df = pd.read_csv(REPO / "data" / "nq_weekly_canonical.csv", parse_dates=["date"])

today = pd.Timestamp(pd.Timestamp.now().date())
# the panel lives on the last chart bar; on a live weekly chart that is the
# FORMING week, so the completed-bar decision is one bar back (close[1])
last_label = df["date"].iloc[-1]
forming = last_label >= today  # a W-FRI week labeled today is never complete
# before today's close; on the close day itself the last bar is still forming
# (session-144 defect fix: == treated the completing week as completed)
i = len(df) - (2 if forming else 1)          # position of the completed bar

close = df["close"].to_numpy(float)

# ---- Pine overlay formulas, translated 1:1 ----
# panel "last closed week" rows use close[1]/close[13] relative to the last
# (forming) chart bar; relative to the completed bar itself that is
# close[i]/close[i-12] etc.
mom12_last = np.log(close[i] / close[i - MOM_LEN]) if close[i - MOM_LEN] > 0 else np.nan
t_long_last = mom12_last > 0

r_w = np.log(close[1:] / close[:-1])
r_w = np.concatenate([[np.nan], r_w])        # r_w[0] undefined
seg = r_w[i - VOL_WIN + 1: i + 1]
r_mean = seg.mean()                          # Pine ta.sma == pandas rolling mean
vol8 = np.sqrt(((seg - r_mean) ** 2).sum() / (VOL_WIN - 1)) * np.sqrt(52.0)
vol_factor = min(1.0, VOL_TGT / (vol8 + EPS))

av = compute_avfi(df)
state_last = str(av["state"].iloc[i])
throttle = THROTTLE if state_last == "reversal_down" else 1.0
u3_size = (vol_factor * throttle) if t_long_last else 0.0

# ---- compare against the stage 7 snapshot card ----
snap = json.loads((REPO / "research" / "stage7_snapshot_current.json").read_text())

checks = {
    "completed_bar_date": bool(str(df["date"].iloc[i].date()) == snap["last_completed_week"]),
    "T_signal": bool(("LONG" if t_long_last else "FLAT") == snap["T_signal"]),
    "vol8_ann": bool(abs(vol8 - snap["U3"]["vol8_ann"]) < 5e-4),
    "vol_factor": bool(abs(vol_factor - snap["U3"]["vol_factor"]) < 5e-4),
    "throttle": bool(abs(throttle - snap["U3"]["throttle"]) < 1e-9),
    "position_size": bool(abs(u3_size - snap["U3"]["position_size"]) < 5e-4),
    "state": bool(state_last == snap["U3"]["avfi_state"]),
}
report = {
    "purpose": "numeric parity of the v3.1 Pine overlay formulas vs stage 7 snapshot",
    "forming_week_detected": bool(forming),
    "completed_bar": str(df["date"].iloc[i].date()),
    "pine_translation": {
        "mom12_last": round(float(mom12_last), 4),
        "t_long_last": bool(t_long_last),
        "vol8_ann": round(float(vol8), 4),
        "vol_factor": round(float(vol_factor), 3),
        "state": state_last,
        "throttle": throttle,
        "u3_size": round(float(u3_size), 3),
    },
    "snapshot": {
        "T_signal": snap["T_signal"],
        "vol8_ann": snap["U3"]["vol8_ann"],
        "vol_factor": snap["U3"]["vol_factor"],
        "state": snap["U3"]["avfi_state"],
        "position_size": snap["U3"]["position_size"],
    },
    "checks": checks,
    "all_passed": all(checks.values()),
    "caveat": ("validates the overlay arithmetic in pandas; Pine v5 syntax itself "
               "is validated only by the TradingView paste (pending cross-check)"),
}

(REPO / "research" / "stage1b_overlay_parity_report.json").write_text(
    json.dumps(report, indent=2))
print(json.dumps(report, indent=2))

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage1b_overlay_parity.py

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage1b_pgt_replication.py (12307 bytes)
"""Stage 1b: PGT plane-geometry terminal-layer replication tests (Amendment 5 draft).

Unit tests of the G1/G2/G3 detector logic in strategies/avfi_pgt_layer.py
against its registered semantics, per the stage-1 discipline requirement:

T25 G1 plane-excitation divergence: at a fresh pbN-week high whose prior
    fresh-high run ended with sp_z >= bandK, the flag fires iff current
    sp_z < prior run-end sp_z; no fire when the prior excursion was
    below the band plane, no fire when current sp_z >= prior.
T26 G2 truncated oscillation: at a fresh high, fires iff sp_z reached
    the same-side band plane within oscWin weeks and never touched the
    opposite plane in that window; no fire on a two-sided window; no
    fire away from fresh extremes.
T27 G3 regime-plane re-cross failure: fires iff sp_z crosses the zero
    (mu_s) plane against the lagging state (down-cross while
    momentum_up; up-cross while momentum_down); wrong state -> no fire.
T28 run chaining: consecutive fresh-extreme bars form ONE run; the
    prior reference is the LAST bar of the previous run; flags fire at
    run starts only.
T29 core parity on canonical data: the layer mutates nothing (sp_z and
    state identical to compute_avfi), and its fresh-extreme convention
    matches the validated tb layer's newHigh/newLow exactly.
T30 validity: every flagged bar has a valid sp_z; G1/G2 flags only from
    the first valid sp_z (bar 759 on canonical data).
T31 scale invariance: prices x1000 leave every flag series identical.
T32 descriptive coverage only: unsealed (pre-2021) flag/run occupancy
    counts, holdout availability as counts (no returns, no forward
    statistics, no evaluation of any PGT hypothesis), plus the overlap
    counts against the closed H8 divTop flags (family relationship).

This stage NEVER computes forward returns, NEVER evaluates any PGT
hypothesis (that waits on a signed, dated Amendment 5), and never runs
any battery. Counts are data coverage, not evidence.

Writes data/avfi_pgt_series.csv + research/stage1b_pgt_replication_report.json.
"""
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path("/home/user/projects/quant/strategies")))
from avfi_replication import compute_avfi
from avfi_pgt_layer import compute_pgt_layer, PGT_DEFAULT_PARAMS

REPO = Path("/home/user/projects/quant")
results = {}
ok = lambda x: bool(x)


def fake_base(n, closes, spz, states):
    """Minimal base frame: constant spread keeps mu_s/sd_s finite."""
    return pd.DataFrame({
        "date": pd.date_range("2000-01-07", periods=n, freq="W-FRI"),
        "close": closes, "spread": [0.1] * n, "sp_z": spz, "state": states,
    })


# ── T25: G1 plane-excitation divergence ─────────────────────────────
n = 60
pbN = PGT_DEFAULT_PARAMS["pbN"]
# closes: flat 100 for pbN bars, jump to 120 (fresh high run of 3), 4-bar
# dip, then a higher run at 141 whose sp_z sits below the prior excursion.
closes = [100.0] * pbN + [120.0, 121.0, 122.0] + [119.0, 118.0, 117.0, 116.0] \
    + [141.0, 142.0, 143.0] + [115.0] * (n - pbN - 10)
spz = [np.nan] * n
states = ["momentum_up"] * n
# run 1 (fresh highs at idx 20..22): prior-extreme excursion sp_z = 2.0
spz[20] = 2.0
spz[21] = 1.9
spz[22] = 1.8
# run 2 (fresh highs at idx 27..29): current sp_z 1.0 < 2.0 -> G1 fires
spz[27] = 1.0
spz[28] = 0.9
spz[29] = 0.8
b = fake_base(n, closes, spz, states)
L = compute_pgt_layer(b)
g1_fires = list(L.index[L["g1Top"]])
results["T25_g1"] = {
    "fires_only_at_run2_start": ok(g1_fires == [pbN + 7]),
    "fired_index": g1_fires,
    "prior_spz": float(L["hiPriorSpz"].iloc[pbN + 7]),
    "no_fire_when_current_ge_prior": None,  # set below
    "no_fire_when_prior_below_band": None,  # set below
}
# variant A: current sp_z >= prior -> no fire
spzA = list(spz); spzA[27] = 2.1
LA = compute_pgt_layer(fake_base(n, closes, spzA, states))
results["T25_g1"]["no_fire_when_current_ge_prior"] = ok(
    not LA["g1Top"].any())
# variant B: prior run-END excursion below the band plane -> no fire
spzB = list(spz); spzB[22] = 1.2
LB = compute_pgt_layer(fake_base(n, closes, spzB, states))
results["T25_g1"]["no_fire_when_prior_below_band"] = ok(
    not LB["g1Top"].any())

# ── T26: G2 truncated oscillation ──────────────────────────────────
oscWin = PGT_DEFAULT_PARAMS["oscWin"]
bandK = PGT_DEFAULT_PARAMS["bandK"]
# fresh high at the last bar; window 26 holds sp_z in [-1.0, 2.0]
n2 = pbN + oscWin + 2
cl2 = [100.0] * pbN + [100.0] * (n2 - pbN - 1) + [150.0]
spz2 = [np.nan] * n2
for i in range(n2 - 1):
    spz2[i] = 2.0 if i % 3 == 0 else -1.0
spz2[-1] = 0.5
L2 = compute_pgt_layer(fake_base(n2, cl2, spz2, ["momentum_up"] * n2))
g2_one_sided = bool(L2["g2Top"].iloc[-1])
# two-sided window: z_min <= -bandK kills it
spz3 = list(spz2)
spz3[n2 - 5] = -1.6
L3 = compute_pgt_layer(fake_base(n2, cl2, spz3, ["momentum_up"] * n2))
g2_two_sided = bool(L3["g2Top"].iloc[-1])
# away from fresh extremes: strictly declining closes -> close is always
# below the rolling max -> no fresh high -> no fire
cl4 = [100.0 - 0.05 * i for i in range(n2)]
L4 = compute_pgt_layer(fake_base(n2, cl4, spz2, ["momentum_up"] * n2))
results["T26_g2"] = {
    "one_sided_fires": ok(g2_one_sided),
    "two_sided_no_fire": ok(not g2_two_sided),
    "not_fresh_extreme_no_fire": ok(not L4["g2Top"].any()),
}

# ── T27: G3 regime-plane re-cross failure ──────────────────────────
n5 = pbN + 10
cl5 = [100.0 + i * 0.0 for i in range(n5)]
spz5 = [1.0] * (n5 - 2) + [1.0, -0.5]   # down-cross at the last bar
st5 = ["momentum_up"] * n5
L5 = compute_pgt_layer(fake_base(n5, cl5, spz5, st5))
cross_dn_momup = bool(L5["g3Top"].iloc[-1])
L5b = compute_pgt_layer(fake_base(n5, cl5, spz5, ["reversal_down"] * n5))
cross_dn_reversal = bool(L5b["g3Top"].iloc[-1])
spz6 = [-1.0] * (n5 - 2) + [-1.0, 0.5]  # up-cross at the last bar
L6 = compute_pgt_layer(fake_base(n5, cl5, spz6, ["momentum_down"] * n5))
cross_up_momdn = bool(L6["g3Bot"].iloc[-1])
L6b = compute_pgt_layer(fake_base(n5, cl5, spz6, ["momentum_up"] * n5))
cross_up_wrong_state = bool(L6b["g3Bot"].iloc[-1])
results["T27_g3"] = {
    "dn_cross_momentum_up_fires": ok(cross_dn_momup),
    "dn_cross_wrong_state_no_fire": ok(not cross_dn_reversal),
    "up_cross_momentum_down_fires": ok(cross_up_momdn),
    "up_cross_wrong_state_no_fire": ok(not cross_up_wrong_state),
}

# ── T28: run chaining (prior = last bar of previous run) ───────────
# fresh-high run at idx 20..22 then a new run at 25; the reference for
# run 2 must be idx 22's sp_z (1.8), not run 1's start.
cl7 = [100.0] * pbN + [120.0, 121.0, 122.0] + [119.0] * 2 \
    + [123.0] + [90.0] * (n - pbN - 6)
spz7 = [np.nan] * n
spz7[20], spz7[21], spz7[22] = 2.0, 1.95, 1.8
spz7[25] = 1.0
L7 = compute_pgt_layer(fake_base(n, cl7, spz7, ["momentum_up"] * n))
results["T28_run_chain"] = {
    "reference_is_run_end": ok(
        abs(float(L7["hiPriorIdx"].iloc[25]) - 22.0) < 1e-12),
    "prior_spz_is_run_end_value": ok(
        abs(float(L7["hiPriorSpz"].iloc[25]) - 1.8) < 1e-12),
    "fires_once_per_new_run": ok(list(L7.index[L7["g1Top"]]) == [25]),
}

# ── T29: core parity on canonical data ─────────────────────────────
df = pd.read_csv(REPO / "data" / "nq_weekly_canonical.csv", parse_dates=["date"])
av = compute_avfi(df)
pgt = compute_pgt_layer(av)
from avfi_tb_layer import compute_tb_layer
tb = compute_tb_layer(av)
results["T29_core_parity"] = {
    "sp_z_untouched": ok(float(np.nanmax(np.abs(
        pgt["sp_z"].to_numpy() - av["sp_z"].to_numpy()))) == 0.0),
    "state_untouched": ok((pgt["state"] == av["state"]).all()),
    "newHigh_matches_tb_layer": ok((pgt["newHigh"] == tb["newHigh"]).all()),
    "newLow_matches_tb_layer": ok((pgt["newLow"] == tb["newLow"]).all()),
}

# ── T30: validity ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
first_valid_spz = int(av["sp_z"].first_valid_index())
flag_cols = ["g1Top", "g1Bot", "g2Top", "g2Bot", "g3Top", "g3Bot",
             "pgtTop", "pgtBot"]
all_flags_valid = all(
    bool(pgt.loc[pgt[c], "sp_z"].notna().all()) for c in flag_cols)
first_flag_idx = min(
    [int(pgt.index[pgt[c]][0]) for c in flag_cols if pgt[c].any()])
results["T30_validity"] = {
    "first_valid_spz_bar": first_valid_spz,
    "first_flag_bar": first_flag_idx,
    "flags_only_after_spz_valid": ok(all_flags_valid
                                     and first_flag_idx >= first_valid_spz),
}

# ── T31: scale invariance ──────────────────────────────────────────
df2 = df.copy()
for col in ["open", "high", "low", "close"]:
    df2[col] = df2[col] * 1000.0
df2["volume"] = df2["volume"] * 7.0
pgt2 = compute_pgt_layer(compute_avfi(df2))
ident = all((pgt[c] == pgt2[c]).all() for c in flag_cols)
results["T31_scale_invariance"] = {"flags_identical": ok(ident)}


def collapse_runs(flags):
    """Occupancy: raw flagged weeks + maximal consecutive runs."""
    raw = int(flags.sum())
    f = flags.to_numpy(dtype=bool)
    runs = 0
    prev = False
    for v in f:
        if v and not prev:
            runs += 1
        prev = v
    return {"weeks": raw, "runs": runs}


# ── T32: descriptive coverage only (no forward returns) ────────────
unsealed = df["date"] <= "2020-12-31"
holdout = df["date"] >= "2021-01-01"
pgt_un, pgt_ho = pgt.loc[unsealed], pgt.loc[holdout]
cov = {}
for c in flag_cols:
    cov[c] = {**collapse_runs(pgt_un[c]),
              "holdout_weeks": int(pgt_ho[c].sum())}
# family relationship vs the closed H8 divTop (occupancy overlap only)
overlap_g1_divtop = int((pgt_un["g1Top"] & tb.loc[unsealed, "divTop"]).sum())
overlap_pgttop_divtop = int((pgt_un["pgtTop"] & tb.loc[unsealed, "divTop"]).sum())
# sp_z-valid weeks actually at risk in the unsealed window
results["T32_coverage"] = {
    "unsealed_weeks": int(unsealed.sum()),
    "unsealed_spz_valid": int(pgt_un["sp_z"].notna().sum()),
    "flags": cov,
    "g1Top_overlap_with_h8_divTop_weeks": overlap_g1_divtop,
    "pgtTop_overlap_with_h8_divTop_weeks": overlap_pgttop_divtop,
    "holdout_note": "availability counts only; no forward returns, "
                    "no PGT evaluation (waits on signed Amendment 5)",
}

all_pass = all([
    results["T25_g1"]["fires_only_at_run2_start"],
    results["T25_g1"]["no_fire_when_current_ge_prior"],
    results["T25_g1"]["no_fire_when_prior_below_band"],
    results["T26_g2"]["one_sided_fires"],
    results["T26_g2"]["two_sided_no_fire"],
    results["T26_g2"]["not_fresh_extreme_no_fire"],
    results["T27_g3"]["dn_cross_momentum_up_fires"],
    results["T27_g3"]["dn_cross_wrong_state_no_fire"],
    results["T27_g3"]["up_cross_momentum_down_fires"],
    results["T27_g3"]["up_cross_wrong_state_no_fire"],
    results["T28_run_chain"]["reference_is_run_end"],
    results["T28_run_chain"]["prior_spz_is_run_end_value"],
    results["T28_run_chain"]["fires_once_per_new_run"],
    results["T29_core_parity"]["sp_z_untouched"],
    results["T29_core_parity"]["state_untouched"],
    results["T29_core_parity"]["newHigh_matches_tb_layer"],
    results["T29_core_parity"]["newLow_matches_tb_layer"],
    results["T30_validity"]["flags_only_after_spz_valid"],
    results["T31_scale_invariance"]["flags_identical"],
])
results["all_passed"] = bool(all_pass)

pgt_out = pgt[["date", "close", "sp_z", "state", "newHigh", "newLow",
               "g1Top", "g1Bot", "g2Top", "g2Bot", "g3Top", "g3Bot",
               "pgtTop", "pgtBot"]]
pgt_out.to_csv(REPO / "data" / "avfi_pgt_series.csv", index=False)
(REPO / "research" / "stage1b_pgt_replication_report.json").write_text(
    json.dumps(results, indent=2, default=float))
print(json.dumps({k: v for k, v in results.items()
                  if k in ("T29_core_parity", "T30_validity",
                           "T32_coverage", "all_passed")},
                 indent=2, default=float))
print("ALL PASSED" if all_pass else "FAILURES PRESENT")

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage1b_pgt_replication.py

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage1b_v32_replication.py (12256 bytes)
"""Stage 1b: v3.2 tops/bottoms layer replication tests (Amendment 3 draft).

Unit tests of the robust-z (IQR, prior-bar window) and CUSUM layers against
the Pine semantics in strategies/avfi_v32_topbottom.pine, per the stage 1
discipline requirement in docs/amendment3_h8_h9_topbottom.md:

T8  percentile_nearest_rank matches hand-computed nearest-rank values on a
    known window; a window containing na yields na (Pine na-poisoning)
T9  robust z uses the PRIOR-bar window: an extreme spread bar is excluded
    from its own reference distribution (sp_rz at the spike bar computed
    without it; differs from the naive inclusive-window z)
T10 fresh extremes: newHigh/newLow == (close == rolling pbN max/min),
    na before pbN bars
T11 climax definition: rho >= climRho AND |z3| <= deadZone
T12 CUSUM: no alarm on a stationary series; alarms after a sustained mean
    shift; statistic resets to 0 at the alarm bar; near_break covers
    exactly breakHold bars after the alarm
T13 scale invariance: prices x1000, volume x7 leave every tops/bottoms
    series unchanged (1e-12)
T14 warmup/validity on canonical data: first valid sp_rz at warmup+normWin
    (260+500=760); CUSUM starts the same bar; flags never fire earlier
T15 divergence flags: divTop == newHigh & sp_rz <= fadeZ (and mirror)

This stage computes OBSERVATION FLAGS ONLY. It never computes forward
returns, never evaluates H8/H9 (that waits on Amendment 3 sign-off), never
touches the sealed 2021+ holdout beyond counting flag occupancy, and never
runs the stage 6b battery. Event counts by window are reported as data
coverage, not as evidence for or against any hypothesis.

Writes data/avfi_v32_tb_series.csv + research/stage1b_v32_replication_report.json.
"""
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path("/home/user/projects/quant/strategies")))
from avfi_replication import compute_avfi, DEFAULT_PARAMS
from avfi_tb_layer import (TB_DEFAULT_PARAMS, percentile_nearest_rank,
                           robust_z, cusum_guard, compute_tb_layer)

REPO = Path("/home/user/projects/quant")
results = {}

# ── T8: percentile nearest-rank, hand-computed ─────────────────────
xs = pd.Series([3.0, 1.0, 4.0, 1.5, 5.0, 9.0, 2.5, 6.0])  # window n=5
# windows (ascending sorted, nearest-rank):
# [3,1,4,1.5,5] sorted [1,1.5,3,4,5]: 50% -> ceil(2.5)=3 -> 3 ; 75% -> ceil(3.75)=4 -> 4
# [1,4,1.5,5,9] sorted [1,1.5,4,5,9]: 50% -> 4 ; 75% -> 5
# [4,1.5,5,9,2.5] sorted [1.5,2.5,4,5,9]: 50% -> 4 ; 75% -> 5
# [1.5,5,9,2.5,6] sorted [1.5,2.5,5,6,9]: 50% -> 5 ; 75% -> 6
got50 = percentile_nearest_rank(xs, 5, 50).to_numpy()
got75 = percentile_nearest_rank(xs, 5, 75).to_numpy()
exp50 = [np.nan, np.nan, np.nan, np.nan, 3.0, 4.0, 4.0, 5.0]
exp75 = [np.nan, np.nan, np.nan, np.nan, 4.0, 5.0, 5.0, 6.0]
d50 = np.nanmax(np.abs(got50 - np.array(exp50)))
d75 = np.nanmax(np.abs(got75 - np.array(exp75)))
xs_na = xs.copy()
xs_na.iloc[6] = np.nan  # window [4,1.5,5,9,na] at idx 6, [1.5,5,9,na,6] at 7
got_na = percentile_nearest_rank(xs_na, 5, 50)
results["T8_pnr_hand_maxdiff"] = float(max(d50, d75))
results["T8_na_window_poisons"] = bool(got_na.iloc[6:].isna().all())

# ── T9: prior-bar exclusion (no self-contamination) ─────────────────
n = 700
base_sp = pd.Series(np.linspace(-1.0, 1.0, n) + np.sin(np.arange(n) * 0.05) * 0.1)
sp = base_sp.copy()
sp.iloc[650] = 25.0  # extreme spike
med, q75, q25, rsig, sp_rz = robust_z(sp, 100)
# window at bar 650 covers 550..649 (no spike); naive inclusive covers 551..650
inc = sp.rolling(100, min_periods=100)
med_inc = inc.apply(lambda w: np.sort(w)[49], raw=True)
q75_inc = inc.apply(lambda w: np.sort(w)[74], raw=True)
q25_inc = inc.apply(lambda w: np.sort(w)[24], raw=True)
rz_inc = (sp - med_inc) / ((q75_inc - q25_inc) / 1.349 + 1e-12)
window_no_spike = sp.iloc[550:650].to_numpy()
results["T9_spike_excluded_from_own_window"] = bool(
    abs(med.iloc[650] - np.sort(window_no_spike)[49]) < 1e-12)
results["T9_excludes_vs_inclusive_z_differs"] = bool(
    abs(sp_rz.iloc[650] - rz_inc.iloc[650]) > 1.0)
results["T9_spike_bar_z_excludes_spike"] = float(sp_rz.iloc[650])

# ── T10: fresh extremes ─────────────────────────────────────────────
cl = pd.Series([10.0, 11.0, 9.0, 12.0, 12.0, 8.0, 8.0, 13.0, 7.0, 7.0, 14.0])
hh = cl.rolling(4, min_periods=4).max()
ll = cl.rolling(4, min_periods=4).min()
new_high = hh.notna() & (cl >= hh)
new_low = ll.notna() & (cl <= ll)
exp_hh = np.array([np.nan, np.nan, np.nan, 12, 12, 12, 12, 13, 13, 13, 14.0])
exp_ll = np.array([np.nan, np.nan, np.nan, 9, 9, 8, 8, 8, 7, 7, 7.0])
exp_nh = np.array([0, 0, 0, 1, 1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0, 1], dtype=bool)
exp_nl = np.array([0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 1, 1, 0, 1, 1, 0], dtype=bool)
results["T10_hh_maxdiff"] = float(np.nanmax(np.abs(hh.to_numpy() - exp_hh)))
results["T10_ll_maxdiff"] = float(np.nanmax(np.abs(ll.to_numpy() - exp_ll)))
results["T10_newhigh_matches"] = bool((new_high.to_numpy() == exp_nh).all())
results["T10_newlow_matches"] = bool((new_low.to_numpy() == exp_nl).all())

# ── T11: climax definition ──────────────────────────────────────────
rho3 = pd.Series([1.4, 1.5, 2.0, 2.0, 0.5])
z3 = pd.Series([0.1, 0.1, 0.25, 0.1, 0.0])
climax = rho3.notna() & (rho3 >= 1.5) & (z3.abs() <= 0.2)
results["T11_climax_matches"] = bool(
    (climax.to_numpy() == np.array([False, True, False, True, False])).all())

# T12: 60 quiet bars (spread 0), TWO shifted bars at 3.0 (increment
# 3.0 - 0.5 = 2.5: first shifted bar accumulates 2.5 < h=4, second
# reaches 5.0 > 4 -> exactly one alarm, at the second shifted bar),
# then quiet again. Single alarm at index 61; near_break covers 61..74
# (alarm bar + 13, inclusive, per the Pine <= breakHold comparison).
m = 160
sp_syn = pd.Series(np.concatenate([np.zeros(60), np.full(2, 3.0), np.zeros(98)]))
mu_syn = pd.Series(np.zeros(m))   # constant reference mean 0
sd_syn = pd.Series(np.full(m, 1.0))  # constant sigma 1.0
cpos, cneg, alarm, near_break = cusum_guard(sp_syn, mu_syn, sd_syn, 0.5, 4.0, 13)
al = alarm.to_numpy()
first_alarm = int(np.argmax(al)) if al.any() else -1
results["T12_no_alarm_before_shift"] = bool(not al[:60].any())
results["T12_alarm_second_shifted_bar"] = bool(first_alarm == 61)
results["T12_single_alarm_in_episode"] = bool(al.sum() == 1)
results["T12_resets_at_alarm"] = bool(cpos.iloc[first_alarm] == 0.0)
nb = near_break.to_numpy()
post = np.zeros(m, dtype=bool)
post[61:75] = True  # 61..74 inclusive = alarm + breakHold
results["T12_nearbreak_hold_exact"] = bool((nb == post).all())
# stationary series: never alarms
sp_stat = pd.Series(np.sin(np.arange(300) * 0.3) * 0.05)
mu_stat = pd.Series(np.zeros(300))
sd_stat = pd.Series(np.full(300, 1.0))
_, _, alarm_stat, _ = cusum_guard(sp_stat, mu_stat, sd_stat, 0.5, 4.0, 13)
results["T12_stationary_never_alarms"] = bool(not alarm_stat.to_numpy().any())

# ── canonical data: T13/T14/T15 + coverage ──────────────────────────
df = pd.read_csv(REPO / "data" / "nq_weekly_canonical.csv", parse_dates=["date"])
base = compute_avfi(df)
tb = compute_tb_layer(base)

df_scaled = df.copy()
for c in ("open", "high", "low", "close"):
    df_scaled[c] = df_scaled[c] * 1000.0
df_scaled["volume"] = df_scaled["volume"] * 7.0
tb_s = compute_tb_layer(compute_avfi(df_scaled))

diffs = {}
for c in ("sp_rz", "rsig_s", "med_s"):
    a_, b_ = tb[c].to_numpy(float), tb_s[c].to_numpy(float)
    mm = ~np.isnan(a_)
    diffs[c] = float(np.nanmax(np.abs(a_[mm] - b_[mm])))
flag_cols = ["newHigh", "newLow", "divTop", "divBot", "climax",
             "climaxTop", "climaxBot", "cusumAlarm", "nearBreak", "tbFlagged"]
flags_same = all((tb[c].to_numpy() == tb_s[c].to_numpy()).all() for c in flag_cols)
results["T13_scale_invariance_maxdiff"] = max(diffs.values())
results["T13_detail"] = diffs
results["T13_flags_identical"] = bool(flags_same)

w, nw = DEFAULT_PARAMS["warmup"], TB_DEFAULT_PARAMS["normWin"]
first_rz = int(tb["sp_rz"].first_valid_index())
first_alarm_idx = int(tb.index[tb["cusumAlarm"]][0]) if tb["cusumAlarm"].any() else None
first_cpos_valid = int(tb["cPos"].first_valid_index())
results["T14_first_sp_rz_index"] = first_rz
results["T14_expected_first_sp_rz"] = w + nw  # spread valid at 260, prior win needs 500
results["T14_first_cusum_active_index"] = first_cpos_valid
results["T14_flags_never_before_validity"] = bool(
    not tb.loc[: w + nw - 1, ["divTop", "divBot"]].to_numpy().any())
# climax flags do NOT depend on sp_rz: they legitimately fire from the
# first pbN-bar extreme (Pine parity). Assert their earliest bar >= pbN-1.
first_climax = int(tb.index[tb["climaxTop"] | tb["climaxBot"]][0]) \
    if (tb["climaxTop"] | tb["climaxBot"]).any() else None
results["T14_first_climax_flag_index"] = first_climax
results["T14_climax_not_before_pbN"] = bool(first_climax is None or first_climax >= 19)

ok15 = ((tb["divTop"] == (tb["newHigh"] & tb["sp_rz"].notna() & (tb["sp_rz"] <= 0.0))).all()
        and (tb["divBot"] == (tb["newLow"] & tb["sp_rz"].notna() & (tb["sp_rz"] >= 0.0))).all())
results["T15_divergence_definitions_exact"] = bool(ok15)

# coverage counts (occupancy only; NO returns, NO evaluation anywhere here)
tb = tb.assign(year=pd.to_datetime(tb["date"]).dt.year)
unsealed = tb[tb["year"] <= 2020]
holdout = tb[tb["year"] >= 2021]
results["coverage_n_bars"] = int(len(tb))
results["coverage_unsealed"] = {c: int(unsealed[c].sum()) for c in flag_cols}
results["coverage_unsealed"]["sp_rz_count"] = int(unsealed["sp_rz"].notna().sum())
results["coverage_holdout_occupancy_only"] = {c: int(holdout[c].sum()) for c in flag_cols}
results["holdout_note"] = ("2021+ counts are flag occupancy only, no forward returns "
                           "or performance statistic computed; H8/H9 evaluation "
                           "awaits Amendment 3 sign-off (and holdout evaluation "
                           "awaits a future registered battery).")

checks = {
    "T8": results["T8_pnr_hand_maxdiff"] < 1e-12 and results["T8_na_window_poisons"],
    "T9": results["T9_spike_excluded_from_own_window"] and results["T9_excludes_vs_inclusive_z_differs"],
    "T10": results["T10_hh_maxdiff"] < 1e-12 and results["T10_ll_maxdiff"] < 1e-12
           and results["T10_newhigh_matches"] and results["T10_newlow_matches"],
    "T11": results["T11_climax_matches"],
    "T12": all(results[k] for k in ("T12_no_alarm_before_shift", "T12_alarm_second_shifted_bar",
                                    "T12_single_alarm_in_episode", "T12_resets_at_alarm",
                                    "T12_nearbreak_hold_exact", "T12_stationary_never_alarms")),
    "T13": results["T13_scale_invariance_maxdiff"] < 1e-12 and results["T13_flags_identical"],
    "T14": (results["T14_first_sp_rz_index"] == results["T14_expected_first_sp_rz"]
            and results["T14_flags_never_before_validity"]
            and results["T14_climax_not_before_pbN"]),
    "T15": results["T15_divergence_definitions_exact"],
}
results["all_passed"] = bool(all(checks.values()))
results["checks"] = {k: bool(v) for k, v in checks.items()}
results["note_execution_context"] = ("authored 11 Nov 2026; unit-test logic first "
                                    "validated via a TypeScript mirror of these "
                                    "tests (hourly session 2); authoritative "
                                    "pandas run executed 11 Nov 2026 on a "
                                    "Python-enabled reset (hourly log, session 4 "
                                    "verification): all T8-T15 pass, coverage "
                                    "counts in exact parity with the mirror.")
results["tradingview_crosscheck"] = "pending, unchanged (needs Andrea's manual chart comparison)"

tb_out = tb.drop(columns=["year"])
tb_out.to_csv(REPO / "data" / "avfi_v32_tb_series.csv", index=False)
(REPO / "research" / "stage1b_v32_replication_report.json").write_text(json.dumps(results, indent=2))
print("all_passed:", results["all_passed"], "| checks:", checks)

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage1b_v32_replication.py
