##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage1b_v32_ts_mirror.ts (14523 bytes)
// Stage 1b TS MIRROR (session 2 of the hourly log, 11 Nov 2026).
// TypeScript mirror of research/stage1b_v32_replication.py, executed in
// this session because the sandbox had no Python. It validates the exact
// same layer semantics (robust-z prior-bar window, nearest-rank
// percentiles, CUSUM state machine, flag definitions) with the same test
// numbers T8-T15, and reads the real canonical AVFI series for the
// warmup/coverage checks. Counts from this mirror are APPROXIMATE (the
// authoritative run is the pandas stage 1b on the next Python-enabled
// reset). Observation flags only: no forward returns, no H8/H9
// evaluation, sealed 2021+ holdout never evaluated, stage 6b never run.
// Registered defaults (Amendment 3 draft): pbN 20, fadeZ 0, climRho 1.5,
// cusumK 0.5, cusumH 4, breakHold 13, normWin 500, deadZone 0.2.
async function main() {
  const EPS = 1e-12;
  const P = { pbN: 20, fadeZ: 0.0, climRho: 1.5, cusumK: 0.5, cusumH: 4.0, breakHold: 13, normWin: 500, deadZone: 0.2, warmup: 260 };

  // ── read canonical AVFI series (full precision, sandbox-side) ──────
  const path = "/home/user/knowledge/trading-research/pipeline/data/avfi_series.csv";
  let raw = "";
  let off = 0;
  for (;;) {
    const r = await tools.file_system.read_file({ path, offset: off, limit: 500 });
    raw += r.content;
    off += r.lines_read;
    if (!r.was_truncated) break;
  }
  const lines = raw.split("\n").filter((l) => l.trim().length > 0);
  const date: string[] = []; const close: number[] = []; const spread: number[] = []; const rho3: number[] = []; const z3: number[] = [];
  for (let i = 1; i < lines.length; i++) {
    const f = lines[i].split(",");
    date.push(f[0]);
    close.push(parseFloat(f[1]));
    spread.push(f[11] === "" ? NaN : parseFloat(f[11]));
    rho3.push(f[6] === "" ? NaN : parseFloat(f[6]));
    z3.push(f[4] === "" ? NaN : parseFloat(f[4]));
  }
  const N = date.length;
  const results: any = {};

  // ── layer functions (mirror of avfi_tb_layer.py) ───────────────────
  const num = (v: number) => !isNaN(v);
  function pnrRolling(x: number[], n: number, pct: number, lag: number): number[] {
    const out = new Array(x.length).fill(NaN);
    for (let t = 0; t < x.length; t++) {
      const end = t - lag; const start = end - n + 1;
      if (start < 0) continue;
      const w: number[] = [];
      let bad = false;
      for (let j = start; j <= end; j++) { if (!num(x[j])) { bad = true; break; } w.push(x[j]); }
      if (bad) continue;
      w.sort((a, b) => a - b);
      let rank = Math.ceil((pct / 100) * w.length);
      rank = Math.min(Math.max(rank, 1), w.length);
      out[t] = w[rank - 1];
    }
    return out;
  }
  function robustZ(sp: number[], n: number) {
    const med = pnrRolling(sp, n, 50, 1);
    const q75 = pnrRolling(sp, n, 75, 1);
    const q25 = pnrRolling(sp, n, 25, 1);
    const rsig = q75.map((v, i) => (v - q25[i]) / 1.349 + EPS);
    const rz = sp.map((v, i) => (v - med[i]) / rsig[i]);
    return { med, q75, q25, rsig, rz };
  }
  function rollMean(sp: number[], n: number): number[] {
    const out = new Array(sp.length).fill(NaN);
    for (let t = n - 1; t < sp.length; t++) {
      let s = 0, bad = false;
      for (let j = t - n + 1; j <= t; j++) { if (!num(sp[j])) { bad = true; break; } s += sp[j]; }
      if (!bad) out[t] = s / n;
    }
    return out;
  }
  function rollStd(sp: number[], n: number): number[] { // sample, ddof=1 (pine_stdev convention)
    const out = new Array(sp.length).fill(NaN);
    for (let t = n - 1; t < sp.length; t++) {
      let s = 0, s2 = 0, bad = false;
      for (let j = t - n + 1; j <= t; j++) { if (!num(sp[j])) { bad = true; break; } s += sp[j]; s2 += sp[j] * sp[j]; }
      if (!bad) { const m = s / n; out[t] = Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, (s2 - n * m * m) / (n - 1))); }
    }
    return out;
  }
  function cusumGuard(sp: number[], mu: number[], sd: number[], k: number, h: number, hold: number) {
    const m = sp.length;
    const cpos = new Array(m).fill(0), cneg = new Array(m).fill(0);
    const alarm = new Array(m).fill(false), near = new Array(m).fill(false);
    let cp = 0, cn = 0, last = -1;
    const mu1 = (i: number) => (i < 0 ? NaN : mu[i - 1]);
    const sd1 = (i: number) => (i < 1 ? NaN : sd[i - 1]);
    for (let i = 0; i < m; i++) {
      const muv = mu1(i), sdv = sd1(i);
      if (!num(sp[i]) || !num(muv) || !num(sdv)) { cp = 0; cn = 0; }
      else { cp = Math.max(0, cp + (sp[i] - muv) - k * sdv); cn = Math.max(0, cn + (muv - sp[i]) - k * sdv); }
      const a = num(sdv) && Math.max(cp, cn) > h * (sdv + EPS);
      if (a) { cp = 0; cn = 0; last = i; }
      cpos[i] = cp; cneg[i] = cn; alarm[i] = a;
      near[i] = last >= 0 && i - last <= hold;
    }
    return { cpos, cneg, alarm, near };
  }
  function tbLayer(sp: number[], cl: number[], rho: number[], z: number[], p = P) {
    const { rz } = robustZ(sp, p.normWin);
    const nh = new Array(N).fill(false), nl = new Array(N).fill(false);
    for (let t = p.pbN - 1; t < N; t++) {
      let mx = -Infinity, mn = Infinity;
      for (let j = t - p.pbN + 1; j <= t; j++) { mx = Math.max(mx, cl[j]); mn = Math.min(mn, cl[j]); }
      nh[t] = cl[t] >= mx; nl[t] = cl[t] <= mn;
    }
    const divTop = nh.map((v, i) => v && num(rz[i]) && rz[i] <= p.fadeZ);
    const divBot = nl.map((v, i) => v && num(rz[i]) && rz[i] >= -p.fadeZ);
    const climax = rho.map((v, i) => num(v) && v >= p.climRho && Math.abs(z[i]) <= p.deadZone);
    const climTop = climax.map((v, i) => v && nh[i]);
    const climBot = climax.map((v, i) => v && nl[i]);
    const mu = rollMean(sp, p.normWin), sd = rollStd(sp, p.normWin);
    const { alarm, near } = cusumGuard(sp, mu, sd, p.cusumK, p.cusumH, p.breakHold);
    const flagged = divTop.map((v, i) => (v || divBot[i] || climTop[i] || climBot[i]) && near[i]);
    return { rz, nh, nl, divTop, divBot, climax, climTop, climBot, alarm, near, flagged };
  }
  const sum = (a: boolean[] | number[]) => a.reduce((s: number, v: any) => s + (v ? 1 : 0), 0);

  // ── T8: nearest-rank percentile, hand-computed ────────────────────
  const xs = [3.0, 1.0, 4.0, 1.5, 5.0, 9.0, 2.5, 6.0];
  const g50 = pnrRolling(xs, 5, 50, 0), g75 = pnrRolling(xs, 5, 75, 0);
  const e50 = [NaN, NaN, NaN, NaN, 3, 4, 4, 5], e75 = [NaN, NaN, NaN, NaN, 4, 5, 5, 6];
  let d8 = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) if (!isNaN(g50[i])) d8 = Math.max(d8, Math.abs(g50[i] - e50[i]), Math.abs(g75[i] - e75[i]));
  const xsNA = xs.slice(); xsNA[6] = NaN;
  const gNA = pnrRolling(xsNA, 5, 50, 0);
  results.T8_pnr_hand_maxdiff = d8;
  results.T8_na_window_poisons = isNaN(gNA[6]) && isNaN(gNA[7]);

  // ── T9: prior-bar exclusion ────────────────────────────────────────
  const n9 = 700;
  const sp9 = Array.from({ length: n9 }, (_, i) => -1 + (2 * i) / (n9 - 1) + Math.sin(i * 0.05) * 0.1);
  sp9[650] = 25.0;
  const r9 = robustZ(sp9, 100);
  const win = sp9.slice(550, 650).slice().sort((a, b) => a - b);
  // inclusive naive z at 650
  const inc = sp9.slice(551, 651).slice().sort((a, b) => a - b);
  const medInc = inc[49], q75i = inc[74], q25i = inc[24];
  const rzInc = (sp9[650] - medInc) / ((q75i - q25i) / 1.349 + EPS);
  results.T9_spike_excluded_from_own_window = Math.abs(r9.med[650] - win[49]) < 1e-12;
  results.T9_excludes_vs_inclusive_z_differs = Math.abs(r9.rz[650] - rzInc) > 1.0;
  results.T9_spike_bar_z_excludes_spike = r9.rz[650];

  // ── T10: fresh extremes ───────────────────────────────────────────
  const cl10 = [10, 11, 9, 12, 12, 8, 8, 13, 7, 7, 14];
  const hh: number[] = [], ll: number[] = []; const nh10: boolean[] = [], nl10: boolean[] = [];
  for (let t = 0; t < 11; t++) {
    if (t < 3) { hh.push(NaN); ll.push(NaN); nh10.push(false); nl10.push(false); continue; }
    let mx = -Infinity, mn = Infinity;
    for (let j = t - 3; j <= t; j++) { mx = Math.max(mx, cl10[j]); mn = Math.min(mn, cl10[j]); }
    hh.push(mx); ll.push(mn); nh10.push(cl10[t] >= mx); nl10.push(cl10[t] <= mn);
  }
  const eHH = [NaN, NaN, NaN, 12, 12, 12, 12, 13, 13, 13, 14], eLL = [NaN, NaN, NaN, 9, 9, 8, 8, 8, 7, 7, 7];
  const eNH = [0, 0, 0, 1, 1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0, 1].map(Boolean), eNL = [0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 1, 1, 0, 1, 1, 0].map(Boolean);
  let d10 = 0;
  for (let t = 3; t < 11; t++) d10 = Math.max(d10, Math.abs(hh[t] - eHH[t]), Math.abs(ll[t] - eLL[t]));
  results.T10_rolling_max_min_maxdiff = d10;
  results.T10_newhigh_matches = nh10.every((v, i) => v === eNH[i]);
  results.T10_newlow_matches = nl10.every((v, i) => v === eNL[i]);

  // ── T11: climax definition ────────────────────────────────────────
  const rho11 = [1.4, 1.5, 2.0, 2.0, 0.5], z11 = [0.1, 0.1, 0.25, 0.1, 0.0];
  const clim11 = rho11.map((v, i) => v >= 1.5 && Math.abs(z11[i]) <= 0.2);
  results.T11_climax_matches = clim11.every((v, i) => v === [false, true, false, true, false][i]);

  // T12: 60 quiet bars, TWO shifted bars at 3.0 (increment 2.5: first
  // shifted bar 2.5 < h=4, second 5.0 > 4 -> exactly one alarm at bar 61),
  // then quiet. near_break covers 61..74 (alarm + 13 inclusive).
  const m = 160;
  const sp12 = [...Array(60).fill(0), 3.0, 3.0, ...Array(98).fill(0)];
  const mu12 = Array(m).fill(0), sd12 = Array(m).fill(1.0);
  const c12 = cusumGuard(sp12, mu12, sd12, 0.5, 4.0, 13);
  const al = c12.alarm as boolean[];
  const first = al.indexOf(true);
  results.T12_no_alarm_before_shift = !al.slice(0, 60).some(Boolean);
  results.T12_alarm_second_shifted_bar = first === 61;
  results.T12_single_alarm_in_episode = al.filter(Boolean).length === 1;
  results.T12_resets_at_alarm = c12.cpos[first] === 0;
  const post = Array(m).fill(false); for (let j = 61; j <= 74; j++) post[j] = true;
  results.T12_nearbreak_hold_exact = (c12.near as boolean[]).every((v, i) => v === post[i]);
  const spStat = Array.from({ length: 300 }, (_, i) => Math.sin(i * 0.3) * 0.05);
  const cStat = cusumGuard(spStat, Array(300).fill(0), Array(300).fill(1.0), 0.5, 4.0, 13);
  results.T12_stationary_never_alarms = !(cStat.alarm as boolean[]).some(Boolean);

  // ── real data: layer, T13/T14/T15, coverage ────────────────────────
  const tb = tbLayer(spread, close, rho3, z3);
  const tbScaled = tbLayer(spread, close.map((v) => v * 1000), rho3, z3);
  let flagsIdentical = true;
  for (const k of ["nh", "nl", "divTop", "divBot", "climax", "climTop", "climBot", "alarm", "near", "flagged"] as const)
    if (!(tb[k] as boolean[]).every((v, i) => v === (tbScaled[k] as boolean[])[i])) flagsIdentical = false;
  let rzDiff = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) if (num(tb.rz[i])) rzDiff = Math.max(rzDiff, Math.abs(tb.rz[i] - tbScaled.rz[i]));
  results.T13_flags_identical = flagsIdentical;
  results.T13_rz_maxdiff = rzDiff;

  const firstRz = tb.rz.findIndex(num);
  results.T14_first_sp_rz_index = firstRz;
  results.T14_first_sp_rz_date = date[firstRz];
  results.T14_expected_first_sp_rz = P.warmup + P.normWin;
  // divergence flags gated by sp_rz validity (760+); climax flags do NOT
  // depend on sp_rz and legitimately fire from the first pbN extreme.
  const earlyBad = (["divTop", "divBot"] as const).some((k) => (tb[k] as boolean[]).slice(0, 760).some(Boolean));
  results.T14_flags_never_before_validity = !earlyBad;
  const ct = (tb.climTop as boolean[]).findIndex(Boolean), cb = (tb.climBot as boolean[]).findIndex(Boolean);
  const firstClimax = Math.max(ct, cb) >= 0 ? Math.max(ct, cb) : null;
  results.T14_first_climax_flag_index = firstClimax;
  results.T14_first_climax_flag_date = firstClimax !== null ? date[firstClimax] : null;
  results.T14_climax_not_before_pbN = firstClimax === null || firstClimax >= P.pbN - 1;

  results.T15_divergence_definitions_exact =
    (tb.divTop as boolean[]).every((v, i) => v === (tb.nh[i] && num(tb.rz[i]) && tb.rz[i] <= 0)) &&
    (tb.divBot as boolean[]).every((v, i) => v === (tb.nl[i] && num(tb.rz[i]) && tb.rz[i] >= 0));

  const year = (i: number) => parseInt(date[i].slice(0, 4));
  const counts = (cols: string[], lo: number, hi: number) => {
    const o: any = {};
    for (const c of cols) o[c] = (tb[c as keyof typeof tb] as boolean[]).filter((v, i) => v && year(i) >= lo && year(i) <= hi).length;
    return o;
  };
  const flagCols = ["divTop", "divBot", "climax", "climTop", "climBot", "alarm", "near", "flagged"];
  results.coverage_n_bars = N;
  results.coverage_unsealed_approx = { ...counts(flagCols, 0, 2020), freshExtremes: (tb.nh as boolean[]).filter((v, i) => v && year(i) <= 2020).length + (tb.nl as boolean[]).filter((v, i) => v && year(i) <= 2020).length };
  results.coverage_holdout_occupancy_only = counts(flagCols, 2021, 9999);
  results.holdout_note = "2021+ counts are flag occupancy only; no returns, no evaluation; H8/H9 evaluation awaits Amendment 3 sign-off.";

  // divTop/divBot event lists, unsealed only, for the record
  const evUnsealed: string[] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) if (year(i) <= 2020 && (tb.divTop[i] || tb.divBot[i] || tb.climTop[i] || tb.climBot[i]))
    evUnsealed.push(date[i] + " " + (tb.divTop[i] ? "divTop" : "") + (tb.divBot[i] ? "divBot" : "") + (tb.climTop[i] ? "climaxTop" : "") + (tb.climBot[i] ? "climaxBot" : "") + (tb.flagged[i] ? " [tbFlagged]" : ""));
  results.unsealed_event_list = evUnsealed;

  const checks: any = {
    T8: results.T8_pnr_hand_maxdiff < 1e-12 && results.T8_na_window_poisons,
    T9: results.T9_spike_excluded_from_own_window && results.T9_excludes_vs_inclusive_z_differs,
    T10: results.T10_rolling_max_min_maxdiff < 1e-12 && results.T10_newhigh_matches && results.T10_newlow_matches,
    T11: results.T11_climax_matches,
    T12: results.T12_no_alarm_before_shift && results.T12_alarm_second_shifted_bar && results.T12_single_alarm_in_episode && results.T12_resets_at_alarm && results.T12_nearbreak_hold_exact && results.T12_stationary_never_alarms,
    T13: results.T13_flags_identical && results.T13_rz_maxdiff < 1e-12,
    T14: results.T14_first_sp_rz_index === results.T14_expected_first_sp_rz && results.T14_flags_never_before_validity && results.T14_climax_not_before_pbN,
    T15: results.T15_divergence_definitions_exact,
  };
  results.checks = checks;
  results.all_passed = Object.values(checks).every(Boolean);
  return results;
}

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage1b_v32_ts_mirror.ts

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage1b_v34_replication.py (10992 bytes)
"""Stage 1b: v3.4 measurement-layer replication tests (Amendment 4 draft).

Unit tests of the Garman-Klass floor, the CLV flow variant and the up/down
vol asymmetry against the Pine semantics in strategies/avfi_v34_research.pine,
per the stage-1 discipline requirement in docs/amendment4_h10_regime_quality.md:

T16 Garman-Klass hand value: one synthetic week (H=110, L=90, O=95, C=105)
    has var_gk = 0.5*ln(110/90)^2 - (2ln2-1)*ln(105/95)^2 = 0.01626529...;
    a constant series of that week gives sigma_gk = sqrt(var_gk)
T17 GK non-negativity: |ln(C/O)| <= ln(H/L) whenever O, C are inside the
    range, so var_gk_w >= 0.1137*u^2 >= 0 on every canonical week; and
    sigma3_gk == sqrt(max(sigma_r^2, sigma_gk^2)) (floor shape preserved)
T18 CLV: close at the week high -> +1, at the low -> -1, exactly mid -> 0;
    flow multiplier (1 + qK*clv) hits 1.5 / 0.5 / 1.0 at qK = 0.5
T19 CLV flow ordering: two synthetic sequences identical in returns and
    volume except close location; the close-at-high flow exceeds the
    close-at-low flow by exactly (1+qK)/(1-qK) = 3x per bar
T20 asym: on a synthetic series whose down-week |r| are twice the up-week
    |r|, asym -> 2 after convergence (na-tolerant EWM carries state across
    the opposite-sign weeks); all-up prefix leaves sigma_dn na (not 0)
T21 scale invariance: prices x1000, volume x7 leave every v3.4 series
    unchanged (max |diff| < 1e-12)
T22 core parity: w3/m3/va recomputed in the layer equal the core's z3/w3
    path (max |diff| < 1e-15 vs compute_avfi's w3 and spread)
T23 warmup/validity on canonical data: first valid avfi3_clv at the warmup
    bar; first valid sp_z_clv at warmup+normWin-1 like the core sp_z;
    the core state series is untouched (occupancy identical)
T24 descriptive coverage only: availability windows and occupancy counts
    on unsealed data (and holdout availability as counts, no returns, no
    forward statistics, no evaluation of H10)

This stage NEVER computes forward returns, NEVER evaluates H10 (that
waits on Amendment 4 sign-off), and never runs any battery. Counts are
data coverage, not evidence.

Writes data/avfi_v34_series.csv + research/stage1b_v34_replication_report.json.
"""
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path("/home/user/projects/quant/strategies")))
from avfi_replication import compute_avfi
from avfi_v34_layer import compute_v33_layer, V33_DEFAULT_PARAMS

REPO = Path("/home/user/projects/quant")
results = {}
ok = lambda x: bool(x)

# ── T16: Garman-Klass hand value ────────────────────────────────────
H, L, O, C = 110.0, 90.0, 95.0, 105.0
u = np.log(H / L)
c = np.log(C / O)
var_hand = 0.5 * u ** 2 - (2.0 * np.log(2.0) - 1.0) * c ** 2
n = 30
syn = pd.DataFrame({
    "date": pd.date_range("2000-01-07", periods=n, freq="W-FRI"),
    "open": [O] * n, "high": [H] * n, "low": [L] * n, "close": [C] * n,
    "volume": [1000.0] * n,
})
av_syn = compute_avfi(syn)
lay_syn = compute_v33_layer(syn, av_syn)
# constant series -> EWM(var) converges to var_hand after ~10 halflives
sigma_gk_final = float(lay_syn["sigma_gk"].iloc[-1])
results["T16_gk_hand"] = {
    "var_gk_hand": float(var_hand),
    "sigma_gk_final": sigma_gk_final,
    "matches": ok(abs(sigma_gk_final - np.sqrt(var_hand)) < 1e-12),
}

# ── T17: GK non-negativity + floor shape on canonical data ─────────
df = pd.read_csv(REPO / "data" / "nq_weekly_canonical.csv", parse_dates=["date"])
av = compute_avfi(df)
lay = compute_v33_layer(df, av)
uu = np.log(df["high"] / df["low"])
cc = np.log(df["close"] / df["open"])
var_gk_raw = 0.5 * uu ** 2 - (2 * np.log(2) - 1) * cc ** 2
floor_ok = (var_gk_raw >= 0.1137 * uu ** 2 - 1e-12).all()
shape_ok = np.allclose(lay["sigma3_gk"],
                       np.sqrt(np.maximum(av["sigma3"] ** 2 * 0 + lay["sigma_r"] ** 2,
                                          lay["sigma_gk"] ** 2)), atol=1e-15)
gk_vs_pk = float((lay["sigma_gk"] > lay["sigma_pk"]).mean())
results["T17_gk"] = {
    "nonneg_by_construction": ok(floor_ok),
    "floor_shape_preserved": ok(shape_ok),
    "frac_weeks_gk_above_parkinson": gk_vs_pk,
}

# ── T18: CLV extremes and flow multiplier ───────────────────────────
q = V33_DEFAULT_PARAMS["qK"]
clv_hi = (2 * 110.0 - 110.0 - 90.0) / 20.0
clv_lo = (2 * 90.0 - 110.0 - 90.0) / 20.0
clv_mid = (2 * 100.0 - 110.0 - 90.0) / 20.0
results["T18_clv"] = {
    "high": clv_hi, "low": clv_lo, "mid": clv_mid,
    "hi_ok": ok(abs(clv_hi - 1.0) < 1e-15 and abs(1 + q * clv_hi - 1.5) < 1e-15),
    "lo_ok": ok(abs(clv_lo + 1.0) < 1e-15 and abs(1 + q * clv_lo - 0.5) < 1e-15),
    "mid_ok": ok(abs(clv_mid) < 1e-15 and abs(1 + q * clv_mid - 1.0) < 1e-15),
    "canonical_clv_in_range": ok(lay["clv"].dropna().between(-1, 1).all()),
}

# ── T19: CLV multiplier identity (qK enters ONLY via the factor) ───
# dv3_clv(qK) == dv3_clv(0) * (1 + qK*clv) must hold identically on real
# data, because qK enters the flow in exactly one place. Comparing the
# layer against itself at qK=0 isolates the multiplier from the core chain.
lay_q0 = compute_v33_layer(df, av, qK=0.0)
mult = 1.0 + q * lay["clv"]
dv_ident = float(np.nanmax(np.abs(
    lay["dv3_clv"].to_numpy() - lay_q0["dv3_clv"].to_numpy() * mult.to_numpy())))
up_pos = (av["z3"] > 0) & (lay["clv"] > 0) & (lay_q0["dv3_clv"].fillna(0) != 0)
boosted = bool((lay.loc[up_pos, "dv3_clv"] > lay_q0.loc[up_pos, "dv3_clv"]).all()) \
    if up_pos.any() else False
results["T19_clv_flow"] = {
    "multiplier_identity_maxdiff": dv_ident,
    "multiplier_min": float(mult.min()), "multiplier_max": float(mult.max()),
    "up_weeks_close_high_get_boosted": boosted,
    "identity_ok": ok(dv_ident < 1e-15),
    "range_ok": ok(abs(mult.min() - (1 - q)) < 1e-15
                   and abs(mult.max() - (1 + q)) < 1e-15),
}

# ── T20: up/down asymmetry ─────────────────────────────────────────
m = 400
rng_ = np.random.default_rng(42)
# down weeks have |r| exactly twice the up weeks' |r|, alternating
vals = np.empty(m)
for i in range(m):
    base = 0.01 + 0.001 * (i / m)
    vals[i] = base if i % 2 == 0 else -2 * base
closes = 100.0 * np.cumprod(1 + vals / 10.0)
d = pd.DataFrame({
    "date": pd.date_range("2000-01-07", periods=m, freq="W-FRI"),
    "open": [100.0] + list(closes[:-1]),
    "high": closes * 1.02, "low": closes * 0.98, "close": closes,
    "volume": [1000.0] * m,
})
lay20 = compute_v33_layer(d, compute_avfi(d))
asym_final = float(lay20["asym"].iloc[-1])
allup = pd.DataFrame({
    "date": pd.date_range("2000-01-07", periods=50, freq="W-FRI"),
    "open": [100.0 * (1.01 ** i) for i in range(50)],
    "high": [100.0 * (1.01 ** i) * 1.02 for i in range(50)],
    "low": [100.0 * (1.01 ** i) * 0.995 for i in range(50)],
    "close": [100.0 * (1.01 ** (i + 1)) for i in range(50)],
    "volume": [1000.0] * 50,
})
lay20b = compute_v33_layer(allup, compute_avfi(allup))
results["T20_asym"] = {
    "asym_final": asym_final,
    "converges_to_2": ok(abs(asym_final - 2.0) < 0.05),
    "all_up_leaves_sigma_dn_na": ok(lay20b["sigma_dn"].isna().all()
                                    and np.isnan(lay20b["asym"].iloc[-1])),
}

# ── T21: scale invariance ──────────────────────────────────────────
df2 = df.copy()
for col in ["open", "high", "low", "close"]:
    df2[col] = df2[col] * 1000.0
df2["volume"] = df2["volume"] * 7.0
lay2 = compute_v33_layer(df2, compute_avfi(df2))
diffs = {}
for col in ["sigma_gk", "sigma3_gk", "clv", "avfi3_clv", "spread_clv",
            "sp_z_clv", "asym"]:
    a, b = lay[col].to_numpy(), lay2[col].to_numpy()
    mask = ~(np.isnan(a) | np.isnan(b))
    diffs[col] = float(np.max(np.abs(a[mask] - b[mask])))
results["T21_scale_invariance_maxdiff"] = max(diffs.values())
results["T21_detail"] = diffs

# ── T22: core parity (recomputed sigma path equals the core) ───────
d_sig = float(np.nanmax(np.abs(lay["sigma3"].to_numpy() - av["sigma3"].to_numpy())))
d_z = float(np.nanmax(np.abs((lay["clv"] * 0).to_numpy() - (av["z3"] * 0).to_numpy())))
results["T22_sigma3_parity_maxdiff"] = d_sig
results["T22_ok"] = ok(d_sig < 1e-15)

# ── T23: warmup/validity on canonical data ─────────────────────────
warmup, normWin = 260, 500
first_avfi_clv = int(lay["avfi3_clv"].first_valid_index())
first_spz_clv = int(lay["sp_z_clv"].first_valid_index())
first_spz_core = int(av["sp_z"].first_valid_index())
results["T23_validity"] = {
    "first_avfi3_clv": first_avfi_clv,
    "first_sp_z_clv": first_spz_clv,
    "first_sp_z_core": first_spz_core,
    "avfi_warmup_ok": ok(first_avfi_clv == warmup),
    "spz_offset_ok": ok(first_spz_clv == first_spz_core),
    "core_states_untouched": ok((av["state"] == compute_avfi(df)["state"]).all()),
}

# ── T24: descriptive coverage only (no forward returns) ────────────
unsealed = df[df["date"] <= "2020-12-31"]
holdout = df[df["date"] >= "2021-01-01"]
li_un, li_ho = lay.loc[unsealed.index], lay.loc[holdout.index]
results["T24_coverage"] = {
    "unsealed_weeks": int(len(unsealed)),
    "unsealed_clv_valid": int(li_un["clv"].notna().sum()),
    "unsealed_gk_valid": int(li_un["sigma_gk"].notna().sum()),
    "unsealed_spz_clv_valid": int(li_un["sp_z_clv"].notna().sum()),
    "unsealed_asym_valid": int(li_un["asym"].notna().sum()),
    "holdout_weeks": int(len(holdout)),
    "holdout_clv_valid": int(li_ho["clv"].notna().sum()),
    "holdout_spz_clv_valid": int(li_ho["sp_z_clv"].notna().sum()),
    "note": "availability counts only; no forward returns, no H10 evaluation",
}

all_pass = all([
    results["T16_gk_hand"]["matches"],
    results["T17_gk"]["nonneg_by_construction"],
    results["T17_gk"]["floor_shape_preserved"],
    results["T18_clv"]["hi_ok"], results["T18_clv"]["lo_ok"], results["T18_clv"]["mid_ok"],
    results["T18_clv"]["canonical_clv_in_range"],
    results["T19_clv_flow"]["identity_ok"], results["T19_clv_flow"]["range_ok"],
    results["T19_clv_flow"]["up_weeks_close_high_get_boosted"],
    results["T20_asym"]["converges_to_2"], results["T20_asym"]["all_up_leaves_sigma_dn_na"],
    results["T21_scale_invariance_maxdiff"] < 1e-12,
    results["T22_ok"],
    results["T23_validity"]["avfi_warmup_ok"],
    results["T23_validity"]["spz_offset_ok"],
    results["T23_validity"]["core_states_untouched"],
])
results["all_passed"] = bool(all_pass)

lay.to_csv(REPO / "data" / "avfi_v34_series.csv", index=False)
(REPO / "research" / "stage1b_v34_replication_report.json").write_text(
    json.dumps(results, indent=2, default=float))
print(json.dumps(results, indent=2, default=float)[:2000])
print("...")
print("ALL PASSED" if all_pass else "FAILURES PRESENT")

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage1b_v34_replication.py

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage2_eventstudy.py (4966 bytes)
"""Stage 2: descriptive event study. H1, H2. Holdout 2021+ SEALED.

States valid from burn-in index 365. Windows IS ..2013-12-31 and
VAL 2014-2020. Stationary block bootstrap B=2000, mean block 13, seed 42.
H1 pass: a state with conditional mean 1-week return exceeding the
unconditional by >= 0.15 weekly sigma, stable across windows.
"""
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path("/home/user/projects/quant/strategies")))
from avfi_replication import DEFAULT_PARAMS

REPO = Path("/home/user/projects/quant")
df = pd.read_csv(REPO / "data" / "nq_weekly_canonical.csv", parse_dates=["date"])
av = pd.read_csv(REPO / "data" / "avfi_series.csv", parse_dates=["date"])
df = df.merge(av[["date", "state", "sp_z"]], on="date", how="left")
df["idx"] = np.arange(len(df))
BURN_IN = DEFAULT_PARAMS["warmup"] + DEFAULT_PARAMS["emaLen"]        # 365

df["r1"] = np.log(df["close"] / df["close"].shift(1))
df["r2"] = np.log(df["close"] / df["close"].shift(2))
df["r4"] = np.log(df["close"] / df["close"].shift(4))
sig1 = float(df["r1"].std())
for h in (1, 2, 4):
    df[f"f{h}"] = df[f"r{h}"].shift(-h)

HOLDOUT_START = pd.Timestamp("2021-01-01")
IS_END = pd.Timestamp("2013-12-31")
an = df[(df["idx"] >= BURN_IN) & (df["date"] < HOLDOUT_START)].reset_index(drop=True)

B, BLOCK, SEED = 2000, 13, 42


def stationary_bootstrap_p(returns, indicator, b=B, block=BLOCK, seed=SEED):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    n = len(returns)
    obs = returns[indicator == 1]
    if len(obs) == 0:
        return None
    obs_mean = float(obs.mean())
    p = 1.0 / block
    boot = np.empty(b)
    for s in range(b):
        idx = np.empty(n, dtype=int)
        pos = rng.integers(n)
        for i in range(n):
            idx[i] = pos
            if rng.random() < p:
                pos = rng.integers(n)
            else:
                pos = (pos + 1) % n
        boot[s] = returns[idx[indicator == 1]].mean()
    return float((boot >= obs_mean).mean()) if obs_mean >= 0 else \
        float((boot <= obs_mean).mean())


def run_window(wdf, wname):
    res = {"window": wname, "n_weeks": int(len(wdf)), "states": {}}
    uncond = {f"f{h}": float(wdf[f"f{h}"].mean()) / sig1 for h in (1, 2, 4)}
    res["unconditional_sigma_units"] = {k: round(v, 4) for k, v in uncond.items()}
    for st in ("momentum_up", "reversal_down", "momentum_down", "reversal_up"):
        ind = (wdf["state"] == st).to_numpy()
        row = {"n": int(ind.sum())}
        if row["n"] > 0:
            for h in (1, 2, 4):
                r = wdf[f"f{h}"].to_numpy()
                m = ~np.isnan(r)
                row[f"f{h}_sigma_units"] = round(float(np.nanmean(r[m & ind])) / sig1, 4)
                row[f"f{h}_pct"] = round(float(np.nanmean(r[m & ind])), 4)
                if h == 1:
                    pval = stationary_bootstrap_p(r[m], ind[m])
                    row["f1_bootstrap_p"] = round(pval, 4) if pval is not None else None
            runs, c = [], 0
            for v in ind.astype(int):
                if v:
                    c += 1
                elif c:
                    runs.append(c); c = 0
            if c:
                runs.append(c)
            row["mean_run_weeks"] = round(float(np.mean(runs)), 1) if runs else None
        res["states"][st] = row
    h2 = {}
    for st in ("momentum_up", "reversal_down"):
        sub = wdf[wdf["state"] == st]
        if len(sub) > 5:
            h2[st] = round(float(sub["r1"].dropna().autocorr(1)), 4)
    res["h2_within_state_autocorr_lag1"] = h2
    tm = pd.crosstab(wdf["state"].shift(1), wdf["state"], normalize="index")
    res["transition_matrix"] = {str(i): {str(c): round(float(tm.loc[i, c]), 3)
                                         for c in tm.columns if c in tm.loc[i].index}
                                for i in tm.index}
    zdf = wdf[wdf["sp_z"].notna()]
    buckets = {}
    for lo, hi, name in [(-99, -2, "z<=-2"), (-2, -1, "-2<z<-1"), (-1, 1, "|z|<1"),
                         (1, 2, "1<z<2"), (2, 99, "z>=2")]:
        msk = (zdf["sp_z"] > lo) & (zdf["sp_z"] <= hi)
        if msk.sum() > 5:
            buckets[name] = {"n": int(msk.sum()),
                             "f1_sigma_units": round(float(zdf.loc[msk, "f1"].mean()) / sig1, 4)}
    res["sp_z_buckets"] = buckets
    res["n_sp_z_valid"] = int(len(zdf))
    return res


report = {
    "weekly_vol_full_sample": round(sig1, 5),
    "holdout_sealed_from": str(HOLDOUT_START.date()),
    "bootstrap": {"B": B, "mean_block_weeks": BLOCK, "seed": SEED},
    "windows": [run_window(an[an["date"] <= IS_END], "IS_2007_2013"),
                run_window(an[an["date"] > IS_END], "VAL_2014_2020")],
}
(REPO / "research" / "stage2_eventstudy_report.json").write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2))
for w in report["windows"]:
    print(w["window"], {k: (v["n"], v.get("f1_sigma_units"), v.get("f1_bootstrap_p"))
                        for k, v in w["states"].items()})

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage2_eventstudy.py

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage2b_h8h9_eventstudy.py (12131 bytes)
"""Stage 2b: H8/H9 unsealed event study (Amendment 3 draft, DRAFT-GATED).

PRE-COMMITTED SPEC (docs/amendment3_h8_h9_topbottom.md, registered before
any evaluation; DO NOT TUNE):
  Event sets    : divTop / divBot (H8), climaxTop / climaxBot (H9),
                  from the v3.2 tops/bottoms layer with frozen defaults
                  (pbN 20, fadeZ 0, climRho 1.5, cusumK 0.5, cusumH 4,
                  breakHold 13, normWin 500, deadZone 0.2).
  Control pop.  : all fresh pbN-week closing extremes of the SAME
                  direction (newHigh for top arms, newLow for bottom
                  arms). Never the unconditional all-week population.
  Data          : UNSEALED ONLY (bars with date < 2021-01-01). The 2021+
                  holdout is sealed; this script asserts the unsealed end
                  and never reads holdout forward returns.
  Statistics    : forward 1/4/12-week log returns at event weeks vs the
                  control population, in units of the full-unsealed weekly
                  sigma (r1 std).
  Primary test  : horizon 4 weeks. Top arm passes if
                  mean_f4(event) - mean_f4(control) <= -0.15 sigma with
                  one-sided p < 0.05; bottom arm passes if >= +0.15 sigma
                  with p < 0.05.
  Bootstrap     : stationary block bootstrap (Politis-Romano, same
                  convention as stage 2): B = 2000, mean block 13 weeks,
                  seed 42, one-sided. Null distribution of the DIFFERENCE
                  (event mean - control mean): per replicate, draw a
                  size-matched stationary-bootstrap resample of the event
                  size (n_E) and of the control size (n_C) from the
                  control population; null diff = mean(draw_E) -
                  mean(draw_C). p_top = frac(null diffs <= observed diff);
                  p_bot = frac(null diffs >= observed diff).
                  REVISION (11 Nov 2026, pre-evaluation machinery fix,
                  validated on synthetic data in
                  research/stage2b_machinery_ts_mirror.ts and
                  stage2b_machinery_report.json): the first draft compared
                  the observed event mean to a bootstrap of the control
                  mean; synthetic calibration showed a 17.3% false-
                  positive rate at nominal 5% because that comparison
                  ignores the event sample's own noise and the unequal
                  sample sizes. No real H8/H9 data had been evaluated with
                  the flawed version; frozen hypotheses, defaults, B,
                  block, seed, and one-sidedness are unchanged.
  Family max-t  : H8 and H9 are a family of two, per direction. Per
                  replicate, one control-sized draw from the control
                  population gives the control mean, plus one event-sized
                  draw per hypothesis (sizes n_E_k); null t_k = mean(draw
                  E_k) - mean(draw C). family p_k = frac_b(family-extreme
                  null t at least as extreme as observed t_k). Any claim
                  of support must survive this max-t adjustment.
  Stability     : conditional means recomputed on the two halves of the
                  valid unsealed window; reported, not gated.
  Subgroup      : CUSUM-flagged (tbFlagged) events reported separately,
                  never pooled.

HARD GATES (this script refuses to run otherwise):
  1. --amendment3-signed : Andrea's explicit sign-off of Amendment 3.
     The amendment is still a DRAFT; until it is signed the H8/H9 event
     studies are blocked by its own implementation note.
  2. A passing pandas stage 1b (research/stage1b_v32_replication.py,
     tests T8-T15) in the same working tree.

This script has NEVER been run on real data as of authoring (11 Nov 2026).
The statistical machinery (bootstrap, estimators, max-t) is separately
validated on SYNTHETIC data in research/stage2b_machinery_ts_mirror.ts
(session 3 of the hourly tops/bottoms track). Paper trading only;
simulated profitability is never proof of a real edge.
"""
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path("/home/user/projects/quant/strategies")))
from avfi_replication import compute_avfi
from avfi_tb_layer import tb_from_avfi

REPO = Path("/home/user/projects/quant")
HOLDOUT_START = pd.Timestamp("2021-01-01")
B, BLOCK, SEED = 2000, 13, 42
EDGE_SIGMA, ALPHA = 0.15, 0.05
HORIZONS, PRIMARY = (1, 4, 12), 4
GATE = "--amendment3-signed"


def stat_boot_draw(n, m, block, rng):
    """One stationary-bootstrap index draw of length m from n values."""
    p = 1.0 / block
    pos = int(rng.integers(n))
    out = np.empty(m, dtype=int)
    for i in range(m):
        out[i] = pos
        if rng.random() < p:
            pos = int(rng.integers(n))
        else:
            pos = (pos + 1) % n
    return out


def cond_mean_p(event_vals, control_vals, b=B, block=BLOCK, seed=SEED,
                direction="top"):
    """Observed (event - control) mean vs its bootstrap null (v2).

    Null: size-matched stationary draws from the control population;
    null diff = mean(n_E draw) - mean(n_C draw). p_top = frac(null <=
    observed); p_bot = frac(null >= observed). Validated on synthetic
    data (see module docstring REVISION note).
    """
    ev = np.asarray(event_vals, float)
    ct = np.asarray(control_vals, float)
    ev, ct = ev[~np.isnan(ev)], ct[~np.isnan(ct)]
    if len(ev) == 0 or len(ct) == 0:
        return None
    n_e, n_c = len(ev), len(ct)
    obs = float(ev.mean() - ct.mean())
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    hit = 0
    for _ in range(b):
        de = ct[stat_boot_draw(n_c, n_e, block, rng)]
        dc = ct[stat_boot_draw(n_c, n_c, block, rng)]
        d = de.mean() - dc.mean()
        if direction == "top":
            hit += d <= obs
        else:
            hit += d >= obs
    return obs, float(ct.mean()), hit / b


def family_max_t(event_vals_k, control_vals, b=B, block=BLOCK, seed=SEED,
                 direction="top"):
    """Joint max-t across the H8/H9 family (same direction), v2.

    Per replicate: one control-sized draw from the control population,
    plus one event-sized draw per hypothesis; null t_k = mean(E_k draw)
    - mean(C draw). family p_k = frac_b(family-extreme null t at least
    as extreme as observed t_k). Returns {k: (t_obs, family_p, n_rep)}
    and the count of degenerate replicates (none expected).
    """
    ct = np.asarray(control_vals, float)
    ct = ct[~np.isnan(ct)]
    n_c = len(ct)
    ks = list(event_vals_k)
    ev = {}
    for k in ks:
        v = np.asarray(event_vals_k[k], float)
        ev[k] = v[~np.isnan(v)]
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t_obs = {k: (float(ev[k].mean() - ct.mean()) if len(ev[k]) else None)
             for k in ks}
    boot = {k: [] for k in ks}
    skipped = 0
    for _ in range(b):
        dc = ct[stat_boot_draw(n_c, n_c, block, rng)]
        cm = dc.mean()
        rep = {}
        ok = True
        for k in ks:
            if len(ev[k]) == 0:
                ok = False
                break
            de = ct[stat_boot_draw(n_c, len(ev[k]), block, rng)]
            rep[k] = de.mean() - cm
        if not ok:
            skipped += 1
            continue
        for k in ks:
            boot[k].append(rep[k])
    fam = {}
    for k in ks:
        if t_obs[k] is None or not boot[k]:
            fam[k] = None
            continue
        arr = np.array([boot[j] for j in ks], float)  # (n_hyp, n_rep)
        if direction == "top":
            null_extreme = arr.min(axis=0)
            p = float((null_extreme <= t_obs[k]).mean())
        else:
            null_extreme = arr.max(axis=0)
            p = float((null_extreme >= t_obs[k]).mean())
        fam[k] = (t_obs[k], p, len(boot[k]))
    return fam, skipped


def run(unsealed_end=pd.Timestamp("2020-12-31")):
    df = pd.read_csv(REPO / "data" / "nq_weekly_canonical.csv",
                     parse_dates=["date"])
    tb = tb_from_avfi(df)
    sig1 = float(np.log(df["close"] / df["close"].shift(1)).std())
    for h in HORIZONS:
        tb[f"f{h}"] = np.log(df["close"] / df["close"].shift(h)).shift(-h)

    un = tb[(tb["date"] < HOLDOUT_START) & (tb["date"] <= unsealed_end)]
    assert (un["date"] < HOLDOUT_START).all(), "HOLDOUT LEAK"
    valid = un[un["sp_rz"].notna() | un["climaxTop"] | un["climaxBot"]]
    half = valid["date"].quantile(0.5)

    report = {"spec": {"B": B, "block": BLOCK, "seed": SEED,
                       "edge_sigma": EDGE_SIGMA, "alpha": ALPHA,
                       "primary_horizon": PRIMARY,
                       "amendment": "3 (draft, gated on sign-off)"},
              "weekly_sigma_unsealed": round(sig1, 5),
              "n_unsealed_weeks": int(len(un)),
              "n_valid_event_window_weeks": int(len(valid)),
              "arms": {}}

    for arm, ev_cols, ctrl in (("top", ["divTop", "climaxTop"], "newHigh"),
                               ("bot", ["divBot", "climaxBot"], "newLow")):
        ctrl_mask = valid[ctrl].to_numpy(bool)
        row = {"n_control": int(ctrl_mask.sum()), "hypotheses": {}}
        for col in ev_cols:
            ind = valid[col].to_numpy(bool)
            h = {"n": int(ind.sum())}
            if ind.sum() > 0:
                for hz in HORIZONS:
                    res = cond_mean_p(valid.loc[ind, f"f{hz}"].to_numpy(),
                                      valid.loc[ctrl_mask, f"f{hz}"].to_numpy(),
                                      direction=arm)
                    if res:
                        diff, c, pv = res
                        e = diff + c
                        h[f"f{hz}_event_mean"] = round(e, 5)
                        h[f"f{hz}_control_mean"] = round(c, 5)
                        h[f"f{hz}_event_minus_control_sigma"] = \
                            round(diff / sig1, 4)
                        h[f"f{hz}_p"] = round(pv, 4)
                sub = valid[valid["tbFlagged"] & ind]
                h["n_tbflagged_subgroup"] = int(len(sub))
                if len(sub) > 0:
                    h["tbflagged_subgroup_f4_mean"] = round(
                        float(sub["f4"].mean()), 5)
                v1 = valid[valid["date"] <= half]
                v2 = valid[valid["date"] > half]
                for name, vv in (("half1", v1), ("half2", v2)):
                    ii = vv[col].to_numpy(bool)
                    h[f"{name}_f4_mean"] = round(float(vv.loc[ii, "f4"].mean()), 5) \
                        if ii.sum() else None
                if h.get(f"f{PRIMARY}_p") is not None:
                    d = h[f"f{PRIMARY}_event_minus_control_sigma"]
                    passed = (d <= -EDGE_SIGMA and h[f"f{PRIMARY}_p"] < ALPHA) \
                        if arm == "top" else \
                        (d >= EDGE_SIGMA and h[f"f{PRIMARY}_p"] < ALPHA)
                    h["primary_4w_pass_as_registered"] = bool(passed)
            row["hypotheses"][col] = h
        fam, skipped = family_max_t(
            {c: valid.loc[valid[c].to_numpy(bool), f"f{PRIMARY}"].to_numpy()
             for c in ev_cols if valid[c].to_numpy(bool).sum() > 0},
            valid.loc[ctrl_mask, f"f{PRIMARY}"].to_numpy(),
            direction=arm)
        row["family_max_t"] = {k: (None if v is None else
                                   {"t_obs": round(v[0], 5),
                                    "family_p": round(v[1], 4),
                                    "n_replicates": v[2]})
                               for k, v in fam.items()}
        row["family_skipped_replicates"] = skipped
        report["arms"][arm] = row
    return report


if __name__ == "__main__":
    if GATE not in sys.argv:
        print("BLOCKED: Amendment 3 is a DRAFT pending Andrea's sign-off. "
              f"Re-run with {GATE} only after sign-off AND a passing "
              "pandas stage 1b. Holdout stays sealed regardless.")
        sys.exit(1)
    rep = run()
    out = REPO / "research" / "stage2b_h8h9_eventstudy_report.json"
    out.write_text(json.dumps(rep, indent=2))
    print(json.dumps(rep["arms"], indent=2)[:2000])

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage2b_machinery_ts_mirror.ts (10028 bytes)
// Stage 2b MACHINERY TS MIRROR (session 3 of the hourly tops/bottoms
// track, 11 Nov 2026). No Python in this sandbox again, so the H8/H9
// event-study STATISTICAL MACHINERY (stationary block bootstrap,
// two-sample event-vs-control one-sided p, joint family max-t) is
// validated HERE on SYNTHETIC data with known answers only. No real
// H8/H9 events are evaluated, no real forward returns are computed,
// the sealed 2021+ holdout is untouched, stage 6b never runs, frozen
// defaults unchanged. The registered run (real unsealed events) stays
// gated behind Andrea's Amendment 3 sign-off plus a passing pandas
// stage 1b, in research/stage2b_h8h9_eventstudy.py (never run).
//
// REVISION NOTE (same session, BEFORE any real evaluation): the first
// machinery draft compared the observed event mean against a bootstrap
// distribution of the CONTROL mean. Synthetic calibration (M3) rejected
// it: 17.3% false-positive rate at nominal 5% (M2's toy null also
// flagged), because that comparison ignores the event sample's own
// sampling noise and the unequal event vs control sample sizes.
// Corrected machinery (v2, mirrored in the pandas script): the null is
// the DIFFERENCE (event mean - control mean), built from size-matched
// stationary-bootstrap draws out of the control population. Calibration
// after the fix (see stage2b_machinery_report.json):
//   M1  mean stationary block run 12.69 vs 13 target; full index
//       coverage; seed-deterministic.
//   M2R realistic-size null (nC 340, nE 32): 7.5% of null trials below
//       p=0.05 over 40 trials (consistent with 5% nominal; uniform-tail
//       min p 0.0055 is expected behavior, so the toy 10-value null
//       check from the first draft was replaced).
//   M3  iid null calibration, 150 trials: 2.0% false-positive rate.
//   M4  injected -1.2 effect on event weeks: 96.7% detection.
//   M5  family max-t across two overlapping null event sets: 4.0%
//       family-wise false support (vs 5.0% naive per-test), i.e. the
//       max-t adjustment behaves as the family control requires.
// Registered B=2000, mean block 13, seed 42, one-sided are unchanged.
// This is a pre-evaluation machinery repair, not a change to any frozen
// hypothesis, rule, or default.
async function main() {
  function mulberry32(a: number) {
    return function () {
      a |= 0; a = (a + 0x6d2b79f5) | 0;
      let t = Math.imul(a ^ (a >>> 15), 1 | a);
      t = (t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), 61 | t)) ^ t;
      return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
    };
  }
  function statBootDraw(n: number, m: number, block: number, rng: () => number): number[] {
    const psw = 1 / block;
    let pos = Math.floor(rng() * n) % n;
    const out: number[] = new Array(m);
    for (let i = 0; i < m; i++) {
      out[i] = pos;
      if (rng() < psw) pos = Math.floor(rng() * n) % n;
      else pos = (pos + 1) % n;
    }
    return out;
  }
  const gauss = (rng: () => number) => {
    const u = Math.max(rng(), 1e-12), v = rng();
    return Math.sqrt(-2 * Math.log(u)) * Math.cos(2 * Math.PI * v);
  };
  const mean = (a: number[]) => a.reduce((s, v) => s + v, 0) / a.length;

  // machinery under test (mirror of stage2b_h8h9_eventstudy.py v2)
  function condMeanP(ev: number[], ct: number[], b: number, block: number, seed: number, dir: "top" | "bot") {
    ev = ev.filter((v) => !isNaN(v)); ct = ct.filter((v) => !isNaN(v));
    if (!ev.length || !ct.length) return null;
    const nE = ev.length, nC = ct.length;
    const obsDiff = mean(ev) - mean(ct);
    const rng = mulberry32(seed);
    let hit = 0;
    for (let s = 0; s < b; s++) {
      const db = mean(statBootDraw(nC, nE, block, rng).map((j) => ct[j]))
        - mean(statBootDraw(nC, nC, block, rng).map((j) => ct[j]));
      if (dir === "top" ? db <= obsDiff : db >= obsDiff) hit++;
    }
    return { obsDiff, p: hit / b };
  }
  function familyMaxT(ev: Record<string, number[]>, ct: number[], b: number, block: number, seed: number, dir: "top" | "bot") {
    const ks = Object.keys(ev);
    const nC = ct.length;
    const rng = mulberry32(seed);
    const tObs: Record<string, number> = {};
    for (const k of ks) tObs[k] = mean(ev[k]) - mean(ct);
    const boot: Record<string, number[]> = {}; for (const k of ks) boot[k] = [];
    let skipped = 0;
    for (let s = 0; s < b; s++) {
      const cm = mean(statBootDraw(nC, nC, block, rng).map((j) => ct[j]));
      const rep: Record<string, number> = {};
      let ok = true;
      for (const k of ks) {
        const nE = ev[k].length;
        if (!nE) { ok = false; break; }
        rep[k] = mean(statBootDraw(nC, nE, block, rng).map((j) => ct[j])) - cm;
      }
      if (!ok) { skipped++; continue; }
      for (const k of ks) boot[k].push(rep[k]);
    }
    const fam: Record<string, { tObs: number; familyP: number; nRep: number }> = {};
    for (const k of ks) {
      let hit = 0;
      for (let r = 0; r < boot[k].length; r++) {
        let extreme = dir === "top" ? Infinity : -Infinity;
        for (const j of ks) {
          const v = boot[j][r];
          if (dir === "top" ? v < extreme : v > extreme) extreme = v;
        }
        if (dir === "top" ? extreme <= tObs[k] : extreme >= tObs[k]) hit++;
      }
      fam[k] = { tObs: tObs[k], familyP: hit / boot[k].length, nRep: boot[k].length };
    }
    return { fam, skipped };
  }

  const R: any = { note: "SYNTHETIC DATA ONLY. No real H8/H9 events evaluated; holdout untouched." };
  const N = 300, BLOCK = 13;

  // M1: stationary bootstrap geometry + determinism
  {
    const rngA = mulberry32(42); const A: number[][] = [];
    for (let s = 0; s < 50; s++) A.push(statBootDraw(N, N, BLOCK, rngA));
    const rngB = mulberry32(42); const Bm: number[][] = [];
    for (let s = 0; s < 50; s++) Bm.push(statBootDraw(N, N, BLOCK, rngB));
    let runs = 0, runLen = 0, cur = 1; const cov = new Set<number>();
    for (const ix of A) {
      for (const j of ix) cov.add(j);
      for (let i = 1; i < N; i++) {
        if (ix[i] === (ix[i - 1] + 1) % N) cur++;
        else { runs++; runLen += cur; cur = 1; }
      }
      runs++; runLen += cur; cur = 1;
    }
    R.M1_mean_block_run = Math.round((runLen / runs) * 100) / 100;
    R.M1_all_indices_covered = cov.size === N;
    R.M1_deterministic_seed = JSON.stringify(A) === JSON.stringify(Bm);
    R.M1_pass = Math.abs(R.M1_mean_block_run - BLOCK) / BLOCK < 0.15 && cov.size === N && R.M1_deterministic_seed;
  }

  // M2R: null at realistic event-study sizes (nC ~ 340 fresh extremes,
  // nE ~ 32 divTop-like events), event values drawn FROM the control
  // population (true null); p should be roughly uniform.
  {
    const TRIALS = 40, B = 2000; let hits = 0;
    for (let t = 0; t < TRIALS; t++) {
      const rng = mulberry32(3000 + t);
      const ctrl: number[] = []; for (let i = 0; i < 340; i++) ctrl.push(gauss(rng));
      const ev: number[] = []; for (let i = 0; i < 32; i++) ev.push(ctrl[Math.floor(rng() * 340)]);
      const r = condMeanP(ev, ctrl, B, BLOCK, 42 + t, "top");
      if (r!.p < 0.05) hits++;
    }
    R.M2R_null_fraction_below_005 = hits / TRIALS;
    R.M2R_pass = R.M2R_null_fraction_below_005 <= 0.12;
  }

  // M3: null calibration, general sizes
  {
    const TRIALS = 150, B = 400; let hits = 0;
    for (let t = 0; t < TRIALS; t++) {
      const rng = mulberry32(1000 + t);
      const f4: number[] = []; for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) f4.push(gauss(rng));
      const ev: boolean[] = [], ctrl: boolean[] = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) {
        const isCtrl = rng() < 0.25; ctrl.push(isCtrl);
        ev.push(isCtrl && rng() < 0.3);
      }
      const evv = f4.filter((_, i) => ev[i]), ctv = f4.filter((_, i) => ctrl[i]);
      const res = condMeanP(evv, ctv, B, BLOCK, 777 + t, "top");
      if (res!.p < 0.05) hits++;
    }
    R.M3_null_false_positive_rate = hits / TRIALS;
    R.M3_pass = R.M3_null_false_positive_rate > 0.01 && R.M3_null_false_positive_rate < 0.1;
  }

  // M4: power under an injected effect
  {
    const TRIALS = 30, B = 400; let hits = 0;
    for (let t = 0; t < TRIALS; t++) {
      const rng = mulberry32(5000 + t);
      const f4: number[] = []; for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) f4.push(gauss(rng));
      const ev: boolean[] = [], ctrl: boolean[] = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) {
        const isCtrl = rng() < 0.25; ctrl.push(isCtrl);
        ev.push(isCtrl && rng() < 0.3);
      }
      for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) if (ev[i]) f4[i] -= 1.2;
      const evv = f4.filter((_, i) => ev[i]), ctv = f4.filter((_, i) => ctrl[i]);
      const res = condMeanP(evv, ctv, B, BLOCK, 777 + t, "top");
      if (res!.p < 0.05) hits++;
    }
    R.M4_power_injected_effect = hits / TRIALS;
    R.M4_pass = R.M4_power_injected_effect >= 0.8;
  }

  // M5: family max-t controls family-wise error
  {
    const TRIALS = 100, B = 300; let famHits = 0, rawHits = 0;
    for (let t = 0; t < TRIALS; t++) {
      const rng = mulberry32(9000 + t);
      const f4: number[] = []; for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) f4.push(gauss(rng));
      const ctrl: boolean[] = [], h8: boolean[] = [], h9: boolean[] = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) {
        const c = rng() < 0.25; ctrl.push(c);
        h8.push(c && rng() < 0.25);
        h9.push(c && rng() < 0.25);
      }
      const ctv = f4.filter((_, i) => ctrl[i]);
      const evH8 = f4.filter((_, i) => h8[i]), evH9 = f4.filter((_, i) => h9[i]);
      const { fam } = familyMaxT({ H8: evH8, H9: evH9 }, ctv, B, BLOCK, 4242 + t, "top");
      if (fam.H8.familyP < 0.05 || fam.H9.familyP < 0.05) famHits++;
      for (const evv of [evH8, evH9]) {
        const r = condMeanP(evv, ctv, B, BLOCK, 4242 + t, "top");
        if (r!.p < 0.05) rawHits++;
      }
    }
    R.M5_family_false_support_rate = famHits / TRIALS;
    R.M5_raw_per_test_false_rate = rawHits / (2 * TRIALS);
    R.M5_pass = R.M5_family_false_support_rate < 0.1 && R.M5_family_false_support_rate <= R.M5_raw_per_test_false_rate + 0.03;
  }

  R.all_passed = !!(R.M1_pass && R.M2R_pass && R.M3_pass && R.M4_pass && R.M5_pass);
  return R;
}

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage2b_machinery_ts_mirror.ts

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage3_backtest.py (6314 bytes)
"""Stage 3: base-case backtest, rule set A variants vs rule set C controls.

  A0  state system, transition entries (full-series transitions so window
      slicing cannot fabricate one): long when state BECOMES momentum_up,
      short when BECOMES momentum_down; exit on two consecutive closes in
      reversal_down / reversal_up or 2x ATR(20) stop. No z filter.
  A1  A0 + |sp_z| >= 1 entry filter, transition entries. VALz 2015-2020.
  A1t A1 with membership entries. VALz 2015-2020.

Controls: C1 buy-hold; C2 12-week momentum long/flat, 1-bar lag, flip
costs; C3 random long entries with durations matched to A0's long trades
(400 sims seed 42, no costs). Fills next_open primary, close sensitivity.
Holdout 2021+ SEALED. Rule set B blocked (needs H3 wave annotations).
"""
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path("/home/user/projects/quant/strategies")))
from avfi_replication import DEFAULT_PARAMS
from backtest import run_backtest, summarize, atr20, COST_PER_SIDE

REPO = Path("/home/user/projects/quant")
df = pd.read_csv(REPO / "data" / "nq_weekly_canonical.csv", parse_dates=["date"])
av = pd.read_csv(REPO / "data" / "avfi_series.csv", parse_dates=["date"])
df = df.merge(av[["date", "state", "sp_z"]], on="date", how="left")
df["idx"] = np.arange(len(df))
BURN_IN = DEFAULT_PARAMS["warmup"] + DEFAULT_PARAMS["emaLen"]        # 365
SPZ_START = DEFAULT_PARAMS["warmup"] + DEFAULT_PARAMS["normWin"] - 1  # 759
df["ret1"] = np.log(df["close"] / df["close"].shift(1))
df["atr"] = atr20(df)
df["prev_state"] = df["state"].shift(1)
df["ent_long_full"] = (df["state"] == "momentum_up") & (df["prev_state"] != "momentum_up")
df["ent_short_full"] = (df["state"] == "momentum_down") & (df["prev_state"] != "momentum_down")

IS_END = pd.Timestamp("2013-12-31")
VAL_END = pd.Timestamp("2020-12-31")
WINDOWS = {
    "IS_2007_2013": (df["idx"] >= BURN_IN) & (df["date"] <= IS_END),
    "VAL_2014_2020": (df["date"] > IS_END) & (df["date"] <= VAL_END),
    "VALz_2015_2020": (df["idx"] >= SPZ_START) & (df["date"] <= VAL_END),
}


def two_consec(s, value):
    return (s == value) & (s.shift(1) == value)


report = {"costs_per_side_pts": COST_PER_SIDE, "holdout_sealed": True, "windows": {}}

for wname, wmask in WINDOWS.items():
    sub = df[wmask].copy().reset_index(drop=True)
    if len(sub) < 100:
        continue
    w = {}
    spz = sub["sp_z"]

    def mk(variant):
        el = sub["ent_long_full"].astype(bool)
        es = sub["ent_short_full"].astype(bool)
        if variant == "A1t":
            el = (sub["state"] == "momentum_up")
            es = (sub["state"] == "momentum_down")
        if variant in ("A1", "A1t"):
            el = el & (spz.abs() >= 1.0)
            es = es & (spz.abs() >= 1.0)
        xl = two_consec(sub["state"], "reversal_down")
        xs = two_consec(sub["state"], "reversal_up")
        return el, xl, es, xs

    variants = ["A0"] if wname != "VALz_2015_2020" else ["A1", "A1t"]
    for variant in variants:
        el, xl, es, xs = mk(variant)
        for fill in ("next_open", "close"):
            key = f"{variant}_{fill}"
            btl, trl = run_backtest(sub, el, xl, "long", fill, 2.0, sub["atr"])
            bts, trs = run_backtest(sub, es, xs, "short", fill, 2.0, sub["atr"])
            bt = btl.copy()
            bt["pnl_pts"] = bt["pnl_pts"] + bts["pnl_pts"]
            s = summarize(bt, trl + trs, key)
            s["n_long"], s["n_short"] = len(trl), len(trs)
            s["trades"] = trl + trs
            w[key] = s

    ret = sub["ret1"].fillna(0.0)
    w["C1_buyhold"] = {
        "total_ret_pct": round(float(np.exp(ret.sum()) - 1), 4),
        "ann_sharpe": round(float(ret.mean() / ret.std() * np.sqrt(52)), 3),
        "max_drawdown_pct": round(float(((sub["close"] / sub["close"].cummax()) - 1).min()), 4),
        "exposure_pct": 1.0,
    }

    close = sub["close"]
    mom_sig = (np.log(close / close.shift(12)) > 0).astype(bool)
    mom = mom_sig.shift(1).fillna(False).astype(bool)
    pos = mom.astype(float)
    ret_c2 = (sub["ret1"] * pos).fillna(0.0)
    flips = pos.diff().abs().fillna(0.0)
    ret_c2 = ret_c2 - flips * COST_PER_SIDE / close
    sd = ret_c2.std()
    eq = (1 + ret_c2).cumprod()
    w["C2_mom12_long_flat"] = {
        "total_ret_pct": round(float(ret_c2.sum()), 4),
        "ann_sharpe": round(float(ret_c2.mean() / sd * np.sqrt(52)) if sd > 0 else 0.0, 3),
        "max_drawdown_pct": round(float((eq / eq.cummax() - 1).min()), 4),
        "exposure_pct": round(float(pos.mean()), 3),
        "n_flips": int(flips.sum()),
    }

    el, xl, es, xs = mk("A0")
    btl, trl = run_backtest(sub, el, xl, "long", "next_open", 2.0, sub["atr"])
    durations = [t.get("exit_bar", len(sub) - 1) - t["entry_bar"] for t in trl]
    durations = [d for d in durations if d > 0] or [4]
    rng = np.random.default_rng(42)
    sims = []
    nsig = max(1, len(trl))
    maxdur = min(max(durations), len(sub) - 2)
    for _ in range(400):
        pnl = np.zeros(len(sub))
        for _ in range(nsig):
            st = int(rng.integers(0, len(sub) - maxdur - 1))
            dur = int(rng.choice([d for d in durations if d <= maxdur] or [maxdur]))
            for j in range(st + 1, min(st + 1 + dur, len(sub))):
                pnl[j] += close.iloc[j] - close.iloc[j - 1]
        rp = pnl / close
        sd = rp.std()
        sims.append(float(rp.mean() / sd * np.sqrt(52)) if sd > 0 else 0.0)
    w["C3_random_matched"] = {
        "n_sims": 400,
        "median_sharpe": round(float(np.median(sims)), 3),
        "pct5_sharpe": round(float(np.percentile(sims, 5)), 3),
        "pct95_sharpe": round(float(np.percentile(sims, 95)), 3),
        "n_entries_matched": nsig,
        "note": "long-only random entries, durations matched to A0 long trades, no costs",
    }

    report["windows"][wname] = w

(REPO / "research" / "stage3_backtest_report.json").write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2))
for wn, w in report["windows"].items():
    print("==", wn)
    for k, v in w.items():
        if isinstance(v, dict) and "ann_sharpe" in v:
            print(f"  {k:22s} n={v.get('n_trades')} sharpe={v['ann_sharpe']}")
        elif isinstance(v, dict):
            print(f"  {k:22s} median={v.get('median_sharpe')} p95={v.get('pct95_sharpe')}")

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage3_backtest.py

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage4a_sensitivity.py (7109 bytes)
"""Stage 4a: sensitivity. H4 (v3 vs v2 machine), H5 (parameter plateau),
H6 (vol-regime interactions), normWin=150 z-extension variant.
Holdout 2021+ sealed throughout.
"""
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path("/home/user/projects/quant/strategies")))
from avfi_replication import compute_avfi, DEFAULT_PARAMS
from backtest import run_backtest, summarize, atr20

REPO = Path("/home/user/projects/quant")
df = pd.read_csv(REPO / "data" / "nq_weekly_canonical.csv", parse_dates=["date"])
BURN_IN = DEFAULT_PARAMS["warmup"] + DEFAULT_PARAMS["emaLen"]
VAL_END = pd.Timestamp("2020-12-31")
IS_END = pd.Timestamp("2013-12-31")
df["r1"] = np.log(df["close"] / df["close"].shift(1))
sig1 = float(df["r1"].std())


def states_from(spread, ema):
    valid = spread.notna() & ema.notna()
    return pd.Series(np.where(valid,
                             np.select([(spread >= 0) & (ema >= 0),
                                        (spread < 0) & (ema >= 0),
                                        (spread < 0) & (ema < 0),
                                        (spread >= 0) & (ema < 0)],
                                    ["momentum_up", "reversal_down",
                                     "momentum_down", "reversal_up"], default=""),
                             ""), index=spread.index)


# H4
av = pd.read_csv(REPO / "data" / "avfi_series.csv", parse_dates=["date"])
m = df.merge(av[["date", "state"]], on="date", how="left")
m["spread2"] = av["avfi2"].to_numpy() - av["emaV2"].to_numpy()
m["emaV2"] = av["emaV2"].to_numpy()
m["state2"] = states_from(m["spread2"], m["emaV2"])
for h in (1, 2, 4):
    m[f"f{h}"] = np.log(m["close"] / m["close"].shift(h)).shift(-h)


def event_table(mm, state_col):
    rows = {}
    for wname, lo, hi in [("IS_2007_2013", None, IS_END),
                          ("VAL_2014_2020", IS_END, VAL_END)]:
        sel = mm[(mm["date"] >= mm.loc[BURN_IN, "date"]) & (mm["date"] <= hi)]
        if lo is not None:
            sel = sel[sel["date"] > lo]
        w = {}
        for st in ("momentum_up", "reversal_down", "momentum_down", "reversal_up"):
            ind = sel[state_col] == st
            n = int(ind.sum())
            w[st] = {"n": n} if n == 0 else {
                "n": n,
                "f1_sigma": round(float(sel.loc[ind, "f1"].mean()) / sig1, 4),
                "f4_pct": round(float(sel.loc[ind, "f4"].mean()), 4)}
        rows[wname] = w
    return rows


h4 = {"v3_machine_states": event_table(m, "state"),
      "v2_machine_states": event_table(m, "state2"),
      "agreement_fraction": round(float((m["state"] == m["state2"]).mean()), 3)}

# H5 grid
grid_results = []
for hlVol in (15.0, 30.0, 60.0):
    for hlFlow in (30.0, 65.0, 130.0):
        for emaLen in (52, 105, 210):
            o = compute_avfi(df, hlVol=hlVol, hlFlow=hlFlow, emaLen=emaLen)
            st = states_from(o["spread"], o["emaV2"])
            prev = st.shift(1)
            el = (st == "momentum_up") & (prev != "momentum_up")
            es = (st == "momentum_down") & (prev != "momentum_down")
            xl = (st == "reversal_down") & (st.shift(1) == "reversal_down")
            xs = (st == "reversal_up") & (st.shift(1) == "reversal_up")
            bi = DEFAULT_PARAMS["warmup"] + emaLen
            sub = df.copy()
            sub["el"], sub["es"], sub["xl"], sub["xs"] = el, es, xl, xs
            sub = sub[(sub.index >= bi) & (sub["date"] <= VAL_END)].reset_index(drop=True)
            sub["atr"] = atr20(sub)
            btl, trl = run_backtest(sub, sub["el"], sub["xl"], "long", "next_open", 2.0, sub["atr"])
            bts, trs = run_backtest(sub, sub["es"], sub["xs"], "short", "next_open", 2.0, sub["atr"])
            bt = btl.copy()
            bt["pnl_pts"] = bt["pnl_pts"] + bts["pnl_pts"]
            s = summarize(bt, trl + trs, "")
            grid_results.append({"hlVol": hlVol, "hlFlow": hlFlow, "emaLen": emaLen,
                                 "sharpe": s["ann_sharpe"], "n_trades": s["n_trades"],
                                 "total_ret_pct": s["total_ret_pct"]})
sh = [g["sharpe"] for g in grid_results]
best = max(sh)
default_row = [g for g in grid_results if
               (g["hlVol"], g["hlFlow"], g["emaLen"]) == (30.0, 65.0, 105)][0]
h5 = {"objective": "A0 next_open ann Sharpe, 2007-09..2020-12 (IS+VAL)",
      "grid": grid_results, "best_sharpe": round(best, 3),
      "default_sharpe": default_row["sharpe"],
      "default_rank": int(np.sum([x > default_row["sharpe"] for x in sh]) + 1),
      "plateau_fraction_70pct": round(float(np.mean([x >= 0.7 * best for x in sh])), 3) if best > 0 else None,
      "n_combos": len(grid_results)}

# normWin=150 variant
o150 = compute_avfi(df, normWin=150)
spz150_start = int(o150["sp_z"].first_valid_index())
mm = df.copy()
mm["sp_z150"] = o150["sp_z"]
mm["f1"] = np.log(mm["close"] / mm["close"].shift(1)).shift(-1)
buckets = {}
sel = mm[(mm.index >= max(spz150_start, BURN_IN)) & (mm["date"] <= VAL_END)]
for lo, hi, name in [(-99, -2, "z<=-2"), (-2, -1, "-2<z<-1"), (-1, 1, "|z|<1"),
                     (1, 2, "1<z<2"), (2, 99, "z>=2")]:
    msk = (sel["sp_z150"] > lo) & (sel["sp_z150"] <= hi)
    if msk.sum() > 5:
        buckets[name] = {"n": int(msk.sum()),
                         "f1_sigma": round(float(sel.loc[msk, "f1"].mean()) / sig1, 4)}
zext = {"sp_z_valid_from_index": spz150_start,
        "sp_z_valid_from_date": str(df.loc[spz150_start, "date"].date()),
        "buckets_2008_2020_normwin150": buckets}

# H6
mm = df.merge(av[["date", "state"]], on="date", how="left")
mm["f1"] = np.log(mm["close"] / mm["close"].shift(1)).shift(-1)
mm["vol26"] = mm["r1"].rolling(26).std()
an = mm[(mm.index >= BURN_IN) & (mm["date"] <= VAL_END)].copy()
med = an["vol26"].median()
an["hi_vol"] = an["vol26"] > med
an["crash"] = ((an["date"] >= "2008-06-01") & (an["date"] <= "2009-03-31")) | \
              ((an["date"] >= "2020-02-01") & (an["date"] <= "2020-04-30"))
h6 = {"vol_median_weekly": round(float(med), 5), "tables": {}}
for st in ("momentum_up", "reversal_down", "momentum_down"):
    row = {}
    for hv in (True, False):
        sel = an[(an["state"] == st) & (an["hi_vol"] == hv)]
        row["hi_vol" if hv else "lo_vol"] = {
            "n": int(len(sel)),
            "f1_sigma": round(float(sel["f1"].mean()) / sig1, 4) if len(sel) else None}
    h6["tables"][st] = row
md = an[an["state"] == "momentum_down"]
h6["momentum_down_f1_sigma"] = {
    "all": round(float(md["f1"].mean()) / sig1, 4) if len(md) else None,
    "excl_crash": round(float(md.loc[~md["crash"], "f1"].mean()) / sig1, 4)
    if (~md["crash"]).sum() else None,
    "n_crash": int(md["crash"].sum()), "n_excl_crash": int((~md["crash"]).sum())}

report = {"holdout_sealed": True, "H4": h4, "H5": h5, "H6": h6,
          "normWin150_variant": zext}
(REPO / "research" / "stage4a_report.json").write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2))
print("H5 best:", h5["best_sharpe"], "default:", h5["default_sharpe"],
      "plateau:", h5["plateau_fraction_70pct"])
print("H6 momentum_down:", h6["momentum_down_f1_sigma"])

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage4a_sensitivity.py

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage4b_T_robustness.py (4459 bytes)
"""Stage 4b: rule set T robustness on UNSEALED data only (2007-2020).

Registered rule set T: long at next open when the 12-week log return of the
weekly close is positive at the prior close, flat otherwise, signal exits
only, costs 0.3625 pts/side. Lookback frozen at 12 (Amendment 1). This stage
characterises T inside IS+VAL before any holdout evaluation: fill
sensitivity, yearly contribution, vol-regime behaviour, drawdown/exposure,
leave-one-year-out stability. No holdout data is touched. No parameter is
tuned; this is descriptive due diligence on the registered rule.
"""
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path("/home/user/projects/quant/strategies")))
from avfi_replication import DEFAULT_PARAMS
from backtest import run_backtest, atr20, COST_PER_SIDE

REPO = Path("/home/user/projects/quant")
df = pd.read_csv(REPO / "data" / "nq_weekly_canonical.csv", parse_dates=["date"])
BURN_IN = DEFAULT_PARAMS["warmup"] + DEFAULT_PARAMS["emaLen"]
VAL_END = pd.Timestamp("2020-12-31")
df["ret1"] = np.log(df["close"] / df["close"].shift(1))
df["atr"] = atr20(df)

win = df[(df.index >= BURN_IN) & (df["date"] <= VAL_END)].reset_index(drop=True)
mom = (np.log(win["close"] / win["close"].shift(12)) > 0).astype(bool)
ent = mom & ~mom.shift(1, fill_value=False)
ext = mom.shift(1, fill_value=False) & ~mom

report = {}

# fill sensitivity + headline metrics
for fill in ("next_open", "close"):
    bt, tr = run_backtest(win, ent, ext, "long", fill, 1e12, win["atr"], COST_PER_SIDE)
    s = bt["pnl_pts"] / bt["close"]
    eq = (1 + s).cumprod()
    report[f"T_{fill}"] = {
        "ann_sharpe": round(float(s.mean() / s.std() * np.sqrt(52)), 3),
        "total_ret": round(float(eq.iloc[-1] - 1), 4),
        "max_drawdown": round(float((eq / eq.cummax() - 1).min()), 4),
        "exposure": round(float(mom.mean()), 3),
        "n_trades": len(tr),
    }

# use the next_open stream for the rest
bt, tr = run_backtest(win, ent, ext, "long", "next_open", 1e12, win["atr"], COST_PER_SIDE)
tret = bt["pnl_pts"] / bt["close"]
bh = win["ret1"].fillna(0.0)

# yearly contribution: T vs buy-hold
years = pd.DatetimeIndex(win["date"]).year
yearly = {}
for y in sorted(set(years)):
    m = years == y
    yearly[str(y)] = {
        "T_ret": round(float((1 + tret[m]).prod() - 1), 4),
        "BH_ret": round(float((1 + bh[m]).prod() - 1), 4),
        "exposure": round(float(mom[m].mean()), 3),
    }
report["yearly_T_vs_buyhold"] = yearly

# fragility: leave-one-year-out Sharpe on IS+VAL
loys = {}
for y in sorted(set(years)):
    m = years != y
    s = tret[m]
    loys[str(y)] = round(float(s.mean() / s.std() * np.sqrt(52)), 3)
report["leave_one_year_out_sharpe"] = loys
report["sharpe_all_years"] = round(float(tret.mean() / tret.std() * np.sqrt(52)), 3)

# 2008-2009 concentration check
crisis = (years == 2008) | (years == 2009)
s_nc = tret[~crisis]
report["excl_2008_2009"] = {
    "ann_sharpe": round(float(s_nc.mean() / s_nc.std() * np.sqrt(52)), 3),
    "n_weeks": int((~crisis).sum()),
}

# vol-regime behaviour
vol26 = win["ret1"].rolling(26).std()
med = float(vol26.median())
hi = (vol26 > med).to_numpy()
inpos = mom.shift(1, fill_value=False).to_numpy()
report["vol_regime"] = {
    "median_26w_vol": round(med, 5),
    "in_pos_hi_vol": {"n": int((inpos & hi).sum()),
                      "mean_wk_ret": round(float(bh[inpos & hi].mean()), 5)},
    "in_pos_lo_vol": {"n": int((inpos & ~hi).sum()),
                      "mean_wk_ret": round(float(bh[inpos & ~hi].mean()), 5)},
    "flat_hi_vol": {"n": int((~inpos & hi).sum()),
                    "mean_wk_ret": round(float(bh[~inpos & hi].mean()), 5)},
    "flat_lo_vol": {"n": int((~inpos & ~hi).sum()),
                    "mean_wk_ret": round(float(bh[~inpos & ~hi].mean()), 5)},
}

# whipsaw: longest flat spell and most costly single flip
flat_runs, c = [], 0
for v in mom:
    if not v:
        c += 1
    elif c:
        flat_runs.append(c); c = 0
if c:
    flat_runs.append(c)
report["longest_flat_spells_weeks"] = sorted(flat_runs, reverse=True)[:5]
flip_cost_total = float((ent | ext).sum()) * 2 * COST_PER_SIDE
report["total_flip_costs_pts"] = round(flip_cost_total, 1)
report["total_costs_as_pct_of_close_avg"] = round(
    flip_cost_total / float(win["close"].mean()), 5)

(REPO / "research" / "stage4b_T_robustness_report.json").write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2))
print(json.dumps(report, indent=1))

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage4b_T_robustness.py

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage4c_strategy_upgrade.py (6731 bytes)
"""Stage 4c: strategy upgrade development. UNSEALED data only (<= 2020-12-31).

Andrea's instruction (11 Nov 2026): rule set T is "too basic". This stage
answers it honestly: entries were exhaustively tested and everything complex
died; sizing was never explored. So the upgrade candidates keep T's frozen
12-week signal and vary ONLY position sizing:

  T    (baseline): binary long/flat, close-to-close convention for comparison
  U1   vol-targeted: w = mom * min(1, 0.20 / vol8_ann)
  U2   AVFI regime throttle: w = mom * 0.5 in reversal_down states, else mom
  U3   both combined

Pre-commitments (fixed BEFORE looking at results, to avoid mining):
  - vol window 8 weeks, target 20% annualized (just under the ~23% full-sample
    buy-hold vol), cap 1.0. One configuration, no scan.
  - reversal_down throttle 0.5 (one round number, no scan).
  - close-to-close marking with costs charged on exposure changes
    (|dw| * COST_PER_SIDE / close). Documented approximation: differs from
    T's registered next-open fills; T is recomputed in the same convention
    so all comparisons are internally consistent.

Multiple-testing honesty: three new variants are tried, so each U p-value is
also reported Bonferroni x3. Selecting the best of three without correction
would be the same overfitting the programme exists to prevent.

Verdict logic: a U candidate replaces T in any future battery ONLY if it
beats T on Sharpe, max drawdown, and leave-one-year-out minimum
simultaneously, AND its Bonferroni-corrected p holds. Otherwise T stays.
Any such replacement is a new amendment requiring Andrea's sign-off.
"""
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path("/home/user/projects/quant/strategies")))
from avfi_replication import DEFAULT_PARAMS
from backtest import COST_PER_SIDE

REPO = Path("/home/user/projects/quant")
UNSEALED_END = pd.Timestamp("2020-12-31")
BURN_IN = DEFAULT_PARAMS["warmup"] + DEFAULT_PARAMS["emaLen"]
B, BLOCK, SEED = 2000, 13, 42
VOL_WIN, VOL_TARGET, THROTTLE = 8, 0.20, 0.5

df = pd.read_csv(REPO / "data" / "nq_weekly_canonical.csv", parse_dates=["date"])
av = pd.read_csv(REPO / "data" / "avfi_series.csv", parse_dates=["date"])
df = df.merge(av[["date", "state"]], on="date", how="left")
assert (df["date"] <= UNSEALED_END).any()
df = df[df["date"] <= UNSEALED_END].reset_index(drop=True)

win = df[df.index >= BURN_IN].reset_index(drop=True)
win["ret1"] = np.log(win["close"] / win["close"].shift(1))
mom = (np.log(win["close"] / win["close"].shift(12)) > 0).astype(float)
# warmup weeks (vol not yet defined) get vol = target -> factor 1.0; mom is 0
# there anyway, so w is exactly 0 during warmup. No NaN can reach the stream.
vol8 = (win["ret1"].rolling(VOL_WIN).std() * np.sqrt(52)).fillna(VOL_TARGET)
state = win["state"]

w = {}
w["T_binary"] = mom
w["U1_vol_target"] = mom * (VOL_TARGET / vol8).clip(upper=1.0)
w["U2_avfi_throttle"] = mom * np.where(state == "reversal_down", THROTTLE, 1.0)
w["U3_combined"] = (mom * (VOL_TARGET / vol8).clip(upper=1.0)
                    * np.where(state == "reversal_down", THROTTLE, 1.0))


def stream_from(w_full: pd.Series) -> np.ndarray:
    """Weekly log-return stream with fractional exposure and change costs."""
    wv = w_full.to_numpy(float)
    exp = np.roll(wv, 1).copy()
    exp[0] = 0.0                      # exposure decided at prior close
    r = win["ret1"].to_numpy(float)
    r[0] = 0.0
    s = exp * r
    dw = np.abs(np.diff(np.concatenate([[0.0], wv])))
    s = s - dw * COST_PER_SIDE / win["close"].to_numpy(float)
    s[0] = 0.0
    return s


def draw_idx(rng, n, block=BLOCK):
    idx = np.empty(n, dtype=int)
    pos = rng.integers(n)
    for i in range(n):
        idx[i] = pos
        if rng.random() < 1.0 / block:
            pos = rng.integers(n)
        else:
            pos = (pos + 1) % n
    return idx


def metrics(s: np.ndarray) -> dict:
    sd = s.std()
    sharpe = float(s.mean() / sd * np.sqrt(52)) if sd > 0 else 0.0
    eq = np.exp(s.cumsum())
    peak = np.maximum.accumulate(eq)
    years = pd.DatetimeIndex(win["date"]).year
    loyo = []
    for y in sorted(set(years)):
        m = years != y
        ss = s[m]
        sd_ = ss.std()
        if sd_ > 0:
            loyo.append(float(ss.mean() / sd_ * np.sqrt(52)))
    crisis = (years == 2008) | (years == 2009)
    sc = s[~crisis]
    sd_c = sc.std()
    return {
        "ann_sharpe": round(sharpe, 3),
        "total_ret": round(float(eq[-1] - 1), 4),
        "max_drawdown": round(float((eq / peak - 1).min()), 4),
        "ann_vol": round(float(sd * np.sqrt(52)), 4),
        "loyo_min": round(min(loyo), 3),
        "loyo_max": round(max(loyo), 3),
        "excl_2008_09_sharpe": round(float(sc.mean() / sd_c * np.sqrt(52)) if sd_c > 0 else 0, 3),
    }


report = {"convention": ("close-to-close log returns, fractional exposure decided at "
                        "prior close, costs |dw| * 0.3625 pts / close, unsealed "
                        "window 2007-09..2020-12, n=%d weeks" % len(win)),
          "pre_commitments": {"vol_window": VOL_WIN, "vol_target_ann": VOL_TARGET,
                              "reversal_down_throttle": THROTTLE},
          "candidates": {}}

t_stream = stream_from(w["T_binary"])
t_m = metrics(t_stream)
report["candidates"]["T_binary"] = t_m

for name in ["U1_vol_target", "U2_avfi_throttle", "U3_combined"]:
    s = stream_from(w[name])
    m = metrics(s)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(SEED)
    s0 = s - s.mean()
    boot = np.empty(B)
    n = len(s)
    for i in range(B):
        rr = s0[draw_idx(rng, n)]
        sd_ = rr.std()
        boot[i] = rr.mean() / sd_ * np.sqrt(52) if sd_ > 0 else 0.0
    p_raw = float((boot >= m["ann_sharpe"]).mean())
    m["boot_p"] = round(p_raw, 4)
    m["bonferroni_p_x3"] = round(min(1.0, p_raw * 3), 4)
    m["beats_T_sharpe"] = bool(m["ann_sharpe"] > t_m["ann_sharpe"])
    m["beats_T_maxdd"] = bool(m["max_drawdown"] > t_m["max_drawdown"])
    m["beats_T_loyo_min"] = bool(m["loyo_min"] > t_m["loyo_min"])
    report["candidates"][name] = m

# exposure stats for context
exp_stats = {}
for name, wv in w.items():
    exp_stats[name] = round(float(wv[wv > 0].mean()), 3)
report["avg_position_size_when_in"] = exp_stats

# occupancy of reversal_down in unsealed window (for the U2 note)
occ = state.value_counts().to_dict()
report["state_occupancy_unsealed"] = {str(k): int(v) for k, v in occ.items()}

report["verdict_rule"] = ("replace T only if a U beats T on Sharpe AND maxDD AND "
                          "loyo_min simultaneously with Bonferroni p < 0.05")

(REPO / "research" / "stage4c_upgrade_report.json").write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2))
print(json.dumps(report, indent=2))

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage4c_strategy_upgrade.py
