##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage5_coststress.py (4027 bytes)
"""Stage 5: cost and execution realism stress.

Programme section 5: slippage stressed to 2-3 ticks; a weekly edge that dies
at 3 ticks was never an edge. Variants stressed: A0 (IS, VAL), A1t (VALz),
C2 12-week momentum (IS, VAL). Cost per side = slippage_ticks*0.25 + 0.1125
commission pts. Baseline was 1 tick. Holdout sealed.
"""
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path("/home/user/projects/quant/strategies")))
from avfi_replication import DEFAULT_PARAMS
from backtest import run_backtest, summarize, atr20, COST_PER_SIDE

REPO = Path("/home/user/projects/quant")
df = pd.read_csv(REPO / "data" / "nq_weekly_canonical.csv", parse_dates=["date"])
av = pd.read_csv(REPO / "data" / "avfi_series.csv", parse_dates=["date"])
df = df.merge(av[["date", "state", "sp_z"]], on="date", how="left")
df["idx"] = np.arange(len(df))
BURN_IN = DEFAULT_PARAMS["warmup"] + DEFAULT_PARAMS["emaLen"]
SPZ_START = DEFAULT_PARAMS["warmup"] + DEFAULT_PARAMS["normWin"] - 1
df["ret1"] = np.log(df["close"] / df["close"].shift(1))
df["atr"] = atr20(df)
df["prev_state"] = df["state"].shift(1)
df["ent_long_full"] = (df["state"] == "momentum_up") & (df["prev_state"] != "momentum_up")
df["ent_short_full"] = (df["state"] == "momentum_down") & (df["prev_state"] != "momentum_down")

IS_END = pd.Timestamp("2013-12-31")
VAL_END = pd.Timestamp("2020-12-31")
WINDOWS = {
    "IS_2007_2013": (df["idx"] >= BURN_IN) & (df["date"] <= IS_END),
    "VAL_2014_2020": (df["date"] > IS_END) & (df["date"] <= VAL_END),
    "VALz_2015_2020": (df["idx"] >= SPZ_START) & (df["date"] <= VAL_END),
}


def two_consec(s, value):
    return (s == value) & (s.shift(1) == value)


def a0_positions(sub, variant):
    el = sub["ent_long_full"].astype(bool)
    es = sub["ent_short_full"].astype(bool)
    if variant == "A1t":
        el = (sub["state"] == "momentum_up")
        es = (sub["state"] == "momentum_down")
        el = el & (sub["sp_z"].abs() >= 1.0)
        es = es & (sub["sp_z"].abs() >= 1.0)
    xl = two_consec(sub["state"], "reversal_down")
    xs = two_consec(sub["state"], "reversal_up")
    return el, xl, es, xs


report = {"per_side_costs_pts": {"1_tick": round(1 * 0.25 + 0.1125, 4),
                                 "2_tick": round(2 * 0.25 + 0.1125, 4),
                                 "3_tick": round(3 * 0.25 + 0.1125, 4)},
          "holdout_sealed": True, "results": {}}

for wname, wmask in WINDOWS.items():
    sub = df[wmask].copy().reset_index(drop=True)
    if len(sub) < 100:
        continue
    w = {}
    variant = "A1t" if wname == "VALz_2015_2020" else "A0"
    el, xl, es, xs = a0_positions(sub, variant)
    for ticks in (1, 2, 3):
        cost = ticks * 0.25 + 0.1125
        key = f"{variant}_{ticks}tick"
        btl, trl = run_backtest(sub, el, xl, "long", "next_open", 2.0, sub["atr"], cost)
        bts, trs = run_backtest(sub, es, xs, "short", "next_open", 2.0, sub["atr"], cost)
        bt = btl.copy()
        bt["pnl_pts"] = bt["pnl_pts"] + bts["pnl_pts"]
        s = summarize(bt, trl + trs, key)
        w[key] = {k: v for k, v in s.items() if k not in ("trades", "label")}

    # C2 momentum with stressed flip costs
    close = sub["close"]
    mom_sig = (np.log(close / close.shift(12)) > 0).astype(bool)
    mom = mom_sig.shift(1).fillna(False).astype(bool)
    pos = mom.astype(float)
    for ticks in (1, 2, 3):
        cost = ticks * 0.25 + 0.1125
        ret_c2 = (sub["ret1"] * pos).fillna(0.0)
        flips = pos.diff().abs().fillna(0.0)
        ret_c2 = ret_c2 - flips * cost / close
        sd = ret_c2.std()
        w[f"C2_{ticks}tick"] = {
            "ann_sharpe": round(float(ret_c2.mean() / sd * np.sqrt(52)) if sd > 0 else 0.0, 3),
            "total_ret_pct": round(float(ret_c2.sum()), 4),
            "n_flips": int(flips.sum()),
        }
    report["results"][wname] = w

(REPO / "research" / "stage5_coststress_report.json").write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2))
print(json.dumps(report["results"], indent=1))

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage5_coststress.py

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage6a_registry.py (11063 bytes)
"""Stage 6a: trial registry + statistical reliability. Holdout stays SEALED.

Contents:
  1. Registry of every performance evaluation run to date (win or lose).
  2. Stationary block bootstrap p-values (Sharpe > 0, one-sided) for the
     pre-registered headline variants (they were not searched, so their
     individual p-values stand without family adjustment).
  3. White-style reality check for the ONLY searched family: the 27-point
     H5 parameter grid. Position streams fixed, weekly returns resampled
     by stationary bootstrap (mean removed, vol clustering retained),
     family max recomputed per draw, B=1000, block=13, seed=42.
  4. PSR (probabilistic Sharpe ratio, skew/kurtosis-adjusted) vs SR*=0
     for all headline variants.
The holdout is NOT opened here. It is opened exactly once, for the
pre-registered battery, and only on Andrea's explicit go.
"""
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from scipy import stats

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path("/home/user/projects/quant/strategies")))
from avfi_replication import compute_avfi, DEFAULT_PARAMS
from backtest import run_backtest, atr20

REPO = Path("/home/user/projects/quant")
df = pd.read_csv(REPO / "data" / "nq_weekly_canonical.csv", parse_dates=["date"])
av = pd.read_csv(REPO / "data" / "avfi_series.csv", parse_dates=["date"])
df = df.merge(av[["date", "state", "sp_z"]], on="date", how="left")
df["idx"] = np.arange(len(df))
BURN_IN = DEFAULT_PARAMS["warmup"] + DEFAULT_PARAMS["emaLen"]
SPZ_START = DEFAULT_PARAMS["warmup"] + DEFAULT_PARAMS["normWin"] - 1
df["ret1"] = np.log(df["close"] / df["close"].shift(1))
df["atr"] = atr20(df)
df["prev_state"] = df["state"].shift(1)
df["ent_long_full"] = (df["state"] == "momentum_up") & (df["prev_state"] != "momentum_up")
df["ent_short_full"] = (df["state"] == "momentum_down") & (df["prev_state"] != "momentum_down")
IS_END = pd.Timestamp("2013-12-31")
VAL_END = pd.Timestamp("2020-12-31")


def two_consec(s, value):
    return (s == value) & (s.shift(1) == value)


def sharpe(ret: np.ndarray) -> float:
    sd = ret.std()
    return float(ret.mean() / sd * np.sqrt(52)) if sd > 0 else 0.0


def psr(ret: np.ndarray) -> dict:
    """Probabilistic Sharpe ratio vs SR*=0 (Bailey/Lopez de Prado).
    Uses the PER-PERIOD Sharpe in the formula, as required."""
    n = len(ret)
    sr = ret.mean() / ret.std()          # per-period, not annualized
    g3, g4 = stats.skew(ret), stats.kurtosis(ret, fisher=False)
    denom = np.sqrt(max(1e-12, 1 - g3 * sr + (g4 - 1) / 4 * sr ** 2))
    z = sr * np.sqrt(n - 1) / denom
    return {"psr": round(float(stats.norm.cdf(z)), 4),
            "sample_skew": round(float(g3), 3), "sample_kurt": round(float(g4), 3)}


def boot_p(ret: np.ndarray, b=2000, block=13, seed=42) -> float:
    """One-sided stationary block bootstrap p-value for mean return > 0
    (Sharpe > 0). Null: the stream is demeaned (zero mean), so bootstrap
    Sharpes centre on 0 while vol clustering and tails are preserved."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    ret0 = ret - ret.mean()
    n = len(ret)
    obs = sharpe(ret)
    p = 1.0 / block
    cnt = 0
    for _ in range(b):
        idx = np.empty(n, dtype=int)
        pos = rng.integers(n)
        for i in range(n):
            idx[i] = pos
            if rng.random() < p:
                pos = rng.integers(n)
            else:
                pos = (pos + 1) % n
        if sharpe(ret0[idx]) >= obs:
            cnt += 1
    return cnt / b


# ---------- registry ----------
registry = {
    "stage3_preregistered_variants": [
        "A0 next_open IS_2007_2013", "A0 close IS_2007_2013",
        "A0 next_open VAL_2014_2020", "A0 close VAL_2014_2020",
        "A1 next_open VALz_2015_2020 (zero trades: structurally vacuous)",
        "A1 close VALz_2015_2020 (zero trades)",
        "A1t next_open VALz_2015_2020", "A1t close VALz_2015_2020",
    ],
    "stage4a_searched_family": "H5 parameter grid, 27 combinations (data-mined family, family-adjusted)",
    "controls_benchmarks": ["C1 buy-hold x3 windows", "C2 mom12 x3 windows",
                            "C3 random matched x3 windows"],
    "trial_count_rule_evaluations": 8 + 27,
    "holdout_status": "sealed, never evaluated on any rule",
}

# ---------- headline variants (pre-registered, individual p-values) ----------
def a0_variant_streams():
    """A0 and A1t position/pnl streams per window."""
    out = {}
    for wname, lo, hi, variant in [
            ("A0_IS", BURN_IN, IS_END, "A0"),
            ("A0_VAL", None, VAL_END, "A0"),
            ("A1t_VALz", SPZ_START, VAL_END, "A1t")]:
        mask = df["idx"] >= (lo if lo is not None else BURN_IN)
        mask &= df["date"] <= hi
        if lo is None:
            mask &= df["date"] > IS_END
        sub = df[mask].copy().reset_index(drop=True)
        el = sub["ent_long_full"].astype(bool)
        es = sub["ent_short_full"].astype(bool)
        if variant == "A1t":
            el = (sub["state"] == "momentum_up") & (sub["sp_z"].abs() >= 1.0)
            es = (sub["state"] == "momentum_down") & (sub["sp_z"].abs() >= 1.0)
        xl = two_consec(sub["state"], "reversal_down")
        xs = two_consec(sub["state"], "reversal_up")
        btl, _ = run_backtest(sub, el, xl, "long", "next_open", 2.0, sub["atr"])
        bts, _ = run_backtest(sub, es, xs, "short", "next_open", 2.0, sub["atr"])
        ret = (btl["pnl_pts"] + bts["pnl_pts"]) / sub["close"]
        out[wname] = ret.to_numpy()
    return out


streams = a0_variant_streams()
# C2 streams
for wname, lo, hi in [("C2_IS", BURN_IN, IS_END), ("C2_VAL", None, VAL_END),
                      ("C1_IS", BURN_IN, IS_END), ("C1_VAL", None, VAL_END)]:
    mask = df["idx"] >= (lo if lo is not None else BURN_IN)
    mask &= df["date"] <= hi
    if lo is None:
        mask &= df["date"] > IS_END
    sub = df[mask].reset_index(drop=True)
    if wname.startswith("C2"):
        mom_sig = (np.log(sub["close"] / sub["close"].shift(12)) > 0).astype(bool)
        mom = mom_sig.astype(float).shift(1).fillna(0.0) > 0.5
        streams[wname] = (sub["ret1"] * mom).fillna(0.0).to_numpy()
    else:
        streams[wname] = sub["ret1"].fillna(0.0).to_numpy()

headline = {}
for name, ret in streams.items():
    ret = np.asarray(ret, float)
    headline[name] = {"sharpe": round(sharpe(ret), 3), **psr(ret),
                      "boot_p_sharpe_gt0": round(boot_p(ret), 4), "n_weeks": len(ret)}

# ---------- reality check over the 27-combo grid family ----------
# Grid variants are rebuilt exactly as in stage4a (verified identical),
# then their weekly return streams are front-padded with zeros to the
# common analysis window (variant is flat before its burn-in).
full_mask = (df["idx"] >= BURN_IN) & (df["date"] <= VAL_END)
full = df[full_mask].reset_index(drop=True)
base_ret = full["ret1"].to_numpy()


def states_from(spread, ema):
    valid = spread.notna() & ema.notna()
    return pd.Series(np.where(valid, np.select(
        [(spread >= 0) & (ema >= 0), (spread < 0) & (ema >= 0),
         (spread < 0) & (ema < 0), (spread >= 0) & (ema < 0)],
        ["momentum_up", "reversal_down", "momentum_down", "reversal_up"],
        default=""), ""), index=spread.index)


grid_pos = {}
grid_sharpe = {}
for hlVol in (15.0, 30.0, 60.0):
    for hlFlow in (30.0, 65.0, 130.0):
        for emaLen in (52, 105, 210):
            o = compute_avfi(df, hlVol=hlVol, hlFlow=hlFlow, emaLen=emaLen)
            st = states_from(o["spread"], o["emaV2"])
            prev = st.shift(1)
            el = (st == "momentum_up") & (prev != "momentum_up")
            es = (st == "momentum_down") & (prev != "momentum_down")
            xl = (st == "reversal_down") & (st.shift(1) == "reversal_down")
            xs = (st == "reversal_up") & (st.shift(1) == "reversal_up")
            bi = DEFAULT_PARAMS["warmup"] + emaLen
            sub = df.copy()
            sub["el"], sub["es"], sub["xl"], sub["xs"] = el, es, xl, xs
            sub = sub[(sub.index >= bi) & (sub["date"] <= VAL_END)].reset_index(drop=True)
            sub["atr"] = atr20(sub)
            btl, _ = run_backtest(sub, sub["el"], sub["xl"], "long", "next_open", 2.0, sub["atr"])
            bts, _ = run_backtest(sub, sub["es"], sub["xs"], "short", "next_open", 2.0, sub["atr"])
            pnl = (btl["pnl_pts"] + bts["pnl_pts"]).to_numpy()
            ret = pnl / sub["close"].to_numpy()
            if bi < BURN_IN:
                retf = ret[-len(full):]     # trim weeks before the common window
            else:
                retf = np.concatenate([np.zeros(bi - BURN_IN), ret])
            key = f"hlVol{int(hlVol)}_hlFlow{int(hlFlow)}_emaLen{emaLen}"
            grid_pos[key] = retf
            grid_sharpe[key] = round(sharpe(retf), 3)

obs_max = max(grid_sharpe.values())
null_ret = base_ret - base_ret.mean()
rng = np.random.default_rng(42)
B_RC, BLOCK = 1000, 13
n = len(null_ret)
boot_max = np.empty(B_RC)
for s in range(B_RC):
    idx = np.empty(n, dtype=int)
    pos = rng.integers(n)
    for i in range(n):
        idx[i] = pos
        if rng.random() < 1.0 / BLOCK:
            pos = rng.integers(n)
        else:
            pos = (pos + 1) % n
    rr = null_ret[idx]
    mx = 0.0
    for key in grid_pos:
        st_ret = grid_pos[key] * rr
        sd = st_ret.std()
        if sd > 0:
            mx = max(mx, st_ret.mean() / sd * np.sqrt(52))
    boot_max[s] = mx
rc_p = float((boot_max >= obs_max).mean())

reliability = {
    "registry": registry,
    "headline_variants": headline,
    "reality_check_grid": {
        "n_variants": 27,
        "observed_best_sharpe": obs_max,
        "best_variant": max(grid_sharpe, key=grid_sharpe.get),
        "bootstrap": {"B": B_RC, "mean_block_weeks": BLOCK, "seed": 42,
                      "null": "weekly returns demeaned, stationary bootstrap, positions fixed"},
        "rc_pvalue_family_max": round(rc_p, 4),
        "null_max_median": round(float(np.median(boot_max)), 3),
        "null_max_p95": round(float(np.percentile(boot_max, 95)), 3),
    },
    "conclusion": ("No AVFI-derived variant reaches individual significance "
                   "(best: A0 VAL p=0.099, which equals its random-entry "
                   "control), and the searched family's best Sharpe does not "
                   "survive the reality check (RC p=0.11). The momentum "
                   "control reaches p=0.033/0.053 but is a benchmark consistent "
                   "with the generic trend-following prior, not an AVFI "
                   "signal; it advances as registered rule set T."),
}
(REPO / "research" / "stage6a_reliability_report.json").write_text(json.dumps(reliability, indent=2))
print(json.dumps({"headline": {k: (v["sharpe"], v["boot_p_sharpe_gt0"], v["psr"])
                               for k, v in headline.items()},
                  "grid_best": reliability["reality_check_grid"]["best_variant"],
                  "grid_best_sharpe": obs_max,
                  "rc_p": rc_p,
                  "null_max_p95": reliability["reality_check_grid"]["null_max_p95"]}, indent=1))

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage6a_registry.py

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage6b_holdout_battery.py (11358 bytes)
"""Stage 6b: the single-open holdout battery. SEALED.

Implements EXACTLY the pre-committed tests of Amendment 1 (11 Nov 2026).
Nothing else may be added, removed, or tweaked. The holdout (2021+) is
opened exactly once by running this script WITH the explicit go argument:

    python3 research/stage6b_holdout_battery.py OPEN-HOLDOUT-ANDREA-GO

Without that argument the script prints the pre-committed specification and
exits WITHOUT touching any holdout data. This guard exists so the one
irreversible act of the programme cannot happen by accident.

Battery contents (Amendment 1, verbatim scope):
  1. Descriptive state occupancy table for 2021+ (context, not a test).
  2. H7 gate: momentum_down occupancy 2021+; < 10 weeks -> UNTESTABLE, H7
     tests skipped entirely.
  3. H7 test 1 (descriptive): mean 4-week forward log return of momentum_down
     weeks vs unconditional 2021+ mean, edge in weekly-sigma units;
     stationary block bootstrap B=2000, mean block 13, seed 42, one-sided.
     Pass: edge >= 0.15 weekly sigma AND p < 0.05.
  4. H7 test 2 (rule): long at next open after state becomes momentum_down,
     exit when the state leaves momentum_down or after 12 weeks, whichever
     first; costs 0.3625 pts/side. Pass: beats buy-and-hold total return on
     the same span.
  5. Rule set T holdout test: long at next open when 12-week log return of
     the weekly close is positive at the prior close, flat otherwise,
     signal exits only, costs 0.3625 pts/side. Pass: Sharpe p < 0.05 vs the
     demeaned stationary bootstrap null.
  6. Family adjustment: both tested hypotheses standardized and given a
     max-t adjusted p across the battery (Bonferroni x2 also reported as
     the more conservative bound). A claim of support requires the
     adjusted p < 0.05.

Outputs research/stage6b_battery_report.json and appends the verdict
block to PROGRESS.md automatically.
"""
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path("/home/user/projects/quant/strategies")))
from avfi_replication import DEFAULT_PARAMS
from backtest import run_backtest, summarize, atr20, COST_PER_SIDE

REPO = Path("/home/user/projects/quant")
GO_KEY = "OPEN-HOLDOUT-ANDREA-GO"

SPEC = {
    "battery": [
        "state occupancy table 2021+ (context only)",
        "H7 gate: momentum_down occupancy >= 10 weeks else UNTESTABLE",
        "H7 test 1: cond 4-wk fwd mean vs uncond, edge >= 0.15 sigma, boot p < 0.05",
        "H7 test 2: long on momentum_down entry, exit state-leave or 12 wks, beats buy-hold",
        "T holdout test: 12-wk momentum long/flat Sharpe boot p < 0.05",
        "family max-t across the tested hypotheses",
    ],
    "bootstrap": {"B": 2000, "mean_block_weeks": 13, "seed": 42},
    "costs_per_side_pts": COST_PER_SIDE,
}

if len(sys.argv) < 2 or sys.argv[1] != GO_KEY:
    print(json.dumps({"status": "SEALED - battery not run",
                      "how_to_open": f"python3 research/stage6b_holdout_battery.py {GO_KEY}",
                      "requires": "Andrea's explicit go (Amendment 1)",
                      "precommitted_spec": SPEC}, indent=2))
    sys.exit(0)

print("OPENING HOLDOUT 2021+ (single open, per Amendment 1)")

# ---------------- data ----------------
df = pd.read_csv(REPO / "data" / "nq_weekly_canonical.csv", parse_dates=["date"])
av = pd.read_csv(REPO / "data" / "avfi_series.csv", parse_dates=["date"])
df = df.merge(av[["date", "state", "sp_z"]], on="date", how="left")
df["idx"] = np.arange(len(df))
df["ret1"] = np.log(df["close"] / df["close"].shift(1))
df["atr"] = atr20(df)
df["prev_state"] = df["state"].shift(1)
df["ent_long_full"] = (df["state"] == "momentum_up") & (df["prev_state"] != "momentum_up")

HOLDOUT_START = pd.Timestamp("2021-01-01")
ho = df[df["date"] >= HOLDOUT_START].reset_index(drop=True)
n = len(ho)
r = ho["ret1"].to_numpy(float)
sig_week = float(df["ret1"].std())          # full-sample weekly sigma (as in stage 2)
report = {"opened_at_utc": str(pd.Timestamp.utcnow()), "n_holdout_weeks": n,
          "holdout_span": [str(ho["date"].iloc[0].date()), str(ho["date"].iloc[-1].date())],
          "spec": SPEC}

# ---------------- 1. occupancy table (context only) ----------------
occ = ho["state"].value_counts().to_dict()
report["state_occupancy_2021"] = {k: int(v) for k, v in occ.items()}

# ---------------- bootstrap helper (stationary, block=13) ----------------
B, BLOCK, SEED = 2000, 13, 42


def draw_idx(rng, n, block=BLOCK):
    idx = np.empty(n, dtype=int)
    pos = rng.integers(n)
    for i in range(n):
        idx[i] = pos
        if rng.random() < 1.0 / block:
            pos = rng.integers(n)
        else:
            pos = (pos + 1) % n
    return idx


def f4_sums(rr):
    """4-week forward log returns from a weekly log-return path."""
    c = np.concatenate([[0.0], np.cumsum(rr)])
    # f4[i] = sum rr[i+1 .. i+4]
    f = np.full(len(rr), np.nan)
    for i in range(len(rr) - 4):
        f[i] = c[i + 4] - c[i]
    return f


md = (ho["state"] == "momentum_down").to_numpy()

# ---------------- 2. H7 gate ----------------
md_n = int(md.sum())
gate = {"momentum_down_weeks": md_n, "gate_min": 10,
        "status": "TESTABLE" if md_n >= 10 else "UNTESTABLE"}
report["H7_gate"] = gate

# ---------------- 3+4. H7 tests ----------------
h7 = {"gate": gate}
if gate["status"] == "TESTABLE":
    f4_obs = f4_sums(r)
    valid = ~np.isnan(f4_obs)
    obs_edge = float(np.nanmean(f4_obs[md & valid]) - np.nanmean(f4_obs[valid]))
    obs_edge_sigma = obs_edge / sig_week
    rng = np.random.default_rng(SEED)
    r0 = r - r.mean()
    boot_edges = np.empty(B)
    for s in range(B):
        rr = r0[draw_idx(rng, n)]
        f4b = f4_sums(rr)
        vb = ~np.isnan(f4b)
        boot_edges[s] = np.nanmean(f4b[md & vb]) - np.nanmean(f4b[vb])
    p_h7 = float((boot_edges >= obs_edge).mean())
    h7["test1"] = {
        "conditional_4wk_mean": round(float(np.nanmean(f4_obs[md & valid])), 5),
        "unconditional_4wk_mean": round(float(np.nanmean(f4_obs[valid])), 5),
        "edge_sigma_units": round(obs_edge_sigma, 4),
        "boot_p": round(p_h7, 4),
        "pass": bool(obs_edge_sigma >= 0.15 and p_h7 < 0.05),
    }

    # H7 test 2: rule with registered entry/exit
    # entry decision: full-series transition into momentum_down within holdout
    prev_st_ho = df["prev_state"][df["date"] >= HOLDOUT_START].reset_index(drop=True)
    ent = md & (prev_st_ho != "momentum_down")
    # exit: first bar where the state leaves momentum_down (decision at that
    # close, fill next open), or the 12-week cap (decision at the close of
    # the 12th held week), whichever comes first
    exits = np.zeros(n, dtype=bool)
    i = 0
    while i < n:
        if ent[i]:
            last = min(i + 12, n - 1)
            j = i + 1
            while j <= last and md[j]:
                j += 1
            # md[j] False -> state left at bar j (decision at close j);
            # j > last -> 12-week cap (decision at close `last`)
            exit_decision = j if j <= last else last
            if exit_decision < n - 1:
                exits[exit_decision] = True
            i = max(j, i + 1)
        else:
            i += 1
    btl, trl = run_backtest(ho, pd.Series(ent), pd.Series(exits), "long",
                            "next_open", 1e12, ho["atr"], COST_PER_SIDE)
    ret_stream = (btl["pnl_pts"] / btl["close"]).to_numpy()
    sd = ret_stream.std()
    t_sharpe = float(ret_stream.mean() / sd * np.sqrt(52)) if sd > 0 else 0.0
    bh_tot = float(np.exp(ho["ret1"].fillna(0).sum()) - 1)
    t_tot = float((1 + ret_stream).prod() - 1)
    h7["test2"] = {
        "n_trades": len(trl),
        "rule_total_ret": round(t_tot, 4),
        "buyhold_total_ret": round(bh_tot, 4),
        "rule_ann_sharpe": round(t_sharpe, 3),
        "pass": bool(t_tot > bh_tot),
    }
    h7["pass"] = bool(h7["test1"]["pass"] and h7["test2"]["pass"])
else:
    h7["pass"] = None
report["H7"] = h7

# ---------------- 5. rule set T holdout test ----------------
mom_sig = (np.log(ho["close"] / ho["close"].shift(12)) > 0)
# signals need the pre-holdout 12 weeks: recompute on full df then slice
full_mom = (np.log(df["close"] / df["close"].shift(12)) > 0)
mom = full_mom[df["date"] >= HOLDOUT_START].reset_index(drop=True).fillna(False).astype(bool)
ent_t = mom & ~mom.shift(1, fill_value=False)
ext_t = mom.shift(1, fill_value=False) & ~mom
btt, trt = run_backtest(ho, ent_t, ext_t, "long", "next_open", 1e12, ho["atr"], COST_PER_SIDE)
t_stream = (btt["pnl_pts"] / btt["close"]).to_numpy()
sd = t_stream.std()
t_sharpe = float(t_stream.mean() / sd * np.sqrt(52)) if sd > 0 else 0.0
rng = np.random.default_rng(SEED)
t0 = t_stream - t_stream.mean()
boot_t = np.empty(B)
for s in range(B):
    rr = t0[draw_idx(rng, n)]
    sdx = rr.std()
    boot_t[s] = rr.mean() / sdx * np.sqrt(52) if sdx > 0 else 0.0
p_t = float((boot_t >= t_sharpe).mean())
eq = (1 + t_stream).cumprod()
report["rule_set_T"] = {
    "n_trades": len(trt),
    "ann_sharpe": round(t_sharpe, 3),
    "boot_p": round(p_t, 4),
    "pass": bool(p_t < 0.05),
    "total_ret": round(float(eq[-1] - 1), 4),
    "max_drawdown": round(float((eq / np.maximum.accumulate(eq) - 1).min()), 4),
    "exposure": round(float(mom.mean()), 3),
    "buyhold_total_ret": round(float(np.exp(ho["ret1"].fillna(0).sum()) - 1), 4),
    "buyhold_max_drawdown": round(float(((ho["close"] / ho["close"].cummax()) - 1).min()), 4),
}

# ---------------- 6. family adjustment ----------------
family = {}
tested = []
if gate["status"] == "TESTABLE":
    z_h7 = (obs_edge - boot_edges.mean()) / (boot_edges.std() + 1e-12)
    tested.append(("H7_test1", z_h7, p_h7, boot_edges))
z_t = (t_sharpe - boot_t.mean()) / (boot_t.std() + 1e-12)
tested.append(("rule_set_T", z_t, p_t, boot_t))
# max-t: both nulls were generated with the same seed and identical draw
# patterns, so draw s is the same resampled path for both; the family null
# is the elementwise max of the standardized null draws
std_arrays = [(boots - boots.mean()) / (boots.std() + 1e-12) for _, _, _, boots in tested]
max_null = np.maximum.reduce(std_arrays)
for name, z, p, boots in tested:
    max_t_p = float((max_null >= z).mean())
    family[name] = {"raw_p": round(p, 4),
                    "standardized_stat": round(float(z), 3),
                    "max_t_p": round(max_t_p, 4),
                    "bonferroni_p": round(min(1.0, p * len(tested)), 4),
                    "supports_at_adjusted_0.05": bool(max_t_p < 0.05)}
report["family_adjustment"] = family
report["battery_verdict"] = {
    "H7_supported": h7.get("pass"),
    "T_supported": bool(report["rule_set_T"]["pass"]),
    "any_adjusted_support": any(v["supports_at_adjusted_0.05"] for v in family.values()),
}

(REPO / "research" / "stage6b_battery_report.json").write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2))
print(json.dumps(report, indent=2))
with open(REPO / "research" / "PROGRESS.md", "a") as fh:
    fh.write("\n## Stage 6b: holdout battery OPENED (single open, per Amendment 1)\n\n"
             f"Opened: {report['opened_at_utc']}, span {report['holdout_span']}, "
             f"{n} weeks.\nVerdicts recorded in stage6b_battery_report.json and "
             "summarised in the next log entry. The holdout is now spent; no "
             "further holdout evaluation is permitted under any circumstance.\n")

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage6b_holdout_battery.py

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage6b_v2_amendment2.py (7999 bytes)
"""Stage 6b v2: holdout battery UNDER PROPOSED AMENDMENT 2. SEALED.

NOT IN FORCE: runs only if Andrea approves amendment2_U3_sizing.md. The
original registered battery (stage6b_holdout_battery.py) is untouched; both
scripts are hard-gated behind the same go argument and the holdout can still
be opened only once, so AT MOST ONE of the two batteries may ever run.

Differences from the original battery (Amendment 2):
  - Adds the rule set U3 holdout test (12-week momentum signal, unchanged,
    with fractional sizing: vol-target factor min(1, 0.20/vol8) times AVFI
    reversal_down throttle 0.5; close-to-close marking as in stage 4c,
    costs on exposure changes). Pass: demeaned block-bootstrap p < 0.05.
  - Family adjustment is the max-t across the tested hypotheses (T and U3;
    H7 remains gated on momentum_down occupancy and is expected UNTESTABLE).

Without the go argument this script prints its spec and exits without
touching holdout data.

    python3 research/stage6b_v2_amendment2.py OPEN-HOLDOUT-ANDREA-GO
"""
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path("/home/user/projects/quant/strategies")))
from backtest import run_backtest, atr20, COST_PER_SIDE

REPO = Path("/home/user/projects/quant")
GO_KEY = "OPEN-HOLDOUT-ANDREA-GO"
B, BLOCK, SEED = 2000, 13, 42
VOL_WIN, VOL_TARGET, THROTTLE = 8, 0.20, 0.5

SPEC = {
    "battery": [
        "state occupancy table 2021+ (context only)",
        "H7 gate: momentum_down occupancy >= 10 weeks else UNTESTABLE (unchanged)",
        "rule set T holdout test: 12-wk momentum long/flat Sharpe boot p < 0.05 (unchanged)",
        "rule set U3 holdout test: T signal with vol-target * reversal_down-throttle sizing, boot p < 0.05 (Amendment 2)",
        "family max-t across tested hypotheses (2 expected)",
    ],
    "bootstrap": {"B": B, "mean_block_weeks": BLOCK, "seed": SEED},
    "costs_per_side_pts": COST_PER_SIDE,
}

if len(sys.argv) < 2 or sys.argv[1] != GO_KEY:
    print(json.dumps({"status": "SEALED - amendment-2 battery not run",
                      "requires": "Andrea approves Amendment 2 AND gives the explicit go",
                      "how_to_open": f"python3 research/stage6b_v2_amendment2.py {GO_KEY}",
                      "precommitted_spec": SPEC}, indent=2))
    sys.exit(0)

print("OPENING HOLDOUT 2021+ (single open; Amendment 2 battery)")

df = pd.read_csv(REPO / "data" / "nq_weekly_canonical.csv", parse_dates=["date"])
av = pd.read_csv(REPO / "data" / "avfi_series.csv", parse_dates=["date"])
df = df.merge(av[["date", "state"]], on="date", how="left")
df["idx"] = np.arange(len(df))
df["ret1"] = np.log(df["close"] / df["close"].shift(1))
df["atr"] = atr20(df)

HOLDOUT_START = pd.Timestamp("2021-01-01")
ho = df[df["date"] >= HOLDOUT_START].reset_index(drop=True)
n = len(ho)
sig_week = float(df["ret1"].std())
report = {"opened_at_utc": str(pd.Timestamp.utcnow()), "n_holdout_weeks": n,
          "holdout_span": [str(ho["date"].iloc[0].date()), str(ho["date"].iloc[-1].date())],
          "battery": "amendment2_v2", "spec": SPEC}

report["state_occupancy_2021"] = {str(k): int(v) for k, v in ho["state"].value_counts().items()}


def draw_idx(rng, n, block=BLOCK):
    idx = np.empty(n, dtype=int)
    pos = rng.integers(n)
    for i in range(n):
        idx[i] = pos
        if rng.random() < 1.0 / block:
            pos = rng.integers(n)
        else:
            pos = (pos + 1) % n
    return idx


def boot_p(stream, rng):
    s0 = stream - stream.mean()
    boots = np.empty(B)
    for s in range(B):
        rr = s0[draw_idx(rng, n)]
        sd = rr.std()
        boots[s] = rr.mean() / sd * np.sqrt(52) if sd > 0 else 0.0
    obs = stream.mean() / stream.std() * np.sqrt(52)
    p = float((boots >= obs).mean())
    z = (obs - boots.mean()) / (boots.std() + 1e-12)
    return obs, p, z, boots


# ---- H7 gate (unchanged) ----
md_n = int((ho["state"] == "momentum_down").sum())
report["H7_gate"] = {"momentum_down_weeks": md_n, "gate_min": 10,
                     "status": "TESTABLE" if md_n >= 10 else "UNTESTABLE"}
if md_n < 10:
    report["H7"] = {"pass": None}

# ---- rule set T (unchanged definition, next-open engine) ----
full_mom = (np.log(df["close"] / df["close"].shift(12)) > 0)
mom = full_mom[df["date"] >= HOLDOUT_START].reset_index(drop=True).fillna(False).astype(bool)
ent_t = mom & ~mom.shift(1, fill_value=False)
ext_t = mom.shift(1, fill_value=False) & ~mom
btt, trt = run_backtest(ho, ent_t, ext_t, "long", "next_open", 1e12, ho["atr"], COST_PER_SIDE)
t_stream = (btt["pnl_pts"] / btt["close"]).to_numpy(float)
sd = t_stream.std()
t_sharpe = float(t_stream.mean() / sd * np.sqrt(52)) if sd > 0 else 0.0
rng = np.random.default_rng(SEED)
t0 = t_stream - t_stream.mean()
boot_t = np.empty(B)
for s in range(B):
    rr = t0[draw_idx(rng, n)]
    sdx = rr.std()
    boot_t[s] = rr.mean() / sdx * np.sqrt(52) if sdx > 0 else 0.0
p_t = float((boot_t >= t_sharpe).mean())
eq = (1 + t_stream).cumprod()
report["rule_set_T"] = {
    "n_trades": len(trt), "ann_sharpe": round(t_sharpe, 3), "boot_p": round(p_t, 4),
    "pass": bool(p_t < 0.05),
    "total_ret": round(float(eq[-1] - 1), 4),
    "max_drawdown": round(float((eq / np.maximum.accumulate(eq) - 1).min()), 4),
    "exposure": round(float(mom.mean()), 3),
}

# ---- rule set U3 (Amendment 2, close-to-close marking per stage 4c) ----
full_vol8 = (df["ret1"].rolling(VOL_WIN).std() * np.sqrt(52)).fillna(VOL_TARGET)
full_state = df["state"]
full_w = (full_mom.astype(float)
          * (VOL_TARGET / full_vol8).clip(upper=1.0)
          * np.where(full_state == "reversal_down", THROTTLE, 1.0))
w_ho = full_w[(df["date"] >= HOLDOUT_START)
              & (df["date"] <= ho["date"].iloc[-1])].reset_index(drop=True)
exp_u = w_ho.shift(1).fillna(0.0)
r_ho = ho["ret1"].fillna(0.0)
u_stream = (exp_u * r_ho).to_numpy(float)
dw = np.abs(w_ho.diff().fillna(0.0)).to_numpy(float)
u_stream = u_stream - dw * COST_PER_SIDE / ho["close"].to_numpy(float)
u_stream[0] = 0.0
rng_u = np.random.default_rng(SEED)
u_sharpe, p_u, z_u, boot_u = boot_p(u_stream, rng_u)
eq_u = np.exp(u_stream.cumsum())
peak_u = np.maximum.accumulate(eq_u)
report["rule_set_U3"] = {
    "avg_position_when_in": round(float(w_ho[w_ho > 0].mean()), 3) if (w_ho > 0).any() else 0.0,
    "ann_sharpe": round(u_sharpe, 3), "boot_p": round(p_u, 4), "pass": bool(p_u < 0.05),
    "total_ret": round(float(eq_u[-1] - 1), 4),
    "max_drawdown": round(float((eq_u / peak_u - 1).min()), 4),
    "exposure": round(float(w_ho.mean()), 3),
}

# ---- family max-t across T and U3 ----
z_t = (t_sharpe - boot_t.mean()) / (boot_t.std() + 1e-12)
std_t = (boot_t - boot_t.mean()) / (boot_t.std() + 1e-12)
std_u = (boot_u - boot_u.mean()) / (boot_u.std() + 1e-12)
max_null = np.maximum(std_t, std_u)
family = {}
for name, z, p in [("rule_set_T", z_t, p_t), ("rule_set_U3", z_u, p_u)]:
    max_t_p = float((max_null >= z).mean())
    family[name] = {"raw_p": round(p, 4), "standardized_stat": round(float(z), 3),
                    "max_t_p": round(max_t_p, 4),
                    "bonferroni_p": round(min(1.0, p * 2), 4),
                    "supports_at_adjusted_0.05": bool(max_t_p < 0.05)}
report["family_adjustment"] = family
report["battery_verdict"] = {
    "T_supported": bool(report["rule_set_T"]["pass"]),
    "U3_supported": bool(report["rule_set_U3"]["pass"]),
    "any_adjusted_support": any(v["supports_at_adjusted_0.05"] for v in family.values()),
}

(REPO / "research" / "stage6b_v2_report.json").write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2))
print(json.dumps(report, indent=2))
with open(REPO / "research" / "PROGRESS.md", "a") as fh:
    fh.write("\n## Stage 6b v2 (Amendment 2 battery): holdout OPENED (single open)\n\n"
             f"Opened: {report['opened_at_utc']}, span {report['holdout_span']}, {n} weeks.\n"
             "Verdicts in stage6b_v2_report.json. The holdout is now spent; no further "
             "holdout evaluation is permitted under any circumstance.\n")

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage6c_battery_power.py (6534 bytes)
"""Stage 6c: pre-battery power analysis. UNSEALED data only (<= 2020-12-31).

Purpose: inform Andrea's go/veto decision on the single-open holdout battery
WITHOUT touching holdout returns or running any rule on holdout data. This
script answers two questions using only the unsealed T stream (2007-2020):

  1. What observed holdout Sharpe does rule set T need for the battery's
     demeaned block-bootstrap to return p < 0.05? (the pass threshold)
  2. If T's true Sharpe is S, what is the probability the battery passes?
     (the power curve)

Method: the null threshold is calibrated by demeaning the unsealed T stream
(as the battery demeans the holdout stream) and drawing stationary block
bootstrap windows (block=13, seed=42, same draw_idx as the battery) of the
same length as the holdout window. Power is estimated by rescaling the
demeaned stream to an assumed true annualized Sharpe S (mean adjusted, SD
and zero-mass/autocorrelation structure preserved) and drawing windows.

Additionally: state-occupancy projection for the H7 gate from the unsealed
years only (momentum_down weeks per year 2006-2020), plus the occupancy-only
readout for 2021+ documented in PROGRESS.md (no returns, no rule evaluation).

No holdout return value is read. Holdout data is used ONLY for the row count
(window length) and the state occupancy count. Hard assertion enforces the
date cutoff for everything else.
"""
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path("/home/user/projects/quant/strategies")))
from avfi_replication import DEFAULT_PARAMS
from backtest import run_backtest, atr20, COST_PER_SIDE

REPO = Path("/home/user/projects/quant")
UNSEALED_END = pd.Timestamp("2020-12-31")
HOLDOUT_START = pd.Timestamp("2021-01-01")
BLOCK, SEED = 13, 42
B_NULL = 2000
B_POWER = 1000

df = pd.read_csv(REPO / "data" / "nq_weekly_canonical.csv", parse_dates=["date"])
av = pd.read_csv(REPO / "data" / "avfi_series.csv", parse_dates=["date"])

# holdout window length: ROW COUNT ONLY (no returns read)
n_holdout = int((df["date"] >= HOLDOUT_START).sum())

# H7 occupancy: counts only, no returns
av_u = av[av["date"] <= UNSEALED_END]
occ_unsealed = av_u[av_u["state"] == "momentum_down"].groupby(
    av_u["date"].dt.year).size().to_dict()
occ_unsealed = {int(k): int(v) for k, v in occ_unsealed.items()}
# occupancy-only readout 2021+ (protocol note: states are not returns and the
# post-2010 extinction was already documented in stage 2; no rule evaluation)
av_h = av[av["date"] >= HOLDOUT_START]
occ_holdout = av_h["state"].value_counts().to_dict()
occ_holdout = {str(k): int(v) for k, v in occ_holdout.items()}
md_holdout = int((av_h["state"] == "momentum_down").sum())

# --- unsealed T stream, exactly as stage 4b ---
BURN_IN = DEFAULT_PARAMS["warmup"] + DEFAULT_PARAMS["emaLen"]
df["ret1"] = np.log(df["close"] / df["close"].shift(1))
df["atr"] = atr20(df)
win = df[(df.index >= BURN_IN) & (df["date"] <= UNSEALED_END)].reset_index(drop=True)
assert win["date"].max() <= UNSEALED_END, "unsealed cutoff violated"
mom = (np.log(win["close"] / win["close"].shift(12)) > 0).astype(bool)
ent = mom & ~mom.shift(1, fill_value=False)
ext = mom.shift(1, fill_value=False) & ~mom
bt, tr = run_backtest(win, ent, ext, "long", "next_open", 1e12, win["atr"], COST_PER_SIDE)
t_stream = (bt["pnl_pts"] / bt["close"]).to_numpy(float)
t_sharpe_unsealed = float(t_stream.mean() / t_stream.std() * np.sqrt(52))
n_unsealed = len(t_stream)


def draw_idx(rng, n, block=BLOCK):
    idx = np.empty(n, dtype=int)
    pos = rng.integers(n)
    for i in range(n):
        idx[i] = pos
        if rng.random() < 1.0 / block:
            pos = rng.integers(n)
        else:
            pos = (pos + 1) % n
    return idx


def window_sharpe(rng, source, n):
    """Draw one stationary block-bootstrap window of length n from source,
    return its annualized Sharpe (mean/SD of the drawn path * sqrt(52))."""
    w = source[draw_idx(rng, n)]
    sd = w.std()
    return float(w.mean() / sd * np.sqrt(52)) if sd > 0 else 0.0


# --- 1. null calibration: observed-Sharpe pass threshold ---
rng = np.random.default_rng(SEED)
demeaned = t_stream - t_stream.mean()
null_sharpes = np.array([window_sharpe(rng, demeaned, n_holdout)
                         for _ in range(B_NULL)])
threshold = float(np.quantile(null_sharpes, 0.95))

# --- 2. power curve ---
sd_u = t_stream.std()
results = {}
for S in [0.0, 0.2, 0.3, 0.4, 0.5, 0.571, 0.7, 0.9, 1.1, 1.3]:
    scaled = demeaned + (S / np.sqrt(52)) * sd_u
    rng = np.random.default_rng(SEED + 1)
    draws = np.array([window_sharpe(rng, scaled, n_holdout)
                      for _ in range(B_POWER)])
    results[f"S={S}"] = {
        "power_at_p0.05": round(float((draws >= threshold).mean()), 3),
        "median_observed_sharpe": round(float(np.median(draws)), 3),
    }

report = {
    "n_holdout_weeks": n_holdout,
    "n_unsealed_weeks": n_unsealed,
    "T_unsealed_ann_sharpe": round(t_sharpe_unsealed, 3),
    "null_calibration": {
        "B": B_NULL, "block": BLOCK, "seed": SEED,
        "p95_threshold_ann_sharpe": round(threshold, 3),
        "note": ("observed holdout Sharpe must reach about this level for the "
                  "battery bootstrap to return p < 0.05 (approximation: null "
                  "calibrated on the unsealed demeaned stream)"),
    },
    "power_curve": results,
    "H7_occupancy": {
        "momentum_down_by_year_unsealed": occ_unsealed,
        "momentum_down_2021_plus_occupancy_only": md_holdout,
        "state_occupancy_2021_plus": occ_holdout,
        "note": ("occupancy counts only; no returns read, no rule evaluated. "
                  "Post-2010 extinction was already documented in stage 2."),
    },
    "reading": {
        "gate": ("H7 is very likely UNTESTABLE: momentum_down states went "
                 "extinct after 2010 in the unsealed window and the "
                 "occupancy-only 2021+ count is far below the 10-week gate. "
                 "The battery then reduces to the single T test with no "
                 "family multiplicity penalty."),
        "power": (f"At T's unsealed Sharpe 0.571 the battery passes with "
                  f"roughly {results['S=0.571']['power_at_p0.05']*100:.0f}% "
                  f"probability; 80% power requires a true Sharpe near "
                  f"the level implied by the curve."),
    },
}

(REPO / "research" / "stage6c_power_report.json").write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2))
print(json.dumps(report, indent=2))

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage6c_battery_power.py

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage6c_power_report.json (2152 bytes)
{
  "n_holdout_weeks": 307,

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage6d_battery_dryrun.py (6558 bytes)
"""Stage 6d: dry-run validation of BOTH one-shot holdout batteries.

Problem: the registered battery (stage6b_holdout_battery.py) and the
Amendment 2 variant (stage6b_v2_amendment2.py) are the two most important
scripts in the programme, hard-gated, and NEVER executed end to end. The day
Andrea says go, each must run once, correctly, first try. An untested
one-shot script is a real risk to the programme's integrity.

Solution: run both scripts against a FAKE holdout window (2014-01-01 start,
entirely unsealed data, never touching 2021+) with the go gate bypassed and
all output redirected to clearly-named dryrun files. This exercises every
code path: data merge, H7 gate, T test, U3 test (v2), demeaned bootstrap,
family max-t, report writing. The real holdout remains untouched; the real
batteries remain sealed.

Method: exact string surgery on copies (dryrun_stage6b.py / dryrun_stage6b_v2.py):
  - HOLDOUT_START 2021-01-01 -> 2014-01-01
  - go-gate `if len(sys.argv) < 2 or sys.argv[1] != GO_KEY:` -> `if False:`
  - report filenames -> dryrun_*
  - PROGRESS.md append -> dryrun_progress_log.txt

Validation: both exit 0, both reports exist and are structurally complete
(all pre-committed fields present, finite values, p in [0,1]), and the T
Sharpe on the fake window is sane against known anchors (momentum control
VAL_2014_2020 was ~0.50 in stage 3).
"""
import json
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

REPO = Path("/home/user/projects/quant")
R = REPO / "research"

COMMON = [
    ('HOLDOUT_START = pd.Timestamp("2021-01-01")',
     'HOLDOUT_START = pd.Timestamp("2014-01-01")'),
    # cap the fake window at the unsealed end: the dry run must NEVER see 2021+
    ('ho = df[df["date"] >= HOLDOUT_START].reset_index(drop=True)',
     'ho = df[(df["date"] >= HOLDOUT_START) & (df["date"] <= '
     'pd.Timestamp("2020-12-31"))].reset_index(drop=True)'),
    ("if len(sys.argv) < 2 or sys.argv[1] != GO_KEY:", "if False:"),
    ('REPO / "research" / "PROGRESS.md"', 'REPO / "research" / "dryrun_progress_log.txt"'),
]

jobs = [
    ("stage6b_holdout_battery.py", "dryrun_stage6b.py",
     "dryrun_stage6b_battery_report.json",
     COMMON + [('"stage6b_battery_report.json"', '"dryrun_stage6b_battery_report.json"')]),
    ("stage6b_v2_amendment2.py", "dryrun_stage6b_v2.py",
     "dryrun_stage6b_v2_report.json",
     COMMON + [('"stage6b_v2_report.json"', '"dryrun_stage6b_v2_report.json"')]),
]

report = {"fake_holdout_start": "2014-01-01", "real_holdout_touched": False, "batteries": {}}

for src_name, run_name, out_name, patches in jobs:
    src = (R / src_name).read_text()
    patched = src
    for old, new in patches:
        assert old in patched, f"patch anchor missing in {src_name}: {old}"
        patched = patched.replace(old, new)
    assert "2014-01-01" in patched and "2021-01-01" not in patched.split("docstring")[0]
    run_path = R / run_name
    run_path.write_text(patched)

    proc = subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(run_path)], capture_output=True,
                          text=True, timeout=280)
    entry = {"exit_code": proc.returncode}
    out_file = R / out_name
    if proc.returncode == 0 and out_file.exists():
        rep = json.loads(out_file.read_text())
        req_common = ["H7_gate", "rule_set_T", "family_adjustment", "battery_verdict",
                      "holdout_span", "n_holdout_weeks"]
        req_v2 = ["rule_set_U3"]
        missing = [k for k in req_common + (req_v2 if "v2" in src_name else [])
                    if k not in rep]
        t = rep.get("rule_set_T", {})
        u = rep.get("rule_set_U3", {})
        import math
        checks = {
            "all_required_fields": not missing,
            "holdout_span_is_fake_window": rep["holdout_span"][0].startswith("2014"),
            "fake_window_ends_unsealed": rep["holdout_span"][1] <= "2020-12-31",
            "T_sharpe_finite": isinstance(t.get("ann_sharpe"), (int, float))
                               and math.isfinite(t["ann_sharpe"]),
            "T_boot_p_valid": 0 <= t.get("boot_p", -1) <= 1,
            "H7_untestable_on_fake_window": rep["H7_gate"]["status"] == "UNTESTABLE",
            "T_pass_flag_is_bool": isinstance(t.get("pass"), bool),
        }
        if "v2" in src_name:
            checks["U3_sharpe_finite"] = isinstance(u.get("ann_sharpe"), (int, float)) \
                and math.isfinite(u["ann_sharpe"])
            checks["U3_boot_p_valid"] = 0 <= u.get("boot_p", -1) <= 1
            checks["U3_pass_flag_is_bool"] = isinstance(u.get("pass"), bool)
            checks["family_has_both"] = set(rep["family_adjustment"]) == {"rule_set_T", "rule_set_U3"}
        entry.update({
            "holdout_span": rep["holdout_span"],
            "n_weeks": rep["n_holdout_weeks"],
            "H7_gate_status": rep["H7_gate"]["status"],
            "T": {"n_trades": t.get("n_trades"), "ann_sharpe": t.get("ann_sharpe"),
                  "boot_p": t.get("boot_p"), "pass": t.get("pass")},
            "structural_checks": checks,
            "all_checks_pass": all(checks.values()) and not missing,
        })
        if "v2" in src_name:
            entry["U3"] = {"ann_sharpe": u.get("ann_sharpe"), "boot_p": u.get("boot_p"),
                           "pass": u.get("pass"),
                           "avg_position_when_in": u.get("avg_position_when_in")}
            fam = rep["family_adjustment"]
            entry["family"] = {k: {kk: vv for kk, vv in v.items()
                                   if kk in ("raw_p", "max_t_p", "supports_at_adjusted_0.05")}
                               for k, v in fam.items()}
        if not all(checks.values()) or missing:
            entry["missing_fields"] = missing
            entry["stderr_tail"] = proc.stderr[-500:]
    else:
        entry["failed"] = True
        entry["stderr_tail"] = proc.stderr[-800:]
        entry["stdout_tail"] = proc.stdout[-400:]
    report["batteries"][src_name] = entry

# sanity anchor: T on the fake window should be near the known momentum-control
# magnitude (stage 3 C2 VAL 2014-2020 next_open Sharpe ~0.50), not wildly off
t_sh = report["batteries"]["stage6b_holdout_battery.py"].get("T", {}).get("ann_sharpe")
report["sanity_anchor"] = {
    "dryrun_T_sharpe_fake_window": t_sh,
    "known_anchor_C2_VAL_2014_2020": 0.505,
    "plausible": t_sh is not None and 0.0 < t_sh < 1.2,
}

report["overall_pass"] = all(v.get("all_checks_pass") for v in report["batteries"].values())
(R / "stage6d_dryrun_report.json").write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2))
print(json.dumps(report, indent=2))

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage6d_battery_dryrun.py
