##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage7_guard_ts_mirror.ts (6307 bytes)
// stage7_guard_ts_mirror.ts
// TypeScript mirror (run_typescript sandbox) of the stage 7 completed-week
// guard, validated 2026-11-11 against the real canonical NQ weekly bar tail
// (60 bars incl. the partial 2026-11-13 bar, n_sessions=3 at fetch time).
//
// Purpose: validate the guard fix applied to stage7_signal_snapshot.py,
// stage7_log_entry.py and stage7_outcome.py:
//   OLD: forming week  := label > today   (a Friday-of-close pre-close
//        refresh produced a same-day-labelled incomplete bar that the guard
//        wrongly treated as a COMPLETED decision close)
//   NEW: completed bar := label < today (strictly before today)
//
// Report: research/stage7_completion_guard_report.json (all 8 checks pass).
// Mirror-side note: check C1's expectation was initially written in the wrong
// unit (the records quote the 12-week LOG return in percent: -0.1069 log ==
// -10.69%); corrected mirror-side, no tool was changed.
//
// Discipline: observation/tooling only. No rule, default, or frozen
// hypothesis changed; no performance statistic computed; the spent 2021+
// holdout never evaluated; battery scripts never run; H8/H9 never re-run;
// paper trading only.

const bars: { date: string; close: number }[] = [
  // 60-bar tail of data/nq_weekly_canonical.csv (date, close), as of the
  // 2026-11-11 fetch; last bar 2026-11-13 is PARTIAL (week still forming).
  ["2025-09-26", 24726.75], ["2025-10-03", 24992.0], ["2025-10-10", 24397.0],
  ["2025-10-17", 24986.5], ["2025-10-24", 25509.25], ["2025-10-31", 26004.0],
  ["2025-11-07", 25166.25], ["2025-11-14", 25094.0], ["2025-11-21", 24305.5],
  ["2025-11-28", 25482.0], ["2025-12-05", 25732.0], ["2025-12-12", 25213.5],
  ["2025-12-19", 25132.7890625], ["2025-12-26", 25863.25], ["2026-01-02", 25385.25],
  ["2026-01-09", 25938.25], ["2026-01-16", 25689.0], ["2026-01-23", 25738.25],
  ["2026-01-30", 25670.0], ["2026-02-06", 25163.25], ["2026-02-13", 24803.25],
  ["2026-02-20", 25067.5], ["2026-02-27", 25004.75], ["2026-03-06", 24670.25],
  ["2026-03-13", 24394.25], ["2026-03-20", 24291.779296875], ["2026-03-27", 23328.5],
  ["2026-04-03", 24218.0], ["2026-04-10", 25281.25], ["2026-04-17", 26825.5],
  ["2026-04-24", 27435.0], ["2026-05-01", 27835.75], ["2026-05-08", 29332.5],
  ["2026-05-15", 29231.75], ["2026-05-22", 29558.75], ["2026-05-29", 30405.25],
  ["2026-06-05", 29026.5], ["2026-06-12", 29662.0], ["2026-06-19", 30263.349609375],
  ["2026-06-26", 29368.25], ["2026-07-03", 29556.0], ["2026-07-10", 30032.25],
  ["2026-07-17", 28773.25], ["2026-07-24", 28282.25], ["2026-07-31", 28404.25],
  ["2026-08-07", 29834.75], ["2026-08-14", 30141.75], ["2026-08-21", 29387.75],
  ["2026-08-28", 29491.75], ["2026-09-04", 29565.25], ["2026-09-11", 29387.0],
  ["2026-09-18", 29613.6796875], ["2026-09-25", 30889.25], ["2026-10-02", 31061.75],
  ["2026-10-09", 31108.75], ["2026-10-16", 29391.5], ["2026-10-23", 29117.75],
  ["2026-10-30", 28534.0], ["2026-11-06", 27085.25], ["2026-11-13", 28622.25],
].map(([d, c]) => ({ date: d as string, close: c as number }));

const cmp = (a: string, b: string) => (a < b ? -1 : a > b ? 1 : 0);

function select(today: string) {
  const last = bars[bars.length - 1].date;
  return {
    old_sel: cmp(last, today) > 0 ? bars[bars.length - 2].date : last,
    new_sel: cmp(last, today) >= 0 ? bars[bars.length - 2].date : last,
  };
}

function cardAt(idx: number) {
  const c = (i: number) => bars[i].close;
  const mom12 = Math.log(c(idx) / c(idx - 12));
  const rets: number[] = [];
  for (let i = idx - 8 + 1; i <= idx; i++) rets.push(Math.log(c(i) / c(i - 1)));
  const mean = rets.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / rets.length;
  const vol8 =
    Math.sqrt(rets.reduce((a, r) => a + (r - mean) ** 2, 0) / (rets.length - 1)) *
    Math.sqrt(52);
  const sig = (i: number) => Math.log(c(i) / c(i - 12)) > 0;
  let ai = idx;
  while (ai > 1 && sig(ai) === sig(ai - 1)) ai--;
  return {
    date: bars[idx].date, close: c(idx),
    mom12_logret: +mom12.toFixed(4), mom12_pct: +(mom12 * 100).toFixed(2),
    T: mom12 > 0 ? "LONG" : "FLAT", signal_since: bars[ai].date,
    vol8_ann_pct: +(vol8 * 100).toFixed(2), vol_factor: +Math.min(1, 0.2 / vol8).toFixed(3),
  };
}

const i1106 = bars.findIndex((b) => b.date === "2026-11-06");
const i1113 = bars.findIndex((b) => b.date === "2026-11-13");
const scenarios = [
  { today: "2026-11-11", desc: "Wed, forming week present (n_sessions=3)" },
  { today: "2026-11-13", desc: "Friday of the close, pre-close (label == today)" },
  { today: "2026-11-15", desc: "Sunday session after the close (post-refresh)" },
].map((s) => ({ ...s, ...select(s.today) }));

const card1106 = cardAt(i1106);
const card1113 = cardAt(i1113);
const avfiState1106 = "reversal_down"; // from data/avfi_series.csv, 2026-11-06

const checks = [
  { id: "G1", name: "Wed: new guard selects 2026-11-06 (partial 11-13 excluded)",
    pass: scenarios[0].new_sel === "2026-11-06" },
  { id: "G2", name: "Wed: old guard agrees",
    pass: scenarios[0].old_sel === "2026-11-06" },
  { id: "G3", name: "Fri pre-close: OLD guard wrongly selected the forming 2026-11-13 (hole demonstrated)",
    pass: scenarios[1].old_sel === "2026-11-13" },
  { id: "G4", name: "Fri pre-close: NEW guard selects 2026-11-06 (hole closed)",
    pass: scenarios[1].new_sel === "2026-11-06" },
  { id: "G5", name: "Sun: new guard selects the completed 2026-11-13",
    pass: scenarios[2].new_sel === "2026-11-13" },
  { id: "C1", name: "card at 2026-11-06 matches frozen records (close 27085.25, mom12 -0.1069 log == -10.69%, FLAT, since 2026-10-30, vol8 23.57%, factor 0.849)",
    pass: card1106.close === 27085.25 && card1106.mom12_logret === -0.1069 &&
          card1106.T === "FLAT" && card1106.signal_since === "2026-10-30" &&
          card1106.vol8_ann_pct === 23.57 && card1106.vol_factor === 0.849 },
  { id: "C2", name: "U3 at 2026-11-06 unchanged: reversal_down throttle 0.5, flat signal -> size 0.00",
    pass: avfiState1106 === "reversal_down" && card1106.T === "FLAT" },
  { id: "C3", name: "card computable at the future completed 2026-11-13 bar (descriptive only)",
    pass: card1113.close === 28622.25 && Number.isFinite(card1113.mom12_logret) },
];

console.log(JSON.stringify({ scenarios, card1106, card1113, checks,
  all_pass: checks.every((c) => c.pass) }, null, 2));

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage7_guard_ts_mirror.ts

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage7_log_entry.py (11668 bytes)
"""Stage 7: weekly paper-log entry generator for the Sunday session.

Composes the stage 7 Sunday-log entry for the last COMPLETED weekly close
(the Friday that just closed) directly from the canonical bars, in the exact
template format of docs/stage7_sunday_log_template.md. Every quantitative
field is computed from data, not typed by hand: T signal and its 12-week
log return, signal age, AVFI state and sp_z, U3 vol factor / throttle /
size, V target / vol factor / size (ruleset_v_spec.md, registered
2026-11-12, forward-track only, no claimed support), and the paper
position size implied by the 30%-of-equity notional cap.

Discipline (hard-coded):
  - one entry per decision close; refuses a close already present in the log
    (append-only log, never edited afterwards);
  - --preview shows the draft WITHOUT touching the log (marked PREVIEW);
  - --append appends the entry to docs/stage7_sunday_log.md;
  - observation only: no performance statistic on any window is computed
    here, the spent 2021+ holdout is never evaluated, paper trading only.

Sizing convention (template): T is signal-exit only, no stop; size is the
MNQ fraction keeping notional under 30% of paper equity (1 NQ notional
equivalent would exceed it at current prices; the cap binds). Paper equity
is parsed from the last "Paper equity:" line of the log.

Usage:
  python3 research/stage7_log_entry.py            # guarded: print entry
  python3 research/stage7_log_entry.py --preview   # ignore log guard, mark PREVIEW
  python3 research/stage7_log_entry.py --append    # guarded append to the log
"""
import argparse
import json
import re
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import date, timedelta

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path("/home/user/projects/quant/strategies")))
from avfi_replication import compute_avfi

REPO = Path("/home/user/projects/quant")
LOG = REPO / "docs" / "stage7_sunday_log.md"
STATE = REPO / "docs" / "stage7_paper_state.json"
VOL_WIN, VOL_TARGET, THROTTLE = 8, 0.20, 0.5
MNQ_POINT_VALUE = 2.0        # USD per index point
NOTIONAL_CAP = 0.30          # of paper equity

df = pd.read_csv(REPO / "data" / "nq_weekly_canonical.csv", parse_dates=["date"])

today = pd.Timestamp(date.today())
last_label = df["date"].iloc[-1]
# Completed bar = Friday label strictly BEFORE today (a Friday-of-close run
# is pre-close: the forming week must never be booked as a decision close).
if last_label >= today:
    last = df.iloc[-2]      # forming week; use the completed one
else:
    last = df.iloc[-1]
pos = int(last.name)
closes = df["close"]

# ---- card fields, computed (same convention as stage7_signal_snapshot.py) ----
mom12 = float(np.log(closes.iloc[pos] / closes.iloc[pos - 12]))
t_signal = mom12 > 0
sig_hist = (np.log(closes / closes.shift(12)) > 0)
age_i = pos
while age_i > 1 and sig_hist.iloc[age_i] == sig_hist.iloc[age_i - 1]:
    age_i -= 1
signal_since = str(df["date"].iloc[age_i].date())

rets = np.log(closes / closes.shift(1)).iloc[pos - VOL_WIN + 1: pos + 1]
vol8_ann = float(rets.std() * np.sqrt(52))
vol_factor = min(1.0, VOL_TARGET / vol8_ann) if vol8_ann > 0 else 1.0

av = compute_avfi(df)
state = str(av["state"].iloc[pos])
sp_z = float(av["sp_z"].iloc[pos])
throttle = THROTTLE if state == "reversal_down" else 1.0
u3_size = (1.0 if t_signal else 0.0) * vol_factor * throttle

# rule set V parallel row (docs/ruleset_v_spec.md, registered 2026-11-12):
# graduated modulation of the TARGET (0.20 -> 0.15 in reversal_down), not of
# the position. Frozen: volTgt 0.20, defTgt 0.15, cap 1.0, signal = T.
v_tgt = 0.15 if state == "reversal_down" else 0.20
v_vol_factor = min(1.0, v_tgt / vol8_ann) if vol8_ann > 0 else 1.0
v_size = v_vol_factor if t_signal else 0.0

decision_close = str(last["date"].date())
decision_price = float(last["close"])

# Fetch-staleness guard: the canonical data must have been fetched AFTER the
# decision close's Friday. If stage0c's refresh failed (or was skipped) the
# canonical file can still hold a PARTIAL same-week fetch (e.g. fetched
# mid-week with n_sessions < 5), which would be booked as a completed week.
# fetched_at_utc's calendar date strictly after the bar's Friday label means
# the fetch saw the whole week; equal-or-earlier means it may not have.
report_path = REPO / "research" / "stage0c_report.json"
stale_fetch = False
if report_path.exists():
    try:
        fetched_at = pd.Timestamp(
            json.loads(report_path.read_text()).get("fetched_at_utc"))
        stale_fetch = (not pd.isna(fetched_at)) and (
            pd.Timestamp(last["date"]).date() >= fetched_at.date())
    except Exception:
        fetched_at = None  # unreadable report: do not block on it
stale_note = (f"n_sessions of the decision bar: {int(last['n_sessions'])}"
              if "n_sessions" in df.columns else "n_sessions unavailable")

# paper equity: last "Paper equity: X USD" line in the log; the paper-state
# file (written by stage7_outcome.py after each resolved week) is
# authoritative when it exists.
equity = 100_000.0
if LOG.exists():
    m = re.findall(r"Paper equity:\s*([\d,.]+)\s*USD", LOG.read_text())
    if m:
        equity = float(m[-1].replace(",", ""))
open_position = None
if STATE.exists():
    st = json.loads(STATE.read_text())
    equity = float(st["equity"])  # authoritative post-outcome equity
    open_position = st.get("position")

# position size under the notional cap (fractional MNQ, paper only)
cap_notional = NOTIONAL_CAP * equity
mnq_fraction = cap_notional / (MNQ_POINT_VALUE * decision_price) if decision_price > 0 else 0.0

# Fill-window dates derive from the DECISION CLOSE, not from today's weekday:
# the template books the planned action at the Monday open FOLLOWING the
# decision close (close + 3 days), and the next decision close is + 7 days.
# (The previous today-based derivation logged Monday close+10 on a Sunday run,
# contradicting the week the outcome resolver actually books.)
next_friday = (pd.Timestamp(decision_close) + timedelta(days=7)).date()
next_monday = (pd.Timestamp(decision_close) + timedelta(days=3)).date()

# ---- guard: one entry per decision close ----
logged = ""
if LOG.exists():
    logged = LOG.read_text()
already = f"Decision close: {decision_close}" in logged

# ---- planned action (position-aware bookkeeping of the SAME frozen rule:
# T is long when the 12-week log return is positive, flat otherwise; signal
# exits only. The paper state decides whether that means buy / hold / exit.) ----
if t_signal and open_position is None:
    planned = (
        f"go LONG at the Monday {next_monday.isoformat()} open: paper buy "
        f"{mnq_fraction:.2f} MNQ equivalent (notional {cap_notional:,.0f} USD, "
        f"30% cap binding), fill logged at open +/- 0.3625 pts/side; no stop, "
        f"signal exits only (exit at the first decision close with a negative "
        f"12-week log return)."
    )
    pre_committed = (
        f"entry fill window: Monday {next_monday.isoformat()} open; exit: "
        f"signal exit at any later decision close where the 12-week log return "
        f"of the weekly close is negative; no stop. Next decision close: "
        f"{next_friday.isoformat()}."
    )
elif t_signal and open_position is not None:
    planned = (
        f"hold the existing paper position ({open_position['frac']:.2f} MNQ, "
        f"entry fill {open_position['fill']:,.2f}); signal still LONG, no new "
        f"buy, no stop."
    )
    pre_committed = (
        f"no order; hold until the signal exits (first decision close with a "
        f"negative 12-week log return, exit filled at the following Monday "
        f"open - 0.3625 pts). Next decision close: {next_friday.isoformat()}."
    )
elif (not t_signal) and open_position is not None:
    planned = (
        f"signal exit at the Monday {next_monday.isoformat()} open: paper sell "
        f"{open_position['frac']:.2f} MNQ (fill at open - 0.3625 pts); the "
        f"12-week log return turned negative, rule set T goes flat."
    )
    pre_committed = (
        f"exit fill window: Monday {next_monday.isoformat()} open; after the "
        f"exit, stay flat until the 12-week log return of a decision close "
        f"turns positive again. Next decision close: {next_friday.isoformat()}."
    )
else:
    planned = f"none; stay flat for the week of {next_monday.isoformat()}. Flat is the decision."
    pre_committed = (
        f"no position; next decision close {next_friday.isoformat()}; if the "
        f"12-week log return of that weekly close turns positive, go long the "
        f"following Monday open under the same notional cap; if still "
        f"negative, stay flat. No stop (no position to protect)."
    )

u3_row = (f"size {u3_size:.2f} (vol factor {vol_factor:.3f}, 8-week realized "
         f"vol {vol8_ann * 100:.1f}% ann., state {state}, throttle {throttle})")

v_row = (f"size {v_size:.2f} (target {v_tgt * 100:.0f}% ann., vol factor "
         f"{v_vol_factor:.3f}, same vol8 {vol8_ann * 100:.1f}%, state {state}; "
         f"graduated target modulation, not a position throttle)")

entry = f"""## Entry: week of {next_monday.isoformat()} (Sunday session, decision close {decision_close})

- Decision close: {decision_close} (Friday that closed)
- Price context: NQ close {decision_price:,.2f}, 12-week log return {mom12 * 100:+.2f}%
- Rule signal (T): {"LONG" if t_signal else "FLAT"} since decision close {signal_since} (computed from data)
- AVFI state: {state}; sp_z: {sp_z:+.2f} (regime context only, never an entry trigger)
- Wave count (context only): n/a pending H3 annotations
- U3 parallel row: {u3_row} (parallel log, no claimed support)
- V parallel row: {v_row} (forward-track only; registered ruleset_v_spec.md, design bookkeeping, no claimed support)
- Planned action: {planned}
- Pre-committed levels: {pre_committed}
- Discipline check: matches the registered rule. YES
- Paper equity: {equity:,.2f} USD
- Outcome: pending (next session after the {next_friday.isoformat()} close)
"""

ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
ap.add_argument("--append", action="store_true", help="append the entry to the Sunday log")
ap.add_argument("--preview", action="store_true", help="ignore the duplicate guard; PREVIEW only, log untouched")
args = ap.parse_args()

if already and not args.preview:
    print(f"REFUSED: decision close {decision_close} already has an entry in the "
          f"Sunday log (append-only, one entry per decision close). Nothing written.")
    sys.exit(0)

if stale_fetch and not args.preview:
    print(f"REFUSED: canonical data was fetched on or before the decision close "
          f"{decision_close}, so that week's bar may be a partial pre-close "
          f"snapshot ({stale_note}). Rerun research/stage0c_canonical.py for a "
          f"fresh fetch, then rerun this. Nothing written.")
    sys.exit(1)

if args.preview:
    if stale_fetch:
        print(f"WARNING: canonical fetch predates the decision close "
              f"{decision_close} ({stale_note}); values may be from a partial "
              f"week. Do not book from this card without a stage0c refresh.")
    print(entry)
    print("(PREVIEW ONLY: not written to the log. Generated from the last "
          "completed weekly close; for the Sunday session after a Friday close, "
          "rerun stage0c first so the close is in the canonical bars.)")
elif args.append:
    if already:
        print("REFUSED: duplicate decision close; use --preview intentionally.")
        sys.exit(1)
    with LOG.open("a") as f:
        f.write("\n" + entry)
    print(f"APPENDED entry for decision close {decision_close} to the Sunday log.")
else:
    print(entry)

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage7_log_entry.py

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage7_outcome.py (13112 bytes)
"""Stage 7: weekly paper-log OUTCOME resolver for the Sunday session.

Companion to stage7_log_entry.py. stage7_log_entry.py writes the forward
decision (BEFORE the week resolves); this script resolves that week AFTER
its Friday close, from the canonical bars only:

  1. finds the latest log entry whose "Outcome:" line is still "pending";
  2. identifies the resolving week (the first completed weekly bar whose
     Friday label is AFTER that entry's decision close);
  3. executes the entry's logged planned action at the resolving week's
     Monday open with the template fill convention (+/- 0.3625 pts/side);
  4. marks the paper account to the resolving week's close, computes the
     week's rule P&L in points/USD, updates the paper equity;
  5. appends the resolved outcome block to the log (append-only: the
     original entry is NEVER edited) and updates docs/stage7_paper_state.json
     (the durable paper-account state: equity + open position).

Paper-account conventions (all from stage7_sunday_log_template.md; no new
rule is introduced anywhere in this script - rule set T's signal/exit logic
is untouched and lives in stage7_log_entry.py; this script only books what
the logged plan already decided):
  - fractional MNQ, 2.0 USD per index point;
  - fills at the weekly (Monday) open +/- 0.3625 pts per side;
  - mark-to-market equity at each resolving Friday close;
  - flat weeks: no position, no P&L, equity unchanged;
  - exit weeks: sell at Monday open - 0.3625; realized position P&L booked.

Discipline (hard-coded):
  - the spent 2021+ holdout is never evaluated and no performance
    statistic beyond this paper account's bookkeeping is computed;
  - refuses to resolve a week whose Friday close is not yet in the
    completed canonical bars (nothing fabricated, ever);
  - refuses to resolve an entry twice (append-only);
  - refuses to resolve an entry that is not the newest one (no backfilling
    of old weeks after the fact);
  - --preview shows the draft WITHOUT touching the log; --append writes.

Usage:
  python3 research/stage7_outcome.py            # guarded: draft or PENDING
  python3 research/stage7_outcome.py --preview # ignore guards, PREVIEW only
  python3 research/stage7_outcome.py --append   # guarded append + state update
"""
import argparse
import json
import re
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import date

import pandas as pd

REPO = Path("/home/user/projects/quant")
LOG = REPO / "docs" / "stage7_sunday_log.md"
STATE = REPO / "docs" / "stage7_paper_state.json"
MNQ_POINT_VALUE = 2.0
SLIP = 0.3625  # pts per side (1 tick + commission), template convention


def load_state(fallback_equity: float) -> dict:
    if STATE.exists():
        return json.loads(STATE.read_text())
    return {"equity": fallback_equity, "position": None}


def entry_blocks(text: str):
    """Split the log into entry blocks (## headers) in file order."""
    parts = re.split(r"(?m)^(?=## )", text)
    return [p for p in parts if p.startswith("## ")]


def main() -> None:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--append", action="store_true",
                    help="append the resolved outcome and update the paper state")
    ap.add_argument("--preview", action="store_true",
                    help="ignore the guards; PREVIEW only, nothing written")
    args = ap.parse_args()

    if not LOG.exists():
        print("No Sunday log found; nothing to resolve.")
        sys.exit(0)
    text = LOG.read_text()
    blocks = entry_blocks(text)

    # OLDEST entry with a pending outcome; also guard out-of-order resolution
    # (weeks resolve strictly in order, before the next entry is written).
    # An entry whose block already contains a resolved outcome is SKIPPED: its
    # original "- Outcome: pending" line stays in the log forever (the log is
    # append-only and never edited), so pending-ness is "pending line AND no
    # resolved-outcome block" - without this, the resolver would re-find the
    # resolved entry every week and refuse forever, stalling the forward test.
    pending_idx, pending_block = None, None
    for i, b in enumerate(blocks):
        if (re.search(r"(?m)^- Outcome: pending", b)
                and not re.search(r"Outcome \(resolved", b)):
            pending_idx, pending_block = i, b
            break
    if pending_block is None:
        print("No entry with a pending outcome; nothing to resolve.")
        sys.exit(0)
    for b in blocks[pending_idx + 1:]:
        if re.search(r"(?m)^- Decision close:", b):
            print("REFUSED: a newer entry exists after the pending one; resolve "
                  "weeks strictly in order (no backfilling). Nothing written.")
            sys.exit(1)
    if re.search(r"Outcome \(resolved", pending_block):
        print("REFUSED: this entry already has a resolved outcome. Nothing written.")
        sys.exit(1)

    m = re.search(r"(?m)^- Decision close:\s*([\d-]+)", pending_block)
    decision_close = m.group(1)
    planned_line = re.search(r"(?m)^- Planned action:\s*(.+)$", pending_block).group(1)

    # equity fallback: last "Paper equity:" line of the log (same parse as
    # stage7_log_entry.py); the state file is authoritative when it exists.
    eq_matches = re.findall(r"Paper equity:\s*([\d,.]+)\s*USD", text)
    fallback_equity = float(eq_matches[-1].replace(",", "")) if eq_matches else 100_000.0
    state = load_state(fallback_equity)

    # ---- resolve the week from the canonical bars ----
    df = pd.read_csv(REPO / "data" / "nq_weekly_canonical.csv", parse_dates=["date"])
    today = pd.Timestamp(date.today())
    dc = pd.Timestamp(decision_close)
    after = df[df["date"] > dc]
    if len(after) == 0:
        print(f"PENDING: no weekly bar after decision close {decision_close} "
              f"yet; rerun after that Friday close (and a stage0c refresh). "
              f"Nothing written.")
        sys.exit(0)
    week = after.iloc[0]
    # Completed only if the resolving Friday is strictly BEFORE today: on
    # that Friday itself the close has not happened yet at run time.
    if pd.Timestamp(week["date"]) >= today:
        print(f"PENDING: the resolving week (closes {week['date'].date()}) is "
              f"still forming; nothing is booked before the Friday close. "
              f"Nothing written.")
        sys.exit(0)

    week_close_date = str(pd.Timestamp(week["date"]).date())

    # Fetch-staleness guard: the resolving week's bar must come from a fetch
    # made AFTER that week's Friday close. If the Sunday stage0c refresh
    # failed, the canonical file may still hold the PARTIAL mid-week fetch
    # (e.g. n_sessions < 5), which must never be booked as a resolved week.
    report_path = REPO / "research" / "stage0c_report.json"
    stale_fetch = False
    if report_path.exists():
        try:
            fetched_at = pd.Timestamp(
                json.loads(report_path.read_text()).get("fetched_at_utc"))
            stale_fetch = (not pd.isna(fetched_at)) and (
                pd.Timestamp(week["date"]).date() >= fetched_at.date())
        except Exception:
            stale_fetch = False  # unreadable report: do not block on it
    n_sess = int(week["n_sessions"]) if "n_sessions" in df.columns else None
    if stale_fetch and not args.preview:
        print(f"REFUSED: canonical data was fetched on or before the resolving "
              f"week's Friday close (week closing {week_close_date}, n_sessions "
              f"{n_sess}), so the bar may be a partial pre-close snapshot. "
              f"Rerun research/stage0c_canonical.py for a fresh fetch, then "
              f"rerun this. Nothing written.")
        sys.exit(1)
    mon_open = float(week["open"])
    week_close = float(week["close"])
    equity = float(state["equity"])
    position = state["position"]  # None or {frac, fill, entry_date}

    # ---- classify the logged plan (bookkeeping only; the decision was ----
    # ---- made in the entry itself, before the week resolved). Match on  ----
    # ---- distinctive substrings of the generator's plan texts so that    ----
    # ---- the hold plan (which also mentions the future exit) is not      ----
    # ---- misread as an exit plan.                                        ----
    plan = "flat"
    if "go LONG" in planned_line and "buy" in planned_line:
        plan = "enter"
    elif "hold the existing paper position" in planned_line:
        plan = "hold"
    elif "signal exit at the Monday" in planned_line:
        plan = "exit"

    outcome_lines = []
    if plan == "enter":
        if position is not None:
            print("REFUSED: plan says enter but a position is already open in "
                  "the paper state; investigate before booking (no double "
                  "positions). Nothing written.")
            sys.exit(1)
        mf = re.search(r"buy\s+([\d.]+)\s+MNQ", planned_line)
        if mf is None:
            print("REFUSED: plan says enter but no MNQ fraction could be "
                  "parsed from the logged planned action; nothing booked.")
            sys.exit(1)
        frac = float(mf.group(1))
        fill = mon_open + SLIP  # buy slippage
        position = {"frac": frac, "fill": fill, "entry_date": week_close_date,
                    "mark": week_close}
        pnl_usd = frac * MNQ_POINT_VALUE * (week_close - fill)
        pnl_pts = week_close - fill
        equity += pnl_usd
        outcome_lines.append(
            f"- Outcome (resolved, week closing {week_close_date}): filled "
            f"{frac:.2f} MNQ at {fill:,.2f} (Monday open {mon_open:,.2f} + "
            f"{SLIP} pts); week close {week_close:,.2f}; week P&L "
            f"{pnl_pts:+,.2f} pts ({pnl_usd:+,.2f} USD, {pnl_usd / max(equity - pnl_usd, 1) * 100:+.2f}% "
            f"of equity); position open.")
    elif plan == "hold" and position is not None:
        prev_mark = float(position.get("mark", position["fill"]))
        pnl_usd = position["frac"] * MNQ_POINT_VALUE * (week_close - prev_mark)
        pnl_pts = week_close - prev_mark
        equity += pnl_usd
        position["mark"] = week_close
        outcome_lines.append(
            f"- Outcome (resolved, week closing {week_close_date}): held "
            f"{position['frac']:.2f} MNQ (entry fill {position['fill']:,.2f}); "
            f"week close {week_close:,.2f}; week P&L {pnl_pts:+,.2f} pts "
            f"({pnl_usd:+,.2f} USD, {pnl_usd / max(equity - pnl_usd, 1) * 100:+.2f}% "
            f"of equity); position open.")
    elif plan == "exit" and position is not None:
        fill = mon_open - SLIP  # sell slippage
        week_pnl_usd = position["frac"] * MNQ_POINT_VALUE * (fill - float(position.get("mark", position["fill"])))
        realized_usd = position["frac"] * MNQ_POINT_VALUE * (fill - position["fill"])
        equity += week_pnl_usd
        outcome_lines.append(
            f"- Outcome (resolved, week closing {week_close_date}): exited "
            f"{position['frac']:.2f} MNQ at {fill:,.2f} (Monday open "
            f"{mon_open:,.2f} - {SLIP} pts) per the logged signal exit; "
            f"realized position P&L {realized_usd:+,.2f} USD "
            f"({(fill - position['fill']):+,.2f} pts, entry {position['fill']:,.2f} "
            f"of {position.get('entry_date', 'n/a')}); flat.")
        position = None
    else:
        # flat week (plan 'flat', or hold/exit with no open position = no-op)
        action = "flat as planned" if plan == "flat" else (
            f"no position to {'hold' if plan == 'hold' else 'exit'}; "
            f"logged plan was a no-op this week")
        outcome_lines.append(
            f"- Outcome (resolved, week closing {week_close_date}): {action}; "
            f"no position, no P&L. Week close {week_close:,.2f}.")

    new_equity = equity
    outcome_lines.append("- Deviation from plan: none (booked from the logged "
                         "plan and the canonical bars only)")
    outcome_lines.append(
        f"- Paper equity: {new_equity:,.2f} USD (post-outcome, week closing "
        f"{week_close_date})")

    block = "\n".join(outcome_lines)

    if args.preview:
        if stale_fetch:
            print(f"WARNING: canonical fetch predates the resolving week "
                  f"(week closing {week_close_date}, n_sessions {n_sess}); "
                  f"values may be from a partial week. Do not book from this "
                  f"preview without a stage0c refresh.")
        print(block)
        print("(PREVIEW ONLY: log and paper state untouched.)")
    elif args.append:
        with LOG.open("a") as f:
            f.write("\n" + block + "\n")
        STATE.write_text(json.dumps(
            {"equity": new_equity, "position": position,
             "last_resolved_week": week_close_date,
             "resolved_decision_close": decision_close}, indent=2))
        print(f"APPENDED outcome for the week closing {week_close_date} "
              f"(entry decision close {decision_close}); paper equity "
              f"{new_equity:,.2f} USD; state file updated.")
    else:
        print(block)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage7_outcome.py

##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage7_outcome_ts_mirror.ts (13252 bytes)
/**
 * stage7_outcome_ts_mirror.ts
 * TypeScript mirror of research/stage7_outcome.py (the weekly paper-log
 * OUTCOME resolver), executed in a no-Python sandbox to validate the
 * resolution logic BEFORE the tool's first real run (Sunday 2026-11-15).
 * Same discipline as the session-2/3 mirrors: the instrument is validated,
 * no real outcome is booked (the real log is untouched; the mirror works on
 * in-memory copies), no performance statistic, holdout never touched.
 *
 * Cases:
 *  R1  real Entry 0 (flat), today = 2026-11-11: PENDING (forming week).
 *  R2  real Entry 0, today = 2026-11-15: resolves flat, equity unchanged.
 *  R3  duplicate guard: nothing pending after a resolution.
 *  R4  enter: fill = Monday open + 0.3625, week P&L, state position + mark.
 *  R5  hold: P&L from prior mark, mark rolled to the new close.
 *  R6  exit: fill = Monday open - 0.3625, week + realized P&L, flat.
 *  R7  out-of-order guard: pending entry with a newer entry after it.
 *  R8  double-enter guard: enter plan with a position already open.
 *  R9  plan classification: hold text (mentions the future exit) is 'hold'.
 *  R10 log equity parse picks up the appended post-outcome equity line.
 */
const SLIP = 0.3625;
const PV = 2.0;

interface Bar { date: string; open: number; close: number }
interface Position { frac: number; fill: number; entry_date: string; mark?: number }
interface State { equity: number; position: Position | null }

// Real canonical tail (pipeline/data/nq_weekly_canonical.csv), as of session.
const REAL_BARS: Bar[] = [
  { date: "2026-10-16", open: 31145.5, close: 29391.5 },
  { date: "2026-10-23", open: 29227.0, close: 29117.75 },
  { date: "2026-10-30", open: 29268.0, close: 28534.0 },
  { date: "2026-11-06", open: 28587.75, close: 27085.25 },
  { date: "2026-11-13", open: 26878.5, close: 28622.25 }, // forming at session time
];

// Real Entry 0 text from docs/stage7_sunday_log.md (verbatim, abridged fields).
const ENTRY0 = `## Entry 0: start-up (Wednesday 2026-11-11)

- Decision close: 2026-11-06 (Friday that closed)
- Price context: NQ close 27,085.25, 12-week log return -10.69%
- Rule signal (T): FLAT since 2026-10-30 decision close (computed from data)
- U3 parallel row: size 0.00 (flat signal; state throttle 0.5, vol factor 0.849)
- Planned action: none; stay flat for the week of 2026-11-09. Flat is the decision.
- Pre-committed levels: next decision close 2026-11-13; if the 12-week log
  return of the weekly close turns positive, go long 1 MNQ notional
  fraction at Monday open (notional under 30% of paper equity); if still
  negative, stay flat. No stop (signal exits only).
- Discipline check: matches the registered rule. YES
- Paper equity: 100,000.00 USD (start)
- Outcome: pending (next session Sunday 2026-11-15)
`;

// Mirror of stage7_outcome.py's plan classification.
function classify(planned: string): string {
  if (planned.includes("go LONG") && planned.includes("buy")) return "enter";
  if (planned.includes("hold the existing paper position")) return "hold";
  if (planned.includes("signal exit at the Monday")) return "exit";
  return "flat";
}

// Mirror of the resolver core. Returns either a refusal/pending notice or
// the outcome block plus the new state.
function resolve(logText: string, bars: Bar[], today: string, state: State): {
  status: "PENDING" | "REFUSED" | "NOTHING" | "RESOLVED";
  block?: string; state?: State; reason?: string } {
  const blocks = logText.split(/(?=## )/).filter((b) => b.startsWith("## "));
  let pendingIdx = -1;
  for (let i = 0; i < blocks.length; i++) {
    if (/^- Outcome: pending/m.test(blocks[i])) { pendingIdx = i; break; }  // OLDEST pending
  }
  if (pendingIdx === -1) return { status: "NOTHING", reason: "no pending entry" };
  const pending = blocks[pendingIdx];
  if (/Outcome \(resolved/.test(pending))
    return { status: "REFUSED", reason: "already resolved" };
  for (let i = pendingIdx + 1; i < blocks.length; i++) {
    if (/^- Decision close:/m.test(blocks[i]))
      return { status: "REFUSED", reason: "newer entry exists; resolve in order" };
  }
  const dc = pending.match(/^- Decision close:\s*([\d-]+)/m)![1];
  const planned = pending.match(/^- Planned action:\s*(.+)$/m)![1];
  const after = bars.filter((b) => b.date > dc);
  if (after.length === 0) return { status: "PENDING", reason: "no bar after decision close" };
  const week = after[0];
  if (week.date > today) return { status: "PENDING", reason: "resolving week still forming" };

  const plan = classify(planned);
  let equity = state.equity;
  let position = state.position ? { ...state.position } : null;
  const lines: string[] = [];

  if (plan === "enter") {
    if (position) return { status: "REFUSED", reason: "enter with position open" };
    const frac = parseFloat(planned.match(/buy\s+([\d.]+)\s+MNQ/)![1]);
    const fill = week.open + SLIP;
    position = { frac, fill, entry_date: week.date, mark: week.close };
    const pnl = frac * PV * (week.close - fill);
    equity += pnl;
    lines.push(`entry fill ${fill.toFixed(2)} frac ${frac} weekPnL ${pnl.toFixed(2)} equity ${equity.toFixed(2)}`);
  } else if (plan === "hold" && position) {
    const prev = position.mark ?? position.fill;
    const pnl = position.frac * PV * (week.close - prev);
    equity += pnl;
    position.mark = week.close;
    lines.push(`hold from ${prev} weekPnL ${pnl.toFixed(2)} equity ${equity.toFixed(2)}`);
  } else if (plan === "exit" && position) {
    const fill = week.open - SLIP;
    const weekPnl = position.frac * PV * (fill - (position.mark ?? position.fill));
    const realized = position.frac * PV * (fill - position.fill);
    equity += weekPnl;
    lines.push(`exit fill ${fill.toFixed(2)} weekPnL ${weekPnl.toFixed(2)} realized ${realized.toFixed(2)} equity ${equity.toFixed(2)}`);
    position = null;
  } else {
    lines.push(`flat/no-op week close ${week.close}`);
  }
  return { status: "RESOLVED", block: lines.join(" | "), state: { equity, position } };
}

const results: Record<string, boolean> = {};
const notes: Record<string, string> = {};

// R1: real Entry 0, today 2026-11-11 (session date): forming week -> PENDING
let r = resolve(ENTRY0, REAL_BARS, "2026-11-11", { equity: 100000, position: null });
results.R1_pending_before_close = r.status === "PENDING";
notes.R1 = r.reason ?? "";

// R2: same, today 2026-11-15 (Sunday session): resolves flat, equity unchanged
r = resolve(ENTRY0, REAL_BARS, "2026-11-15", { equity: 100000, position: null });
results.R2_flat_resolution =
  r.status === "RESOLVED" &&
  r.state!.equity === 100000 &&
  r.state!.position === null &&
  r.block!.includes("flat/no-op");
notes.R2 = r.block ?? "";

// R3: duplicate guard: after resolution the entry no longer says pending.
const resolvedLog = ENTRY0.replace(
  "- Outcome: pending (next session Sunday 2026-11-15)",
  "- Outcome: pending (next session Sunday 2026-11-15)\n- Outcome (resolved, week closing 2026-11-13): flat as planned...");
r = resolve(resolvedLog, REAL_BARS, "2026-11-15", { equity: 100000, position: null });
results.R3_no_backfill = r.status === "REFUSED" || r.status === "NOTHING";
notes.R3 = r.reason ?? "";

// R4: enter case. cap math: 30% of 100k = 30k notional; frac = 30000/(2*27085.25)
// = 0.5538 -> logged (and booked) as 0.55; the resolver books the LOGGED size.
const frac = 0.55;
const entryLog = ENTRY0.replace(
  "- Planned action: none; stay flat for the week of 2026-11-09. Flat is the decision.",
  `- Planned action: go LONG at the Monday 2026-11-16 open: paper buy ${frac.toFixed(2)} MNQ equivalent (notional 30,000 USD, 30% cap binding), fill logged at open +/- 0.3625 pts/side; no stop, signal exits only.`);
r = resolve(entryLog, REAL_BARS, "2026-11-15", { equity: 100000, position: null });
const expFill = 26878.5 + SLIP;
const expPnl = frac * PV * (28622.25 - expFill);
results.R4_enter_math =
  r.status === "RESOLVED" &&
  Math.abs(r.state!.position!.fill - expFill) < 1e-9 &&
  Math.abs(r.state!.position!.mark! - 28622.25) < 1e-9 &&
  Math.abs(r.state!.equity - (100000 + expPnl)) < 1e-6;
notes.R4 = `fill ${expFill}, pnl ${expPnl.toFixed(2)}, equity ${(100000 + expPnl).toFixed(2)}`;

// R5: hold case from the R4 position; next week bar
// R5: hold case. The entry's decision close is 2026-11-13 (the Friday of the
// entry week); the resolving week is the next bar, closing 2026-11-20.
const holdBars: Bar[] = [...REAL_BARS, { date: "2026-11-20", open: 28700, close: 28000 }];
const holdLog = ENTRY0
  .replace("- Decision close: 2026-11-06 (Friday that closed)",
           "- Decision close: 2026-11-13 (Friday that closed)")
  .replace(
  "- Planned action: none; stay flat for the week of 2026-11-09. Flat is the decision.",
  "- Planned action: hold the existing paper position (0.55 MNQ, entry fill 26,878.86); signal still LONG, no new buy, no stop.");
r = resolve(holdLog, holdBars, "2026-11-22", {
  equity: 100000 + expPnl, position: { frac, fill: expFill, entry_date: "2026-11-13", mark: 28622.25 } });
const holdPnl = frac * PV * (28000 - 28622.25);
results.R5_hold_math =
  r.status === "RESOLVED" &&
  Math.abs(r.state!.equity - (100000 + expPnl + holdPnl)) < 1e-6 &&
  Math.abs(r.state!.position!.mark! - 28000) < 1e-9;
notes.R5 = `hold pnl ${holdPnl.toFixed(2)}, equity ${(100000 + expPnl + holdPnl).toFixed(2)}`;

// R6: exit case from the R5 position; exit Monday open 28700 - slip
// R6: exit case. Decision close 2026-11-20 (Friday of the hold week); the
// resolving week closes 2026-11-27, exit filled at its Monday open 27900.
const exitLog = ENTRY0
  .replace("- Decision close: 2026-11-06 (Friday that closed)",
           "- Decision close: 2026-11-20 (Friday that closed)")
  .replace(
  "- Planned action: none; stay flat for the week of 2026-11-09. Flat is the decision.",
  "- Planned action: signal exit at the Monday 2026-11-23 open: paper sell 0.55 MNQ (fill at open - 0.3625 pts); the 12-week log return turned negative, rule set T goes flat.");
const exitBars: Bar[] = [...holdBars, { date: "2026-11-27", open: 27900, close: 27700 }];
const eqAfterHold = 100000 + expPnl + holdPnl;
r = resolve(exitLog, exitBars, "2026-11-29", {
  equity: eqAfterHold, position: { frac, fill: expFill, entry_date: "2026-11-13", mark: 28000 } });
const exitFill = 27900 - SLIP;
const exitWeekPnl = frac * PV * (exitFill - 28000);
const realized = frac * PV * (exitFill - expFill);
results.R6_exit_math =
  r.status === "RESOLVED" &&
  r.state!.position === null &&
  Math.abs(r.state!.equity - (eqAfterHold + exitWeekPnl)) < 1e-6;
notes.R6 = `exit fill ${exitFill}, week pnl ${exitWeekPnl.toFixed(2)}, realized ${realized.toFixed(2)}, final equity ${(eqAfterHold + exitWeekPnl).toFixed(2)}`;

// R7: out-of-order guard: pending Entry 1 followed by a newer Entry 2.
const twoEntryLog = ENTRY0 + ENTRY0.replace("## Entry 0", "## Entry 2");
r = resolve(twoEntryLog, REAL_BARS, "2026-11-15", { equity: 100000, position: null });
results.R7_out_of_order_refused = r.status === "REFUSED" && r.reason!.includes("resolve in order");
notes.R7 = r.reason ?? "";

// R8: double-enter guard.
r = resolve(entryLog, REAL_BARS, "2026-11-15", {
  equity: 100000, position: { frac: 0.55, fill: 27000, entry_date: "2026-11-13", mark: 27500 } });
results.R8_double_enter_refused = r.status === "REFUSED" && r.reason!.includes("enter with position open");
notes.R8 = r.reason ?? "";

// R9: classification of the hold plan text (mentions exit + Monday).
results.R9_hold_not_exit = classify(
  "hold the existing paper position (0.55 MNQ, entry fill 26,878.86); signal still LONG, no new buy, no stop.") === "hold";
results.R9_exit_classified = classify(
  "signal exit at the Monday 2026-11-23 open: paper sell 0.55 MNQ (fill at open - 0.3625 pts); the 12-week log return turned negative, rule set T goes flat.") === "exit";
results.R9_enter_classified = classify(
  "go LONG at the Monday open: paper buy 0.55 MNQ equivalent; no stop, signal exits only.") === "enter";
results.R9_flat_classified = classify(
  "none; stay flat for the week of 2026-11-09. Flat is the decision.") === "flat";

// R10: log equity parse (stage7_log_entry.py regex) picks up the appended
// post-outcome equity line as the LAST match.
const logWithOutcome = ENTRY0 +
  "- Outcome (resolved, week closing 2026-11-13): flat as planned; no position, no P&L. Week close 28,622.25.\n" +
  "- Deviation from plan: none (booked from the logged plan and the canonical bars only)\n" +
  "- Paper equity: 100,000.00 USD (post-outcome, week closing 2026-11-13)\n";
const matches = logWithOutcome.match(/Paper equity:\s*([\d,.]+)\s*USD/g)!;
const last = matches[matches.length - 1];
results.R10_equity_parse = matches.length === 2 && last.includes("100,000.00");
notes.R10 = `matches=${matches.length}, last=${last}`;

const allPassed = Object.values(results).every(Boolean);
const report = {
  mirror: "stage7_outcome_ts_mirror.ts (mirror of research/stage7_outcome.py)",
  executed: new Date().toISOString(),
  scope: "logic validation only; the real Sunday log and paper state untouched; "
    + "no performance statistic computed; the spent 2021+ holdout never touched",
  cases: results, notes,
  all_passed: allPassed,
};
console.log(JSON.stringify(report, null, 2));
if (!allPassed) process.exit(1);

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/research/stage7_outcome_ts_mirror.ts
